ubhatauvaḍḍhakaṃ|

sattavidhaina  saṅkhārakkhandhau – atthi kusalau, atthi akusalau, atthi abyākatau; atthi kāmāvacarau, atthi rūpāvacarau, atthi arūpāvacarau, atthi apariyāpan‍nau| aivaṃ sattavidhaina saṅkhārakkhandhau|

aparaupi sattavidhaina  saṅkhārakkhandhau – atthi sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau, atthi dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau, atthi adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau; atthi kāmāvacarau, atthi rūpāvacarau, atthi arūpāvacarau, atthi apariyāpan‍nau…pai॰… atthi ajjhattārammaṇau, atthi bahiddhārammaṇau atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇau; atthi kāmāvacarau, atthi rūpāvacarau, atthi arūpāvacarau, atthi apariyāpan‍nau| aivaṃ sattavidhaina saṅkhārakkhandhau|

catuvīsatividhaina saṅkhārakkhandhau – cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā saṅkhārakkhandhau atthi kusalau, atthi akusalau, atthi abyākatau; sautasamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… ghānasamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… kāyasamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… manausamphassapac‍cayā saṅkhārakkhandhau atthi kusalau, atthi akusalau, atthi abyākatau; cakkhusamphassajā caitanā…pai॰… manausamphassajā caitanā| aivaṃ catuvīsatividhaina saṅkhārakkhandhau|

aparaupi  catuvīsatividhaina saṅkhārakkhandhau – cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā saṅkhārakkhandhau atthi sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau…pai॰… atthi ajjhattārammaṇau, atthi bahiddhārammaṇau, atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇau; cakkhusamphassajā caitanā…pai॰… manausamphassajā caitanā; sautasamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… ghānasamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… kāyasamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… manausamphassapac‍cayā saṅkhārakkhandhau atthi sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau…pai॰… atthi ajjhattārammaṇau, atthi bahiddhārammaṇau, atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇau; cakkhusamphassajā caitanā, sautasamphassajā caitanā, ghānasamphassajā caitanā, jivhāsamphassajā caitanā, kāyasamphassajā caitanā, manausamphassajā caitanā| aivaṃ catuvīsatividhaina saṅkhārakkhandhau|

tiṃsatividhaina  saṅkhārakkhandhau – cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā saṅkhārakkhandhau atthi kāmāvacarau, atthi rūpāvacarau, atthi arūpāvacarau, atthi apariyāpan‍nau; sautasamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… ghānasamphassapac‍cayā  …pai॰… jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… kāyasamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… manausamphassapac‍cayā saṅkhārakkhandhau atthi kāmāvacarau, atthi rūpāvacarau, atthi arūpāvacarau, atthi apariyāpan‍nau; cakkhusamphassajā caitanā…pai॰… manausamphassajā caitanā| aivaṃ tiṃsatividhaina saṅkhārakkhandhau|

bahuvidhaina saṅkhārakkhandhau – cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā saṅkhārakkhandhau atthi kusalau, atthi akusalau, atthi abyākatau, atthi kāmāvacarau, atthi rūpāvacarau, atthi arūpāvacarau, atthi apariyāpan‍nau; sautasamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… ghānasamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… kāyasamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… manausamphassapac‍cayā saṅkhārakkhandhau atthi kusalau, atthi akusalau, atthi abyākatau, atthi kāmāvacarau, atthi rūpāvacarau, atthi arūpāvacarau, atthi apariyāpan‍nau; cakkhusamphassajā caitanā, sautasamphassajā caitanā, ghānasamphassajā caitanā, jivhāsamphassajā caitanā, kāyasamphassajā caitanā, manausamphassajā caitanā| aivaṃ bahuvidhaina saṅkhārakkhandhau|

aparaupi bahuvidhaina saṅkhārakkhandhau – cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā saṅkhārakkhandhau atthi sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau, atthi dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau, atthi adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau…pai॰… atthi ajjhattārammaṇau, atthi bahiddhārammaṇau, atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇau, atthi kāmāvacarau, atthi rūpāvacarau, atthi  arūpāvacarau, atthi apariyāpan‍nau; sautasamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… ghānasamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… kāyasamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… manausamphassapac‍cayā saṅkhārakkhandhau atthi sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau  …pai॰… atthi ajjhattārammaṇau, atthi bahiddhārammaṇau, atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇau, atthi kāmāvacarau, atthi rūpāvacarau, atthi arūpāvacarau, atthi apariyāpan‍nau; cakkhusamphassajā caitanā, sautasamphassajā caitanā, ghānasamphassajā caitanā, jivhāsamphassajā caitanā, kāyasamphassajā caitanā, manausamphassajā caitanā| aivaṃ bahuvidhaina saṅkhārakkhandhau|

ayaṃ vuc‍cati saṅkhārakkhandhau|

5. viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau

121. tattha  katamau viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau? aikavidhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – phassasampayuttau|

duvidhaina  viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – atthi sahaitukau, atthi ahaitukau|

tividhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – atthi kusalau, atthi akusalau, atthi abyākatau|

catubbidhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – atthi kāmāvacarau, atthi rūpāvacarau, atthi arūpāvacarau, atthi apariyāpan‍nau|

pañ‍cavidhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – atthi sukhindriyasampayuttau, atthi dukkhindriyasampayuttau, atthi saumanassindriyasampayuttau, atthi daumanassindriyasampayuttau, atthi upaikkhindriyasampayuttau| aivaṃ pañ‍cavidhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau|

chabbidhaina  viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| aivaṃ chabbidhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau|

sattavidhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, manaudhātu, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu| aivaṃ sattavidhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau|

aṭṭhavidhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ atthi sukhasahagataṃ, atthi dukkhasahagataṃ, manaudhātu, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu| aivaṃ aṭṭhavidhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau|

navavidhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, manaudhātu, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu atthi kusalaṃ, atthi akusalaṃ, atthi abyākataṃ| aivaṃ navavidhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau|

dasavidhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ atthi sukhasahagataṃ, atthi dukkhasahagataṃ, manaudhātu, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu atthi kusalaṃ, atthi akusalaṃ, atthi abyākataṃ| aivaṃ dasavidhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau|

122. aikavidhaina  viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – phassasampayuttau|

duvidhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – atthi sahaitukau, atthi ahaitukau|

tividhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – atthi sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau, atthi dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau, atthi adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau| atthi  vipākau, atthi vipākadhammadhammau, atthi naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau| atthi upādin‍nupādāniyau, atthi anupādin‍nupādāniyau, atthi anupādin‍naanupādāniyau| atthi saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau, atthi asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau, atthi asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikau| atthi savitak‍kasavicārau, atthi avitak‍kavicāramattau, atthi avitak‍kaavicārau| atthi pītisahagatau, atthi sukhasahagatau, atthi upaikkhāsahagatau| atthi dassanaina pahātabbau, atthi bhāvanāya pahātabbau, atthi naiva dassanaina na bhāvanāya pahātabbau| atthi  dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau, atthi bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau, atthi naiva dassanaina na bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau| atthi ācayagāmī, atthi apacayagāmī, atthi naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī| atthi saikkhau, atthi asaikkhau, atthi naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau| atthi parittau, atthi mahaggatau, atthi appamāṇau| atthi parittārammaṇau, atthi mahaggatārammaṇau, atthi appamāṇārammaṇau| atthi hīnau, atthi majjhimau, atthi paṇītau| atthi micchattaniyatau, atthi sammattaniyatau, atthi aniyatau| atthi maggārammaṇau, atthi maggahaitukau, atthi maggādhipati| atthi uppan‍nau, atthi anuppan‍nau, atthi uppādī| atthi atītau, atthi anāgatau, atthi pac‍cuppan‍nau| atthi atītārammaṇau, atthi anāgatārammaṇau, atthi pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇau| atthi ajjhattau, atthi bahiddhau, atthi ajjhattabahiddhau| atthi ajjhattārammaṇau, atthi bahiddhārammaṇau, atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇau…pai॰…| aivaṃ dasavidhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau|

123. aikavidhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – phassasampayuttau|

duvidhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – atthi haitusampayuttau, atthi haituvippayuttau| atthi na haitu sahaitukau, atthi na haitu ahaitukau| atthi laukiyau, atthi laukuttarau| atthi kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyau, atthi kainaci na viñ‍ñaiyyau| atthi sāsavau, atthi anāsavau| atthi āsavasampayuttau, atthi āsavavippayuttau| atthi āsavavippayuttasāsavau, atthi āsavavippayuttaanāsavau| atthi saṃyaujaniyau, atthi asaṃyaujaniyau| atthi saṃyaujanasampayuttau, atthi saṃyaujanavippayuttau| atthi saṃyaujanavippayuttasaṃyaujaniyau, atthi saṃyaujanavippayuttaasaṃyaujaniyau|

atthi ganthaniyau, atthi aganthaniyau| atthi ganthasampayuttau, atthi ganthavippayuttau| atthi ganthavippayuttaganthaniyau, atthi ganthavippayuttaaganthaniyau| atthi aughaniyau, atthi anaughaniyau| atthi aughasampayuttau, atthi aughavippayuttau| atthi aughavippayuttaaughaniyau, atthi aughavippayuttaanaughaniyau| atthi yauganiyau, atthi ayauganiyau| atthi yaugasampayuttau, atthi yaugavippayuttau| atthi yaugavippayuttayauganiyau, atthi yaugavippayuttaayauganiyau| atthi nīvaraṇiyau, atthi anīvaraṇiyau| atthi nīvaraṇasampayuttau, atthi nīvaraṇavippayuttau| atthi nīvaraṇavippayuttanīvaraṇiyau, atthi nīvaraṇavippayuttaanīvaraṇiyau|

atthi  parāmaṭṭhau, atthi aparāmaṭṭhau| atthi parāmāsasampayuttau, atthi parāmāsavippayuttau| atthi parāmāsavippayuttaparāmaṭṭhau, atthi parāmāsavippayuttaaparāmaṭṭhau| atthi upādin‍nau, atthi anupādin‍nau| atthi upādāniyau, atthi anupādāniyau| atthi upādānasampayuttau, atthi upādānavippayuttau| atthi upādānavippayuttaupādāniyau, atthi upādānavippayuttaanupādāniyau| atthi saṃkilaisikau, atthi asaṃkilaisikau| atthi saṃkiliṭṭhau, atthi asaṃkiliṭṭhau| atthi kilaisasampayuttau, atthi kilaisavippayuttau| atthi kilaisavippayuttasaṃkilaisikau, atthi kilaisavippayuttaasaṃkilaisikau| atthi dassanaina pahātabbau, atthi na dassanaina pahātabbau| atthi bhāvanāya pahātabbau, atthi na bhāvanāya pahātabbau| atthi dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau, atthi na dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau| atthi bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau, atthi na bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau|

atthi savitak‍kau, atthi avitak‍kau| atthi savicārau, atthi avicārau| atthi sappītikau, atthi appītikau| atthi pītisahagatau, atthi na pītisahagatau| atthi sukhasahagatau, atthi na sukhasahagatau| atthi upaikkhāsahagatau, atthi na upaikkhāsahagatau| atthi kāmāvacarau, atthi na kāmāvacarau| atthi rūpāvacarau, atthi na rūpāvacarau| atthi arūpāvacarau, atthi na arūpāvacarau, atthi pariyāpan‍nau, atthi apariyāpan‍nau| atthi niyyānikau, atthi aniyyānikau| atthi niyatau, atthi aniyatau| atthi sauttarau, atthi anuttarau| atthi saraṇau, atthi araṇau|

tividhaina  viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – atthi kusalau, atthi akusalau, atthi abyākatau…pai॰…| aivaṃ dasavidhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau|

ubhatauvaḍḍhakaṃ|

sattavidhaina  viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – atthi kusalau, atthi akusalau, atthi abyākatau, atthi  kāmāvacarau, atthi rūpāvacarau, atthi arūpāvacarau, atthi apariyāpan‍nau| aivaṃ sattavidhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau|

aparaupi sattavidhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – atthi sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau, atthi dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau, atthi adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau, atthi kāmāvacarau, atthi rūpāvacarau, atthi arūpāvacarau, atthi apariyāpan‍nau…pai॰… atthi ajjhattārammaṇau, atthi bahiddhārammaṇau, atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇau, atthi kāmāvacarau, atthi rūpāvacarau, atthi arūpāvacarau, atthi apariyāpan‍nau| aivaṃ sattavidhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau|

catuvīsatividhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau atthi kusalau, atthi akusalau, atthi abyākatau; sautasamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… ghānasamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… kāyasamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… manausamphassapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau atthi kusalau, atthi akusalau, atthi abyākatau; cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| aivaṃ catuvīsatividhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau|

aparaupi catuvīsatividhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau atthi sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau, atthi dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau, atthi adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau…pai॰… atthi ajjhattārammaṇau, atthi bahiddhārammaṇau, atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇau; cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ; sautasamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… ghānasamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… kāyasamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… manausamphassapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau atthi ajjhattārammaṇau, atthi bahiddhārammaṇau, atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇau; cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| aivaṃ catuvīsatividhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau|

tiṃsatividhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau atthi kāmāvacarau, atthi rūpāvacarau, atthi arūpāvacarau, atthi apariyāpan‍nau; sautasamphassapac‍cayā  …pai॰… ghānasamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… kāyasamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… manausamphassapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau atthi kāmāvacarau, atthi rūpāvacarau, atthi arūpāvacarau, atthi apariyāpan‍nau; cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| aivaṃ tiṃsatividhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau|

bahuvidhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau atthi kusalau, atthi akusalau, atthi abyākatau, atthi kāmāvacarau, atthi rūpāvacarau, atthi arūpāvacarau, atthi apariyāpan‍nau, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ; sautasamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… ghānasamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā  …pai॰… kāyasamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… manausamphassapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau atthi kusalau, atthi akusalau, atthi abyākatau, atthi kāmāvacarau, atthi rūpāvacarau, atthi arūpāvacarau, atthi apariyāpan‍nau, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| aivaṃ bahuvidhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau|

aparaupi  bahuvidhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau atthi sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau…pai॰… atthi ajjhattārammaṇau, atthi bahiddhārammaṇau, atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇau, atthi kāmāvacarau, atthi rūpāvacarau, atthi arūpāvacarau, atthi apariyāpan‍nau; sautasamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… ghānasamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… jivhāsamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… kāyasamphassapac‍cayā…pai॰… manausamphassapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau atthi sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau…pai॰… atthi ajjhattārammaṇau, atthi bahiddhārammaṇau, atthi ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇau, atthi kāmāvacarau, atthi rūpāvacarau, atthi arūpāvacarau, atthi apariyāpan‍nau; cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| aivaṃ bahuvidhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau|

ayaṃ vuc‍cati viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau|

abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ|

3. pañhāpucchakaṃ

150. pañ‍cakkhandhā  – rūpakkhandhau, vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau|

151. pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ kati kusalā, kati  akusalā, kati abyākatā…pai॰… kati saraṇā, kati araṇā?

1. tikaṃ

152. rūpakkhandhau  abyākatau| cattārau khandhā siyā kusalā, siyā akusalā, siyā abyākatā| dvai khandhā na vattabbā – ‘‘sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā’’tipi, ‘‘dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā’’tipi, ‘‘adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā’’tipi| tayau khandhā siyā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā| rūpakkhandhau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau| cattārau khandhā siyā vipākā, siyā vipākadhammadhammā, siyā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā| rūpakkhandhau siyā upādin‍nupādāniyau, siyā anupādin‍nupādāniyau| cattārau khandhā siyā upādin‍nupādāniyā, siyā anupādin‍nupādāniyā, siyā anupādin‍naanupādāniyā|

rūpakkhandhau asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikau| cattārau khandhā siyā saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā, siyā asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā, siyā asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikā| rūpakkhandhau avitak‍kaavicārau| tayau khandhā siyā savitak‍kasavicārā, siyā avitak‍kavicāramattā, siyā avitak‍kaavicārā| saṅkhārakkhandhau siyā savitak‍kasavicārau, siyā avitak‍kavicāramattau, siyā avitak‍kaavicārau, siyā na vattabbau – ‘‘savitak‍kasavicārau’’tipi, ‘‘avitak‍kavicāramattau’’tipi, ‘‘avitak‍kaavicārau’’tipi| rūpakkhandhau na vattabbau – ‘‘pītisahagatau’’tipi, ‘‘sukhasahagatau’’tipi, ‘‘upaikkhāsahagatau’’tipi| vaidanākkhandhau siyā pītisahagatau na sukhasahagatau na upaikkhāsahagatau, siyā na vattabbau – ‘‘pītisahagatau’’ti| tayau  khandhā siyā  pītisahagatā, siyā sukhasahagatā, siyā upaikkhāsahagatā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘pītisahagatā’’tipi, ‘‘sukhasahagatā’’tipi, ‘‘upaikkhāsahagatā’’tipi|

rūpakkhandhau naiva dassanaina na bhāvanāya pahātabbau| cattārau khandhā siyā dassanaina pahātabbā, siyā bhāvanāya pahātabbā, siyā naiva dassanaina na bhāvanāya pahātabbā| rūpakkhandhau naiva dassanaina na bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau| cattārau khandhā siyā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā, siyā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā, siyā naiva dassanaina na bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā| rūpakkhandhau naivācayagāmināpacayagāmī| cattārau khandhā siyā ācayagāminau, siyā apacayagāminau, siyā naivācayagāmināpacayagāminau| rūpakkhandhau naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau| cattārau khandhā siyā saikkhā, siyā asaikkhā, siyā naivasaikkhanāsaikkhā| rūpakkhandhau parittau| cattārau khandhā siyā parittā, siyā mahaggatā, siyā appamāṇā| rūpakkhandhau anārammaṇau| cattārau khandhā siyā parittārammaṇā, siyā mahaggatārammaṇā, siyā appamāṇārammaṇā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘parittārammaṇā’’tipi, ‘‘mahaggatārammaṇā’’tipi, ‘‘appamāṇārammaṇā’’tipi| rūpakkhandhau majjhimau| cattārau khandhā siyā hīnā, siyā majjhimā, siyā paṇītā| rūpakkhandhau aniyatau| cattārau khandhā siyā micchattaniyatā, siyā sammattaniyatā, siyā aniyatā|

rūpakkhandhau anārammaṇau| cattārau khandhā siyā maggārammaṇā, siyā maggahaitukā, siyā maggādhipatinau, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘maggārammaṇā’’tipi, ‘‘maggahaitukā’’tipi, ‘‘maggādhipatinau’’tipi; siyā uppan‍nā, siyā anuppan‍nā, siyā uppādinau; siyā atītā, siyā anāgatā, siyā pac‍cuppan‍nā| rūpakkhandhau anārammaṇau| cattārau khandhā siyā atītārammaṇā, siyā anāgatārammaṇā, siyā pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘atītārammaṇā’’tipi, ‘‘anāgatārammaṇā’’tipi, ‘‘pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇā’’tipi; siyā ajjhattā, siyā bahiddhā, siyā ajjhattabahiddhā| rūpakkhandhau anārammaṇau| cattārau khandhā siyā ajjhattārammaṇā, siyā bahiddhārammaṇā, siyā ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘ajjhattārammaṇā’’tipi, ‘‘bahiddhārammaṇā’’tipi, ‘‘ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇā’’tipi| cattārau khandhā anidassanaappaṭighā| rūpakkhandhau siyā sanidassanasappaṭighau, siyā anidassanasappaṭighau, siyā anidassanaappaṭighau|

2. dukaṃ

153. cattārau  khandhā na haitū| saṅkhārakkhandhau siyā haitu, siyā na haitu| rūpakkhandhau ahaitukau| cattārau khandhā siyā sahaitukā, siyā ahaitukā| rūpakkhandhau haituvippayuttau| cattārau khandhā siyā haitusampayuttā, siyā haituvippayuttā| rūpakkhandhau na vattabbau – ‘‘haitu caiva sahaitukau cā’’tipi, ‘‘sahaitukau caiva na ca haitū’’tipi| tayau khandhā na vattabbā – ‘‘haitū caiva sahaitukā cā’’ti, siyā sahaitukā caiva na ca haitū, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘sahaitukā caiva na ca haitū’’ti| saṅkhārakkhandhau siyā haitu caiva sahaitukau ca, siyā  sahaitukau caiva na ca haitu, siyā na vattabbau – ‘‘haitu caiva sahaitukau cā’’tipi, ‘‘sahaitukau caiva na ca haitū’’tipi| rūpakkhandhau na vattabbau – ‘‘haitu caiva haitusampayuttau cā’’tipi, ‘‘haitusampayuttau caiva na ca haitū’’tipi| tayau khandhā na vattabbā – ‘‘haitū caiva haitusampayuttā cā’’ti, siyā haitusampayuttā caiva na ca haitū, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘haitusampayuttā caiva na ca haitū’’ti| saṅkhārakkhandhau siyā haitu caiva haitusampayuttau ca, siyā haitusampayuttau caiva na  ca haitu, siyā na vattabbau – ‘‘haitu caiva haitusampayuttau cā’’tipi, ‘‘haitusampayuttau caiva na ca haitū’’tipi| rūpakkhandhau na haitu ahaitukau| tayau khandhā siyā na haitū sahaitukā, siyā na haitū ahaitukā| saṅkhārakkhandhau siyā na haitu sahaitukau, siyā na haitu ahaitukau, siyā na vattabbau – ‘‘na haitu sahaitukau’’tipi, ‘‘na haitu ahaitukau’’tipi|

sappac‍cayā, saṅkhatā|

cattārau khandhā anidassanā| rūpakkhandhau siyā sanidassanau, siyā anidassanau| cattārau khandhā appaṭighā| rūpakkhandhau siyā sappaṭighau, siyā appaṭighau| rūpakkhandhau rūpaṃ| cattārau khandhā arūpā| rūpakkhandhau laukiyau| cattārau khandhā siyā laukiyā, siyā laukuttarā; kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyā, kainaci na viñ‍ñaiyyā|

cattārau khandhā nau āsavā| saṅkhārakkhandhau siyā āsavau, siyā nau āsavau| rūpakkhandhau sāsavau| cattārau khandhā  siyā sāsavā, siyā anāsavā| rūpakkhandhau āsavavippayuttau| cattārau khandhā siyā āsavasampayuttā, siyā āsavavippayuttā| rūpakkhandhau  na vattabbau – ‘‘āsavau caiva sāsavau cā’’ti, sāsavau caiva nau ca āsavau| tayau khandhā na vattabbā – ‘‘āsavā caiva sāsavā cā’’ti, siyā sāsavā caiva nau ca āsavā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘sāsavā caiva nau ca āsavā’’ti| saṅkhārakkhandhau siyā āsavau caiva sāsavau ca, siyā sāsavau caiva nau ca āsavau, siyā na vattabbau – ‘‘āsavau caiva sāsavau cā’’tipi, ‘‘sāsavau caiva nau ca āsavau’’tipi| rūpakkhandhau na vattabbau – ‘‘āsavau caiva āsavasampayuttau cā’’tipi, ‘‘āsavasampayuttau caiva nau ca āsavau’’tipi| tayau khandhā na vattabbā – ‘‘āsavā caiva āsavasampayuttā cā’’ti, siyā āsavasampayuttā caiva nau ca āsavā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘āsavasampayuttā caiva nau ca āsavā’’ti| saṅkhārakkhandhau siyā āsavau caiva āsavasampayuttau ca, siyā āsavasampayuttau caiva nau ca āsavau, siyā na vattabbau – ‘‘āsavau caiva āsavasampayuttau cā’’tipi, ‘‘āsavasampayuttau caiva nau ca āsavau’’tipi| rūpakkhandhau āsavavippayuttasāsavau| cattārau khandhā siyā āsavavippayuttasāsavā, siyā āsavavippayuttaanāsavā, siyā  na vattabbā – ‘‘āsavavippayuttasāsavā’’tipi, ‘‘āsavavippayuttaanāsavā’’tipi|

cattārau khandhā nau saṃyaujanā| saṅkhārakkhandhau siyā saṃyaujanaṃ, siyā nau saṃyaujanaṃ| rūpakkhandhau saṃyaujaniyau| cattārau khandhā  siyā saṃyaujaniyā, siyā asaṃyaujaniyā| rūpakkhandhau saṃyaujanavippayuttau| cattārau khandhā siyā saṃyaujanasampayuttā, siyā saṃyaujanavippayuttā| rūpakkhandhau na vattabbau – ‘‘saṃyaujanañ‍caiva saṃyaujaniyau cā’’ti, ‘‘saṃyaujaniyau caiva nau ca saṃyaujanaṃ’’| tayau khandhā na vattabbā – ‘‘saṃyaujanā caiva saṃyaujaniyā cā’’ti, siyā saṃyaujaniyā caiva  nau ca saṃyaujanā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘saṃyaujaniyā caiva nau ca saṃyaujanā’’ti| saṅkhārakkhandhau siyā saṃyaujanañ‍caiva saṃyaujaniyau ca, siyā saṃyaujaniyau caiva nau ca saṃyaujanaṃ, siyā na vattabbau – ‘‘saṃyaujanañ‍caiva saṃyaujaniyau cā’’tipi, ‘‘saṃyaujaniyau caiva nau ca saṃyaujana’’ntipi| rūpakkhandhau na vattabbau – ‘‘saṃyaujanañ‍caiva saṃyaujanasampayuttau cā’’tipi, ‘‘saṃyaujanasampayuttau caiva nau ca saṃyaujana’’ntipi| tayau khandhā na vattabbā – ‘‘saṃyaujanā caiva saṃyaujanasampayuttā cā’’ti, siyā saṃyaujanasampayuttā caiva nau ca saṃyaujanā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘saṃyaujanasampayuttā caiva nau ca saṃyaujanā’’ti| saṅkhārakkhandhau siyā saṃyaujanañ‍caiva saṃyaujanasampayuttau ca, siyā saṃyaujanasampayuttau caiva nau ca saṃyaujanaṃ, siyā na vattabbau – ‘‘saṃyaujanañ‍caiva saṃyaujanasampayuttau  cā’’tipi, ‘‘saṃyaujanasampayuttau caiva nau ca saṃyaujana’’ntipi| rūpakkhandhau saṃyaujanavippayuttasaṃyaujaniyau| cattārau khandhā siyā saṃyaujanavippayuttasaṃyaujaniyā, siyā saṃyaujanavippayuttaasaṃyaujaniyā, siyā na  vattabbā – ‘‘saṃyaujanavippayuttasaṃyaujaniyā’’tipi, ‘‘saṃyaujanavippayuttaasaṃyaujaniyā’’tipi|

cattārau khandhā nau ganthā| saṅkhārakkhandhau siyā ganthau, siyā nau ganthau| rūpakkhandhau ganthaniyau| cattārau khandhā siyā ganthaniyā, siyā aganthaniyā| rūpakkhandhau ganthavippayuttau| cattārau khandhā siyā ganthasampayuttā, siyā ganthavippayuttā| rūpakkhandhau na vattabbau – ‘‘ganthau caiva ganthaniyau cā’’ti, ‘‘ganthaniyau caiva nau ca ganthau’’| tayau khandhā na vattabbā  – ‘‘ganthā caiva ganthaniyā cā’’ti, siyā ganthaniyā caiva nau ca ganthā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘ganthaniyā caiva nau ca ganthā’’ti| saṅkhārakkhandhau siyā ganthau caiva ganthaniyau ca, siyā ganthaniyau caiva nau ca ganthau, siyā na vattabbau – ‘‘ganthau caiva ganthaniyau cā’’tipi, ‘‘ganthaniyau caiva nau ca ganthau’’tipi| rūpakkhandhau na vattabbau – ‘‘ganthau caiva ganthasampayuttau cā’’tipi, ‘‘ganthasampayuttau caiva nau ca ganthau’’tipi| tayau khandhā na vattabbā – ‘‘ganthā caiva ganthasampayuttā cā’’ti, siyā ganthasampayuttā caiva nau ca ganthā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘ganthasampayuttā caiva nau ca ganthā’’ti| saṅkhārakkhandhau siyā ganthau caiva ganthasampayuttau ca, siyā ganthasampayuttau caiva nau ca ganthau, siyā na vattabbau – ‘‘ganthau caiva ganthasampayuttau cā’’tipi, ‘‘ganthasampayuttau caiva nau ca ganthau’’ti pi| rūpakkhandhau ganthavippayuttaganthaniyau| cattārau khandhā siyā ganthavippayuttaganthaniyā, siyā ganthavippayuttaaganthaniyā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘ganthavippayuttaganthaniyā’’tipi, ‘‘ganthavippayuttaaganthaniyā’’tipi|

cattārau khandhā nau aughā…pai॰… nau yaugā…pai॰… nau  nīvaraṇā| saṅkhārakkhandhau siyā nīvaraṇaṃ, siyā nau nīvaraṇaṃ| rūpakkhandhau nīvaraṇiyau| cattārau khandhā siyā nīvaraṇiyā, siyā anīvaraṇiyā| rūpakkhandhau nīvaraṇavippayuttau| cattārau khandhā siyā nīvaraṇasampayuttā, siyā nīvaraṇavippayuttā| rūpakkhandhau na vattabbau – ‘‘nīvaraṇañ‍caiva nīvaraṇiyau cā’’ti, ‘‘nīvaraṇiyau caiva nau ca nīvaraṇaṃ’’| tayau khandhā na vattabbā – ‘‘nīvaraṇā caiva nīvaraṇiyā cā’’ti, siyā nīvaraṇiyā caiva nau ca nīvaraṇā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘nīvaraṇiyā caiva nau ca nīvaraṇā’’ti| saṅkhārakkhandhau siyā nīvaraṇañ‍caiva nīvaraṇiyau  ca, siyā nīvaraṇiyau caiva nau ca nīvaraṇaṃ, siyā na vattabbau – ‘‘nīvaraṇañ‍caiva nīvaraṇiyau cā’’tipi, ‘‘nīvaraṇiyau caiva nau ca nīvaraṇa’’ntipi| rūpakkhandhau na vattabbau – ‘‘nīvaraṇañ‍caiva nīvaraṇasampayuttau cā’’tipi, ‘‘nīvaraṇasampayuttau caiva nau ca nīvaraṇa’’ntipi| tayau khandhā na vattabbā – ‘‘nīvaraṇā caiva nīvaraṇasampayuttā cā’’ti, siyā nīvaraṇasampayuttā caiva nau ca nīvaraṇā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘nīvaraṇasampayuttā caiva nau ca nīvaraṇā’’ti| saṅkhārakkhandhau siyā nīvaraṇañ‍caiva nīvaraṇasampayuttau ca, siyā nīvaraṇasampayuttau caiva nau ca nīvaraṇaṃ, siyā na vattabbau – ‘‘nīvaraṇañ‍caiva nīvaraṇasampayuttau cā’’tipi, ‘‘nīvaraṇasampayuttau caiva nau  ca nīvaraṇa’’ntipi| rūpakkhandhau  nīvaraṇavippayuttanīvaraṇiyau| cattārau khandhā siyā nīvaraṇavippayuttanīvaraṇiyā, siyā nīvaraṇavippayuttaanīvaraṇiyā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘nīvaraṇavippayuttanīvaraṇiyā’’tipi, ‘‘nīvaraṇavippayuttaanīvaraṇiyā’’tipi|

cattārau khandhā nau parāmāsā| saṅkhārakkhandhau siyā parāmāsau, siyā nau parāmāsau| rūpakkhandhau parāmaṭṭhau| cattārau khandhā siyā parāmaṭṭhā, siyā aparāmaṭṭhā| rūpakkhandhau parāmāsavippayuttau| tayau khandhā siyā parāmāsasampayuttā, siyā parāmāsavippayuttā| saṅkhārakkhandhau siyā parāmāsasampayuttau, siyā parāmāsavippayuttau, siyā na vattabbau – ‘‘parāmāsasampayuttau’’tipi, ‘‘parāmāsavippayuttau’’tipi| rūpakkhandhau na vattabbau – ‘‘parāmāsau caiva parāmaṭṭhau cā’’ti, ‘‘parāmaṭṭhau caiva nau ca parāmāsau’’| tayau khandhā na vattabbā – ‘‘parāmāsā caiva parāmaṭṭhā cā’’ti, siyā parāmaṭṭhā caiva nau ca parāmāsā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘parāmaṭṭhā caiva nau ca parāmāsā’’ti| saṅkhārakkhandhau siyā parāmāsau caiva parāmaṭṭhau ca, siyā parāmaṭṭhau caiva nau ca parāmāsau, siyā na vattabbau – ‘‘parāmāsau caiva parāmaṭṭhau cā’’tipi, ‘‘parāmaṭṭhau caiva nau ca parāmāsau’’tipi| rūpakkhandhau parāmāsavippayuttaparāmaṭṭhau| cattārau khandhā siyā parāmāsavippayuttaparāmaṭṭhā, siyā parāmāsavippayuttaaparāmaṭṭhā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘parāmāsavippayuttaparāmaṭṭhā’’tipi, ‘‘parāmāsavippayuttaaparāmaṭṭhā’’tipi|

rūpakkhandhau  anārammaṇau| cattārau khandhā sārammaṇā| viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau cittaṃ| cattārau khandhā nau cittā| tayau khandhā caitasikā| dvai khandhā acaitasikā| tayau khandhā cittasampayuttā| rūpakkhandhau cittavippayuttau| viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau na vattabbau – ‘‘cittaina sampayuttau’’tipi, ‘‘cittaina vippayuttau’’tipi| tayau khandhā cittasaṃsaṭṭhā| rūpakkhandhau cittavisaṃsaṭṭhau| viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau na vattabbau – ‘‘cittaina saṃsaṭṭhau’’tipi, ‘‘cittaina visaṃsaṭṭhau’’tipi| tayau khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānā| viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau nau cittasamuṭṭhānau| rūpakkhandhau siyā cittasamuṭṭhānau, siyā nau cittasamuṭṭhānau| tayau khandhā cittasahabhunau| viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau nau cittasahabhū| rūpakkhandhau siyā cittasahabhū, siyā nau cittasahabhū| tayau khandhā cittānuparivattinau| viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau nau cittānuparivatti| rūpakkhandhau  siyā cittānuparivatti, siyā nau cittānuparivatti| tayau khandhā cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānā| dvai khandhā nau cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānā| tayau khandhā cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhunau| dvai khandhā nau cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhunau| tayau khandhā cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattinau| dvai khandhā nau cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattinau|

viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau ajjhattikau| tayau khandhā bāhirā| rūpakkhandhau siyā ajjhattikau, siyā bāhirau| cattārau khandhā nau upādā| rūpakkhandhau siyā upādā, siyā nau upādā, siyā upādin‍nā, siyā anupādin‍nā| cattārau  khandhā nau upādānā| saṅkhārakkhandhau siyā upādānaṃ, siyā nau upādānaṃ| rūpakkhandhau upādāniyau| cattārau khandhā siyā upādāniyā, siyā anupādāniyā| rūpakkhandhau upādānavippayuttau| cattārau khandhā siyā upādānasampayuttā, siyā upādānavippayuttā| rūpakkhandhau na vattabbau – ‘‘upādānañ‍caiva upādāniyau cā’’ti, ‘‘upādāniyau caiva nau ca upādānaṃ’’| tayau khandhā na vattabbā – ‘‘upādānañ‍caiva upādāniyā cā’’ti, siyā upādāniyā caiva nau ca upādānā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘upādāniyā caiva nau ca upādānā’’ti| saṅkhārakkhandhau siyā upādānañ‍caiva upādāniyau ca, siyā upādāniyau caiva nau ca upādānaṃ, siyā na vattabbau – ‘‘upādānañ‍caiva upādāniyau cā’’tipi, ‘‘upādāniyau caiva nau ca upādāna’’ntipi| rūpakkhandhau na vattabbau – ‘‘upādānañ‍caiva upādānasampayuttau  cā’’tipi, ‘‘upādānasampayuttau caiva nau ca upādāna’’ntipi| tayau khandhā na vattabbā – ‘‘upādānā caiva upādānasampayuttā cā’’ti, siyā upādānasampayuttā caiva nau ca upādānā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘upādānasampayuttā caiva nau ca upādānā’’ti| saṅkhārakkhandhau siyā upādānañ‍caiva upādānasampayuttau ca, siyā upādānasampayuttau caiva nau ca upādānaṃ, siyā na vattabbau – ‘‘upādānañ‍caiva  upādānasampayuttau cā’’tipi, ‘‘upādānasampayuttau caiva nau ca upādāna’’ntipi| rūpakkhandhau upādānavippayuttaupādāniyau| cattārau khandhā siyā upādānavippayuttaupādāniyā, siyā upādānavippayuttaanupādāniyā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘upādānavippayuttaupādāniyā’’tipi, ‘‘upādānavippayuttaanupādāniyā’’tipi|

cattārau khandhā nau kilaisā| saṅkhārakkhandhau siyā kilaisau, siyā nau kilaisau| rūpakkhandhau saṃkilaisikau| cattārau khandhā siyā saṃkilaisikā, siyā asaṃkilaisikā| rūpakkhandhau asaṃkiliṭṭhau| cattārau khandhā siyā saṃkiliṭṭhā, siyā asaṃkiliṭṭhā| rūpakkhandhau kilaisavippayuttau| cattārau khandhā siyā kilaisasampayuttā, siyā kilaisavippayuttā| rūpakkhandhau na vattabbau – ‘‘kilaisau caiva saṃkilaisikau cā’’ti, ‘‘saṃkilaisikau caiva nau ca kilaisau’’| tayau khandhā na vattabbā – ‘‘kilaisā caiva saṃkilaisikā cā’’ti, siyā saṃkilaisikā caiva nau ca kilaisā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘saṃkilaisikā caiva nau ca kilaisā’’ti| saṅkhārakkhandhau siyā kilaisau caiva saṃkilaisikau ca, siyā saṃkilaisikau caiva nau ca kilaisau, siyā na vattabbau – ‘‘kilaisau caiva saṃkilaisikau cā’’tipi, ‘‘saṃkilaisikau caiva nau ca kilaisau’’tipi| rūpakkhandhau na vattabbau – ‘‘kilaisau caiva saṃkiliṭṭhau cā’’tipi, ‘‘saṃkiliṭṭhau caiva nau  ca kilaisau’’tipi| tayau khandhā na vattabbā – ‘‘kilaisau caiva saṃkiliṭṭhā cā’’ti, siyā saṃkiliṭṭhā caiva nau ca kilaisā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘saṃkiliṭṭhā caiva nau ca kilaisā’’ti| saṅkhārakkhandhau siyā kilaisau caiva saṃkiliṭṭhau ca, siyā saṃkiliṭṭhau caiva nau ca kilaisau, siyā na vattabbau – ‘‘kilaisau caiva saṃkiliṭṭhau cā’’tipi, ‘‘saṃkiliṭṭhau caiva nau ca kilaisau’’tipi|

rūpakkhandhau na vattabbau – ‘‘kilaisau caiva kilaisasampayuttau cā’’tipi, ‘‘kilaisasampayuttau caiva nau ca kilaisau’’tipi| tayau khandhā na vattabbā – ‘‘kilaisā caiva kilaisasampayuttā cā’’ti, siyā kilaisasampayuttā caiva nau ca kilaisā, siyā na vattabbā – kilaisasampayuttā caiva nau ca kilaisā’’ti| saṅkhārakkhandhau siyā kilaisau caiva kilaisasampayuttau ca, siyā kilaisasampayuttau caiva nau ca kilaisau, siyā na vattabbau – ‘‘kilaisau caiva kilaisasampayuttau cā’’tipi, ‘‘kilaisasampayuttau caiva nau ca kilaisau’’tipi| rūpakkhandhau kilaisavippayuttasaṃkilaisikau| cattārau khandhā siyā kilaisavippayuttasaṃkilaisikā, siyā  kilaisavippayuttaasaṃkilaisikā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘kilaisavippayuttasaṃkilaisikā’’tipi, ‘‘kilaisavippayuttaasaṃkilaisikā’’tipi|

rūpakkhandhau na dassanaina pahātabbau| cattārau khandhā siyā dassanaina pahātabbā, siyā na dassanaina pahātabbā| rūpakkhandhau na bhāvanāya pahātabbau| cattārau khandhā siyā bhāvanāya pahātabbā, siyā na bhāvanāya pahātabbā| rūpakkhandhau na dassanaina pahātabbahaitukau| cattārau khandhā siyā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā, siyā na dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā| rūpakkhandhau na bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukau| cattārau khandhā siyā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā, siyā na bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā| rūpakkhandhau avitak‍kau| cattārau khandhā siyā savitak‍kā, siyā avitak‍kā| rūpakkhandhau avicārau| cattārau  khandhā siyā savicārā, siyā avicārā| rūpakkhandhau appītikau, cattārau khandhā siyā sappītikā, siyā appītikā| rūpakkhandhau na pītisahagatau| cattārau khandhā siyā pītisahagatā, siyā na pītisahagatā| dvai khandhā na sukhasahagatā| tayau khandhā siyā sukhasahagatā, siyā na sukhasahagatā| dvai khandhā na upaikkhāsahagatā| tayau khandhā siyā upaikkhāsahagatā, siyā na upaikkhāsahagatā|

rūpakkhandhau kāmāvacarau| cattārau khandhā siyā kāmāvacarā, siyā na kāmāvacarā| rūpakkhandhau na rūpāvacarau| cattārau khandhā siyā rūpāvacarā, siyā na rūpāvacarā| rūpakkhandhau na arūpāvacarau| cattārau khandhā siyā arūpāvacarā, siyā na arūpāvacarā| rūpakkhandhau pariyāpan‍nau| cattārau khandhā siyā pariyāpan‍nā, siyā apariyāpan‍nā| rūpakkhandhau aniyyānikau| cattārau khandhā siyā niyyānikā, siyā aniyyānikā| rūpakkhandhau aniyatau| cattārau khandhā siyā niyatā, siyā aniyatā| rūpakkhandhau sauttarau| cattārau khandhā siyā sauttarā, siyā anuttarā| rūpakkhandhau araṇau| cattārau khandhā siyā saraṇā, siyā araṇāti|

pañhāpucchakaṃ|

khandhavibhaṅgau niṭṭhitau|

2. āyatanavibhaṅgau

1. suttantabhājanīyaṃ

154. dvādasāyatanāni  – cakkhāyatanaṃ, rūpāyatanaṃ, sautāyatanaṃ, saddāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, gandhāyatanaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ, rasāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ|

cakkhuṃ anic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ anattā vipariṇāmadhammaṃ| rūpā anic‍cā dukkhā anattā vipariṇāmadhammā| sautaṃ anic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ anattā vipariṇāmadhammaṃ| saddā anic‍cā dukkhā anattā vipariṇāmadhammā| ghānaṃ anic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ anattā vipariṇāmadhammaṃ| gandhā anic‍cā dukkhā anattā vipariṇāmadhammā| jivhā anic‍cā dukkhā anattā vipariṇāmadhammā| rasā anic‍cā dukkhā anattā vipariṇāmadhammā| kāyau anic‍cau dukkhau anattā vipariṇāmadhammau| phauṭṭhabbā anic‍cā dukkhā anattā vipariṇāmadhammā| manau anic‍cau dukkhau anattā vipariṇāmadhammau| dhammā anic‍cā dukkhā anattā vipariṇāmadhammā|

suttantabhājanīyaṃ|

2. abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ

155. dvādasāyatanāni  – cakkhāyatanaṃ, sautāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ, rūpāyatanaṃ, saddāyatanaṃ, gandhāyatanaṃ, rasāyatanaṃ, phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ|

156. tattha katamaṃ cakkhāyatanaṃ? yaṃ cakkhu cakkhuṃ (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰) dha॰ sa॰ 596-599 catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau attabhāvapariyāpan‍nau anidassanau sappaṭighau, yaina cakkhunā  anidassanaina sappaṭighaina rūpaṃ sanidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ passi vā passati vā passissati vā passai vā, cakkhumpaitaṃ cakkhāyatanampaitaṃ cakkhudhātupaisā cakkhundriyampaitaṃ laukaupaisau dvārāpaisā samuddaupaisau paṇḍarampaitaṃ khaittampaitaṃ vatthumpaitaṃ naittampaitaṃ nayanampaitaṃ aurimaṃ tīrampaitaṃ suñ‍ñau gāmaupaisau| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘cakkhāyatanaṃ’’|

157. tattha  katamaṃ sautāyatanaṃ? yaṃ sautaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau attabhāvapariyāpan‍nau anidassanau sappaṭighau, yaina sautaina anidassanaina sappaṭighaina saddaṃ anidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ suṇi vā suṇāti vā suṇissati vā suṇai vā, sautampaitaṃ sautāyatanampaitaṃ sautadhātupaisā sautindriyampaitaṃ laukaupaisau dvārāpaisā samuddaupaisau paṇḍarampaitaṃ khaittampaitaṃ vatthumpaitaṃ aurimaṃ tīrampaitaṃ suñ‍ñau gāmaupaisau| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sautāyatanaṃ’’|

158. tattha katamaṃ ghānāyatanaṃ? yaṃ ghānaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau attabhāvapariyāpan‍nau anidassanau sappaṭighau, yaina ghānaina anidassanaina sappaṭighaina gandhaṃ anidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ ghāyi vā ghāyati vā ghāyissati vā ghāyai vā, ghānampaitaṃ ghānāyatanampaitaṃ ghānadhātupaisā ghānindriyampaitaṃ laukaupaisau dvārāpaisā samuddaupaisau paṇḍarampaitaṃ khaittampaitaṃ vatthumpaitaṃ aurimaṃ tīrampaitaṃ suñ‍ñau gāmaupaisau| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘ghānāyatanaṃ’’|

159. tattha katamaṃ jivhāyatanaṃ? yā jivhā catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau attabhāvapariyāpan‍nau anidassanau sappaṭighau, yāya jivhāya anidassanāya sappaṭighāya rasaṃ anidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ sāyi vā sāyati vā sāyissati vā sāyai vā, jivhāpaisā jivhāyatanampaitaṃ  jivhādhātupaisā jīvhindriyampaitaṃ laukaupaisau dvārāpaisā samuddaupaisau paṇḍarampaitaṃ khaittampaitaṃ vatthumpaitaṃ aurimaṃ tīrampaitaṃ suñ‍ñau gāmaupaisau| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘jivhāyatanaṃ’’|

160. tattha katamaṃ kāyāyatanaṃ? yau kāyau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau attabhāvapariyāpan‍nau anidassanau sappaṭighau, yaina kāyaina anidassanaina sappaṭighaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ anidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ phusi vā phusati vā phusissati vā phusai vā, kāyaupaisau kāyāyatanampaitaṃ kāyadhātupaisā kāyindriyampaitaṃ laukaupaisau dvārāpaisā samuddaupaisau paṇḍarampaitaṃ khaittampaitaṃ vatthumpaitaṃ aurimaṃ tīrampaitaṃ suñ‍ñau gāmaupaisau| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘kāyāyatanaṃ’’|

161. tattha  katamaṃ manāyatanaṃ? aikavidhaina manāyatanaṃ – phassasampayuttaṃ|

duvidhaina manāyatanaṃ – atthi sahaitukaṃ, atthi ahaitukaṃ|

tividhaina manāyatanaṃ – atthi kusalaṃ, atthi akusalaṃ, atthi abyākataṃ|

catubbidhaina manāyatanaṃ – atthi kāmāvacaraṃ, atthi rūpāvacaraṃ, atthi arūpāvacaraṃ, atthi apariyāpan‍naṃ|

pañ‍cavidhaina manāyatanaṃ – atthi sukhindriyasampayuttaṃ, atthi dukkhindriyasampayuttaṃ, atthi saumanassindriyasampayuttaṃ, atthi daumanassindriyasampayuttaṃ, atthi upaikkhindriyasampayuttaṃ|

chabbidhaina manāyatanaṃ – cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| aivaṃ chabbidhaina manāyatanaṃ|

sattavidhaina manāyatanaṃ – cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, manaudhātu, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu| aivaṃ sattavidhaina manāyatanaṃ|

aṭṭhavidhaina manāyatanaṃ – cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ  atthi sukhasahagataṃ, atthi dukkhasahagataṃ, manaudhātu, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu| aivaṃ aṭṭhavidhaina manāyatanaṃ|

navavidhaina manāyatanaṃ – cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, manaudhātu, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu atthi kusalaṃ, atthi akusalaṃ, atthi abyākataṃ| aivaṃ navavidhaina manāyatanaṃ|

dasavidhaina manāyatanaṃ – cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ atthi sukhasahagataṃ, atthi dukkhasahagataṃ, manaudhātu, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu atthi kusalaṃ, atthi akusalaṃ, atthi abyākataṃ| aivaṃ dasavidhaina manāyatanaṃ|

aikavidhaina manāyatanaṃ – phassasampayuttaṃ|

duvidhaina manāyatanaṃ – atthi sahaitukaṃ, atthi ahaitukaṃ|

tividhaina  manāyatanaṃ – atthi sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ, atthi dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ, atthi adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ…pai॰…| aivaṃ bahuvidhaina manāyatanaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘manāyatanaṃ’’|

162. tattha  katamaṃ rūpāyatanaṃ? yaṃ rūpaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya vaṇṇanibhā sanidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ nīlaṃ pītakaṃ lauhitakaṃ pītaṃ lauhitaṃ (sī॰) audātaṃ kāḷakaṃ mañ‍jiṭṭhakaṃ mañ‍jaiṭṭhakaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) hari harivaṇṇaṃ ambaṅkuravaṇṇaṃ dīghaṃ rassaṃ aṇuṃ thūlaṃ vaṭṭaṃ parimaṇḍalaṃ caturassaṃ chaḷaṃsaṃ aṭṭhaṃsaṃ sauḷasaṃsaṃ nin‍naṃ thalaṃ chāyā ātapau ālaukau andhakārau abbhā mahikā dhūmau rajau candamaṇḍalassa vaṇṇanibhā sūriyamaṇḍalassa suriyamaṇḍalassa (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) vaṇṇanibhā tārakarūpānaṃ vaṇṇanibhā ādāsamaṇḍalassa vaṇṇanibhā maṇisaṅkhamuttavaiḷuriyassa vaṇṇanibhā jātarūparajatassa vaṇṇanibhā, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya vaṇṇanibhā sanidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ, yaṃ rūpaṃ sanidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ cakkhunā anidassanaina sappaṭighaina passi vā passati vā passissati vā passai vā, rūpampaitaṃ rūpāyatanampaitaṃ rūpadhātupaisā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘rūpāyatanaṃ’’|

163. tattha  katamaṃ saddāyatanaṃ? yau saddau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassanau sappaṭighau bhairisaddau mudiṅgasaddau mutiṅgasaddhau (sī॰) saṅkhasaddau paṇavasaddau gītasaddau vāditasaddau sammasaddau pāṇisaddau sattānaṃ nigghausasaddau dhātūnaṃ san‍nighātasaddau vātasaddau udakasaddau manussasaddau amanussasaddau, yau vā panañ‍ñaupi atthi saddau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassanau sappaṭighau, yaṃ saddaṃ anidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ sautaina anidassanaina sappaṭighaina suṇi vā suṇāti vā suṇissati vā suṇai vā, saddaupaisau saddāyatanampaitaṃ saddadhātupaisā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saddāyatanaṃ’’|

164. tattha katamaṃ gandhāyatanaṃ? yau gandhau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassanau sappaṭighau mūlagandhau sāragandhau tacagandhau pattagandhau pupphagandhau phalagandhau āmagandhau vissagandhau sugandhau duggandhau, yau vā panañ‍ñaupi atthi gandhau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassanau sappaṭighau, yaṃ gandhaṃ anidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ ghānaina anidassanaina sappaṭighaina ghāyi vā ghāyati vā ghāyissati vā ghāyai vā, gandhaupaisau gandhāyatanampaitaṃ gandhadhātupaisā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘gandhāyatanaṃ’’|

165. tattha katamaṃ rasāyatanaṃ? yau rasau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassanau sappaṭighau mūlarasau khandharasau tacarasau pattarasau puppharasau phalarasau ambilaṃ madhuraṃ tittakaṃ kaṭukaṃ lauṇikaṃ lapilakaṃ (sī॰), lampikaṃ (ka॰ sī॰) khārikaṃ lambikaṃ kasāvau sādu asādu, yau vā panañ‍ñaupi atthi rasau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassanau sappaṭighau, yaṃ rasaṃ anidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ jivhāya anidassanāya sappaṭighāya sāyi vā sāyati vā sāyissati vā sāyai vā, rasaupaisau rasāyatanampaitaṃ rasadhātupaisā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘rasāyatanaṃ’’|

166. tattha katamaṃ phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ? pathavīdhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātu kakkhaḷaṃ mudukaṃ saṇhaṃ pharusaṃ sukhasamphassaṃ dukkhasamphassaṃ garukaṃ lahukaṃ, yaṃ phauṭṭhabbaṃ anidassanaṃ sappaṭighaṃ kāyaina anidassanaina sappaṭighaina phusi vā phusati vā phusissati vā phusai vā, phauṭṭhabbaupaisau phauṭṭhabbāyatanampaitaṃ phauṭṭhabbadhātupaisā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ’’|

167. tattha  katamaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau, yañ‍ca rūpaṃ anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammāyatanapariyāpan‍naṃ, asaṅkhatā ca dhātu|

tattha katamau vaidanākkhandhau? aikavidhaina vaidanākkhandhau – phassasampayuttau| duvidhaina vaidanākkhandhau – atthi sahaitukau, atthi ahaitukau| tividhaina vaidanākkhandhau – atthi kusalau, atthi akusalau, atthi abyākatau…pai॰… aivaṃ dasavidhaina vaidanākkhandhau…pai॰… aivaṃ bahuvidhaina vaidanākkhandhau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vaidanākkhandhau’’|

tattha katamau sañ‍ñākkhandhau? aikavidhaina sañ‍ñākkhandhau – phassasampayuttau| duvidhaina sañ‍ñākkhandhau – atthi sahaitukau, atthi ahaitukau| tividhaina sañ‍ñākkhandhau – atthi kusalau, atthi akusalau, atthi  abyākatau…pai॰… aivaṃ dasavidhaina sañ‍ñākkhandhau…pai॰… aivaṃ bahuvidhaina sañ‍ñākkhandhau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sañ‍ñākkhandhau’’|

tattha katamau saṅkhārakkhandhau? aikavidhaina saṅkhārakkhandhau – cittasampayuttau| duvidhaina saṅkhārakkhandhau – atthi haitu, atthi na haitu| tividhaina saṅkhārakkhandhau – atthi kusalau, atthi akusalau, atthi abyākatau…pai॰… aivaṃ dasavidhaina saṅkhārakkhandhau…pai॰… aivaṃ bahuvidhaina saṅkhārakkhandhau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārakkhandhau’’|

tattha katamaṃ  rūpaṃ anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammāyatanapariyāpan‍naṃ? itthindriyaṃ purisindriyaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau| idaṃ vuc‍cati rūpaṃ ‘‘anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammāyatanapariyāpan‍naṃ’’|

tattha katamā asaṅkhatā dhātu? rāgakkhayau, dausakkhayau, mauhakkhayau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘asaṅkhatā dhātu’’|

idaṃ vuc‍cati dhammāyatanaṃ|

abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ|

3. pañhāpucchakaṃ

168. dvādasāyatanāni  – cakkhāyatanaṃ, rūpāyatanaṃ, sautāyatanaṃ, saddāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, gandhāyatanaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ, rasāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ|

169. dvādasan‍naṃ āyatanānaṃ kati kusalā, kati akusalā, kati abyākatā…pai॰… kati saraṇā, kati araṇā?

1. tikaṃ

170. dasāyatanā abyākatā| dvāyatanā siyā kusalā, siyā akusalā, siyā abyākatā| dasāyatanā na vattabbā – ‘‘sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā’’tipi, ‘‘dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā’’tipi, ‘‘adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā’’tipi| manāyatanaṃ siyā sukhāya vaidanāya  sampayuttaṃ, siyā dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ, siyā dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘sukhāya vaidanāya sampayutta’’ntipi, ‘‘dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayutta’’ntipi, ‘‘adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayutta’’ntipi| dasāyatanā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā| dvāyatanā siyā vipākā, siyā vipākadhammadhammā, siyā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā|

pañ‍cāyatanā upādin‍nupādāniyā| saddāyatanaṃ  anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ| cattārau āyatanā siyā upādin‍nupādāniyā, siyā anupādin‍nupādāniyā, siyā anupādāniyā| dvāyatanā siyā upādin‍nupādāniyā, siyā anupādin‍nupādāniyā, siyā anupādin‍naanupādāniyā| dasāyatanā asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā| dvāyatanā siyā saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā, siyā asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā, siyā asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikā| dasāyatanā avitak‍kaavicārā| manāyatanaṃ siyā savitak‍kasavicāraṃ, siyā avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ, siyā avitak‍kaavicāraṃ| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā savitak‍kasavicāraṃ, siyā avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ, siyā avitak‍kaavicāraṃ, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘savitak‍kasavicāra’’ntipi, ‘‘avitak‍kavicāramatta’’ntipi, ‘‘avitak‍kaavicāra’’ntipi| dasāyatanā na vattabbā – ‘‘pītisahagatā’’tipi, ‘‘sukhasahagatā’’tipi, ‘‘upaikkhāsahagatā’’tipi| dvāyatanā siyā pītisahagatā, siyā  sukhasahagatā, siyā upaikkhāsahagatā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘pītisahagatā’’tipi, ‘‘sukhasahagatā’’tipi, ‘‘upaikkhāsahagatā’’tipi|

dasāyatanā naiva dassanaina na bhāvanāya pahātabbā| dvāyatanā siyā dassanaina pahātabbā, siyā bhāvanāya pahātabbā, siyā naiva dassanaina na bhāvanāya pahātabbā| dasāyatanā naiva dassanaina na bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā| dvāyatanā siyā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā, siyā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā, siyā naiva dassanaina na bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā| dasāyatanā naivācayagāmināpacayagāminau| dvāyatanā siyā ācayagāminau, siyā apacayagāminau, siyā naivācayagāmināpacayagāminau| dasāyatanā  naivasaikkhanāsaikkhā| dvāyatanā siyā saikkhā, siyā asaikkhā, siyā naivasaikkhanāsaikkhā| dasāyatanā parittā| dvāyatanā siyā parittā, siyā mahaggatā, siyā appamāṇā| dasāyatanā anārammaṇā| dvāyatanā siyā parittārammaṇā, siyā mahaggatārammaṇā, siyā appamāṇārammaṇā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘parittārammaṇā’’tipi, ‘‘mahaggatārammaṇā’’tipi, ‘‘appamāṇārammaṇā’’tipi| dasāyatanā majjhimā| dvāyatanā siyā hīnā, siyā majjhimā, siyā paṇītā| dasāyatanā aniyatā| dvāyatanā siyā micchattaniyatā, siyā sammattaniyatā, siyā aniyatā|

dasāyatanā anārammaṇā| dvāyatanā siyā maggārammaṇā, siyā maggahaitukā, siyā maggādhipatinau, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘maggārammaṇā’’tipi, ‘‘maggahaitukā’’tipi, ‘‘maggādhipatinau’’tipi| pañ‍cāyatanā siyā uppan‍nā, siyā uppādinau, na vattabbā – ‘‘anuppan‍nā’’ti| saddāyatanaṃ siyā uppan‍naṃ, siyā anuppan‍naṃ, na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘uppādī’’ti| pañ‍cāyatanā siyā uppan‍nā, siyā anuppan‍nā, siyā uppādinau| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā uppan‍naṃ, siyā anuppan‍naṃ, siyā uppādi, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘uppan‍na’’ntipi, ‘‘anuppan‍na’’ntipi, ‘‘uppādī’’tipi| aikādasāyatanā siyā atītā, siyā anāgatā, siyā pac‍cuppan‍nā| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā atītaṃ, siyā anāgataṃ, siyā pac‍cuppan‍naṃ, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘atīta’’ntipi, ‘‘anāgata’’ntipi, ‘‘pac‍cuppan‍na’’ntipi| dasāyatanā anārammaṇā| dvāyatanā siyā  atītārammaṇā, siyā anāgatārammaṇā, siyā pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇā, siyā na vattabbā  – ‘‘atītārammaṇā’’tipi, ‘‘anāgatārammaṇā’’tipi, ‘‘pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇā’’tipi; siyā ajjhattā, siyā bahiddhā, siyā ajjhattabahiddhā| dasāyatanā anārammaṇā| dvāyatanā siyā ajjhattārammaṇā, siyā bahiddhārammaṇā, siyā ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘ajjhattārammaṇā’’tipi, ‘‘bahiddhārammaṇā’’tipi, ‘‘ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇā’’tipi| rūpāyatanaṃ sanidassanasappaṭighaṃ| navāyatanā anidassanasappaṭighā| dvāyatanā anidassanaappaṭighā|

2. dukaṃ

171. aikādasāyatanā na haitū| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā haitu, siyā na haitu| dasāyatanā ahaitukā| dvāyatanā siyā sahaitukā, siyā ahaitukā| dasāyatanā haituvippayuttā| dvāyatanā siyā haitusampayuttā, siyā haituvippayuttā| dasāyatanā na vattabbā – ‘‘haitū  caiva sahaitukā cā’’tipi, ‘‘sahaitukā caiva na ca haitū’’tipi| manāyatanaṃ na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘haitu caiva sahaitukañ‍cā’’ti, siyā sahaitukañ‍caiva na ca haitu, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘sahaitukañ‍caiva na ca haitū’’ti| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā haitu caiva sahaitukañ‍ca, siyā sahaitukañ‍caiva na ca haitu, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘haitu caiva sahaitukañ‍cā’’tipi, ‘‘sahaitukañ‍caiva na ca haitū’’tipi| dasāyatanā na vattabbā – ‘‘haitū caiva haitusampayuttā cā’’tipi, ‘‘haitusampayuttā caiva na ca haitū’’tipi| manāyatanaṃ na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘haitu caiva haitusampayuttañ‍cā’’ti, siyā haitusampayuttañ‍caiva na ca haitu, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘haitusampayuttañ‍caiva na  ca haitū’’ti| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā haitu caiva haitusampayuttañ‍ca, siyā haitusampayuttañ‍caiva na ca haitu, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘haitu caiva haitusampayuttañ‍cā’’tipi, ‘‘haitusampayuttañ‍caiva na ca haitū’’tipi| dasāyatanā na haitūahaitukā| manāyatanaṃ siyā na haitusahaitukaṃ, siyā na haituahaitukaṃ| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā na haitusahaitukaṃ, siyā na haituahaitukaṃ, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘na haitusahaituka’’ntipi, ‘‘na haituahaituka’’ntipi|

aikādasāyatanā sappac‍cayā| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā sappac‍cayaṃ, siyā appac‍cayaṃ| aikādasāyatanā saṅkhatā| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā saṅkhataṃ, siyā asaṅkhataṃ| rūpāyatanaṃ sanidassanaṃ| aikādasāyatanā  anidassanā| dasāyatanā  sappaṭighā| dvāyatanā appaṭighā| dasāyatanā rūpā| manāyatanaṃ arūpaṃ| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā rūpaṃ, siyā arūpaṃ| dasāyatanā laukiyā| dvāyatanā siyā laukiyā, siyā laukuttarā; kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyā, kainaci na viñ‍ñaiyyā|

aikādasāyatanā nau āsavā| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā āsavau, siyā nau āsavau| dasāyatanā sāsavā| dvāyatanā siyā sāsavā, siyā anāsavā| dasāyatanā āsavavippayuttā| dvāyatanā siyā āsavasampayuttā, siyā āsavavippayuttā| dasāyatanā na vattabbā – ‘‘āsavā caiva sāsavā cā’’ti, ‘‘sāsavā caiva nau ca āsavā’’| manāyatanaṃ na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘āsavau caiva sāsavañ‍cā’’ti, siyā sāsavañ‍caiva nau ca āsavau, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘sāsavañ‍caiva nau ca āsavau’’ti| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā āsavau caiva sāsavañ‍ca, siyā sāsavañ‍caiva nau ca  āsavau, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘āsavau caiva sāsavañ‍cā’’tipi, ‘‘sāsavañ‍caiva nau ca āsavau’’tipi| dasāyatanā na vattabbā – ‘‘āsavā caiva āsavasampayuttā cā’’tipi, ‘‘āsavasampayuttā caiva nau ca āsavā’’tipi| manāyatanaṃ na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘āsavau caiva āsavasampayuttañ‍cā’’ti, siyā āsavasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca āsavau, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘āsavasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca āsavau’’ti| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā  āsavau caiva āsavasampayuttañ‍ca, siyā āsavasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca āsavau, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘āsavau caiva āsavasampayuttañ‍cā’’tipi, ‘‘āsavasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca āsavau’’tipi| dasāyatanā āsavavippayuttasāsavā| dvāyatanā siyā āsavavippayuttasāsavā, siyā āsavavippayuttaanāsavā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘āsavavippayuttasāsavā’’tipi, ‘‘āsavavippayuttaanāsavā’’tipi|

aikādasāyatanā nau saṃyaujanā| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā saṃyaujanaṃ, siyā nau saṃyaujanaṃ| dasāyatanā saṃyaujaniyā| dvāyatanā siyā saṃyaujaniyā, siyā asaṃyaujaniyā| dasāyatanā saṃyaujanavippayuttā| dvāyatanā siyā saṃyaujanasampayuttā, siyā saṃyaujanavippayuttā| dasāyatanā na vattabbā – ‘‘saṃyaujanā caiva saṃyaujaniyā cā’’ti, saṃyaujaniyā caiva nau ca saṃyaujanā| manāyatanaṃ na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘saṃyaujanañ‍caiva saṃyaujaniyañ‍cā’’ti, siyā saṃyaujaniyañ‍caiva nau ca saṃyaujanaṃ, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘saṃyaujaniyañ‍caiva nau ca saṃyaujana’’nti| dhammāyatanaṃ  siyā saṃyaujanañ‍caiva saṃyaujaniyañ‍ca, siyā saṃyaujaniyañ‍caiva nau ca saṃyaujanaṃ, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘saṃyaujanañ‍caiva  saṃyaujaniya’’ntipi, ‘‘saṃyaujaniyañ‍caiva nau ca saṃyaujana’’ntipi| dasāyatanā na vattabbā – ‘‘saṃyaujanā caiva saṃyaujanasampayuttā cā’’tipi, ‘‘saṃyaujanasampayuttā caiva nau ca saṃyaujanā’’tipi| manāyatanaṃ  na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘saṃyaujanañ‍caiva saṃyaujanasampayuttañ‍cā’’ti, siyā saṃyaujanasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca saṃyaujanaṃ, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘saṃyaujanasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca saṃyaujana’’nti| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā saṃyaujanañ‍caiva saṃyaujanasampayuttañ‍ca, siyā saṃyaujanasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca saṃyaujanaṃ, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘saṃyaujanañ‍caiva saṃyaujanasampayuttañ‍cā’’tipi, ‘‘saṃyaujanasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca saṃyaujana’’ntipi| dasāyatanā saṃyaujanavippayuttasaṃyaujaniyā| dvāyatanā siyā saṃyaujanavippayuttasaṃyaujaniyā, siyā saṃyaujanavippayuttaasaṃyaujaniyā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘saṃyaujanavippayuttasaṃyaujaniyā’’tipi, ‘‘saṃyaujanavippayuttaasaṃyaujaniyā’’tipi|

aikādasāyatanā nau ganthā| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā ganthau, siyā nau ganthau| dasāyatanā ganthaniyā| dvāyatanā siyā ganthaniyā, siyā aganthaniyā| dasāyatanā ganthavippayuttā| dvāyatanā siyā ganthasampayuttā, siyā ganthavippayuttā| dasāyatanā na vattabbā – ‘‘ganthā caiva ganthaniyā cā’’ti, ganthaniyā caiva nau ca ganthā| manāyatanaṃ na vattabbaṃ  – ‘‘ganthau caiva ganthaniyañ‍cā’’ti, siyā ganthaniyañ‍caiva nau ca ganthau, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘ganthaniyañ‍caiva nau ca ganthau’’ti| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā ganthau caiva ganthaniyañ‍ca, siyā ganthaniyañ‍caiva nau ca ganthau, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘ganthau caiva ganthaniyañ‍cā’’tipi, ‘‘ganthaniyañ‍caiva nau ca ganthau’’tipi| dasāyatanā na vattabbā – ‘‘ganthā caiva ganthasampayuttā cā’’tipi, ‘‘ganthasampayuttā caiva nau ca ganthā’’tipi| manāyatanaṃ na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘ganthau caiva ganthasampayuttañ‍cā’’ti, siyā ganthasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca ganthau, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘ganthasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca ganthau’’ti| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā ganthau caiva ganthasampayuttañ‍ca, siyā ganthasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca ganthau, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘ganthau caiva ganthasampayuttañ‍cā’’tipi, ‘‘ganthasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca ganthau’’tipi| dasāyatanā ganthavippayuttaganthaniyā| dvāyatanā siyā ganthavippayuttaganthaniyā, siyā ganthavippayuttaaganthaniyā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘ganthavippayuttaganthaniyā’’tipi, ‘‘ganthavippayuttaaganthaniyā’’tipi|

aikādasāyatanā nau aughā…pai॰… nau yaugā…pai॰… nau nīvaraṇā| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā  nīvaraṇaṃ, siyā nau nīvaraṇaṃ| dasāyatanā nīvaraṇiyā| dvāyatanā siyā  nīvaraṇiyā, siyā anīvaraṇiyā| dasāyatanā nīvaraṇavippayuttā| dvāyatanā siyā nīvaraṇasampayuttā, siyā nīvaraṇavippayuttā| dasāyatanā na vattabbā – ‘‘nīvaraṇā caiva nīvaraṇiyā cā’’ti, nīvaraṇiyā caiva nau ca nīvaraṇā| manāyatanaṃ na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘nīvaraṇañ‍caiva nīvaraṇiyañ‍cā’’ti, siyā nīvaraṇiyañ‍caiva nau ca nīvaraṇaṃ, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘nīvaraṇiyañ‍caiva nau ca nīvaraṇa’’nti| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā nīvaraṇañ‍caiva nīvaraṇiyañ‍ca, siyā nīvaraṇiyañ‍caiva nau ca nīvaraṇaṃ, siyā  na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘nīvaraṇañ‍caiva nīvaraṇiyañ‍cā’’tipi, ‘‘nīvaraṇiyañ‍caiva nau ca nīvaraṇa’’ntipi| dasāyatanā na vattabbā – ‘‘nīvaraṇā caiva nīvaraṇasampayuttā cā’’tipi, ‘‘nīvaraṇasampayuttā caiva nau ca nīvaraṇā’’tipi| manāyatanaṃ na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘nīvaraṇañ‍caiva nīvaraṇasampayuttañ‍cā’’ti, siyā nīvaraṇasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca nīvaraṇaṃ, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘nīvaraṇasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca nīvaraṇa’’nti| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā nīvaraṇañ‍caiva nīvaraṇasampayuttañ‍ca, siyā nīvaraṇasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca nīvaraṇaṃ, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘nīvaraṇañ‍caiva nīvaraṇasampayuttañ‍cā’’tipi, ‘‘nīvaraṇasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca nīvaraṇa’’ntipi| dasāyatanā nīvaraṇavippayuttanīvaraṇiyā| dvāyatanā siyā nīvaraṇavippayuttanīvaraṇiyā, siyā nīvaraṇavippayuttaanīvaraṇiyā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘nīvaraṇavippayuttanīvaraṇiyā’’tipi, ‘‘nīvaraṇavippayuttaanīvaraṇiyā’’tipi|

aikādasāyatanā nau parāmāsā| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā parāmāsau, siyā nau parāmāsau| dasāyatanā parāmaṭṭhā| dvāyatanā siyā parāmaṭṭhā, siyā aparāmaṭṭhā| dasāyatanā parāmāsavippayuttā| manāyatanaṃ siyā parāmāsasampayuttaṃ, siyā parāmāsavippayuttaṃ| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā parāmāsasampayuttaṃ, siyā parāmāsavippayuttaṃ, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘parāmāsasampayutta’’ntipi, ‘‘parāmāsavippayutta’’ntipi| dasāyatanā na  vattabbā – ‘‘parāmāsā caiva parāmaṭṭhā cā’’ti, ‘‘parāmaṭṭhā caiva nau ca parāmāsā’’| manāyatanaṃ na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘parāmāsau caiva parāmaṭṭhañ‍cā’’ti, siyā parāmaṭṭhañ‍caiva nau ca parāmāsau, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘parāmaṭṭhañ‍caiva nau ca parāmāsau’’ti| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā parāmāsau caiva parāmaṭṭhañ‍ca, siyā parāmaṭṭhañ‍caiva nau ca parāmāsau, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘parāmāsau caiva parāmaṭṭhañ‍cā’’tipi, ‘‘parāmaṭṭhañ‍caiva nau ca parāmāsau’’tipi| dasāyatanā parāmāsavippayuttaparāmaṭṭhā| dvāyatanā siyā parāmāsavippayuttaparāmaṭṭhā, siyā parāmāsavippayuttaaparāmaṭṭhā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘parāmāsavippayuttaparāmaṭṭhā’’tipi, ‘‘parāmāsavippayuttaaparāmaṭṭhā’’tipi|

dasāyatanā anārammaṇā| manāyatanaṃ sārammaṇaṃ| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā sārammaṇaṃ, siyā anārammaṇaṃ| manāyatanaṃ cittaṃ| aikādasāyatanā nau cittā| aikādasāyatanā acaitasikā| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā caitasikaṃ, siyā acaitasikaṃ| dasāyatanā cittavippayuttā| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā cittasampayuttaṃ, siyā cittavippayuttaṃ| manāyatanaṃ na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘cittaina sampayutta’’ntipi, ‘‘cittaina vippayutta’’ntipi| dasāyatanā cittavisaṃsaṭṭhā| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā cittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ, siyā cittavisaṃsaṭṭhaṃ| manāyatanaṃ na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘cittaina saṃsaṭṭha’’ntipi, ‘‘cittaina visaṃsaṭṭha’’ntipi| chāyatanā nau cittasamuṭṭhānā| chāyatanā siyā cittasamuṭṭhānā, siyā nau cittasamuṭṭhānā| aikādasāyatanā nau cittasahabhunau| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā cittasahabhū, siyā nau cittasahabhū| aikādasāyatanā nau cittānuparivattinau| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā cittānuparivatti, siyā nau cittānuparivatti| aikādasāyatanā nau cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānā| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānaṃ, siyā nau cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānaṃ| aikādasāyatanā nau cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhunau| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhū, siyā nau cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhū| aikādasāyatanā nau cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattinau| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivatti, siyā nau cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivatti|

chāyatanā ajjhattikā| chāyatanā bāhirā| navāyatanā upādā| dvāyatanā nau upādā| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā upādā, siyā nau upādā| pañ‍cāyatanā upādin‍nā| saddāyatanaṃ anupādin‍naṃ| chāyatanā siyā upādin‍nā, siyā anupādin‍nā| aikādasāyatanā nau upādānā| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā upādānaṃ, siyā nau upādānaṃ| dasāyatanā upādāniyā| dvāyatanā siyā upādāniyā, siyā anupādāniyā| dasāyatanā upādānavippayuttā| dvāyatanā siyā upādānasampayuttā, siyā upādānavippayuttā| dasāyatanā na vattabbā – ‘‘upādānā caiva upādāniyā cā’’ti, upādāniyā caiva nau ca upādānā| manāyatanaṃ na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘upādānañ‍caiva upādāniyañ‍cā’’ti, siyā upādāniyañ‍caiva nau ca upādānaṃ, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘upādāniyañ‍caiva nau ca upādāna’’nti| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā upādānañ‍caiva  upādāniyañ‍ca, siyā upādāniyañ‍caiva nau ca upādānaṃ, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘upādānañ‍caiva  upādāniyañ‍cā’’tipi, ‘‘upādāniyañ‍caiva nau ca upādāna’’ntipi| dasāyatanā na vattabbā – ‘‘upādānā caiva upādānasampayuttā cā’’tipi, ‘‘upādānasampayuttā caiva nau ca upādānā’’tipi| manāyatanaṃ na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘upādāniyañ‍caiva upādānasampayuttañ‍cā’’ti, siyā upādānasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca upādānaṃ, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘upādānasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca upādāna’’nti| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā upādānañ‍caiva upādānasampayuttañ‍ca, siyā upādānasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca upādānaṃ, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘upādānañ‍caiva upādānasampayuttañ‍cā’’tipi, ‘‘upādānasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca upādāna’’ntipi| dasāyatanā upādānavippayuttaupādāniyā| dvāyatanā siyā upādānasampayuttaupādāniyā, siyā upādānavippayuttaanupādāniyā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘upādānavippayuttaupādāniyā’’tipi, ‘‘upādānavippayuttaanupādāniyā’’tipi|

aikādasāyatanā nau kilaisā| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā kilaisau, siyā nau kilaisau| dasāyatanā saṃkilaisikā| dvāyatanā siyā saṃkilaisikā, siyā asaṃkilaisikā| dasāyatanā asaṃkiliṭṭhā| dvāyatanā siyā saṃkiliṭṭhā, siyā asaṃkiliṭṭhā| dasāyatanā kilaisavippayuttā| dvāyatanā siyā kilaisasampayuttā, siyā kilaisavippayuttā| dasāyatanā na vattabbā – ‘‘kilaisā caiva saṃkilaisikā cā’’ti, ‘‘saṃkilaisikā caiva nau ca kilaisā’’| manāyatanaṃ na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘kilaisau caiva saṃkilaisikañ‍cā’’ti, siyā saṃkilaisikañ‍caiva nau ca kilaisau, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘saṃkilaisikañ‍caiva nau ca kilaisau’’ti| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā kilaisau caiva saṃkilaisikañ‍ca, siyā saṃkilaisikañ‍caiva nau ca kilaisau, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘kilaisau caiva saṃkilaisikañ‍cā’’tipi, ‘‘saṃkilaisikañ‍caiva nau ca kilaisau’’tipi| dasāyatanā na vattabbā – ‘‘kilaisā caiva saṃkiliṭṭhā cā’’tipi, ‘‘saṃkiliṭṭhā caiva nau ca  kilaisā’’tipi| manāyatanaṃ na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘kilaisau caiva saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍cā’’ti, siyā saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍caiva nau ca kilaisau, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍caiva nau ca kilaisau’’ti| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā kilaisau caiva saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍ca, siyā saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍caiva nau ca kilaisau, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘kilaisau caiva saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍cā’’tipi, ‘‘saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍caiva nau ca kilaisau’’tipi|

dasāyatanā na vattabbā – ‘‘kilaisā caiva kilaisasampayuttā cā’’tipi, ‘‘kilaisasampayuttā caiva nau ca na kilaisā’’tipi| manāyatanaṃ na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘kilaisau caiva kilaisasampayuttañ‍cā’’ti, siyā kilaisasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca kilaisau, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘kilaisasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca kilaisau’’ti| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā kilaisau caiva kilaisasampayuttañ‍ca, siyā kilaisasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca kilaisau, siyā na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘kilaisau caiva kilaisasampayuttañ‍cā’’tipi, ‘‘kilaisasampayuttañ‍caiva nau  ca kilaisau’’tipi| dasāyatanā kilaisavippayuttasaṃkilaisikā| dvāyatanā siyā kilaisavippayuttasaṃkilaisikā, siyā kilaisavippayuttaasaṃkilaisikā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘kilaisavippayuttasaṃkilaisikā’’tipi, ‘‘kilaisavippayuttaasaṃkilaisikā’’tipi|

dasāyatanā na dassanaina pahātabbā| dvāyatanā siyā dassanaina pahātabbā, siyā na dassanaina pahātabbā| dasāyatanā na bhāvanāya pahātabbā| dvāyatanā siyā bhāvanāya pahātabbā, siyā na bhāvanāya pahātabbā| dasāyatanā na dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā| dvāyatanā siyā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā, siyā na dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā| dasāyatanā na bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā| dvāyatanā siyā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā, siyā na bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā| dasāyatanā avitak‍kā| dvāyatanā siyā savitak‍kā, siyā avitak‍kā| dasāyatanā avicārā| dvāyatanā siyā savicārā, siyā avicārā| dasāyatanā appītikā| dvāyatanā siyā sappītikā, siyā appītikā| dasāyatanā na pītisahagatā| dvāyatanā siyā pītisahagatā, siyā na pītisahagatā| dasāyatanā na sukhasahagatā| dvāyatanā siyā sukhasahagatā, siyā na sukhasahagatā| dasāyatanā na upaikkhāsahagatā| dvāyatanā siyā upaikkhāsahagatā, siyā na upaikkhāsahagatā|

dasāyatanā kāmāvacarā| dvāyatanā siyā kāmāvacarā, siyā na kāmāvacarā| dasāyatanā na rūpāvacarā| dvāyatanā siyā rūpāvacarā, siyā na rūpāvacarā| dasāyatanā na arūpāvacarā| dvāyatanā siyā arūpāvacarā, siyā na arūpāvacarā| dasāyatanā pariyāpan‍nā| dvāyatanā siyā pariyāpan‍nā, siyā apariyāpan‍nā| dasāyatanā aniyyānikā| dvāyatanā siyā niyyānikā, siyā aniyyānikā| dasāyatanā aniyatā| dvāyatanā siyā niyatā, siyā aniyatā| dasāyatanā sauttarā| dvāyatanā siyā sauttarā, siyā anuttarā| dasāyatanā araṇā| dvāyatanā siyā saraṇā, siyā araṇāti|

pañhāpucchakaṃ|

āyatanavibhaṅgau niṭṭhitau|

3. dhātuvibhaṅgau

1. suttantabhājanīyaṃ

172. cha  dhātuyau – pathavīdhātu paṭhavīdhātu (sī॰ syā॰) aivamuparipi, āpaudhātu, taijaudhātu, vāyaudhātu, ākāsadhātu, viñ‍ñāṇadhātu|

173. tattha katamā pathavīdhātu? pathavīdhātudvayaṃ – atthi ajjhattikā, atthi bāhirā| tattha katamā ajjhattikā pathavīdhātu? yaṃ ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ kakkhaḷaṃ kharigataṃ kakkhaḷattaṃ kakkhaḷabhāvau ajjhattaṃ upādin‍naṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – kaisā laumā nakhā dantā tacau maṃsaṃ nhāru nahāru (sī॰) aṭṭhi aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ aṭṭhimiñ‍jā (sī॰) vak‍kaṃ hadayaṃ yakanaṃ kilaumakaṃ pihakaṃ papphāsaṃ antaṃ antaguṇaṃ udariyaṃ karīsaṃ, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ kakkhaḷaṃ kharigataṃ kakkhaḷattaṃ kakkhaḷabhāvau ajjhattaṃ upādin‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘ajjhattikā pathavīdhātu’|

tattha katamā bāhirā pathavīdhātu? yaṃ bāhiraṃ kakkhaḷaṃ kharigataṃ kakkhaḷattaṃ kakkhaḷabhāvau bahiddhā anupādin‍naṃ, saiyyathidaṃ  – ayau lauhaṃ tipu sīsaṃ sajjhaṃ sajjhu (syā॰) muttā maṇi vaiḷuriyau saṅkhau silā pavāḷaṃ rajataṃ jātarūpaṃ lauhitaṅkau lauhitaṅgau (syā॰ ka॰), lauhitakau (?) masāragal‍laṃ tiṇaṃ kaṭṭhaṃ sakkharā kaṭhalaṃ kathalaṃ (ka॰) bhūmi pāsāṇau pabbatau, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi bāhiraṃ kakkhaḷaṃ kharigataṃ kakkhaḷattaṃ kakkhaḷabhāvau bahiddhā anupādin‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘bāhirā pathavīdhātu’| yā ca ajjhattikā pathavīdhātu yā ca bāhirā pathavīdhātu, tadaikajjhaṃ abhisañ‍ñūhitvā abhisaṅkhipitvā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘pathavīdhātu’’|

174. tattha  katamā āpaudhātu? āpaudhātudvayaṃ – atthi ajjhattikā, atthi bāhirā| tattha katamā ajjhattikā āpaudhātu? yaṃ ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ āpau āpaugataṃ sinaihau sinaihagataṃ snaihau snaihagataṃ (syā॰) bandhanattaṃ rūpassa ajjhattaṃ upādin‍naṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – pittaṃ saimhaṃ pubbau lauhitaṃ saidau maidau assu vasā khaiḷau siṅghāṇikā lasikā muttaṃ, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi ajjhattaṃ  pac‍cattaṃ āpau āpaugataṃ sinaihau sinaihagataṃ bandhanattaṃ rūpassa ajjhattaṃ upādin‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘ajjhattikā āpaudhātu’|

tattha katamā bāhirā āpaudhātu? yaṃ bāhiraṃ āpau āpaugataṃ sinaihau sinaihagataṃ bandhanattaṃ rūpassa bahiddhā anupādin‍naṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – mūlarasau khandharasau tacarasau pattarasau puppharasau phalarasau khīraṃ dadhi sappi navanītaṃ tailaṃ madhu phāṇitaṃ bhummāni vā udakāni antalikkhāni vā, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi bāhiraṃ āpau āpaugataṃ sinaihau sinaihagataṃ bandhanattaṃ rūpassa bahiddhā anupādin‍naṃ  – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘bāhirā āpaudhātu’| yā ca ajjhattikā āpaudhātu yā ca bāhirā āpaudhātu, tadaikajjhaṃ abhisañ‍ñūhitvā abhisaṅkhipitvā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘āpaudhātu’’|

175. tattha katamā taijaudhātu? taijaudhātudvayaṃ – atthi ajjhattikā, atthi bāhirā| tattha katamā ajjhattikā taijaudhātu? yaṃ ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ taijau taijaugataṃ usmā usmāgataṃ usumaṃ usumagataṃ ajjhattaṃ upādin‍naṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – yaina ca santappati yaina ca jīrīyati yaina ca pariḍayhati yaina ca asitapītakhāyitasāyitaṃ sammā pariṇāmaṃ gacchati, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ taijau taijaugataṃ usmā usmāgataṃ usumaṃ usumagataṃ ajjhattaṃ upādin‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘ajjhattikā taijaudhātu’|

tattha katamā bāhirā taijaudhātu? yaṃ bāhiraṃ taijau taijaugataṃ usmā usmāgataṃ usumaṃ usumagataṃ bahiddhā anupādin‍naṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – kaṭṭhaggi palālaggi sakalikaggi (sabbattha) tiṇaggi gaumayaggi thusaggi saṅkāraggi indaggi aggisantāpau sūriyasantāpau kaṭṭhasan‍nicayasantāpau tiṇasan‍nicayasantāpau dhañ‍ñasan‍nicayasantāpau bhaṇḍasan‍nicayasantāpau, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi bāhiraṃ taijau taijaugataṃ usmā usmāgataṃ usumaṃ usumagataṃ bahiddhā anupādin‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘bāhirā taijaudhātu’| yā  ca ajjhattikā taijaudhātu yā ca bāhirā taijaudhātu, tadaikajjhaṃ abhisañ‍ñūhitvā abhisaṅkhipitvā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘taijaudhātu’’|

176. tattha  katamā vāyaudhātu? vāyaudhātudvayaṃ – atthi ajjhattikā, atthi bāhirā| tattha katamā ajjhattikā vāyaudhātu? yaṃ ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ vāyau vāyaugataṃ thambhitattaṃ rūpassa ajjhattaṃ upādin‍naṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – uddhaṅgamā vātā adhaugamā vātā kucchisayā vātā kauṭṭhāsayā kauṭṭhasayā (sī॰ syā॰) vātā aṅgamaṅgānusārinau vātā satthakavātā khurakavātā uppalakavātā assāsau passāsau iti vā, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ vāyau vāyaugataṃ thambhitattaṃ rūpassa ajjhattaṃ upādin‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘ajjhattikā vāyaudhātu’|

tattha katamā bāhirā vāyaudhātu? yaṃ bāhiraṃ vāyau vāyaugataṃ thambhitattaṃ rūpassa bahiddhā anupādin‍naṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – puratthimā vātā pacchimā vātā uttarā vātā dakkhiṇā vātā sarajā vātā arajā vātā sītā vātā uṇhā vātā parittā vātā adhimattā vātā kāḷavātā vairambhavātā pakkhavātā supaṇṇavātā tālavaṇṭavātā vidhūpanavātā, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi bāhiraṃ vāyau vāyaugataṃ thambhitattaṃ rūpassa bahiddhā anupādin‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘bāhirā vāyaudhātu’| yā ca ajjhattikā vāyaudhātu yā ca bāhirā vāyaudhātu, tadaikajjhaṃ abhisañ‍ñūhitvā abhisaṅkhipitvā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vāyaudhātu’’|

177. tattha katamā ākāsadhātu? ākāsadhātudvayaṃ – atthi ajjhattikā, atthi bāhirā| tattha katamā ajjhattikā ākāsadhātu? yaṃ  ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ ākāsau ākāsagataṃ aghaṃ aghagataṃ vivarau vivaragataṃ asamphuṭṭhaṃ maṃsalauhitaihi ajjhattaṃ upādin‍naṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – kaṇṇacchiddaṃ nāsacchiddaṃ mukhadvāraṃ, yaina ca asitapītakhāyitasāyitaṃ ajjhauharati, yattha ca asitapītakhāyitasāyitaṃ santiṭṭhati, yaina ca asitapītakhāyitasāyitaṃ adhaubhāgaṃ nikkhamati, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ ākāsau ākāsagataṃ aghaṃ aghagataṃ vivarau vivaragataṃ asamphuṭṭhaṃ maṃsalauhitaihi ajjhattaṃ upādin‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘ajjhattikā ākāsadhātu’|

tattha katamā bāhirā ākāsadhātu? yaṃ bāhiraṃ ākāsau ākāsagataṃ aghaṃ aghagataṃ vivarau  vivaragataṃ  asamphuṭṭhaṃ catūhi mahābhūtaihi  bahiddhā anupādin‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘bāhirā ākāsadhātu’| yā ca ajjhattikā ākāsadhātu yā ca bāhirā ākāsadhātu, tadaikajjhaṃ abhisañ‍ñūhitvā abhisaṅkhipitvā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘ākāsadhātu’’|

178. tattha katamā viñ‍ñāṇadhātu? cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, sautaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇadhātu’’|

imā cha dhātuyau|

179. aparāpi cha dhātuyau – sukhadhātu, dukkhadhātu, saumanassadhātu, daumanassadhātu, upaikkhādhātu, avij‍jādhātu|

180. tattha katamā sukhadhātu? yaṃ kāyikaṃ sātaṃ kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ kāyasamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ kāyasamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sukhadhātu’’|

tattha katamā  dukkhadhātu? yaṃ kāyikaṃ asātaṃ kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ kāyasamphassajaṃ asātaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidayitaṃ kāyasamphassajā asātā dukkhā vaidanā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘dukkhadhātu’’|

tattha katamā saumanassadhātu? yaṃ caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saumanassadhātu’’|

tattha katamā daumanassadhātu? yaṃ caitasikaṃ asātaṃ caitasikaṃ dukkhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ asātaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā asātā dukkhā vaidanā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘daumanassadhātu’’|

tattha katamā upaikkhādhātu? yaṃ caitasikaṃ naiva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ  vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘upaikkhādhātu’’|

tattha katamā avij‍jādhātu? yaṃ añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ anabhisamayau ananubaudhau asambaudhau appaṭivaidhau asaṅgāhaṇā apariyaugāhaṇā asamapaikkhanā apac‍cavaikkhaṇā apac‍cakkhakammaṃ dummaijjhaṃ bālyaṃ asampajañ‍ñaṃ  mauhau pamauhau sammauhau avij‍jā avij‍jaughau avij‍jāyaugau avij‍jānusayau avij‍jāpariyuṭṭhānaṃ  avij‍jālaṅgī mauhau akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jādhātu’’|

imā cha dhātuyau|

181. aparāpi cha dhātuyau – kāmadhātu, byāpādadhātu, vihiṃsādhātu, naikkhammadhātu, abyāpādadhātu, avihiṃsādhātu|

182. tattha katamā kāmadhātu? kāmapaṭisaṃyuttau tak‍kau vitak‍kau saṅkappau appanā byappanā caitasau abhiniraupanā micchāsaṅkappau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati kāmadhātu| haiṭṭhatau avīcinirayaṃ pariyantaṃ karitvā uparitau paranimmitavasavattī daivai antau karitvā yaṃ aitasmiṃ antarai aitthāvacarā aittha pariyāpan‍nā khandhadhātuāyatanā rūpā rūpaṃ (syā॰) vaidanā sañ‍ñā saṅkhārā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati  ‘‘kāmadhātu’’|

tattha katamā byāpādadhātu? byāpādapaṭisaṃyuttau tak‍kau vitak‍kau…pai॰… micchāsaṅkappau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘byāpādadhātu’’| dasasu vā āghātavatthūsu cittassa āghātau paṭighātau paṭighaṃ paṭiviraudhau kaupau pakaupau sampakaupau dausau padausau sampadausau cittassa byāpatti manaupadausau kaudhau kujjhanā kujjhitattaṃ dausau dussanā dussitattaṃ byāpatti byāpaj‍janā viraudhau paṭiviraudhau caṇḍik‍kaṃ asuraupau anattamanatā cittassa – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘byāpādadhātu’’|

tattha katamā vihiṃsādhātu? vihiṃsāpaṭisaṃyuttau tak‍kau vitak‍kau…pai॰… micchāsaṅkappau  – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vihiṃsādhātu’’| idhaikac‍cau pāṇinā vā laiḍḍunā vā daṇḍaina vā satthaina vā raj‍juyā vā añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina sattai vihaiṭhaiti, yā aivarūpā haiṭhanā vihaiṭhanā hiṃsanā vihiṃsanā rausanā virausanā parūpaghātau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vihiṃsādhātu’’|

tattha katamā naikkhammadhātu? naikkhammapaṭisaṃyuttau tak‍kau vitak‍kau…pai॰… sammāsaṅkappau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘naikkhammadhātu’’| sabbaipi kusalā dhammā ‘‘naikkhammadhātu’’|

tattha  katamā abyāpādadhātu? abyāpādapaṭisaṃyuttau tak‍kau vitak‍kau…pai॰… sammāsaṅkappau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘abyāpādadhātu’’| yā sattaisu maitti maittāyanā maittāyitattaṃ maittācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘abyāpādadhātu’’|

tattha katamā avihiṃsādhātu? avihiṃsāpaṭisaṃyuttau tak‍kau vitak‍kau saṅkappau appanā byappanā  caitasau abhiniraupanā sammāsaṅkappau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avihiṃsādhātu’’| yā sattaisu karuṇā karuṇāyanā karuṇāyitattaṃ karuṇācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avihiṃsādhātu’’|

imā cha dhātuyau|

iti imāni tīṇi chak‍kāni tadaikajjhaṃ abhisañ‍ñūhitvā abhisaṅkhipitvā aṭṭhārasa dhātuyau haunti|

suttantabhājanīyaṃ|

2. abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ

183. aṭṭhārasa dhātuyau – cakkhudhātu, rūpadhātu, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, sautadhātu, saddadhātu, sautaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, ghānadhātu, gandhadhātu, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, jivhādhātu, rasadhātu, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, kāyadhātu, phauṭṭhabbadhātu, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, manaudhātu, dhammadhātu, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu|

184. tattha katamā cakkhudhātu? yaṃ cakkhu catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau…pai॰… suñ‍ñau gāmaupaisau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘cakkhudhātu’’|

tattha katamā rūpadhātu? yaṃ rūpaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya vaṇṇanibhā…pai॰… rūpadhātupaisā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘rūpadhātu’’|

tattha katamā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātu? cakkhuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca rūpai ca uppaj‍jati cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jācakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātu’’|

tattha  katamā sautadhātu? yaṃ sautaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau…pai॰… suñ‍ñau gāmaupaisau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sautadhātu’’|

tattha katamā saddadhātu? yau saddau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassanau sappaṭighau…pai॰… saddadhātupaisā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saddadhātu’’|

tattha katamā sautaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu? sautañ‍ca paṭic‍ca saddai ca uppaj‍jati cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jāsautaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sautaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu’’|

tattha katamā ghānadhātu? yaṃ  ghānaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau…pai॰… suñ‍ñau gāmaupaisau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘ghānadhātu’’|

tattha katamā gandhadhātu? yau gandhau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassanau sappaṭighau…pai॰… gandhadhātupaisā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘gandhadhātu’’|

tattha  katamā ghānaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu? ghānañ‍ca paṭic‍ca gandhai ca uppaj‍jati cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jāghānaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘ghānaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu’’|

tattha katamā jivhādhātu? yā jivhā catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau…pai॰… suñ‍ñau gāmaupaisau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘jivhādhātu’’|

tattha katamā rasadhātu? yau rasau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya anidassanau sappaṭighau…pai॰… rasadhātupaisā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘rasadhātu’’|

tattha katamā jivhāviñ‍ñāṇadhātu? jivhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca rasai ca uppaj‍jati cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ  viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jājivhāviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘jivhāviñ‍ñāṇadhātu’’|

tattha katamā kāyadhātu? yau kāyau catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau…pai॰… suñ‍ñau gāmaupaisau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘kāyadhātu’’|

tattha  katamā phauṭṭhabbadhātu? pathavīdhātu…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbadhātupaisā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘phauṭṭhabbadhātu’’|

tattha katamā kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu? kāyañ‍ca paṭic‍ca phauṭṭhabbai ca uppaj‍jati cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jākāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu’’|

tattha katamā manaudhātu? cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā uppaj‍jitvā niruddhasamanantarā uppaj‍jati cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jāmanaudhātu; sautaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā…pai॰… jivhāviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā uppaj‍jitvā niruddhasamanantarā uppaj‍jati cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ  hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jāmanaudhātu  sabbadhammaisu vā pana paṭhamasaman‍nāhārau uppaj‍jati cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jāmanaudhātu – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘manaudhātu’’|

tattha katamā dhammadhātu? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau, yañ‍ca rūpaṃ anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammāyatanapariyāpan‍naṃ, asaṅkhatā ca dhātu|

tattha katamau vaidanākkhandhau? aikavidhaina vaidanākkhandhau – phassasampayuttau| duvidhaina vaidanākkhandhau – atthi sahaitukau, atthi ahaitukau| tividhaina vaidanākkhandhau – atthi kusalau, atthi akusalau, atthi abyākatau…pai॰… aivaṃ dasavidhaina vaidanākkhandhau…pai॰… aivaṃ bahuvidhaina vaidanākkhandhau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vaidanākkhandhau’’|

tattha katamau sañ‍ñākkhandhau? aikavidhaina sañ‍ñākkhandhau – phassasampayuttau| duvidhaina sañ‍ñākkhandhau – atthi sahaitukau, atthi ahaitukau| tividhaina sañ‍ñākkhandhau – atthi kusalau, atthi akusalau, atthi abyākatau…pai॰… aivaṃ dasavidhaina sañ‍ñākkhandhau…pai॰… aivaṃ bahuvidhaina sañ‍ñākkhandhau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sañ‍ñākkhandhau’’|

tattha katamau saṅkhārakkhandhau? aikavidhaina saṅkhārakkhandhau – cittasampayuttau| duvidhaina saṅkhārakkhandhau – atthi haitu, atthi ahaitu| tividhaina saṅkhārakkhandhau – atthi kusalau, atthi akusalau, atthi abyākatau…pai॰… aivaṃ  dasavidhaina saṅkhārakkhandhau…pai॰… aivaṃ bahuvidhaina saṅkhārakkhandhau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārakkhandhau’’|

tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammāyatanapariyāpan‍naṃ? itthindriyaṃ…pai॰… kabaḷīkārau āhārau – idaṃ vuc‍cati rūpaṃ ‘‘anidassanaappaṭighaṃ dhammāyatanapariyāpan‍naṃ’’|

tattha katamā asaṅkhatā dhātu? rāgakkhayau, dausakkhayau, mauhakkhayau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘asaṅkhatā dhātu’’| ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘dhammadhātu’’|

tattha  katamā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu? cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā uppaj‍jitvā niruddhasamanantarā uppaj‍jati manaudhātu, manaudhātuyā uppaj‍jitvā niruddhasamanantarā  uppaj‍jati cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu; sautaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā  …pai॰… jivhāviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā uppaj‍jitvā niruddhasamanantarā uppaj‍jati manaudhātu, manaudhātuyāpi uppaj‍jitvā niruddhasamanantarā uppaj‍jati cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu manañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dhammai ca uppaj‍jati cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu’’|

abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ|

3. pañhāpucchakaṃ

aṭṭhārasa dhātuyau – cakkhudhātu, rūpadhātu, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, sautadhātu, saddadhātu, sautaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, ghānadhātu, gandhadhātu, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, jivhādhātu, rasadhātu, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, kāyadhātu, phauṭṭhabbadhātu, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, manaudhātu, dhammadhātu, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu|

186. aṭṭhārasan‍naṃ dhātūnaṃ kati kusalā, kati akusalā, kati abyākatā…pai॰… kati saraṇā, kati araṇā?

1. tikaṃ

187. sauḷasa  dhātuyau abyākatā| dvai dhātuyau siyā kusalā, siyā akusalā, siyā abyākatā| dasa dhātuyau na vattabbā – ‘‘sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā’’tipi, ‘‘dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā’’tipi, ‘‘adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā’’tipi| pañ‍ca dhātuyau  adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā| kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu siyā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā| manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu siyā  sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā| dhammadhātu siyā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā’’tipi, ‘‘dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā’’tipi, ‘‘adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā’’tipi|

dasa dhātuyau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā| pañ‍ca  dhātuyau vipākā| manaudhātu siyā vipākā, siyā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā| dvai dhātuyau siyā vipākā, siyā vipākadhammadhammā, siyā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā|

dasa dhātuyau upādin‍nupādāniyā| saddadhātu anupādin‍nupādāniyā| pañ‍ca dhātuyau siyā upādin‍nupādāniyā, siyā anupādin‍nupādāniyā| dvai dhātuyau siyā upādin‍nupādāniyā, siyā anupādin‍nupādāniyā, siyā anupādin‍naanupādāniyā|
