navindriyā saṃyaujanavippayuttasaṃyaujaniyā| tīṇindriyā saṃyaujanavippayuttaasaṃyaujaniyā| daumanassindriyaṃ na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘saṃyaujanavippayuttasaṃyaujaniya’’ntipi, ‘‘saṃyaujanavippayuttaasaṃyaujaniya’’ntipi| tīṇindriyā siyā saṃyaujanavippayuttasaṃyaujaniyā, siyā saṃyaujanavippayuttaasaṃyaujaniyā| cha indriyā siyā saṃyaujanavippayuttasaṃyaujaniyā, siyā saṃyaujanavippayuttaasaṃyaujaniyā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘saṃyaujanavippayuttasaṃyaujaniyā’’tipi, ‘‘saṃyaujanavippayuttaasaṃyaujaniyā’’tipi|

nau ganthā| dasindriyā ganthaniyā| tīṇindriyā aganthaniyā| navindriyā siyā ganthaniyā, siyā aganthaniyā| pan‍narasindriyā ganthavippayuttā| daumanassindriyaṃ ganthasampayuttaṃ| cha indriyā siyā ganthasampayuttā, siyā ganthavippayuttā| dasindriyā na vattabbā – ‘‘ganthā caiva ganthaniyā cā’’ti, ganthaniyā caiva nau ca ganthā| tīṇindriyā na vattabbā – ‘‘ganthā caiva ganthaniyā cā’’tipi, ‘‘ganthaniyā caiva nau ca ganthā’’tipi| navindriyā na vattabbā – ‘‘ganthā  caiva ganthaniyā cā’’ti, siyā ganthaniyā caiva nau ca ganthā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘ganthaniyā caiva nau ca ganthā’’ti|

pan‍narasindriyā  na vattabbā – ‘‘ganthā caiva ganthasampayuttā cā’’tipi, ‘‘ganthasampayuttā caiva nau ca ganthā’’tipi| daumanassindriyaṃ na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘ganthau caiva ganthasampayutta’’ñ‍cāti, ganthasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca ganthau| cha indriyā na vattabbā – ‘‘ganthā caiva ganthasampayuttā cā’’ti, siyā ganthasampayuttā caiva nau ca ganthā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘ganthasampayuttā caiva nau ca ganthā’’ti|

navindriyā ganthavippayuttaganthaniyā| tīṇindriyā ganthavippayuttaaganthaniyā| daumanassindriyaṃ na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘ganthavippayuttaganthaniya’’ntipi, ‘‘ganthavippayuttaaganthaniya’’ntipi| tīṇindriyā siyā ganthavippayuttaganthaniyā, siyā ganthavippayuttaaganthaniyā| cha indriyā siyā ganthavippayuttaganthaniyā, siyā ganthavippayuttaaganthaniyā, siyā na vattabbā  – ‘‘ganthavippayuttaganthaniyā’’tipi, ‘‘ganthavippayuttaaganthaniyā’’tipi|

nau aughā…pai॰… nau yaugā…pai॰… nau nīvaraṇā| dasindriyā nīvaraṇiyā| tīṇindriyā anīvaraṇiyā| navindriyā siyā nīvaraṇiyā, siyā anīvaraṇiyā| pan‍narasindriyā nīvaraṇavippayuttā| daumanassindriyaṃ nīvaraṇasampayuttaṃ| cha indriyā siyā nīvaraṇasampayuttā, siyā nīvaraṇavippayuttā| dasindriyā na vattabbā – ‘‘nīvaraṇā caiva nīvaraṇiyā cā’’ti, nīvaraṇiyā caiva nau ca nīvaraṇā| tīṇindriyā na vattabbā – ‘‘nīvaraṇā caiva nīvaraṇiyā cā’’tipi, ‘‘nīvaraṇiyā caiva nau ca nīvaraṇā’’tipi| navindriyā na vattabbā – ‘‘nīvaraṇā caiva nīvaraṇiyā cā’’ti, siyā nīvaraṇiyā caiva nau ca nīvaraṇā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘nīvaraṇiyā caiva nau ca nīvaraṇā’’ti|

pan‍narasindriyā na vattabbā – ‘‘nīvaraṇā caiva nīvaraṇasampayuttā cā’’tipi, ‘‘nīvaraṇasampayuttā caiva nau ca nīvaraṇā’’tipi| daumanassindriyaṃ na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘nīvaraṇañ‍caiva nīvaraṇasampayuttañ‍cā’’ti, nīvaraṇasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca nīvaraṇaṃ| cha indriyā na  vattabbā – ‘‘nīvaraṇā caiva nīvaraṇasampayuttā cā’’ti, siyā nīvaraṇasampayuttā caiva nau ca nīvaraṇā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘nīvaraṇasampayuttā caiva nau ca nīvaraṇā’’ti|

navindriyā nīvaraṇavippayuttanīvaraṇiyā| tīṇindriyā nīvaraṇavippayuttaanīvaraṇiyā| daumanassindriyaṃ na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘nīvaraṇavippayuttanīvaraṇiya’’ntipi, ‘‘nīvaraṇavippayuttaanīvaraṇiya’’ntipi| tīṇindriyā siyā nīvaraṇavippayuttanīvaraṇiyā, siyā nīvaraṇavippayuttaanīvaraṇiyā| cha indriyā siyā nīvaraṇavippayuttanīvaraṇiyā, siyā nīvaraṇavippayuttaanīvaraṇiyā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘nīvaraṇavippayuttanīvaraṇiyā’’tipi, ‘‘nīvaraṇavippayuttaanīvaraṇiyā’’tipi|

nau parāmāsā| dasindriyā parāmaṭṭhā| tīṇindriyā aparāmaṭṭhā| navindriyā siyā parāmaṭṭhā, siyā aparāmaṭṭhā| sauḷasindriyā parāmāsavippayuttā| cha indriyā siyā parāmāsasampayuttā, siyā parāmāsavippayuttā| dasindriyā na vattabbā – ‘‘parāmāsā caiva parāmaṭṭhā cā’’ti, parāmaṭṭhā caiva nau ca parāmāsā| tīṇindriyā na vattabbā – ‘‘parāmāsā caiva parāmaṭṭhā  cā’’tipi, ‘‘parāmaṭṭhā caiva nau ca parāmāsā’’tipi| navindriyā na vattabbā – ‘‘parāmāsā caiva parāmaṭṭhā cā’’ti, siyā parāmaṭṭhā caiva nau ca parāmāsā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘parāmaṭṭhā caiva nau ca parāmāsā’’ti| dasindriyā parāmāsavippayuttaparāmaṭṭhā| tīṇindriyā parāmāsavippayuttaaparāmaṭṭhā| tīṇindriyā siyā parāmāsavippayuttaparāmaṭṭhā, siyā parāmāsavippayuttaaparāmaṭṭhā| cha indriyā siyā parāmāsavippayuttaparāmaṭṭhā, siyā parāmāsavippayuttaaparāmaṭṭhā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘parāmāsavippayuttaparāmaṭṭhā’’tipi, ‘‘parāmāsavippayuttaaparāmaṭṭhā’’tipi|

sattindriyā anārammaṇā| cuddasindriyā sārammaṇā| jīvitindriyaṃ siyā sārammaṇaṃ, siyā anārammaṇaṃ| aikavīsatindriyā nau  cittā| manindriyaṃ cittaṃ| tairasindriyā caitasikā| aṭṭhindriyā acaitasikā| jīvitindriyaṃ siyā caitasikaṃ, siyā acaitasikaṃ| tairasindriyā cittasampayuttā| sattindriyā cittavippayuttā| jīvitindriyaṃ siyā cittasampayuttaṃ, siyā cittavippayuttaṃ| manindriyaṃ na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘cittaina sampayutta’’ntipi, ‘‘cittaina vippayutta’’ntipi|

tairasindriyā  cittasaṃsaṭṭhā| sattindriyā cittavisaṃsaṭṭhā| jīvitindriyaṃ siyā cittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ, siyā cittavisaṃsaṭṭhaṃ| manindriyaṃ na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘cittaina saṃsaṭṭha’’ntipi, ‘‘cittaina visaṃsaṭṭha’’ntipi| tairasindriyā cittasamuṭṭhānā| aṭṭhindriyā nau cittasamuṭṭhānā| jīvitindriyaṃ siyā cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ, siyā nau cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ|

tairasindriyā cittasahabhunau| aṭṭhindriyā nau cittasahabhunau| jīvitindriyaṃ siyā cittasahabhū, siyā nau cittasahabhū| tairasindriyā cittānuparivattinau| aṭṭhindriyā nau cittānuparivattinau| jīvitindriyaṃ siyā cittānuparivatti, siyā nau cittānuparivatti|

tairasindriyā cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānā| aṭṭhindriyā nau cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānā| jīvitindriyaṃ siyā cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānaṃ, siyā nau cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānaṃ| tairasindriyā  cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhunau| aṭṭhindriyā nau cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhunau| jīvitindriyaṃ siyā cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhū, siyā nau cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhū| tairasindriyā cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattinau| aṭṭhindriyā nau cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattinau| jīvitindriyaṃ siyā cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivatti, siyā nau cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivatti| cha indriyā ajjhattikā| sauḷasindriyā bāhirā|

sattindriyā  upādā| cuddasindriyā nau upādā| jīvitindriyaṃ siyā upādā, siyā nau upādā| navindriyā upādin‍nā| cattārindriyā anupādin‍nā| navindriyā siyā upādin‍nā, siyā anupādin‍nā| nau upādānā| dasindriyā upādāniyā| tīṇindriyā anupādāniyā| navindriyā siyā upādāniyā, siyā anupādāniyā| sauḷasindriyā upādānavippayuttā| cha indriyā siyā upādānasampayuttā, siyā upādānavippayuttā| dasindriyā na vattabbā – ‘‘upādānā caiva upādāniyā cā’’ti, upādāniyā caiva nau ca upādānā| tīṇindriyā na vattabbā – ‘‘upādānā caiva upādāniyā cā’’tipi, ‘‘upādāniyā caiva nau ca upādānā’’tipi| navindriyā na vattabbā – ‘‘upādānā caiva upādāniyā cā’’ti, siyā upādāniyā caiva nau ca upādānā| dasindriyā siyā upādāniyā caiva nau ca upādānā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘upādāniyā caiva nau ca upādānā’’ti|

sauḷasindriyā  na vattabbā – ‘‘upādānā caiva upādānasampayuttā cā’’tipi, ‘‘upādānasampayuttā caiva nau ca upādānā’’tipi| cha indriyā na vattabbā – ‘‘upādānā caiva upādānasampayuttā cā’’ti, siyā upādānasampayuttā caiva nau ca upādānā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘upādānasampayuttā caiva nau ca upādānā’’ti| dasindriyā upādānavippayuttaupādāniyā| tīṇindriyā upādānavippayuttaanupādāniyā| tīṇindriyā  siyā upādānavippayuttaupādāniyā, siyā upādānavippayuttaanupādāniyā| cha indriyā siyā upādānavippayuttaupādāniyā, siyā upādānavippayuttaanupādāniyā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘upādānavippayuttaupādāniyā’’tipi, ‘‘upādānavippayuttaanupādāniyā’’tipi|

nau  kilaisā| dasindriyā saṃkilaisikā| tīṇindriyā asaṃkilaisikā| navindriyā siyā saṃkilaisikā, siyā asaṃkilaisikā| pan‍narasindriyā asaṃkiliṭṭhā| daumanassindriyaṃ saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ| cha indriyā siyā saṃkiliṭṭhā, siyā asaṃkiliṭṭhā| pan‍narasindriyā kilaisavippayuttā| daumanassindriyaṃ kilaisasampayuttaṃ| cha indriyā siyā kilaisasampayuttā, siyā kilaisavippayuttā| dasindriyā na vattabbā – ‘‘kilaisā caiva saṃkilaisikā cā’’ti, saṃkilaisikā caiva nau ca kilaisā| tīṇindriyā na vattabbā – ‘‘kilaisā caiva saṃkilaisikā cā’’tipi, ‘‘saṃkilaisikā caiva nau ca kilaisā’’tipi| navindriyā na vattabbā – ‘‘kilaisā caiva saṃkilaisikā cā’’ti, siyā saṃkilaisikā caiva nau ca kilaisā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘saṃkilaisikā caiva nau ca kilaisā’’ti|

pan‍narasindriyā na vattabbā – ‘‘kilaisā caiva saṃkiliṭṭhā cā’’tipi, ‘‘saṃkiliṭṭhā caiva nau ca kilaisā’’tipi| daumanassindriyaṃ na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘kilaisau  caiva saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍cā’’ti, saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍caiva nau ca kilaisau| cha indriyā na vattabbā – ‘‘kilaisā caiva saṃkiliṭṭhā cā’’ti, siyā saṃkiliṭṭhā caiva nau ca kilaisā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘saṃkiliṭṭhā caiva  nau ca kilaisā’’ti|

pan‍narasindriyā na vattabbā – ‘‘kilaisā caiva kilaisasampayuttā cā’’tipi, ‘‘kilaisasampayuttā caiva nau ca kilaisā’’tipi| daumanassindriyaṃ na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘kilaisau caiva kilaisasampayuttañ‍cā’’ti, kilaisasampayuttañ‍caiva nau ca kilaisau| cha indriyā na vattabbā – ‘‘kilaisā  caiva kilaisasampayuttā cā’’ti, siyā kilaisasampayuttā caiva nau ca kilaisā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘kilaisasampayuttā caiva nau ca kilaisā’’ti| navindriyā kilaisavippayuttasaṃkilaisikā| tīṇindriyā kilaisavippayuttaasaṃkilaisikā| daumanassindriyaṃ na vattabbaṃ – ‘‘kilaisavippayuttasaṃkilaisika’’ntipi, ‘‘kilaisavippayuttaasaṃkilaisika’’ntipi| tīṇindriyā siyā kilaisavippayuttasaṃkilaisikā, siyā kilaisavippayuttaasaṃkilaisikā| cha indriyā siyā kilaisavippayuttasaṃkilaisikā, siyā kilaisavippayuttaasaṃkilaisikā, siyā na vattabbā – ‘‘kilaisavippayuttasaṃkilaisikā’’tipi, ‘‘kilaisavippayuttaasaṃkilaisikā’’tipi|

pan‍narasindriyā  na dassanaina pahātabbā| sattindriyā siyā dassanaina pahātabbā, siyā na dassanaina pahātabbā| pan‍narasindriyā na bhāvanāya pahātabbā| sattindriyā siyā bhāvanāya pahātabbā, siyā na bhāvanāya pahātabbā| pan‍narasindriyā na dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā| sattindriyā siyā dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā, siyā na dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā| pan‍narasindriyā na bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā| sattindriyā siyā bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā, siyā na bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā|

navindriyā avitak‍kā| daumanassindriyaṃ savitak‍kaṃ| dvādasindriyā siyā savitak‍kā, siyā avitak‍kā| navindriyā avicārā| daumanassindriyaṃ savicāraṃ| dvādasindriyā siyā savicārā, siyā avicārā| aikādasindriyā appītikā| aikādasindriyā siyā sappītikā, siyā appītikā| aikādasindriyā na pītisahagatā| aikādasindriyā siyā pītisahagatā, siyā na pītisahagatā| dvādasindriyā na sukhasahagatā| dasindriyā siyā sukhasahagatā, siyā na sukhasahagatā| dvādasindriyā na upaikkhāsahagatā| dasindriyā siyā upaikkhāsahagatā, siyā na upaikkhāsahagatā|

dasindriyā kāmāvacarā| tīṇindriyā na kāmāvacarā| navindriyā siyā kāmāvacarā, siyā na kāmāvacarā| tairasindriyā na rūpāvacarā| navindriyā siyā rūpāvacarā, siyā na rūpāvacarā| cuddasindriyā na arūpāvacarā| aṭṭhindriyā siyā arūpāvacarā, siyā na arūpāvacarā| dasindriyā pariyāpan‍nā| tīṇindriyā apariyāpan‍nā| navindriyā siyā pariyāpan‍nā, siyā apariyāpan‍nā| aikādasindriyā  aniyyānikā| anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ  niyyānikaṃ| dasindriyā siyā niyyānikā, siyā aniyyānikā| dasindriyā aniyatā| anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ niyataṃ| aikādasindriyā siyā niyatā, siyā aniyatā| dasindriyā sauttarā| tīṇindriyā anuttarā| navindriyā siyā sauttarā, siyā anuttarā| pan‍narasindriyā araṇā| daumanassindriyaṃ saraṇaṃ| cha  indriyā siyā saraṇā, siyā araṇāti|

pañhāpucchakaṃ|

indriyavibhaṅgau niṭṭhitau|

6. paṭic‍casamuppādavibhaṅgau

1. suttantabhājanīyaṃ

225. avij‍jāpac‍cayā  saṅkhārā, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ, saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

226. tattha katamā avij‍jā? dukkhai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, dukkhasamudayai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya añ‍ñāṇaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jā’’|

tattha katamai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā? puñ‍ñābhisaṅkhārau, apuñ‍ñābhisaṅkhārau, ānaiñ‍jābhisaṅkhārau, kāyasaṅkhārau, vacīsaṅkhārau, cittasaṅkhārau|

tattha katamau puñ‍ñābhisaṅkhārau? kusalā caitanā kāmāvacarā rūpāvacarā dānamayā sīlamayā bhāvanāmayā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘puñ‍ñābhisaṅkhārau’’|

tattha katamau apuñ‍ñābhisaṅkhārau? akusalā caitanā kāmāvacarā  – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘apuñ‍ñābhisaṅkhārau’’|

tattha  katamau ānaiñ‍jābhisaṅkhārau? kusalā caitanā arūpāvacarā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘ānaiñ‍jābhisaṅkhārau’’|

tattha katamau kāyasaṅkhārau? kāyasañ‍caitanā kāyasaṅkhārau, vacīsañ‍caitanā vacīsaṅkhārau, manausañ‍caitanā cittasaṅkhārau| imai vuc‍canti ‘‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā’’|

227. tattha  katamaṃ saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ? cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ’’|

228. tattha katamaṃ viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ? atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ| tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’| tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ? cattārau mahābhūtā, catun‍nañ‍ca mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya rūpaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’| iti idañ‍ca nāmaṃ, idañ‍ca rūpaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ’’|

229. tattha katamaṃ nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ, sautāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ’’|

230. tattha katamau saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassau? cakkhusamphassau sautasamphassau ghānasamphassau jivhāsamphassau kāyasamphassau manausamphassau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassau’’|

231. tattha  katamā phassapac‍cayā vaidanā? cakkhusamphassajā vaidanā, sautasamphassajā vaidanā, ghānasamphassajā vaidanā, jivhāsamphassajā vaidanā, kāyasamphassajā vaidanā, manausamphassajā vaidanā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘phassapac‍cayā vaidanā’’|

232. tattha  katamā vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā? rūpataṇhā, saddataṇhā, gandhataṇhā, rasataṇhā, phauṭṭhabbataṇhā, dhammataṇhā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā’’|

233. tattha katamaṃ taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ? kāmupādānaṃ, diṭṭhupādānaṃ, sīlabbatupādānaṃ, attavādupādānaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ’’|

234. tattha katamau upādānapac‍cayā bhavau? bhavau duvidhaina  – atthi kammabhavau, atthi upapattibhavau| tattha katamau kammabhavau? puñ‍ñābhisaṅkhārau, apuñ‍ñābhisaṅkhārau, ānaiñ‍jābhisaṅkhārau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘kammabhavau’’| sabbampi bhavagāmikammaṃ kammabhavau|

tattha katamau upapattibhavau? kāmabhavau, rūpabhavau, arūpabhavau, sañ‍ñābhavau, asañ‍ñābhavau, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñābhavau, aikavaukārabhavau, catuvaukārabhavau, pañ‍cavaukārabhavau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘upapattibhavau’’| iti ayañ‍ca kammabhavau, ayañ‍ca upapattibhavau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘upādānapac‍cayā bhavau’’|

235. tattha  katamā bhavapac‍cayā jāti? yā taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ tamhi tamhi sattanikāyai jāti sañ‍jāti auk‍kanti abhinibbatti, khandhānaṃ pātubhāvau, āyatanānaṃ paṭilābhau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘bhavapac‍cayā jāti’’|

236. tattha katamaṃ jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ? atthi jarā, atthi maraṇaṃ| tattha katamā jarā? yā taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ tamhi tamhi sattanikāyai jarā jīraṇatā khaṇḍic‍caṃ pālic‍caṃ valittacatā āyunau saṃhāni indriyānaṃ paripākau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘jarā’’|

tattha katamaṃ maraṇaṃ? yā taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ tamhā tamhā sattanikāyā cuti cavanatā bhaidau antaradhānaṃ mac‍cu maraṇaṃ kālakiriyā kālaṃkiriyā (ka॰) khandhānaṃ bhaidau kaḷaivarassa nikkhaipau jīvitindriyassupacchaidau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘maraṇaṃ’’| iti ayañ‍ca jarā, idañ‍ca maraṇaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ’’|

237. tattha  katamau saukau? ñātibyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa, bhaugabyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa, raugabyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa, sīlabyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa, diṭṭhibyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa, añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina byasanaina saman‍nāgatassa, añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina dukkhadhammaina phuṭṭhassa saukau saucanā saucitattaṃ antausaukau antauparisaukau caitasau parijjhāyanā daumanassaṃ saukasal‍laṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saukau’’|

238. tattha katamau paridaivau? ñātibyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa, bhaugabyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa, raugabyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa, sīlabyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa, diṭṭhibyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa, añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina byasanaina saman‍nāgatassa, añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina dukkhadhammaina phuṭṭhassa ādaivau paridaivau  ādaivanā paridaivanā ādaivitattaṃ paridaivitattaṃ vācā palāpau vippalāpau lālappau lālappanā lālappitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati paridaivau’’|

239. tattha katamaṃ dukkhaṃ? yaṃ kāyikaṃ asātaṃ kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ kāyasamphassajaṃ asātaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidayitaṃ kāyasamphassajā asātā dukkhā vaidanā – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘dukkhaṃ’’|

240. tattha  katamaṃ daumanassaṃ? yaṃ caitasikaṃ asātaṃ, caitasikaṃ dukkhaṃ, caitausamphassajaṃ asātaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidayitaṃ, caitausamphassajā asātā dukkhā vaidanā – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘daumanassaṃ’’|

241. tattha katamau upāyāsau? ñātibyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa, bhaugabyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa, raugabyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa, sīlabyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa, diṭṭhibyasanaina vā phuṭṭhassa, añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina byasanaina saman‍nāgatassa, añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina dukkhadhammaina phuṭṭhassa āyāsau upāyāsau āyāsitattaṃ upāyāsitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘upāyāsau’’|

242. aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautīti, aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa saṅgati hauti, samāgamau hauti, samaudhānaṃ hauti, pātubhāvau hauti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

suttantabhājanīyaṃ|

2. abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ

1. pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

243. avij‍jāpac‍cayā  saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayā  bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

avij‍jāpac‍cayā  saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau  hauti|

avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ|

2. haitucatuk‍kaṃ

244. avij‍jāpac‍cayā  saṅkhārau avij‍jāhaitukau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārahaitukaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ viñ‍ñāṇahaitukaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmahaitukaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau chaṭṭhāyatanahaitukau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā phassahaitukā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā vaidanāhaitukā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ taṇhāhaitukaṃ; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau avij‍jāhaitukau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārahaitukaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ viñ‍ñāṇahaitukaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā phassau nāmahaitukau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā phassahaitukā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā vaidanāhaitukā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ taṇhāhaitukaṃ; upādānapac‍cayā  bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau avij‍jāhaitukau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārahaitukaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ viñ‍ñāṇahaitukaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ  nāmarūpahaitukaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau chaṭṭhāyatanahaitukau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā phassahaitukā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā vaidanāhaitukā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ taṇhāhaitukaṃ; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau avij‍jāhaitukau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārahaitukaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ viñ‍ñāṇahaitukaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ nāmarūpahaitukaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau chaṭṭhāyatanahaitukau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā phassahaitukā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā vaidanāhaitukā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ taṇhāhaitukaṃ; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

haitucatuk‍kaṃ|

3. sampayuttacatuk‍kaṃ

245. avij‍jāpac‍cayā  saṅkhārau avij‍jāsampayuttau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasampayuttaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmasampayuttaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau chaṭṭhāyatanasampayuttau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā phassasampayuttā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā vaidanāsampayuttā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ taṇhāsampayuttaṃ; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau avij‍jāsampayuttau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasampayuttaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā phassau nāmasampayuttau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā phassasampayuttā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā vaidanāsampayuttā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ taṇhāsampayuttaṃ; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

avij‍jāpac‍cayā  saṅkhārau avij‍jāsampayuttau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ  viñ‍ñāṇasampayuttaṃ nāmaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmarūpasampayuttaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau chaṭṭhāyatanasampayuttau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā phassasampayuttā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā vaidanāsampayuttā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ taṇhāsampayuttaṃ; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau avij‍jāsampayuttau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasampayuttaṃ nāmaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ nāmasampayuttaṃ chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau chaṭṭhāyatanasampayuttau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā phassasampayuttā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā vaidanāsampayuttā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ upādānasampayuttaṃ; upādānapac‍cayā  bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa  samudayau hauti|

sampayuttacatuk‍kaṃ|

4. añ‍ñamañ‍ñacatuk‍kaṃ

246. avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayāpi avij‍jā; saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayāpi saṅkhārau; viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayāpi viñ‍ñāṇaṃ; nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayāpi nāmaṃ; chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayāpi chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ; phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayāpi phassau; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayāpi vaidanā; taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayāpi taṇhā; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayāpi avij‍jā; saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayāpi saṅkhārau; viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayāpi viñ‍ñāṇaṃ; nāmapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayāpi nāmaṃ; phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayāpi  phassau; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayāpi vaidanā; taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayāpi taṇhā; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

avij‍jāpac‍cayā  saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayāpi avij‍jā; saṅkhārapac‍cayā  viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayāpi saṅkhārau; viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayāpi viñ‍ñāṇaṃ; nāmarūpapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayāpi nāmarūpaṃ; chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayāpi chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ; phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayāpi phassau; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayāpi vaidanā; taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayāpi taṇhā; upādānapac‍cayā  bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayāpi avij‍jā; saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayāpi saṅkhārau; viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayāpi viñ‍ñāṇaṃ; nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayāpi nāmarūpaṃ; chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayāpi chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ; phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayāpi phassau; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayāpi vaidanā; taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayāpi taṇhā; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

añ‍ñamañ‍ñacatuk‍kaṃ|

mātikā

247. saṅkhārapac‍cayā avij‍jā…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā avij‍jā…pai॰… nāmapac‍cayā  avij‍jā…pai॰… chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā avij‍jā…pai॰… phassapac‍cayā  avij‍jā…pai॰… vaidanāpac‍cayā avij‍jā…pai॰… taṇhāpac‍cayā avij‍jā…pai॰… upādānapac‍cayā avij‍jā…pai॰… avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayā  upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

mātikā|

5. pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ

248. katamai  dhammā akusalā? yasmiṃ samayai akusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ diṭṭhigatasampayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā phauṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

249. tattha katamā avij‍jā? yaṃ añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pai॰… avij‍jālaṅgī mauhau akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jā’’|

tattha katamau avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā  sañ‍caitayitattaṃ caitayitattaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau’’|

tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ’’|

tattha  katamau chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau? yau phassau phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau’’|

tattha katamā phassapac‍cayā vaidanā? yaṃ caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘phassapac‍cayā vaidanā’’|

tattha  katamā vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā? yau rāgau sārāgau anunayau anuraudhau nandī nandirāgau cittassa sārāgau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā’’|

tattha katamaṃ taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ? yā diṭṭhi diṭṭhigataṃ  diṭṭhigahanaṃ diṭṭhikantārau diṭṭhivisūkāyikaṃ diṭṭhivipphanditaṃ diṭṭhisaṃyaujanaṃ gāhau patiṭṭhāhau abhinivaisau parāmāsau kummaggau micchāpathau micchattaṃ titthāyatanaṃ vipariyāsaggāhau vipariyaisaggāhau (bahūsu) – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ’’|

tattha katamau upādānapac‍cayā bhavau? ṭhapaitvā upādānaṃ, vaidanākkhandhau sañ‍ñākkhandhau saṅkhārakkhandhau viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘upādānapac‍cayā bhavau’’|

tattha katamā bhavapac‍cayā jāti? yā taisaṃ taisaṃ dhammānaṃ jāti sañ‍jāti nibbatti abhinibbatti pātubhāvau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘bhavapac‍cayā jāti’’|

tattha katamaṃ jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ? atthi jarā, atthi maraṇaṃ| tattha katamā jarā? yā taisaṃ taisaṃ dhammānaṃ jarā jīraṇatā āyunau saṃhāni – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘jarā’’| tattha katamaṃ maraṇaṃ? yau taisaṃ taisaṃ dhammānaṃ khayau vayau bhaidau paribhaidau anic‍catā antaradhānaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘maraṇaṃ’’| iti ayañ‍ca jarā, idañ‍ca maraṇaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ’’|

aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautīti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa  saṅgati hauti, samāgamau hauti, samaudhānaṃ hauti, pātubhāvau hauti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

250. tasmiṃ  samayai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā  vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayā  upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

251. tattha katamā avij‍jā? yaṃ añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pai॰… avij‍jālaṅgī mauhau akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jā’’|

tattha katamau avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau’’|

tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ’’|

nāmapac‍cayā phassauti| tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? ṭhapaitvā phassaṃ, vaidanākkhandhau sañ‍ñākkhandhau saṅkhārakkhandhau viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’|

tattha katamau nāmapac‍cayā phassau? yau phassau phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmapac‍cayā phassau’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

tasmiṃ samayai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

253. tattha katamā avij‍jā? yaṃ añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pai॰… avij‍jālaṅgī mauhau akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jā’’|

tattha katamau avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau’’|

tattha  katamaṃ saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ’’|

tattha  katamaṃ viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ? atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ| tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’| tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ? cakkhāyatanassa upacayau, sautāyatanassa upacayau, ghānāyatanassa upacayau, jivhāyatanassa upacayau, kāyāyatanassa upacayau, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ cittajaṃ cittahaitukaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’| iti idañ‍ca nāmaṃ, idañ‍ca rūpaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ’’|

nāmarūpapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatananti| atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ| tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’| tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ? yaṃ rūpaṃ nissāya manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu vattati – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’| iti idañ‍ca nāmaṃ, idañ‍ca rūpaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmarūpaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ nāmarūpapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ? yaṃ  cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmarūpapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ’’|

tattha  katamau chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau? yau phassau phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

254. tasmiṃ samayai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

255. tattha katamā avij‍jā? yaṃ añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pai॰… avij‍jālaṅgī mauhau akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jā’’|

tattha katamau avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau’’|

tattha  katamaṃ saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ’’|

tattha  katamaṃ viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ? atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ| tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’| tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ? cakkhāyatanassa upacayau, sautāyatanassa upacayau, ghānāyatanassa upacayau, jivhāyatanassa upacayau, kāyāyatanassa upacayau, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ cittajaṃ cittahaitukaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’| iti idañ‍ca nāmaṃ, idañ‍ca rūpaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ’’|

nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatananti| atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ| tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’| tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ? cattārau  ca mahābhūtā, yañ‍ca rūpaṃ nissāya manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu vattati – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’| iti idañ‍ca nāmaṃ, idañ‍ca rūpaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmarūpaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ, sautāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ’’|

tattha katamau chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau? yau phassau phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

pac‍cayacatuk‍kaṃ|

6. haitucatuk‍kaṃ

256. tasmiṃ samayai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau avij‍jāhaitukau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārahaitukaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ viñ‍ñāṇahaitukaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmahaitukaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau chaṭṭhāyatanahaitukau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā phassahaitukā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā vaidanāhaitukā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ taṇhāhaitukaṃ; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

257. tattha  katamā avij‍jā? yaṃ añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pai॰… avij‍jālaṅgī mauhau akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jā’’|

tattha katamau avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau avij‍jāhaitukau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau avij‍jāhaitukau’’|

tattha  katamaṃ saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārahaitukaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārahaitukaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ viñ‍ñāṇahaitukaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ viñ‍ñāṇahaitukaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmahaitukaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmahaitukaṃ’’|

tattha katamau chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau chaṭṭhāyatanahaitukau? yau phassau phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau chaṭṭhāyatanahaitukau’’|

tattha katamā phassapac‍cayā vaidanā phassahaitukā? yaṃ caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘phassapac‍cayā vaidanā phassahaitukā’’|

tattha katamā vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā vaidanāhaitukā? yau rāgau sārāgau…pai॰… cittassa sārāgau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā vaidanāhaitukā’’|

tattha katamaṃ taṇhāpac‍cayā  upādānaṃ taṇhāhaitukaṃ? yā diṭṭhi diṭṭhigataṃ…pai॰… titthāyatanaṃ vipariyāsaggāhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ taṇhāhaitukaṃ’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti’’ti|

258. tasmiṃ  samayai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau avij‍jāhaitukau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārahaitukaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ viñ‍ñāṇahaitukaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā phassau nāmahaitukau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā phassahaitukā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā vaidanāhaitukā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ taṇhāhaitukaṃ; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

259. tattha  katamā avij‍jā? yaṃ añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pai॰… avij‍jālaṅgī mauhau akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jā’’|

tattha katamau avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau avij‍jāhaitukau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau avij‍jāhaitukau’’|

tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārahaitukaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārahaitukaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ viñ‍ñāṇahaitukaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ viñ‍ñāṇahaitukaṃ’’|

nāmapac‍cayā phassau nāmahaitukauti| tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? ṭhapaitvā phassaṃ, vaidanākkhandhau sañ‍ñākkhandhau saṅkhārakkhandhau viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’|

tattha katamau nāmapac‍cayā phassau nāmahaitukau? yau phassau phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmapac‍cayā phassau nāmahaitukau’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

260. tasmiṃ samayai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau avij‍jāhaitukau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārahaitukaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ viñ‍ñāṇahaitukaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmarūpahaitukaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau chaṭṭhāyatanahaitukau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā phassahaitukā, vaidanāpac‍cayā  taṇhā vaidanāhaitukā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ taṇhāhaitukaṃ; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā  jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

261. tattha katamā avij‍jā? yaṃ añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pai॰… avij‍jālaṅgī mauhau akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jā’’|

tattha  katamau avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau avij‍jāhaitukau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau avij‍jāhaitukau’’|

tattha  katamaṃ saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārahaitukaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārahaitukaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ viñ‍ñāṇahaitukaṃ? atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ| tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’| tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ? cakkhāyatanassa upacayau, sautāyatanassa upacayau, ghānāyatanassa upacayau, jivhāyatanassa upacayau, kāyāyatanassa upacayau, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ cittajaṃ cittahaitukaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’| iti idañ‍ca nāmaṃ, idañ‍ca rūpaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ viñ‍ñāṇahaitukaṃ’’|

nāmarūpapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmarūpahaitukanti| atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ| tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’| tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ? yaṃ rūpaṃ nissāya manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu vattati – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’| iti idañ‍ca nāmaṃ, idañ‍ca rūpaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmarūpaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ nāmarūpapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmarūpahaitukaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmarūpapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmarūpahaitukaṃ’’|

tattha katamau chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau chaṭṭhāyatanahaitukau? yau phassau phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau chaṭṭhāyatanahaitukau’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

262. tasmiṃ  samayai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau avij‍jāhaitukau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā  viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārahaitukaṃ viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ viñ‍ñāṇahaitukaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ nāmarūpahaitukaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau chaṭṭhāyatanahaitukau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā phassahaitukā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā vaidanāhaitukā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ taṇhāhaitukaṃ; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

263. tattha katamā avij‍jā? yaṃ añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pai॰… avij‍jālaṅgī mauhau akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jā’’|

tattha katamau avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau avij‍jāhaitukau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau avij‍jāhaitukau’’|

tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārahaitukaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārahaitukaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ viñ‍ñāṇahaitukaṃ? atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ| tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’| tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ? cakkhāyatanassa upacayau, sautāyatanassa upacayau, ghānāyatanassa upacayau, jivhāyatanassa upacayau, kāyāyatanassa upacayau, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ cittajaṃ cittahaitukaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’| iti idañ‍ca nāmaṃ, idañ‍ca rūpaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ viñ‍ñāṇahaitukaṃ’’|

nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ nāmarūpahaitukanti| atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ| tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’| tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ? cattārau ca mahābhūtā, yañ‍ca rūpaṃ nissāya manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu vattati – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’| iti idañ‍ca nāmaṃ, idañ‍ca rūpaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmarūpaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ nāmarūpahaitukaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ, sautāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ nāmarūpahaitukaṃ’’|

tattha  katamau chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau chaṭṭhāyatanahaitukau? yau phassau phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau chaṭṭhāyatanahaitukau’’|

tattha katamā phassapac‍cayā vaidanā phassahaitukā? yaṃ caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘phassapac‍cayā vaidanā phassahaitukā’’|

tattha katamā vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā vaidanāhaitukā? yau rāgau sārāgau…pai॰… cittassa sārāgau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā vaidanāhaitukā’’|

tattha katamaṃ taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ taṇhāhaitukaṃ? yā diṭṭhi diṭṭhigataṃ…pai॰… titthāyatanaṃ vipariyāsaggāhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ taṇhāhaitukaṃ’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

haitucatuk‍kaṃ|

7. sampayuttacatuk‍kaṃ

tasmiṃ samayai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau avij‍jāsampayuttau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasampayuttaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmasampayuttaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau chaṭṭhāyatanasampayuttau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā phassasampayuttā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā vaidanāsampayuttā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ taṇhāsampayuttaṃ; upādānapac‍cayā  bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

265. tattha katamā avij‍jā? yaṃ añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pai॰… avij‍jālaṅgī mauhau akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jā’’|

tattha  katamau avij‍jāpac‍cayā  saṅkhārau avij‍jāsampayuttau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau avij‍jāsampayuttau’’|

tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasampayuttaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasampayuttaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmasampayuttaṃ? yaṃ  cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmasampayuttaṃ’’|

tattha katamau chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau chaṭṭhāyatanasampayuttau? yau phassau phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau chaṭṭhāyatanasampayuttau’’|

tattha katamā phassapac‍cayā vaidanā phassasampayuttā? yaṃ caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘phassapac‍cayā vaidanā phassasampayuttā’’|

tattha katamā vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā vaidanāsampayuttā? yau rāgau sārāgau…pai॰… cittassa sārāgau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā vaidanāsampayuttā’’|

tattha  katamaṃ taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ taṇhāsampayuttaṃ? yā diṭṭhi diṭṭhigataṃ…pai॰… titthāyatanaṃ vipariyāsaggāhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ taṇhāsampayuttaṃ’’  …pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

266. tasmiṃ samayai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau avij‍jāsampayuttau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasampayuttaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā phassau nāmasampayuttau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā phassasampayuttā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā vaidanāsampayuttā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ taṇhāsampayuttaṃ; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

267. tattha katamā avij‍jā? yaṃ añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pai॰… avij‍jālaṅgī mauhau akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jā’’|

tattha katamau avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau avij‍jāsampayuttau? yā  caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau avij‍jāsampayuttau’’|

tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasampayuttaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasampayuttaṃ’’|

nāmapac‍cayā phassau nāmasampayuttauti| tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? ṭhapaitvā phassaṃ, vaidanākkhandhau sañ‍ñākkhandhau saṅkhārakkhandhau  viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’|

tattha katamau nāmapac‍cayā phassau nāmasampayuttau? yau phassau phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmapac‍cayā phassau nāmasampayuttau’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

268. tasmiṃ  samayai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau avij‍jāsampayuttau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasampayuttaṃ nāmaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmasampayuttaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau chaṭṭhāyatanasampayuttau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā phassasampayuttā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā vaidanāsampayuttā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ taṇhāsampayuttaṃ; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā  jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

269. tattha katamā avij‍jā? yaṃ añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pai॰… avij‍jālaṅgī mauhau akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jā’’|

tattha katamau avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau avij‍jāsampayuttau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau avij‍jāsampayuttau’’|

tattha  katamaṃ saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ …pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ  viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasampayuttaṃ nāmaṃ? atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ| tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’| tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ? cakkhāyatanassa upacayau, sautāyatanassa upacayau, ghānāyatanassa upacayau, jivhāyatanassa upacayau, kāyāyatanassa upacayau, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ cittajaṃ cittahaitukaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’| iti idañ‍ca nāmaṃ, idañ‍ca rūpaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasampayuttaṃ nāmaṃ’’|

nāmarūpapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmasampayuttanti| atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ| tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’| tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ? yaṃ rūpaṃ nissāya manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu vattati – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’| iti idañ‍ca nāmaṃ, idañ‍ca rūpaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmarūpaṃ’’|

tattha  katamaṃ nāmarūpapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmasampayuttaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmarūpapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmasampayuttaṃ’’|

tattha katamau chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau chaṭṭhāyatanasampayuttau? yau phassau phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau chaṭṭhāyatanasampayuttau’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

270. tasmiṃ  samayai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau avij‍jāsampayuttau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasampayuttaṃ nāmaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ nāmasampayuttaṃ  chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau chaṭṭhāyatanasampayuttau, phassapac‍cayā  vaidanā phassasampayuttā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā vaidanāsampayuttā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ taṇhāsampayuttaṃ; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

271. tattha katamā avij‍jā? yaṃ añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pai॰… avij‍jālaṅgī mauhau akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jā’’|

tattha katamau avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau avij‍jāsampayuttau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau avij‍jāsampayuttau’’|

tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasampayuttaṃ nāmaṃ? atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ| tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’| tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ? cakkhāyatanassa upacayau, sautāyatanassa upacayau, ghānāyatanassa upacayau, jivhāyatanassa upacayau, kāyāyatanassa upacayau, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi  rūpaṃ cittajaṃ cittahaitukaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’| iti idañ‍ca nāmaṃ, idañ‍ca rūpaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasampayuttaṃ nāmaṃ’’|

nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ nāmasampayuttaṃ chaṭṭhāyatananti| atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ| tattha  katamaṃ nāmaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’| tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ? cattārau ca mahābhūtā, yañ‍ca rūpaṃ nissāya manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu vattati – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’| iti idañ‍ca nāmaṃ, idañ‍ca rūpaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmarūpaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ nāmasampayuttaṃ chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ, sautāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ  – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ nāmasampayuttaṃ chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ’’|

tattha  katamau chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau chaṭṭhāyatanasampayuttau? yau phassau phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau chaṭṭhāyatanasampayuttau’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

sampayuttacatuk‍kaṃ|

8. añ‍ñamañ‍ñacatuk‍kaṃ

272. tasmiṃ samayai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayāpi avij‍jā; saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayāpi saṅkhārau; viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayāpi viñ‍ñāṇaṃ; nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayāpi nāmaṃ; chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayāpi chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ; phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayāpi phassau; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayāpi vaidanā; taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayāpi taṇhā; upādānapac‍cayā  bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

273. tattha  katamā avij‍jā? yaṃ añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pai॰… avij‍jālaṅgī mauhau akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jā’’|

tattha katamau avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau’’|

tattha katamā saṅkhārapac‍cayāpi avij‍jā? yaṃ añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pai॰… avij‍jālaṅgī mauhau akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārapac‍cayāpi avij‍jā’’|

tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ|

tattha katamau viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayāpi saṅkhārau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayāpi saṅkhārau’’|

tattha  katamaṃ viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ nāmapac‍cayāpi viñ‍ñāṇaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmapac‍cayāpi viñ‍ñāṇaṃ’’|

tattha  katamaṃ nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ’’|

tattha  katamaṃ chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayāpi nāmaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayāpi nāmaṃ’’|

tattha katamau chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau? yau phassau phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau’’|

tattha katamaṃ phassapac‍cayāpi chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘phassapac‍cayāpi chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ’’|

tattha katamā phassapac‍cayā vaidanā? yaṃ caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘phassapac‍cayā vaidanā’’|

tattha katamau vaidanāpac‍cayāpi phassau? yau phassau phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vaidanāpac‍cayāpi phassau’’|

tattha katamā vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā? yau rāgau sārāgau…pai॰… cittassa sārāgau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā’’|

tattha katamā taṇhāpac‍cayāpi vaidanā? yaṃ caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘taṇhāpac‍cayāpi vaidanā’’|

tattha katamaṃ taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ? yā diṭṭhi diṭṭhigataṃ…pai॰… titthāyatanaṃ vipariyāsaggāhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ’’|

tattha katamā upādānapac‍cayāpi taṇhā? yau rāgau…pai॰… cittassa  sārāgau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘upādānapac‍cayāpi taṇhā’’|

tattha  katamau upādānapac‍cayā bhavau? ṭhapaitvā upādānaṃ, vaidanākkhandhau sañ‍ñākkhandhau saṅkhārakkhandhau viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘upādānapac‍cayā bhavau’’|

tattha katamā bhavapac‍cayā jāti? yā taisaṃ taisaṃ dhammānaṃ jāti sañ‍jāti nibbatti abhinibbati pātubhāvau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘bhavapac‍cayā jāti’’|

tattha katamaṃ jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ? atthi jarā, atthi maraṇaṃ| tattha katamā jarā? yā taisaṃ taisaṃ dhammānaṃ jarā jīraṇatā āyunau saṃhāni – ayaṃ vuc‍cati  ‘‘jarā’’| tattha katamaṃ maraṇaṃ? yau taisaṃ taisaṃ dhammānaṃ khayau vayau bhaidau paribhaidau anic‍catā antaradhānaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘maraṇaṃ’’| iti ayañ‍ca jarā, idañ‍ca maraṇaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ’’|

aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautīti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa saṅgati hauti, samāgamau hauti, samaudhānaṃ hauti, pātubhāvau hauti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

274. tasmiṃ samayai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayāpi avij‍jā; saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayāpi saṅkhārau; viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayāpi viñ‍ñāṇaṃ; nāmapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayāpi nāmaṃ; phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayāpi phassau; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayāpi vaidanā; taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayāpi taṇhā; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

275. tattha katamā avij‍jā? yaṃ añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pai॰… avij‍jālaṅgī mauhau akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jā’’|

tattha katamau avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau’’|

tattha  katamā saṅkhārapac‍cayāpi avij‍jā? yaṃ añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pai॰… avij‍jālaṅgī mauhau akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārapac‍cayāpi avij‍jā’’|

tattha  katamaṃ saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ’’|

tattha katamau viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayāpi saṅkhārau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayāpi saṅkhārau’’|

tattha katamaṃ viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ nāmapac‍cayāpi viñ‍ñāṇaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmapac‍cayāpi viñ‍ñāṇaṃ’’|

nāmapac‍cayā  phassauti| tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? ṭhapaitvā phassaṃ, vaidanākkhandhau sañ‍ñākkhandhau saṅkhārakkhandhau viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’|

tattha katamau nāmapac‍cayā phassau? yau phassau phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmapac‍cayā phassau’’|

tattha katamaṃ phassapac‍cayāpi nāmaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘phassapac‍cayāpi nāmaṃ’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

276. tasmiṃ samayai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayāpi avij‍jā; saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayāpi saṅkhārau; viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayāpi viñ‍ñāṇaṃ; nāmarūpapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayāpi nāmarūpaṃ; chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā  phassau, phassapac‍cayāpi chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ; phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayāpi phassau; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayāpi vaidanā; taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayāpi taṇhā; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

277. tattha katamā avij‍jā? yaṃ añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pai॰… avij‍jālaṅgī mauhau akusamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jā’’|

tattha katamau avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau|

tattha  katamā saṅkhārapac‍cayāpi avij‍jā? yaṃ añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pai॰… avij‍jālaṅgī mauhau akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārapac‍cayāpi avij‍jā’’|

tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ  vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ’’|

tattha katamau viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayāpi saṅkhārau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayāpi saṅkhārau’’|

tattha katamaṃ viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ? atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ| tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’| tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ? cakkhāyatanassa upacayau, sautāyatanassa upacayau, ghānāyatanassa upacayau, jivhāyatanassa upacayau, kāyāyatanassa upacayau, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ cittajaṃ cittahaitukaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’| iti idañ‍ca nāmaṃ, idañ‍ca rūpaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ’’|

nāmarūpapac‍cayāpi viñ‍ñāṇanti| atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ| tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’| tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ? yaṃ rūpaṃ nissāya manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu vattati – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’| iti idañ‍ca nāmaṃ, idañ‍ca rūpaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmarūpaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ nāmarūpapac‍cayāpi viñ‍ñāṇaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmarūpapac‍cayāpi viñ‍ñāṇaṃ’’|

nāmarūpapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatananti| atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ| tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’| tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ? yaṃ rūpaṃ nissāya manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu vattati – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’| iti idañ‍ca nāmaṃ, idañ‍ca rūpaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmarūpaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ nāmarūpapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ  vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmarūpapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayāpi nāmarūpaṃ? atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ| tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati  ‘‘nāmaṃ’’| tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ? cakkhāyatanassa upacayau, sautāyatanassa upacayau, ghānāyatanassa upacayau, jivhāyatanassa upacayau, kāyāyatanassa upacayau, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ cittajaṃ cittahaitukaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’| iti idañ‍ca nāmaṃ, idañ‍ca rūpaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayāpi nāmarūpaṃ’’|

tattha katamau chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau? yau phassau phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau’’|

tattha katamaṃ phassapac‍cayāpi chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘phassapac‍cayāpi chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

278. tasmiṃ  samayai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayāpi avij‍jā; saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayāpi saṅkhārau; viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayāpi viñ‍ñāṇaṃ; nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayāpi nāmarūpaṃ; chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayāpi chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ; phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayāpi phassau; vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayāpi vaidanā; taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayāpi taṇhā; upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa  samudayau hauti|

279. tattha katamā avij‍jā? yaṃ añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pai॰… avij‍jālaṅgī mauhau akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jā’’|

tattha katamau avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau’’|

tattha katamā saṅkhārapac‍cayāpi avij‍jā? yaṃ añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pai॰… avij‍jālaṅgī mauhau akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārapac‍cayāpi avij‍jā’’|

tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ’’|

tattha katamau viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayāpi saṅkhārau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayāpi saṅkhārau’’|

tattha  katamaṃ viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ? atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ| tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati nāmaṃ| tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ? cakkhāyatanassa upacayau, sautāyatanassa upacayau, ghānāyatanassa upacayau, jivhāyatanassa upacayau, kāyāyatanassa upacayau, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ cittajaṃ cittahaitukaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ  – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’| iti idañ‍ca nāmaṃ, idañ‍ca rūpaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ’’|

nāmarūpapac‍cayāpi viñ‍ñāṇanti| atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ| tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’| tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ? yaṃ rūpaṃ nissāya manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu vattati – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’| iti idañ‍ca nāmaṃ, idañ‍ca rūpaṃ| idaṃ  vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmarūpaṃ’’|

tattha  katamaṃ nāmarūpapac‍cayāpi viñ‍ñāṇaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmarūpapac‍cayāpi viñ‍ñāṇaṃ’’|

nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatananti| atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ| tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’| tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ? cattārau ca mahābhūtā, yañ‍ca rūpaṃ nissāya manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu vattati – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’| iti idañ‍ca nāmaṃ, idañ‍ca rūpaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmarūpaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ? cakkhāyatanaṃ, sautāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayāpi nāmarūpaṃ? atthi nāmaṃ, atthi rūpaṃ| tattha katamaṃ nāmaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmaṃ’’| tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ? cakkhāyatanassa upacayau, sautāyatanassa upacayau, ghānāyatanassa upacayau, jivhāyatanassa upacayau, kāyāyatanassa upacayau, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi atthi rūpaṃ cittajaṃ cittahaitukaṃ cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘rūpaṃ’’| iti idañ‍ca nāmaṃ, idañ‍ca rūpaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayāpi nāmarūpaṃ’’|

tattha katamau chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau? yau phassau phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau’’|

tattha  katamaṃ phassapac‍cayāpi chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘phassapac‍cayāpi chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ’’|

tattha katamā phassapac‍cayā vaidanā? yaṃ caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘phassapac‍cayā vaidanā’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

añ‍ñamañ‍ñacatuk‍kaṃ|

9. akusalaniddaisau

280. katamai  dhammā akusalā? yasmiṃ samayai akusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ diṭṭhigatasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… saumanassasahagataṃ diṭṭhigatavippayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pai॰… saumanassasahagataṃ  diṭṭhigatavippayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā phauṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

281. tattha katamā avij‍jā? yaṃ añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pai॰… avij‍jālaṅgī mauhau akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jā’’|

tattha katamau avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau…pai॰…|

tattha  katamau taṇhāpac‍cayā adhimaukkhau? yau cittassa adhimaukkhau adhimuc‍canā tadadhimuttatā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘taṇhāpac‍cayā adhimaukkhau’’|

tattha katamau adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau? ṭhapaitvā adhimaukkhaṃ, vaidanākkhandhau sañ‍ñākkhandhau saṅkhārakkhandhau viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘adhimaukkhapac‍cayā  bhavau’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

282. katamai  dhammā akusalā? yasmiṃ samayai akusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ diṭṭhigatasampayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā phauṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

283. tattha  katamā avij‍jā? yaṃ añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pai॰… avij‍jālaṅgī mauhau akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati avij‍jā…pai॰…|

tattha katamā phassapac‍cayā vaidanā? yaṃ caitasikaṃ naiva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘phassapac‍cayā vaidanā’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

284. katamai dhammā akusalā? yasmiṃ samayai akusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ diṭṭhigatasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ diṭṭhigatavippayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ diṭṭhigatavippayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā phauṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

285. tattha katamā avij‍jā…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

286. katamai  dhammā akusalā? yasmiṃ samayai akusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti daumanassasahagataṃ paṭighasampayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pai॰… daumanassasahagataṃ paṭighasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā phauṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā paṭighaṃ, paṭighapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

287. tattha katamā avij‍jā? yaṃ añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pai॰… avij‍jālaṅgī mauhau akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jā’’…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau’’|

tattha katamā phassapac‍cayā vaidanā? yaṃ  caitasikaṃ asātaṃ caitasikaṃ dukkhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ asātaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā asātā dukkhā vaidanā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘phassapac‍cayā vaidanā’’|

tattha katamaṃ vaidanāpac‍cayā paṭighaṃ? yau cittassa āghātau…pai॰… caṇḍik‍kaṃ asuraupau asulaupau (syā॰) anattamanatā cittassa – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vaidanāpac‍cayā paṭighaṃ’’|

tattha katamau paṭighapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau? yau cittassa adhimaukkhau adhimuc‍canā tadadhimuttatā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘paṭighapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau’’|

tattha  katamau adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau? ṭhapaitvā adhimaukkhaṃ, vaidanākkhandhau sañ‍ñākkhandhau saṅkhārakkhandhau viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

288. katamai dhammā akusalā? yasmiṃ samayai akusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ vicikicchāsampayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā phauṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā vicikicchā, vicikicchāpac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

289. tattha katamā avij‍jā? yaṃ añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pai॰… avij‍jālaṅgī mauhau akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jā’’…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau’’|

tattha katamā phassapac‍cayā vaidanā? yaṃ caitasikaṃ naiva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ, caitausamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘phassapac‍cayā vaidanā’’|

tattha  katamā vaidanāpac‍cayā vicikicchā? yā kaṅkhā kaṅkhāyanā kaṅkhāyitattaṃ vimati vicikicchā  dvaiḷhakaṃ dvidhāpathau dvaidhāpathau (sī॰ syā॰) saṃsayau anaikaṃsaggāhau āsappanā parisappanā apariyaugāhaṇā apariyaugāhanā (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰) chambhitattaṃ cittassa manauvilaikhau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vaidanāpac‍cayā vicikicchā’’|

tattha katamau vicikicchāpac‍cayā bhavau? ṭhapaitvā vicikicchaṃ, vaidanākkhandhau sañ‍ñākkhandhau saṅkhārakkhandhau viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vicikicchāpac‍cayā bhavau’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

290. katamai  dhammā akusalā? yasmiṃ samayai akusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ uddhac‍casampayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā phauṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā uddhac‍caṃ, uddhac‍capac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

291. tattha  katamā avij‍jā? yaṃ añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ…pai॰… avij‍jālaṅgī mauhau akusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jā’’…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau’’|

tattha katamā phassapac‍cayā vaidanā? yaṃ caitasikaṃ naiva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘phassapac‍cayā vaidanā’’|

tattha katamaṃ vaidanāpac‍cayā uddhac‍caṃ? yaṃ cittassa uddhac‍caṃ avūpasamau caitasau vikkhaipau bhantattaṃ cittassa  – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vaidanāpac‍cayā uddhac‍caṃ’’|

tattha katamau uddhac‍capac‍cayā adhimaukkhau? yau  cittassa adhimaukkhau adhimuc‍canā tadadhimuttatā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘uddhac‍capac‍cayā adhimaukkhau’’|

tattha katamau adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau? ṭhapaitvā adhimaukkhaṃ, vaidanākkhandhau sañ‍ñākkhandhau saṅkhārakkhandhau viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

akusalaniddaisau|

10. kusalaniddaisau

292. katamai  dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā phauṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai kusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau, pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

293. tattha katamai kusalamūlā? alaubhau, adausau, amauhau|

tattha  katamau alaubhau? yau alaubhau alubbhanā alubbhitattaṃ asārāgau asāraj‍janā asāraj‍jitattaṃ anabhijjhā alaubhau kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘alaubhau’’|

tattha katamau adausau? yau adausau adussanā adussitattaṃ abyāpādau  abyāpaj‍jau adausau kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘adausau’’|

tattha katamau amauhau? yā pañ‍ñā pajānanā…pai॰… amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘amauhau’’| imai vuc‍canti ‘‘kusalamūlā’’|

tattha  katamau kusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘kusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau’’|

tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ…pai॰… nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ…pai॰… chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau…pai॰… phassapac‍cayā vaidanā…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘phassapac‍cayā vaidanā’’|

tattha  katamau vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau? yā saddhā saddahanā aukappanā abhippasādau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau’’|

tattha katamau pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau? yau cittassa adhimaukkhau adhimuc‍canā tadadhimuttatā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau’’|

tattha katamau adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau? ṭhapaitvā adhimaukkhaṃ, vaidanākkhandhau sañ‍ñākkhandhau saṅkhārakkhandhau viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

294. katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pai॰… saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pai॰… saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā phauṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai kusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā  viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau, pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

295. tattha  katamai kusalamūlā? alaubhau, adausau|

tattha katamau alaubhau? yau alaubhau alubbhanā alubbhitattaṃ asārāgau asāraj‍janā asāraj‍jitattaṃ anabhijjhā alaubhau kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘alaubhau’’|

tattha katamau adausau? yau adausau adussanā adussitattaṃ abyāpādau abyāpaj‍jau adausau kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘adausau’’| imai vuc‍canti ‘‘kusalamūlā’’|

tattha  katamau kusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘kusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau’’…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau’’|

tattha katamā phassapac‍cayā vaidanā? yaṃ caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘phassapac‍cayā vaidanā’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

296. katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā phauṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai kusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau, pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā  bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

297. tattha  katamai kusalamūlā? alaubhau, adausau, amauhau – imai vuc‍canti ‘‘kusalamūlā’’|

tattha katamau kusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘kusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau’’…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati – ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau’’|

tattha katamā phassapac‍cayā vaidanā? yaṃ caitasikaṃ naiva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘phassapac‍cayā vaidanā’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ  ñāṇavippayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā phauṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai kusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau, pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

299. tattha katamai kusalamūlā? alaubhau, adausau – imai vuc‍canti  ‘‘kusalamūlā’’|

tattha katamau kusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘kusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

300. katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ paṭhavīkasiṇaṃ (sī॰ syā॰), tasmiṃ samayai kusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā  viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau, pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

301. tattha katamai kusalamūlā? alaubhau, adausau, amauhau – imai vuc‍canti ‘‘kusalamūlā’’|

tattha katamau kusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘kusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|
