302. katamai  dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai arūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja  viharati, tasmiṃ samayai kusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau, pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

303. tattha katamai kusalamūlā? alaubhau, adausau, amauhau – imai vuc‍canti ‘‘kusalamūlā’’|

tattha katamau kusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘kusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau’’…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau’’|

tattha katamā phassapac‍cayā vaidanā? yaṃ caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘phassapac‍cayā vaidanā’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

304. katamai  dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ  upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai kusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau, pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā  jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitaisaṃ dhammānaṃ samudayau hauti|

305. tattha katamai kusalamūlā? alaubhau, adausau, amauhau|

tattha  katamau alaubhau…pai॰… adausau…pai॰… amauhau? yā pañ‍ñā pajānanā…pai॰… amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘amauhau’’| imai vuc‍canti ‘‘kusalamūlā’’|

tattha katamau kusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘kusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau’’…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau’’|

tattha katamā phassapac‍cayā vaidanā? yaṃ caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘phassapac‍cayā vaidanā’’|

tattha katamau vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau? yā saddhā saddahanā aukappanā abhippasādau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau’’|

tattha katamau pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau? yau cittassa adhimaukkhau adhimuc‍canā tadadhimuttatā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau’’|

tattha katamau adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau? ṭhapaitvā adhimaukkhaṃ, vaidanākkhandhau sañ‍ñākkhandhau saṅkhārakkhandhau viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau’’…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ’’|

aivamaitaisaṃ  dhammānaṃ samudayau hautīti| aivamaitaisaṃ dhammānaṃ saṅgati hauti, samāgamau hauti, samaudhānaṃ hauti, pātubhāvau hauti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitaisaṃ dhammānaṃ samudayau hautī’’ti|

kusalaniddaisau|

11. abyākataniddaisau

306. katamai  dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

307. tattha katamau saṅkhārau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārau’’|

tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jācakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jācakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ’’|

tattha katamau chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau? yau phassau phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau’’|

tattha katamā phassapac‍cayā vaidanā? yaṃ caitasikaṃ naiva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘phassapac‍cayā vaidanā’’|

tattha  katamau vaidanāpac‍cayā bhavau? ṭhapaitvā vaidanaṃ, sañ‍ñākkhandhau  saṅkhārakkhandhau viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau  – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vaidanāpac‍cayā bhavau’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

308. tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārahaitukaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ viñ‍ñāṇahaitukaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmahaitukaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau chaṭṭhāyatanahaitukau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā phassahaitukā, vaidanāpac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

309. tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārasampayuttaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasampayuttaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ nāmasampayuttaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau chaṭṭhāyatanasampayuttau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā phassasampayuttā, vaidanāpac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

310. tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayāpi saṅkhārau; viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayāpi  viñ‍ñāṇaṃ; nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayāpi nāmaṃ; chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayāpi chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ; phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayāpi phassau; vaidanāpac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā  jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

311. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ saddārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ gandhārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ rasārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti sukhasahagataṃ phauṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

312. tattha  katamau saṅkhārau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārau’’…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau’’|

tattha katamā phassapac‍cayā vaidanā? yaṃ kāyikaṃ sātaṃ kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ kāyasamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ kāyasamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘phassapac‍cayā vaidanā’’|

tattha katamau vaidanāpac‍cayā bhavau? ṭhapaitvā vaidanaṃ, sañ‍ñākkhandhau saṅkhārakkhandhau viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vaidanāpac‍cayā bhavau’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

313. katamai  dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākā manaudhātu uppan‍nā hauti upaikkhāsahagatā rūpārammaṇā vā saddārammaṇā vā gandhārammaṇā vā rasārammaṇā vā phauṭṭhabbārammaṇā vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārapac‍cayā  viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

314. tattha katamau saṅkhārau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārau’’|

tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanaudhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ’’…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau’’|

tattha katamā phassapac‍cayā vaidanā? yaṃ caitasikaṃ naiva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ  vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘phassapac‍cayā vaidanā’’|

tattha katamau vaidanāpac‍cayā adhimaukkhau? yau cittassa adhimaukkhau adhimuc‍canā tadadhimuttatā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vaidanāpac‍cayā adhimaukkhau’’|

tattha  katamau adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau? ṭhapaitvā adhimaukkhaṃ, vaidanākkhandhau sañ‍ñākkhandhau saṅkhārakkhandhau viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

315. katamai  dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu uppan‍nā hauti saumanassasahagatā rūpārammaṇā vā saddārammaṇā vā gandhārammaṇā vā rasārammaṇā vā phauṭṭhabbārammaṇā vā dhammārammaṇā vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

316. tattha katamau saṅkhārau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārau’’|

tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ’’…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau’’|

tattha katamā phassapac‍cayā vaidanā? yaṃ caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘phassapac‍cayā vaidanā’’|

tattha  katamau vaidanāpac‍cayā adhimaukkhau? yau cittassa adhimaukkhau adhimuc‍canā tadadhimuttatā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vaidanāpac‍cayā adhimaukkhau’’|

tattha katamau adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau? ṭhapaitvā adhimaukkhaṃ, vaidanākkhandhau sañ‍ñākkhandhau saṅkhārakkhandhau viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

317. katamai  dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu uppan‍nā hauti upaikkhāsahagatā rūpārammaṇā vā saddārammaṇā vā gandhārammaṇā vā rasārammaṇā vā phauṭṭhabbārammaṇā vā dhammārammaṇā vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

318. tattha katamau saṅkhārau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārau’’|

tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ’’…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau’’|

tattha katamā phassapac‍cayā vaidanā? yaṃ caitasikaṃ naiva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘phassapac‍cayā vaidanā’’|

tattha katamau vaidanāpac‍cayā adhimaukkhau? yau cittassa adhimaukkhau adhimuc‍canā tadadhimuttatā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vaidanāpac‍cayā adhimaukkhau’’|

tattha  katamau adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau? ṭhapaitvā adhimaukkhaṃ, vaidanākkhandhau sañ‍ñākkhandhau saṅkhārakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

319. katamai  dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu uppan‍nā hauti saumanassasahagatā ñāṇasampayuttā…pai॰… saumanassasahagatā ñāṇasampayuttā sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… saumanassasahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā…pai॰… saumanassasahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagatā  ñāṇasampayuttā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagatā ñāṇasampayuttā sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā sasaṅkhāraina rūpārammaṇā vā…pai॰… dhammārammaṇā vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau, pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

320. tattha katamau saṅkhārau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārau’’|

tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ’’…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘phassapac‍cayā vaidanā’’|

tattha katamau vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau? yā  saddhā saddahanā aukappanā abhippasādau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau’’|

tattha katamau pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau? yau cittassa adhimaukkhau adhimuc‍canā tadadhimuttatā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau’’|

tattha  katamau adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau? ṭhapaitvā adhimaukkhaṃ, vaidanākkhandhau sañ‍ñākkhandhau saṅkhārakkhandhau viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

321. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti – imai dhammā kusalā|

tassaiva rūpāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau, pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

322. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai arūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja  viharati, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti – imai dhammā kusalā|

tassaiva arūpāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau, pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

323. katamai  dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti – imai dhammā kusalā|

tassaiva laukuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa katattā bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau, pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā  jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitaisaṃ dhammānaṃ samudayau hauti|

324. katamai  dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai akusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ saddārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ gandhārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ rasārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti dukkhasahagataṃ phauṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

325. tattha katamau saṅkhārau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārau’’|

330. katamai  dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai manaudhātu uppan‍nā hauti kiriyā naiva kusalā nākusalā na ca kammavipākā upaikkhāsahagatā rūpārammaṇā vā…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbārammaṇā vā…pai॰… manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu uppan‍nā hauti kiriyā  naiva kusalā nākusalā na ca kammavipākā saumanassasahagatā rūpārammaṇā vā…pai॰… dhammārammaṇā vā…pai॰… manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu uppan‍nā hauti kiriyā naiva kusalā nākusalā na ca kammavipākā upaikkhāsahagatā rūpārammaṇā vā…pai॰… dhammārammaṇā vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

331. katamai  dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu uppan‍nā hauti kiriyā naiva kusalā nākusalā na ca kammavipākā saumanassasahagatā ñāṇasampayuttā…pai॰… saumanassasahagatā ñāṇasampayuttā sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… saumanassasahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā…pai॰… saumanassasahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagatā ñāṇasampayuttā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagatā ñāṇasampayuttā sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā sasaṅkhāraina rūpārammaṇā vā…pai॰… dhammārammaṇā vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā  chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau, pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā  jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

333. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai arūpāvacaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti kiriyaṃ naiva kusalaṃ nākusalaṃ na ca kammavipākaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāraṃ sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, tasmiṃ samayai saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau, pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā  jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

abyākataniddaisau|

12. avij‍jāmūlakakusalaniddaisau

334. katamai  dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pai॰… dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau, pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

335. tattha katamau avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau’’…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau’’|

tattha katamā phassapac‍cayā vaidanā? yaṃ caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘phassapac‍cayā vaidanā’’|

tattha  katamau vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau? yā saddhā saddahanā aukappanā abhippasādau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau’’|

tattha katamau pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau? yau cittassa adhimaukkhau adhimuc‍canā tadadhimuttatā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau’’|

tattha katamau adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau? ṭhapaitvā adhimaukkhaṃ, vaidanākkhandhau sañ‍ñākkhandhau  saṅkhārakkhandhau viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

336. tasmiṃ samayai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā  nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau, pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

337. tasmiṃ  samayai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau, pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

338. tasmiṃ samayai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau, pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

339. katamai  dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ…pai॰… saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ  sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pai॰… dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau, pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

340. katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vivic‍caiva kāmaihi  …pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai avij‍jāpac‍cayā  saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau, pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

341. katamai  dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai arūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, tasmiṃ samayai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau, pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

342. katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau, pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitaisaṃ dhammānaṃ samudayau hauti|

avij‍jāmūlakakusalaniddaisau|

13. kusalamūlakavipākaniddaisau

343. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacarassa kusalassa  kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai  kusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

344. tattha katamau kusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘kusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

345. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ saddārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ gandhārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ rasārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti sukhasahagataṃ phauṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… manaudhātu uppan‍nā hauti upaikkhāsahagatā rūpārammaṇā vā…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbārammaṇā vā…pai॰… manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu uppan‍nā hauti saumanassasahagatā rūpārammaṇā vā…pai॰… dhammārammaṇā vā…pai॰… manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu uppan‍nā hauti upaikkhāsahagatā  rūpārammaṇā vā…pai॰… dhammārammaṇā vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai kusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

346. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu uppan‍nā hauti saumanassasahagatā ñāṇasampayuttā…pai॰… saumanassasahagatā ñāṇasampayuttā sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… saumanassasahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā…pai॰… saumanassasahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagatā ñāṇasampayuttā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagatā ñāṇasampayuttā sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā sasaṅkhāraina rūpārammaṇā vā…pai॰… dhammārammaṇā  vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai kusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau, pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

347. katamai  dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti – imai dhammā kusalā|

tassaiva rūpāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai kusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau, pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

348. katamai  dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai arūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti – imai dhammā kusalā|

tassaiva arūpāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, tasmiṃ samayai kusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau, pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

349. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti – imai dhammā kusalā|

tassaiva laukuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa katattā bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ, tasmiṃ samayai kusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā pasādau, pasādapac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitaisaṃ dhammānaṃ samudayau hauti|

kusalamūlakavipākaniddaisau|

14. akusalamūlakavipākaniddaisau

350. katamai  dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai akusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai akusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā  viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

351. tattha katamau akusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ  – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘akusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau’’…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

352. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai akusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ saddārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ gandhārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ rasārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti dukkhasahagataṃ phauṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… manaudhātu uppan‍nā hauti upaikkhāsahagatā rūpārammaṇā vā…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbārammaṇā vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai akusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau  hauti|

353. katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai akusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu uppan‍nā hauti upaikkhāsahagatā rūpārammaṇā vā…pai॰… dhammārammaṇā vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai akusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ, nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā adhimaukkhau, adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

354. tattha katamau akusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau? yā caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘akusalamūlapac‍cayā saṅkhārau’’|

tattha katamaṃ saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ’’|

tattha  katamaṃ viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘nāmapac‍cayā chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ’’|

tattha  katamau chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau? yau phassau phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chaṭṭhāyatanapac‍cayā phassau’’|

tattha katamā phassapac‍cayā vaidanā? yaṃ caitasikaṃ naiva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘phassapac‍cayā vaidanā’’|

tattha katamau vaidanāpac‍cayā adhimaukkhau? yau cittassa adhimaukkhau adhimuc‍canā tadadhimuttatā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vaidanāpac‍cayā adhimaukkhau’’|

tattha katamau adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau? ṭhapaitvā adhimaukkhaṃ, vaidanākkhandhau sañ‍ñākkhandhau saṅkhārakkhandhau viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘adhimaukkhapac‍cayā bhavau’’|

tattha katamā bhavapac‍cayā jāti? yā taisaṃ taisaṃ dhammānaṃ jāti sañ‍jāti nibbatti abhinibbatti pātubhāvau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘bhavapac‍cayā jāti’’|

tattha katamaṃ jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ? atthi jarā, atthi maraṇaṃ| tattha katamā jarā? yā taisaṃ taisaṃ dhammānaṃ jarā jīraṇatā āyunau saṃhāni  – ayaṃ vuc‍cati jarā| tattha katamaṃ maraṇaṃ? yau taisaṃ taisaṃ dhammānaṃ khayau vayau bhaidau paribhaidau anic‍catā antaradhānaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘maraṇaṃ’’| iti ayañ‍ca jarā, idañ‍ca maraṇaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ’’|

aivamaitassa  kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautīti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa saṅgati hauti, samāgamau hauti, samaudhānaṃ  hauti, pātubhāvau hauti| taina  vuc‍cati aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

akusalamūlakavipākaniddaisau|

abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ|

paṭic‍casamuppādavibhaṅgau pac‍cayākāravibhaṅgau (sī॰ syā॰) niṭṭhitau|

7. satipaṭṭhānavibhaṅgau

1. suttantabhājanīyaṃ

355. cattārau  satipaṭṭhānā – idha bhikkhu ajjhattaṃ kāyai kāyānupassī viharati bahiddhā kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ajjhattabahiddhā kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ, ajjhattaṃ vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati bahiddhā vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati ajjhattabahiddhā vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ, ajjhattaṃ cittai cittānupassī viharati bahiddhā cittai cittānupassī viharati ajjhattabahiddhā cittai cittānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ, ajjhattaṃ dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati bahiddhā dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ajjhattabahiddhā dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ|

1. kāyānupassanāniddaisau

356. kathañ‍ca  bhikkhu ajjhattaṃ kāyai kāyānupassī viharati? idha bhikkhu ajjhattaṃ kāyaṃ uddhaṃ pādatalā adhau kaisamatthakā tacapariyantaṃ pūraṃ nānappakārassa asucinau pac‍cavaikkhati – ‘‘atthi imasmiṃ kāyai kaisā laumā nakhā dantā tacau maṃsaṃ nhāru nahāru (sī॰) aṭṭhi aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ aṭṭhimiñ‍jā (sī॰) vak‍kaṃ hadayaṃ yakanaṃ kilaumakaṃ pihakaṃ papphāsaṃ antaṃ antaguṇaṃ udariyaṃ karīsaṃ pittaṃ saimhaṃ pubbau lauhitaṃ saidau maidau assu vasā khaiḷau siṅghāṇikā lasikā mutta’’nti| sau taṃ nimittaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti svāvatthitaṃ vavatthapaiti| sau  taṃ nimittaṃ āsaivitvā bhāvaitvā bahulīkaritvā svāvatthitaṃ vavatthapaitvā bahiddhā kāyai cittaṃ upasaṃharati|

kathañ‍ca  bhikkhu bahiddhā kāyai kāyānupassī viharati? idha bhikkhu bahiddhā kāyaṃ uddhaṃ pādatalā adhau kaisamatthakā tacapariyantaṃ pūraṃ nānappakārassa asucinau pac‍cavaikkhati – ‘‘atthissa kāyai kaisā laumā  nakhā dantā tacau maṃsaṃ nhāru aṭṭhi aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ vak‍kaṃ hadayaṃ yakanaṃ kilaumakaṃ pihakaṃ papphāsaṃ antaṃ antaguṇaṃ udariyaṃ karīsaṃ pittaṃ saimhaṃ pubbau lauhitaṃ saidau maidau assu vasā khaiḷau siṅghāṇikā lasikā mutta’’nti| sau taṃ nimittaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti svāvatthitaṃ vavatthapaiti| sau taṃ nimittaṃ āsaivitvā bhāvaitvā bahulīkaritvā svāvatthitaṃ vavatthapaitvā ajjhattabahiddhā kāyai cittaṃ upasaṃharati|

kathañ‍ca  bhikkhu ajjhattabahiddhā kāyai kāyānupassī viharati? idha bhikkhu ajjhattabahiddhā kāyaṃ uddhaṃ pādatalā adhau kaisamatthakā tacapariyantaṃ pūraṃ nānappakārassa asucinau pac‍cavaikkhati – ‘‘atthi imasmiṃ kāyai kaisā laumā nakhā dantā tacau maṃsaṃ nhāru aṭṭhi aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ vak‍kaṃ hadayaṃ yakanaṃ kilaumakaṃ pihakaṃ papphāsaṃ antaṃ antaguṇaṃ udariyaṃ karīsaṃ pittaṃ saimhaṃ pubbau lauhitaṃ saidau maidau assu vasā khaiḷau siṅghāṇikā lasikā mutta’’nti| aivaṃ bhikkhu ajjhattabahiddhā kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ|

357. anupassīti| tattha katamā anupassanā? yā pañ‍ñā pajānanā…pai॰… amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘anupassanā’’| imāya anupassanāya upaitau hauti samupaitau upāgatau samupāgatau upapan‍nau sampan‍nau saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘anupassī’’ti|

358. viharatīti| iriyati vattati pālaiti yapaiti yāpaiti carati viharati| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘viharatī’’ti|

359. ātāpīti| tattha katamau ātāpau katamaṃ ātāpaṃ (sabbattha)? yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau…pai॰… sammāvāyāmau  – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘ātāpau’’| iminā ātāpaina upaitau hauti samupaitau upāgatau samupāgatau upapan‍nau sampan‍nau saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘ātāpī’’ti|

360. sampajānauti| tattha katamaṃ sampajañ‍ñaṃ? yā pañ‍ñā pajānanā…pai॰… amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sampajañ‍ñaṃ’’| iminā sampajañ‍ñaina upaitau hauti samupaitau upāgatau samupāgatau upapan‍nau  sampan‍nau saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘sampajānau’’ti|

361. satimāti| tattha katamā sati? yā sati anussati…pai॰… sammāsati – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sati’’| imāya satiyā upaitau hauti samupaitau upāgatau samupāgatau upapan‍nau sampan‍nau saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘satimā’’ti|

362. vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassanti| tattha katamau laukau? svaiva kāyau laukau| pañ‍capi upādānakkhandhā laukau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘laukau’’| tattha katamā abhijjhā? yau rāgau sārāgau…pai॰… cittassa sārāgau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘abhijjhā’’| tattha katamaṃ daumanassaṃ? yaṃ caitasikaṃ asātaṃ caitasikaṃ dukkhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ asātaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā asātā dukkhā vaidanā – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘daumanassaṃ’’| iti ayañ‍ca abhijjhā idañ‍ca daumanassaṃ imamhi laukai vinītā haunti paṭivinītā santā samitā vūpasantā atthaṅgatā abbhatthaṅgatā appitā byappitā sausitā visausitā byantīkatā| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassa’’nti|

kāyānupassanāniddaisau|

2. vaidanānupassanāniddaisau

363. kathañ‍ca  bhikkhu ajjhattaṃ vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati? idha bhikkhu sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ  vaidayamānau ‘‘sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’’ti pajānāti, dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘‘dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’’ti pajānāti, adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘‘adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’’ti pajānāti, sāmisaṃ vā sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘‘sāmisaṃ sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’’ti pajānāti, nirāmisaṃ vā sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘‘nirāmisaṃ sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’’ti pajānāti, sāmisaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘‘sāmisaṃ dukkhaṃ  vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’’ti pajānāti, nirāmisaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘‘nirāmisaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’’ti pajānāti, sāmisaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘‘sāmisaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’’ti pajānāti, nirāmisaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘‘nirāmisaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’’ti  pajānāti| sau taṃ nimittaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti svāvatthitaṃ vavatthapaiti| sau taṃ nimittaṃ āsaivitvā bhāvaitvā bahulīkaritvā svāvatthitaṃ vavatthapaitvā bahiddhā vaidanāsu cittaṃ upasaṃharati|

kathañ‍ca bhikkhu bahiddhā vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati? idha bhikkhu sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānaṃ ‘‘sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayatī’’ti pajānāti, dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānaṃ ‘‘dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayatī’’ti pajānāti, adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānaṃ ‘‘adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayatī’’ti pajānāti, sāmisaṃ  vā sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānaṃ ‘‘sāmisaṃ sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayatī’’ti pajānāti, nirāmisaṃ vā sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānaṃ ‘‘nirāmisaṃ sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayatī’’ti pajānāti, sāmisaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānaṃ ‘‘sāmisaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayatī’’ti pajānāti, nirāmisaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānaṃ ‘‘nirāmisaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayatī’’ti pajānāti, sāmisaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānaṃ ‘‘sāmisaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayatī’’ti pajānāti, nirāmisaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānaṃ ‘‘nirāmisaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayatī’’ti pajānāti| sau taṃ nimittaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti svāvatthitaṃ vavatthapaiti| sau taṃ nimittaṃ āsaivitvā bhāvaitvā bahulīkaritvā svāvatthitaṃ vavatthapaitvā ajjhattabahiddhā vaidanāsu cittaṃ upasaṃharati|

kathañ‍ca bhikkhu ajjhattabahiddhā vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati? idha bhikkhu sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ ‘‘sukhā vaidanā’’ti pajānāti, dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ ‘‘dukkhā vaidanā’’ti pajānāti, adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ ‘‘adukkhamasukhā vaidanā’’ti pajānāti, sāmisaṃ vā sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ ‘‘sāmisā  sukhā vaidanā’’ti pajānāti, nirāmisaṃ vā sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ ‘‘nirāmisā sukhā vaidanā’’ti pajānāti, sāmisaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ ‘‘sāmisā dukkhā vaidanā’’ti pajānāti, nirāmisaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ ‘‘nirāmisā dukkhā vaidanā’’ti pajānāti, sāmisaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ ‘‘sāmisā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā’’ti pajānāti, nirāmisaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ  ‘‘nirāmisā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā’’ti pajānāti| aivaṃ bhikkhu ajjhattabahiddhā vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ|

364. anupassīti  …pai॰… viharatīti…pai॰… ātāpīti…pai॰… sampajānauti…pai॰…  satimāti…pai॰… vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassanti| tattha katamau laukau? sāyaiva vaidanā laukau| pañ‍capi upādānakkhandhā laukau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘laukau’’| tattha katamā abhijjhā? yau rāgau sārāgau…pai॰… cittassa sārāgau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘abhijjhā’’| tattha katamaṃ daumanassaṃ? yaṃ caitasikaṃ asātaṃ caitasikaṃ dukkhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ asātaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā asātā dukkhā vaidanā – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘daumanassaṃ’’| iti ayañ‍ca abhijjhā idañ‍ca daumanassaṃ imamhi laukai vinītā haunti paṭivinītā santā samitā vūpasantā atthaṅgatā abbhatthaṅgatā appitā byappitā sausitā visausitā byantīkatā| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassa’’nti|

vaidanānupassanāniddaisau|

3. cittānupassanāniddaisau

365. kathañ‍ca bhikkhu ajjhattaṃ cittai cittānupassī viharati? idha bhikkhu sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘sarāgaṃ mai citta’’nti pajānāti, vītarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘vītarāgaṃ mai citta’’nti pajānāti, sadausaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘sadausaṃ mai citta’’nti pajānāti, vītadausaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘vītadausaṃ mai citta’’nti pajānāti, samauhaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘samauhaṃ mai citta’’nti pajānāti, vītamauhaṃ  vā cittaṃ ‘‘vītamauhaṃ mai citta’’nti pajānāti, saṃkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘saṃkhittaṃ mai citta’’nti pajānāti, vikkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘vikkhittaṃ mai citta’’nti  pajānāti, mahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘mahaggataṃ mai citta’’nti pajānāti, amahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘amahaggataṃ mai citta’’nti pajānāti, sauttaraṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘sauttaraṃ mai citta’’nti pajānāti, anuttaraṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘anuttaraṃ mai citta’’nti pajānāti, samāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘samāhitaṃ mai citta’’nti pajānāti, asamāhitaṃ  vā cittaṃ ‘‘asamāhitaṃ mai citta’’nti pajānāti, vimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘vimuttaṃ mai citta’’nti pajānāti, avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘avimuttaṃ mai citta’’nti pajānāti| sau taṃ nimittaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti svāvatthitaṃ vavatthapaiti| sau taṃ nimittaṃ āsaivitvā bhāvaitvā bahulīkaritvā svāvatthitaṃ vavatthapaitvā bahiddhā cittai cittaṃ upasaṃharati|

kathañ‍ca bhikkhu bahiddhā cittai cittānupassī viharati? idha bhikkhu sarāgaṃ vāssa cittaṃ ‘‘sarāgamassa citta’’nti pajānāti, vītarāgaṃ vāssa cittaṃ ‘‘vītarāgamassa citta’’nti pajānāti, sadausaṃ vāssa cittaṃ ‘‘sadausamassa citta’’nti  pajānāti, vītadausaṃ vāssa cittaṃ ‘‘vītadausamassa citta’’nti pajānāti, samauhaṃ vāssa cittaṃ ‘‘samauhamassa citta’’nti pajānāti, vītamauhaṃ vāssa cittaṃ ‘‘vītamauhamassa citta’’nti pajānāti, saṃkhittaṃ vāssa cittaṃ ‘‘saṃkhittamassa citta’’nti pajānāti, vikkhittaṃ vāssa cittaṃ ‘‘vikkhittamassa citta’’nti pajānāti, mahaggataṃ vāssa cittaṃ ‘‘mahaggatamassa citta’’nti pajānāti, amahaggataṃ vāssa cittaṃ ‘‘amahaggatamassa citta’’nti pajānāti, sauttaraṃ vāssa cittaṃ ‘‘sauttaramassa citta’’nti pajānāti, anuttaraṃ vāssa  cittaṃ ‘‘anuttaramassa citta’’nti pajānāti, samāhitaṃ vāssa cittaṃ ‘‘samāhitamassa citta’’nti pajānāti, asamāhitaṃ vāssa cittaṃ ‘‘asamāhitamassa citta’’nti pajānāti, vimuttaṃ vāssa cittaṃ ‘‘vimuttamassa citta’’nti pajānāti, avimuttaṃ vāssa cittaṃ ‘‘avimuttamassa citta’’nti pajānāti| sau taṃ nimittaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti svāvatthitaṃ vavatthapaiti| sau taṃ nimittaṃ āsaivitvā bhāvaitvā bahulīkaritvā svāvatthitaṃ vavatthapaitvā ajjhattabahiddhā cittai cittaṃ upasaṃharati|

kathañ‍ca bhikkhu ajjhattabahiddhā cittai cittānupassī viharati? idha bhikkhu sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘sarāgaṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, vītarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘vītarāgaṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, sadausaṃ  vā cittaṃ ‘‘sadausaṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, vītadausaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘vītadausaṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, samauhaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘samauhaṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, vītamauhaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘vītamauhaṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, saṃkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘saṃkhittaṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, vikkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘vikkhittaṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, mahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘mahaggataṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, amahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘amahaggataṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, sauttaraṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘sauttaraṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, anuttaraṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘anuttaraṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, samāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘samāhitaṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, asamāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘asamāhitaṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, vimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘vimuttaṃ citta’’nti pajānāti, avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘‘avimuttaṃ citta’’nti pajānāti| aivaṃ bhikkhu  ajjhattabahiddhā cittai cittānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ|

366. anupassīti…pai॰… viharatīti…pai॰… ātāpīti…pai॰… sampajānauti…pai॰… satimāti…pai॰… vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassanti| tattha katamau laukau? taṃyaiva cittaṃ laukau| pañ‍capi upādānakkhandhā laukau – tattha katamā abhijjhā? yau  rāgau sārāgau…pai॰… cittassa sārāgau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘abhijjhā’’| tattha katamaṃ daumanassaṃ? yaṃ caitasikaṃ asātaṃ caitasikaṃ dukkhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ asātaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā asātā dukkhā vaidanā – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘daumanassaṃ’’| iti ayañ‍ca abhijjhā idañ‍ca daumanassaṃ imamhi laukai vinītā haunti paṭivinītā santā samitā vūpasantā atthaṅgatā abbhatthaṅgatā appitā byappitā sausitā visausitā byantīkatā| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassa’’nti|

cittānupassanāniddaisau|

4. dhammānupassanāniddaisau

367. kathañ‍ca bhikkhu ajjhattaṃ dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati? idha bhikkhu santaṃ vā  ajjhattaṃ kāmacchandaṃ ‘‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ kāmacchandau’’ti pajānāti, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ kāmacchandaṃ ‘‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ kāmacchandau’’ti pajānāti, yathā ca anuppan‍nassa kāmacchandassa uppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca uppan‍nassa  kāmacchandassa pahānaṃ hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca pahīnassa kāmacchandassa āyatiṃ anuppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti| santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ byāpādaṃ…pai॰… santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ thinamiddhaṃ thīnamiddhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) …pai॰… santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ…pai॰… santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ vicikicchaṃ ‘‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ vicikicchā’’ti pajānāti, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ vicikicchaṃ ‘‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ vicikicchā’’ti  pajānāti, yathā ca anuppan‍nāya vicikicchāya uppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca uppan‍nāya vicikicchāya pahānaṃ hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca pahīnāya vicikicchāya āyatiṃ anuppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti|

santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ ‘‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ satisambaujjhaṅgau’’ti pajānāti, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ ‘‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ satisambaujjhaṅgau’’ti pajānāti, yathā ca anuppan‍nassa satisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca uppan‍nassa satisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūrī hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgaṃ…pai॰… santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ vīriyasambaujjhaṅgaṃ viriyasambaujjhaṅgaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) …pai॰… santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ pītisambaujjhaṅgaṃ  …pai॰… santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ passaddhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ…pai॰… santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ samādhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ…pai॰… santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ ‘‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau’’ti pajānāti, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ  ‘‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau’’ti pajānāti, yathā ca anuppan‍nassa upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa uppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca uppan‍nassa upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūrī hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti| sau taṃ nimittaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti svāvatthitaṃ vavatthapaiti| sau taṃ nimittaṃ āsaivitvā bhāvaitvā bahulīkaritvā svāvatthitaṃ vavatthapaitvā bahiddhā dhammaisu cittaṃ upasaṃharati|

kathañ‍ca bhikkhu bahiddhā dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati? idha bhikkhu santaṃ vāssa kāmacchandaṃ ‘‘atthissa kāmacchandau’’ti pajānāti, asantaṃ vāssa kāmacchandaṃ ‘‘natthissa kāmacchandau’’ti pajānāti, yathā ca anuppan‍nassa kāmacchandassa uppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca uppan‍nassa kāmacchandassa pahānaṃ hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca pahīnassa kāmacchandassa āyatiṃ anuppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti| santaṃ vāssa byāpādaṃ…pai॰… santaṃ vāssa thinamiddhaṃ…pai॰… santaṃ vāssa uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ…pai॰… santaṃ vāssa vicikicchaṃ ‘‘atthissa vicikicchā’’ti pajānāti, asantaṃ vāssa vicikicchaṃ ‘‘natthissa vicikicchā’’ti pajānāti, yathā ca anuppan‍nāya vicikicchāya  uppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca uppan‍nāya vicikicchāya pahānaṃ hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca pahīnāya vicikicchāya āyatiṃ anuppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti|

santaṃ vāssa satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ ‘‘atthissa satisambaujjhaṅgau’’ti  pajānāti, asantaṃ vāssa satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ ‘‘natthissa satisambaujjhaṅgau’’ti pajānāti, yathā ca anuppan‍nassa satisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca uppan‍nassa satisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūrī hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti| santaṃ vāssa dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgaṃ…pai॰… santaṃ vāssa vīriyasambaujjhaṅgaṃ…pai॰… santaṃ vāssa pītisambaujjhaṅgaṃ…pai॰… santaṃ vāssa passaddhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ…pai॰… santaṃ vāssa samādhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ…pai॰… santaṃ vāssa upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ ‘‘atthissa upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau’’ti pajānāti, asantaṃ vāssa upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ  ‘‘natthissa upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau’’ti pajānāti, yathā ca anuppan‍nassa upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa uppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca uppan‍nassa upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūrī hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti| sau taṃ nimittaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti svāvatthitaṃ vavatthapaiti| sau taṃ nimittaṃ āsaivitvā bhāvaitvā bahulīkaritvā svāvatthitaṃ vavatthapaitvā ajjhattabahiddhā dhammaisu cittaṃ upasaṃharati|

kathañ‍ca bhikkhu ajjhattabahiddhā dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati? idha bhikkhu santaṃ vā kāmacchandaṃ ‘‘atthi kāmacchandau’’ti pajānāti, asantaṃ vā kāmacchandaṃ ‘‘natthi kāmacchandau’’ti pajānāti, yathā ca anuppan‍nassa kāmacchandassa uppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca uppan‍nassa kāmacchandassa pahānaṃ hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca pahīnassa kāmacchandassa āyatiṃ anuppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti| santaṃ vā byāpādaṃ…pai॰… santaṃ vā thinamiddhaṃ…pai॰… santaṃ vā uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ…pai॰… santaṃ vā vicikicchaṃ ‘‘atthi vicikicchā’’ti pajānāti, asantaṃ vā vicikicchaṃ ‘‘natthi vicikicchā’’ti  pajānāti, yathā ca anuppan‍nāya vicikicchāya uppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca uppan‍nāya vicikicchāya pahānaṃ hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca pahīnāya vicikicchāya āyatiṃ anuppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti|

santaṃ  vā satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ ‘‘atthi satisambaujjhaṅgau’’ti pajānāti, asantaṃ vā satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ ‘‘natthi satisambaujjhaṅgau’’ti pajānāti, yathā ca anuppan‍nassa satisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca uppan‍nassa satisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūrī hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti| santaṃ vā dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgaṃ…pai॰… santaṃ vā vīriyasambaujjhaṅgaṃ…pai॰… santaṃ vā pītisambaujjhaṅgaṃ…pai॰… santaṃ vā passaddhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ…pai॰… santaṃ vā samādhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ…pai॰… santaṃ vā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ ‘‘atthi upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau’’ti pajānāti, asantaṃ vā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ ‘‘natthi upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau’’ti pajānāti, yathā ca anuppan‍nassa upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa uppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca uppan‍nassa upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūrī hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti| aivaṃ bhikkhu ajjhattabahiddhā  dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ|

368. anupassīti| tattha katamā anupassanā? yā  pañ‍ñā pajānanā…pai॰… amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi – ayaṃ vuc‍cati anupassanā| imāya anupassanāya upaitau hauti samupaitau upāgatau samupāgatau upapan‍nau sampan‍nau saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘anupassī’’ti|

369. viharatīti| iriyati vattati pālaiti yapaiti yāpaiti carati viharati| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘viharatī’’ti|

370. ātāpīti| tattha katamau ātāpau? yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau…pai॰… sammāvāyāmau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘ātāpau’’| iminā ātāpaina upaitau hauti…pai॰… saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘ātāpī’’ti|

371. sampajānauti| tattha katamaṃ sampajañ‍ñaṃ? yā pañ‍ñā pajānanā…pai॰… amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sampajañ‍ñaṃ’’| iminā sampajañ‍ñaina upaitau hauti…pai॰… saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘sampajānau’’ti|

372. satimāti| tattha katamā sati? yā sati anussati…pai॰… sammāsati – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sati’’| imāya satiyā upaitau hauti…pai॰… saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘satimā’’ti|

373. vinaiyya  laukai abhijjhādaumanassanti| tattha katamau laukau? taiva dhammā laukau| pañ‍capi upādānakkhandhā laukau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘laukau’’| tattha katamā abhijjhā? yau rāgau sārāgau…pai॰… cittassa sārāgau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘abhijjhā’’| tattha katamaṃ daumanassaṃ? yaṃ caitasikaṃ asātaṃ caitasikaṃ dukkhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ asātaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā asātā dukkhā vaidanā – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘daumanassaṃ’’| iti ayañ‍ca abhijjhā idañ‍ca daumanassaṃ imamhi laukai vinītā haunti paṭivinītā santā samitā vūpasantā atthaṅgatā abbhatthaṅgatā appitā byappitā sausitā visausitā byantīkatā| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassa’’nti|

dhammānupassanāniddaisau|

suttantabhājanīyaṃ|

2. abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ

374. cattārau satipaṭṭhānā – idha bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati, vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati, cittai cittānupassī viharati, dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati|

375. kathañ‍ca  bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ  jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ kāyai kāyānupassī, yā tasmiṃ samayai sati anussati sammāsati  satisambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘satipaṭṭhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā satipaṭṭhānasampayuttā|

376. kathañ‍ca  bhikkhu vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī, yā tasmiṃ samayai sati anussati sammāsati satisambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati satipaṭṭhānaṃ| avasaisā dhammā satipaṭṭhānasampayuttā|

377. kathañ‍ca bhikkhu cittai cittānupassī viharati? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ cittai cittānupassī, yā tasmiṃ samayai sati anussati sammāsati satisambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘satipaṭṭhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā satipaṭṭhānasampayuttā|

378. kathañ‍ca bhikkhu dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ dhammaisu dhammānupassī, yā tasmiṃ samayai sati anussati sammāsati satisambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ  – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘satipaṭṭhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā satipaṭṭhānasampayuttā|

379. tattha katamaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ  apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ dhammaisu dhammānupassī, yā tasmiṃ samayai sati anussati sammāsati satisambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘satipaṭṭhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā satipaṭṭhānasampayuttā|

380. cattārau satipaṭṭhānā – idha bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati, vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati, cittai cittānupassī viharati, dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati|

381. kathañ‍ca  bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva laukuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa katattā bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ kāyai kāyānupassī, yā tasmiṃ samayai sati anussati sammāsati satisambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ  – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘satipaṭṭhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā satipaṭṭhānasampayuttā|

382. kathañ‍ca bhikkhu vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva laukuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa katattā bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī, yā tasmiṃ samayai sati anussati sammāsati satisambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘satipaṭṭhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā satipaṭṭhānasampayuttā|

383. kathañ‍ca bhikkhu cittai cittānupassī viharati? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā  vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva laukuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa katattā bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ cittai cittānupassī, yā tasmiṃ samayai sati anussati sammāsati satisambaujjhaṅgau  maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘satipaṭṭhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā satipaṭṭhānasampayuttā|

384. kathañ‍ca  bhikkhu dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva laukuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa katattā bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ dhammaisu dhammānupassī, yā tasmiṃ samayai sati anussati sammāsati satisambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘satipaṭṭhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā satipaṭṭhānasampayuttā|

385. tattha katamaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva laukuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa katattā bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai sati anussati sammāsati satisambaujjhaṅgau  maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘satipaṭṭhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā satipaṭṭhānasampayuttā|

abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ|

3. pañhāpucchakaṃ

386. cattārau  satipaṭṭhānā – idha bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ, vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ, cittai cittānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ, dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ|

387. catun‍naṃ  satipaṭṭhānānaṃ kati kusalā, kati akusalā, kati abyākatā…pai॰… kati saraṇā, kati araṇā?

1. tikaṃ

388. siyā kusalā, siyā abyākatā| siyā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā| siyā vipākā siyā vipākadhammadhammā| anupādin‍naanupādāniyā| asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikā| siyā savitak‍kasavicārā, siyā avitak‍kavicāramattā, siyā avitak‍kaavicārā| siyā pītisahagatā, siyā sukhasahagatā, siyā upaikkhāsahagatā| naiva dassanaina na bhāvanāya pahātabbā| naiva dassanaina na bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā| siyā apacayagāminau, siyā naivācayagāmināpacayagāminau| siyā saikkhā, siyā asaikkhā| appamāṇā| appamāṇārammaṇā| paṇītā| siyā sammattaniyatā, siyā aniyatā| na maggārammaṇā, siyā maggahaitukā, siyā maggādhipatinau, siyā na vattabbā maggahaitukātipi, maggādhipatinautipi| siyā uppan‍nā, siyā anuppan‍nā, siyā uppādinau| siyā atītā, siyā anāgatā, siyā pac‍cuppan‍nā| na vattabbā atītārammaṇātipi, anāgatārammaṇātipi, pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇātipi| siyā ajjhattā, siyā bahiddhā, siyā ajjhattabahiddhā| bahiddhārammaṇā| anidassanaappaṭighā|

2. dukaṃ

389. na  haitū| sahaitukā| haitusampayuttā| na vattabbā haitū caiva sahaitukā cāti, sahaitukā caiva na ca haitū| na vattabbā haitū caiva haitusampayuttā cāti, haitusampayuttā caiva na ca haitū| na haitū sahaitukā|

sappac‍cayā| saṅkhatā| anidassanā| appaṭighā| arūpā| laukuttarā| kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyā, kainaci na viñ‍ñaiyyā| nau āsavā| anāsavā| āsavavippayuttā| na vattabbā āsavā caiva sāsavācātipi, sāsavā caiva nau ca āsavātipi| na vattabbā āsavā caiva āsavasampayuttācātipi, āsavasampayuttā caiva nau ca āsavātipi| āsavavippayuttā| anāsavā| nau saṃyaujanā…pai॰… nau ganthā…pai॰… nau aughā…pai॰… nau  yaugā…pai॰… nau nīvaraṇā…pai॰… nau parāmāsā…pai॰… sārammaṇā| nau cittā| caitasikā| cittasampayuttā| cittasaṃsaṭṭhā| cittasamuṭṭhānā| cittasahabhunau| cittānuparivattinau| cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānā| cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhunau| cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattinau| bāhirā| nau upādā| anupādin‍nā| nau upādānā…pai॰… nau kilaisā…pai॰… na dassanaina pahātabbā, na bhāvanāya pahātabbā| na dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā| na bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā|

siyā savitak‍kā, siyā avitak‍kā| siyā savicārā, siyā avicārā| siyā sappītikā, siyā appītikā| siyā pītisahagatā, siyā na pītisahagatā| siyā sukhasahagatā, siyā na sukhasahagatā| siyā upaikkhāsahagatā, siyā na upaikkhāsahagatā| na kāmāvacarā| na rūpāvacarā| na arūpāvacarā| apariyāpan‍nā| siyā niyyānikā, siyā aniyyānikā| siyā niyatā, siyā aniyatā| anuttarā| araṇāti|

pañhāpucchakaṃ|

satipaṭṭhānavibhaṅgau niṭṭhitau|

8. sammappadhānavibhaṅgau

1. suttantabhājanīyaṃ

390. cattārau  sammappadhānā – idha bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ  dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati|

391. kathañ‍ca bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati? tattha katamai anuppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā? tīṇi akusalamūlāni – laubhau, dausau, mauhau| tadaikaṭṭhā ca kilaisā| taṃsampayuttau vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau, taṃsamuṭṭhānaṃ kāyakammaṃ vacīkammaṃ manaukammaṃ – imai vuc‍canti ‘‘anuppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā’’| iti imaisaṃ anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati|

392. chandaṃ janaitīti| tattha katamau chandau? yau chandau chandikatā chandīkatā (syā॰) kattukamyatā kusalau  dhammacchandau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chandau’’| imaṃ chandaṃ janaiti sañ‍janaiti uṭṭhapaiti samuṭṭhapaiti uṭṭhāpaiti samuṭṭhāpaiti (syā॰) aivamuparipi nibbattaiti abhinibbattaiti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘chandaṃ janaitī’’ti|

393. vāyamatīti| tattha katamau vāyāmau? yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau…pai॰… sammāvāyāmau – ayaṃ  vuc‍cati ‘‘vāyāmau’’| iminā vāyāmaina upaitau hauti samupaitau upāgatau samupāgatau upapan‍nau sampan‍nau saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘vāyamatī’’ti|

394. vīriyaṃ  ārabhatīti| tattha katamaṃ vīriyaṃ? yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau…pai॰… sammāvāyāmau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vīriyaṃ’’| imaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabhati samārabhati āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘vīriyaṃ ārabhatī’’ti|

395. cittaṃ paggaṇhātīti| tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘cittaṃ’’| imaṃ cittaṃ paggaṇhāti sampaggaṇhāti upatthambhaiti pac‍cupatthambhaiti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘cittaṃ paggaṇhātī’’ti|

396. padahatīti| tattha katamaṃ padhānaṃ? yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau…pai॰… sammāvāyāmau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘padhānaṃ’’| iminā padhānaina upaitau hauti…pai॰… saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘padahatī’’ti|

397. kathañ‍ca bhikkhu uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati? tattha katamai uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā? tīṇi akusalamūlāni – laubhau, dausau, mauhau| tadaikaṭṭhā ca kilaisā| taṃsampayuttau vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau, taṃsamuṭṭhānaṃ kāyakammaṃ vacīkammaṃ manaukammaṃ – imai vuc‍canti ‘‘uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā’’| iti imaisaṃ uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati|

398. chandaṃ  janaitīti| tattha katamau chandau? yau chandau chandikatā kattukamyatā kusalau  dhammacchandau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chandau’’| imaṃ chandaṃ janaiti sañ‍janaiti uṭṭhapaiti samuṭṭhapaiti nibbattaiti abhinibbattaiti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘chandaṃ janaitī’’ti|

399. vāyamatīti| tattha katamau vāyāmau? yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau…pai॰… sammāvāyāmau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vāyāmau’’| iminā vāyāmaina upaitau hauti…pai॰… saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘vāyamatī’’ti|

400. vīriyaṃ  ārabhatīti| tattha katamaṃ vīriyaṃ? yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau…pai॰… sammāvāyāmau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vīriyaṃ’’| imaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabhati samārabhati āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘vīriyaṃ ārabhatī’’ti|

401. cittaṃ paggaṇhātīti| tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘cittaṃ’’| imaṃ cittaṃ paggaṇhāti sampaggaṇhāti upatthambhaiti pac‍cupatthambhaiti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘cittaṃ paggaṇhātī’’ti|

402. padahatīti| tattha katamaṃ padhānaṃ? yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau…pai॰… sammāvāyāmau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘padhānaṃ’’| iminā padhānaina upaitau hauti…pai॰… saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘padahatī’’ti|

403. kathañ‍ca bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati? tattha  katamai  anuppan‍nā kusalā dhammā? tīṇi kusalamūlāni – alaubhau, adausau, amauhau| taṃsampayuttau vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau| taṃsamuṭṭhānaṃ kāyakammaṃ, vacīkammaṃ, manaukammaṃ – imai vuc‍canti ‘‘anuppan‍nā kusalā dhammā’’| iti imaisaṃ anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati|

404. chandaṃ janaitīti…pai॰… vāyamatīti…pai॰… vīriyaṃ ārabhatīti…pai॰… cittaṃ paggaṇhātīti…pai॰… padahatīti| tattha katamaṃ padhānaṃ? yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau  …pai॰… sammāvāyāmau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘padhānaṃ’’| iminā padhānaina upaitau hauti…pai॰… saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘padahatī’’ti|

405. kathañ‍ca bhikkhu uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati? tattha katamai uppan‍nā kusalā dhammā? tīṇi kusalamūlāni – alaubhau, adausau, amauhau| taṃsampayuttau vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau, taṃsamuṭṭhānaṃ kāyakammaṃ vacīkammaṃ manaukammaṃ – imai vuc‍canti ‘‘uppan‍nā kusalā dhammā’’| iti imaisaṃ uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati|

406. ṭhitiyāti| yā ṭhiti sau asammausau, yau asammausau sau bhiyyaubhāvau, yau bhiyyaubhāvau taṃ vaipul‍laṃ, yaṃ vaipul‍laṃ sā bhāvanā, yā bhāvanā sā pāripūrī|

407. chandaṃ janaitīti…pai॰… vāyamatīti…pai॰… vīriyaṃ ārabhatīti…pai॰… cittaṃ paggaṇhātīti…pai॰… padahatīti| tattha katamaṃ padhānaṃ? yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau…pai॰… sammāvāyāmau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘padhānaṃ’’| iminā padhānaina upaitau hauti…pai॰… saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘padahatī’’ti|

suttantabhājanīyaṃ|

2. abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ

408. cattārau  sammappadhānā – idha bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ  akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati|

409. kathañ‍ca bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ  diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā  vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati|

410. chandaṃ janaitīti| tattha katamau chandau? yau chandau chandikatā kattukamyatā kusalau dhammacchandau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chandau’’| imaṃ chandaṃ janaiti sañ‍janaiti uṭṭhapaiti samuṭṭhapaiti nibbattaiti abhinibbattaiti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘chandaṃ janaitī’’ti|

411. vāyamatīti| tattha katamau vāyāmau? yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau…pai॰… sammāvāyāmau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vāyāmau’’| iminā vāyāmaina upaitau hauti samupaitau upāgatau samupāgatau upapan‍nau sampan‍nau saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘vāyamatī’’ti|

412. vīriyaṃ ārabhatīti| tattha katamaṃ vīriyaṃ? yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau…pai॰… sammāvāyāmau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – idaṃ  vuc‍cati ‘‘vīriyaṃ’’| imaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabhati samārabhati āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘vīriyaṃ ārabhatī’’ti|

413. cittaṃ  paggaṇhātīti| tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu  – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘cittaṃ’’| imaṃ cittaṃ paggaṇhāti sampaggaṇhāti upatthambhaiti pac‍cupatthambhaiti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘cittaṃ paggaṇhātī’’ti|

414. padahatīti| tattha katamaṃ sammappadhānaṃ? yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau…pai॰… sammāvāyāmau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sammappadhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā sammappadhānasampayuttā|

415. kathañ‍ca bhikkhu uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai  uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati|

416. chandaṃ  janaitīti…pai॰… vāyamatīti…pai॰… vīriyaṃ ārabhatīti…pai॰… cittaṃ paggaṇhātīti…pai॰… padahatīti| tattha katamaṃ sammappadhānaṃ? yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau…pai॰… sammāvāyāmau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sammappadhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā  sammappadhānasampayuttā|

417. kathañ‍ca bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati|

418. chandaṃ janaitīti…pai॰… vāyamatīti…pai॰… vīriyaṃ ārabhatīti…pai॰… cittaṃ paggaṇhātīti…pai॰… padahatīti| tattha katamaṃ sammappadhānaṃ? yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau  …pai॰… sammāvāyāmau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sammappadhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā sammappadhānasampayuttā|

419. kathañ‍ca bhikkhu uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya  vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati|

420. ṭhitiyāti| yā ṭhiti sau asammausau, yau asammausau sau bhiyyaubhāvau, yau bhiyyaubhāvau taṃ vaipul‍laṃ, yaṃ vaipul‍laṃ sā bhāvanā, yā bhāvanā sā pāripūrī|

421. chandaṃ janaitīti| tattha katamau chandau? yau chandau chandikatā kattukamyatā kusalau dhammacchandau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chandau’’| imaṃ chandaṃ janaiti sañ‍janaiti uṭṭhapaiti samuṭṭhapaiti nibbattaiti abhinibbattaiti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘chandaṃ janaitī’’ti|

422. vāyamatīti| tattha katamau vāyāmau? yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau…pai॰… sammāvāyāmau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vāyāmau’’| iminā vāyāmaina upaitau hauti…pai॰… saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘vāyamatī’’ti|

423. vīriyaṃ ārabhatīti| tattha katamaṃ vīriyaṃ? yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau…pai॰… sammāvāyāmau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vīriyaṃ’’| imaṃ vīriyaṃ ārabhati samārabhati āsaivati  bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘vīriyaṃ ārabhatī’’ti|

424. cittaṃ paggaṇhātīti| tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu  – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘cittaṃ’’| imaṃ cittaṃ paggaṇhāti sampaggaṇhāti upatthambhaiti pac‍cupatthambhaiti| taina  vuc‍cati ‘‘cittaṃ paggaṇhātī’’ti|

425. padahatīti| tattha katamaṃ sammappadhānaṃ? yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau…pai॰… sammāvāyāmau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sammappadhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā sammappadhānasampayuttā|

426. tattha katamaṃ sammappadhānaṃ? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, yau tasmiṃ samayai caitasikau vīriyārambhau…pai॰… sammāvāyāmau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sammappadhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā sammappadhānasampayuttā|

abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ|

3. pañhāpucchakaṃ

427. cattārau  sammappadhānā – idha bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya…pai॰… anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya…pai॰… uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya  bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati|

428. catun‍naṃ sammappadhānānaṃ kati kusalā, kati akusalā, kati abyākatā…pai॰… kati saraṇā, kati araṇā?

1. tikaṃ

429. kusalāyaiva| siyā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā| vipākadhammadhammā| anupādin‍naanupādāniyā| asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikā| siyā savitak‍kasavicārā, siyā avitak‍kavicāramattā, siyā avitak‍kaavicārā| siyā pītisahagatā, siyā sukhasahagatā, siyā upaikkhāsahagatā| naiva dassanaina na bhāvanāya pahātabbā| naiva dassanaina na bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā| apacayagāminau| saikkhā| appamāṇā| appamāṇārammaṇā| paṇītā| sammattaniyatā| na maggārammaṇā| maggahaitukā| siyā maggādhipatinau, siyā na vattabbā maggādhipatinauti| siyā uppan‍nā, siyā anuppan‍nā, na vattabbā uppādinauti| siyā atītā, siyā anāgatā, siyā pac‍cuppan‍nā| na vattabbā atītārammaṇātipi, anāgatārammaṇātipi, pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇātipi| siyā ajjhattā, siyā bahiddhā, siyā ajjhattabahiddhā| bahiddhārammaṇā| anidassanaappaṭighā|

2. dukaṃ

430. na haitū| sahaitukā| haitusampayuttā| na vattabbā haitū caiva sahaitukā cāti, sahaitukā caiva na ca haitū| na vattabbā haitū caiva haitusampayuttā cāti, haitusampayuttā caiva na ca haitū| na  haitū sahaitukā| sappac‍cayā| saṅkhatā| anidassanā| appaṭighā| arūpā| laukuttarā| kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyā, kainaci na viñ‍ñaiyyā| nau āsavā| anāsavā| āsavavippayuttā| na vattabbā āsavā caiva sāsavā cātipi, sāsavā caiva nau ca āsavātipi| na vattabbā āsavā caiva āsavasampayuttā cātipi, āsavasampayuttā caiva nau ca āsavātipi| āsavavippayuttā| anāsavā| nau saṃyaujanā…pai॰… nau ganthā…pai॰… nau aughā…pai॰… nau yaugā…pai॰… nau nīvaraṇā…pai॰… nau parāmāsā…pai॰… sārammaṇā| nau cittā| caitasikā| cittasampayuttā| cittasaṃsaṭṭhā| cittasamuṭṭhānā| cittasahabhunau| cittānuparivattinau| cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānā| cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhunau| cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattinau| bāhirā| nau upādā| anupādin‍nā| nau upādānā…pai॰… nau kilaisā…pai॰… na dassanaina pahātabbā| na bhāvanāya pahātabbā| na dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā| na bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā| siyā savitak‍kā, siyā avitak‍kā| siyā savicārā, siyā avicārā| siyā sappītikā, siyā appītikā| siyā pītisahagatā, siyā na pītisahagatā| siyā sukhasahagatā, siyā na sukhasahagatā| siyā upaikkhāsahagatā, siyā na upaikkhāsahagatā| na kāmāvacarā| na rūpāvacarā| na arūpāvacarā| apariyāpan‍nā| niyyānikā| niyatā| anuttarā| araṇāti|

pañhāpucchakaṃ|

sammappadhānavibhaṅgau niṭṭhitau|

9. iddhipādavibhaṅgau

1. suttantabhājanīyaṃ

431. cattārau  iddhipādā – idha bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, cittasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti|

1. chandiddhipādau
