432. kathañ‍ca bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti? chandaṃ cai bhikkhu adhipatiṃ karitvā labhati samādhiṃ, labhati cittassaikaggataṃ cittassa aikaggataṃ (sī॰ syā॰) – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chandasamādhi’’| sau anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| imai vuc‍canti ‘‘padhānasaṅkhārā’’| iti ayañ‍ca chandasamādhi, imai ca padhānasaṅkhārā| tadaikajjhaṃ abhisañ‍ñahitvā abhisaṅkhipitvā chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārautvaiva saṅkhyaṃ saṅkhaṃ (sī॰) gacchati|

433. tattha  katamau chandau? yau chandau chandikatā kattukamyatā kusalau dhammacchandau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chandau’’|

tattha  katamau samādhi? yā  cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhārau avikkhaipau avisāhaṭamānasatā samathau samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘samādhi’’|

tattha katamau padhānasaṅkhārau? yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau nik‍kamau parak‍kamau uyyāmau vāyāmau ussāhau ussauḷhī thāmau ṭhiti asithilaparak‍kamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāhau  vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘padhānasaṅkhārau’’| iti iminā ca chandaina, iminā ca samādhinā, iminā ca padhānasaṅkhāraina upaitau hauti samupaitau upāgatau samupāgatau upapan‍nau sampan‍nau saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgatau’’ti|

434. iddhīti| yā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ iddhi samiddhi ijjhanā samijjhanā lābhau paṭilābhau patti sampatti phusanā sacchikiriyā upasampadā|

‘‘iddhipādau’’ti| tathābhūtassa vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau|

‘‘iddhipādaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti| tai dhammai āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘iddhipādaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti|

2. vīriyiddhipādau

435. kathañ‍ca bhikkhu vīriyasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti? vīriyaṃ cai bhikkhu adhipatiṃ karitvā labhati samādhiṃ labhati cittassaikaggataṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vīriyasamādhi’’| sau anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ  pahānāya…pai॰… anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya…pai॰… uppan‍nānaṃ  kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| imai vuc‍canti ‘‘padhānasaṅkhārā’’| iti ayañ‍ca vīriyasamādhi, imai ca padhānasaṅkhārā; tadaikajjhaṃ abhisañ‍ñūhitvā abhisaṅkhipitvā vīriyasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārautvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati|

436. tattha katamaṃ vīriyaṃ? yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau…pai॰… sammāvāyāmau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vīriyaṃ’’|

tattha  katamau samādhi? yā cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhārau avikkhaipau avisāhaṭamānasatā samathau samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘samādhi’’|

tattha  katamau padhānasaṅkhārau? yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau…pai॰… sammāvāyāmau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘padhānasaṅkhārau’’| iti iminā ca vīriyaina, iminā ca samādhinā, iminā ca padhānasaṅkhāraina upaitau hauti…pai॰… saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘vīriyasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgatau’’ti|

437. iddhīti| yā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ iddhi samiddhi ijjhanā samijjhanā lābhau paṭilābhau patti sampatti phusanā sacchikiriyā upasampadā|

iddhipādauti| tathābhūtassa vaidanākkhandhau…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau|

iddhipādaṃ bhāvaitīti| tai dhammai āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘iddhipādaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti|

3. cittiddhipādau

438. kathañ‍ca  bhikkhu cittasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti? cittaṃ cai bhikkhu adhipatiṃ karitvā labhati samādhiṃ labhati cittassaikaggataṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘cittasamādhi’’| sau anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya…pai॰… anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya…pai॰… uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| imai vuc‍canti ‘‘padhānasaṅkhārā’’| iti ayañ‍ca cittasamādhi, imai ca padhānasaṅkhārā; tadaikajjhaṃ abhisañ‍ñūhitvā abhisaṅkhipitvā cittasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārautvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati|

439. tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘cittaṃ’’|

tattha katamau samādhi? yā cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti…pai॰… sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘samādhi’’|

tattha katamau padhānasaṅkhārau? yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau…pai॰… sammāvāyāmau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘padhānasaṅkhārau’’| iti iminā ca cittaina, iminā ca samādhinā, iminā ca padhānasaṅkhāraina  upaitau hauti…pai॰… saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘cittasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgatau’’ti|

440. iddhīti| yā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ iddhi samiddhi ijjhanā samijjhanā  lābhau paṭilābhau patti sampatti phusanā sacchikiriyā upasampadā|

iddhipādauti| tathābhūtassa vaidanākkhandhau…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau|

iddhipādaṃ  bhāvaitīti| tai dhammai āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘iddhipādaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti|

4. vīmaṃsiddhipādau

441. kathañ‍ca bhikkhu vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti? vīmaṃsaṃ cai bhikkhu adhipatiṃ karitvā labhati samādhiṃ labhati cittassaikaggataṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vīmaṃsāsamādhi’’| sau anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya…pai॰… anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya…pai॰… uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| imai vuc‍canti ‘‘padhānasaṅkhārā’’| iti ayañ‍ca vīmaṃsāsamādhi, imai ca padhānasaṅkhārā; tadaikajjhaṃ abhisañ‍ñūhitvā abhisaṅkhipitvā vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārautvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati|

442. tattha katamā vīmaṃsā? yā pañ‍ñā pajānanā…pai॰… amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vīmaṃsā’’|

tattha katamau samādhi? yā cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti…pai॰… sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘samādhi’’|

tattha katamau padhānasaṅkhārau? yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau  …pai॰… sammāvāyāmau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘padhānasaṅkhārau’’| iti imāya ca vīmaṃsāya, iminā ca samādhinā, iminā ca padhānasaṅkhāraina upaitau hauti…pai॰… saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgatau’’ti|

443. iddhīti| yā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ iddhi samiddhi ijjhanā samijjhanā lābhau paṭilābhau patti sampatti phusanā sacchikiriyā upasampadā|

iddhipādauti| tathābhūtassa vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇakhandhau|

iddhipādaṃ bhāvaitīti| tai dhammai āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘iddhipādaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti|

suttantabhājanīyaṃ|

2. abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ

444. cattārau iddhipādā – idha bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, cittasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti|

1. chandiddhipādau

445. kathañ‍ca bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ  bhāvaiti|

446. tattha katamau chandau? yau chandau chandikatā kattukamyatā kusalau dhammacchandau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chandau’’|

tattha katamau samādhi? yā cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti…pai॰… sammāsamādhi samādhisambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘samādhi’’|

tattha  katamau padhānasaṅkhārau? yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau…pai॰… sammāvāyāmau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘padhānasaṅkhārau’’| iti iminā ca chandaina, iminā ca samādhinā, iminā ca padhānasaṅkhāraina upaitau hauti…pai॰… saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgatau’’ti|

447. iddhīti| yā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ iddhi samiddhi ijjhanā samijjhanā lābhau paṭilābhau patti sampatti phusanā sacchikiriyā upasampadā|

iddhipādauti| tathābhūtassa phassau…pai॰… paggāhau avikkhaipau|

iddhipādaṃ bhāvaitīti| tai dhammai āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘iddhipādaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti|

2. vīriyiddhipādau

448. kathañ‍ca bhikkhu vīriyasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai vīriyasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ  bhāvaiti|

449. tattha katamaṃ vīriyaṃ? yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau…pai॰… sammāvāyāmau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vīriyaṃ’’|

tattha katamau samādhi? yā cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti…pai॰… sammāsamādhi samādhisambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘samādhi’’|

tattha katamau padhānasaṅkhārau? yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau…pai॰… sammāvāyāmau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘padhānasaṅkhārau’’| iti iminā ca vīriyaina, iminā ca samādhinā, iminā ca padhānasaṅkhāraina upaitau hauti…pai॰… saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘vīriyasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgatau’’ti|

450. iddhīti| yā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ iddhi samiddhi ijjhanā samijjhanā lābhau paṭilābhau patti sampatti phusanā sacchikiriyā upasampadā|

iddhipādauti| tathābhūtasa phassau…pai॰… paggāhau avikkhaipau|

iddhipādaṃ bhāvaitīti| tai dhammai āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘iddhipādaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti|

3. cittiddhipādau

451. kathañ‍ca  bhikkhu cittasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ  apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai cittasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ  bhāvaiti|

452. tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘cittaṃ’’|

tattha katamau samādhi? yā cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti…pai॰… sammāsamādhi samādhisambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘samādhi’’|

tattha katamau padhānasaṅkhārau? yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau…pai॰… sammāvāyāmau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘padhānasaṅkhārau’’| iti iminā ca cittaina, iminā ca samādhinā, iminā ca padhānasaṅkhāraina upaitau hauti…pai॰… saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘cittasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgatau’’ti|

453. iddhīti| yā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ iddhi samiddhi ijjhanā samijjhanā lābhau paṭilābhau patti sampatti phusanā sacchikiriyā upasampadā|

iddhipādauti| tathābhūtassa phassau…pai॰… paggāhau avikkhaipau|

iddhipādaṃ bhāvaitīti| tai dhammai āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘iddhipādaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti|

4. vīmaṃsiddhipādau

454. kathañ‍ca bhikkhu vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ  iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti|

455. tattha  katamā vīmaṃsā? yā pañ‍ñā pajānanā…pai॰… amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vīmaṃsā’’|

tattha katamau samādhi? yā cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti…pai॰… sammāsamādhi samādhisambaujjhaṅgau  maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘samādhi’’|

tattha katamau padhānasaṅkhārau? yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau…pai॰… sammāvāyāmau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘padhānasaṅkhārau’’| iti imāya ca vīmaṃsāya, iminā ca samādhinā, iminā ca padhānasaṅkhāraina upaitau hauti samupaitau upāgatau samupāgatau  upapan‍nau sampan‍nau saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgatau’’ti

456. iddhīti| yā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ iddhi samiddhi ijjhanā samijjhanā lābhau paṭilābhau patti sampatti phusanā sacchikiriyā upasampadā|

iddhipādauti| tathābhūtassa phassau…pai॰… paggāhau avikkhaipau|

iddhipādaṃ bhāvaitīti| tai dhammai āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘iddhipādaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti|

457. cattārau iddhipādā – chandiddhipādau, vīriyiddhipādau, cittiddhipādau, vīmaṃsiddhipādau|

458. tattha katamau chandiddhipādau? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya  bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, yau tasmiṃ samayai chandau chandikatā kattukamyatā kusalau dhammacchandau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘chandiddhipādau’’| avasaisā dhammā chandiddhipādasampayuttā|

459. tattha katamau vīriyiddhipādau? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja  viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, yau tasmiṃ samayai caitasikau vīriyārambhau…pai॰… sammāvāyāmau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vīriyiddhipādau’’| avasaisā dhammā vīriyiddhipādasampayuttā|

460. tattha katamau cittiddhipādau? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ  apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi  …pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, yaṃ tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘cittiddhipādau’’| avasaisā dhammā cittiddhipādasampayuttā|

461. tattha katamau vīmaṃsiddhipādau? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja  viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñā pajānanā…pai॰… amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vīmaṃsiddhipādau’’| avasaisā dhammā vīmaṃsiddhipādasampayuttā|

abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ|

3. pañhāpucchakaṃ

462. cattārau iddhipādā – idha bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… cittasamādhi…pai॰… vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti|

463. catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ kati kusalā, kati akusalā, kati abyākatā…pai॰… kati saraṇā, kati araṇā?

1. tikaṃ

464. kusalāyaiva| siyā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā| vipākadhammadhammā| anupādin‍naanupādāniyā| asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikā| siyā savitak‍kasavicārā, siyā avitak‍kavicāramattā, siyā avitak‍kaavicārā| siyā  pītisahagatā, siyā sukhasahagatā, siyā upaikkhāsahagatā| naiva dassanaina na bhāvanāya pahātabbā| naiva dassanaina na bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā| apacayagāminau| saikkhā| appamāṇā| appamāṇārammaṇā| paṇītā| sammattaniyatā| na maggārammaṇā, maggahaitukā, na maggādhipatinau| siyā uppan‍nā, siyā anuppan‍nā, na vattabbā uppādinauti| siyā atītā, siyā anāgatā, siyā pac‍cuppan‍nā| na vattabbā atītārammaṇātipi, anāgatārammaṇātipi, pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇātipi| siyā ajjhattā, siyā bahiddhā, siyā ajjhattabahiddhā| bahiddhārammaṇā| anidassanaappaṭighā|

2. dukaṃ

465. vīmaṃsiddhipādau  haitu, tayau iddhipādā na haitū| sahaitukā| haitusampayuttā| vīmaṃsiddhipādau haitu caiva sahaitukau ca, tayau iddhipādā na vattabbā haitū caiva sahaitukā cāti, sahaitukā caiva na ca haitū| vīmaṃsiddhipādau haitu caiva haitusampayuttau ca, tayau iddhipādā na vattabbā haitū caiva haitusampayuttā cāti, haitusampayuttā caiva na ca haitū| tayau iddhipādā na haitū sahaitukā, vīmaṃsiddhipādau na vattabbau na haitu sahaitukautipi, na haitu ahaitukautipi| sappac‍cayā| saṅkhatā| anidassanā| appaṭighā| arūpā| laukuttarā| kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyā, kainaci na viñ‍ñaiyyā| nau āsavā| anāsavā| āsavavippayuttā| na vattabbā āsavā caiva sāsavā cātipi, sāsavā caiva nau ca āsavātipi| na vattabbā āsavā caiva āsavasampayuttā cātipi, āsavasampayuttā caiva nau ca āsavātipi| āsavavippayuttā| anāsavā|

nau saṃyaujanā…pai॰… nau ganthā…pai॰… nau aughā…pai॰… nau yaugā…pai॰… nau nīvaraṇā…pai॰… nau parāmāsā…pai॰… sārammaṇā| tayau iddhipādā nau cittā, cittiddhipādau  cittaṃ| tayau iddhipādā caitasikā, cittiddhipādau acaitasikau| tayau iddhipādā cittasampayuttā, cittiddhipādau na vattabbau cittaina sampayuttautipi, cittaina vippayuttautipi| tayau iddhipādā cittasaṃsaṭṭhā, cittiddhipādau na vattabbau cittaina saṃsaṭṭhautipi, cittaina visaṃsaṭṭhautipi| tayau iddhipādā cittasamuṭṭhānā, cittiddhipādau nau cittasamuṭṭhānau| tayau iddhipādā cittasahabhunau, cittiddhipādau nau cittasahabhū| tayau iddhipādā cittānuparivattinau, cittiddhipādau nau cittānuparivatti| tayau iddhipādā cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānā, cittiddhipādau nau cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānau| tayau iddhipādā cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhunau, cittiddhipādau nau cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhū| tayau iddhipādā cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattinau, cittiddhipādau nau cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivatti|

tayau iddhipādā bāhirā, cittiddhipādau ajjhattikau| nau upādā| anupādin‍nā| nau upādānā…pai॰… nau kilaisā…pai॰… na dassanaina pahātabbā| na bhāvanāya pahātabbā| na dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā| na bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā| siyā savitak‍kā, siyā avitak‍kā| siyā savicārā, siyā avicārā| siyā sappītikā, siyā appītikā| siyā pītisahagatā, siyā na pītisahagatā| siyā sukhasahagatā, siyā na sukhasahagatā| siyā upaikkhāsahagatā, siyā na upaikkhāsahagatā| na kāmāvacarā| na rūpāvacarā| na arūpāvacarā| apariyāpan‍nā| niyyānikā| niyatā| anuttarā| araṇāti|

pañhāpucchakaṃ|

iddhipādavibhaṅgau niṭṭhitau|

10. baujjhaṅgavibhaṅgau

1. suttantabhājanīyaṃ

466. satta  baujjhaṅgā – satisambaujjhaṅgau, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau, vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau, pītisambaujjhaṅgau, passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau, samādhisambaujjhaṅgau, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau|

467. tattha katamau satisambaujjhaṅgau? idha bhikkhu satimā hauti paramaina satinaipak‍kaina saman‍nāgatau, cirakatampi cirabhāsitampi saritā hauti anussaritā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘satisambaujjhaṅgau’’|

sau tathā satau viharantau taṃ dhammaṃ pañ‍ñāya pavicinati pavicarati parivīmaṃsamāpaj‍jati – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau’’|

tassa taṃ dhammaṃ pañ‍ñāya pavicinatau pavicaratau parivīmaṃsamāpaj‍jatau āraddhaṃ hauti vīriyaṃ asal‍līnaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau’’|

āraddhavīriyassa uppaj‍jati pīti nirāmisā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘pītisambaujjhaṅgau’’|

pītimanassa kāyaupi passambhati, cittampi passambhati – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau’’|

passaddhakāyassa  sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘samādhisambaujjhaṅgau’’|

sau tathā samāhitaṃ cittaṃ sādhukaṃ ajjhupaikkhitā hauti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau’’|

468. satta baujjhaṅgā – satisambaujjhaṅgau, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau, vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau, pītisambaujjhaṅgau, passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau, samādhisambaujjhaṅgau, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau|

469. tattha  katamau satisambaujjhaṅgau? atthi ajjhattaṃ dhammaisu sati, atthi bahiddhā dhammaisu sati| yadapi ajjhattaṃ dhammaisu sati tadapi satisambaujjhaṅgau abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati| yadapi bahiddhā  dhammaisu sati tadapi satisambaujjhaṅgau abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati|

tattha katamau dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau? atthi ajjhattaṃ dhammaisu pavicayau, atthi bahiddhā dhammaisu pavicayau| yadapi ajjhattaṃ dhammaisu pavicayau tadapi dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati| yadapi bahiddhā dhammaisu pavicayau tadapi dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati|

tattha katamau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau? atthi kāyikaṃ vīriyaṃ, atthi caitasikaṃ vīriyaṃ| yadapi kāyikaṃ vīriyaṃ tadapi vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati| yadapi caitasikaṃ vīriyaṃ tadapi vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati|

tattha katamau pītisambaujjhaṅgau? atthi savitak‍kasavicārā pīti, atthi avitak‍kaavicārā pīti| yadapi savitak‍kasavicārā pīti tadapi pītisambaujjhaṅgau abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati| yadapi avitak‍kaavicārā pīti tadapi pītisambaujjhaṅgau abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati|

tattha  katamau passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau? atthi kāyapassaddhi kāyappassaddhi (syā॰ ka॰), atthi cittapassaddhi cittappassaddhi (syā॰ ka॰)| yadapi kāyapassaddhi tadapi passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati| yadapi cittapassaddhi tadapi passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati|

tattha katamau samādhisambaujjhaṅgau? atthi savitak‍kau savicārau samādhi, atthi avitak‍kau avicārau samādhi| yadapi savitak‍kau savicārau samādhi tadapi samādhisambaujjhaṅgau abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati| yadapi avitak‍kau avicārau samādhi tadapi samādhisambaujjhaṅgau abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati|

tattha katamau upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau? atthi ajjhattaṃ dhammaisu upaikkhā, atthi bahiddhā dhammaisu upaikkhā| yadapi ajjhattaṃ dhammaisu upaikkhā tadapi upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati| yadapi  bahiddhā dhammaisu upaikkhā tadapi  upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati|

470. satta  baujjhaṅgā – satisambaujjhaṅgau, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau, vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau, pītisambaujjhaṅgau, passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau, samādhisambaujjhaṅgau, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau|

471. tattha katamau satisambaujjhaṅgau? idha bhikkhu satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… vīriyasambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… pītisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… passaddhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… samādhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ|

suttantabhājanīyaṃ|

2. abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ

472. satta  baujjhaṅgā – satisambaujjhaṅgau, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau, vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau, pītisambaujjhaṅgau, passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau, samādhisambaujjhaṅgau, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau|

473. tattha katamai satta baujjhaṅgā? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai satta baujjhaṅgā haunti – satisambaujjhaṅgau…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau|

474. tattha katamau satisambaujjhaṅgau? yā sati anussati…pai॰… sammāsati satisambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘satisambaujjhaṅgau’’|

tattha  katamau dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau? yā pañ‍ñā pajānanā…pai॰… amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau’’|

tattha katamau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau? yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau…pai॰… sammāvāyāmau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau’’|

tattha katamau pītisambaujjhaṅgau? yā pīti pāmauj‍jaṃ āmaudanā pamaudanā hāsau pahāsau vitti audagyaṃ audaggaṃ (sī॰) dha॰ sa॰ 9 attamanatā cittassa pītisambaujjhaṅgau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘pītisambaujjhaṅgau’’|

tattha  katamau passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau? yā vaidanākkhandhassa sañ‍ñākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhassa passaddhi paṭippassaddhi passambhanā paṭippassambhanā paṭippassambhitattaṃ passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau’’|

tattha  katamau samādhisambaujjhaṅgau? yā cittassa ṭhiti…pai॰… sammāsamādhi samādhisambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘samādhisambaujjhaṅgau’’|

tattha katamau upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau? yā upaikkhā upaikkhanā ajjhupaikkhanā majjhattatā cittassa upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau’’| imai vuc‍canti satta baujjhaṅgā| avasaisā dhammā sattahi baujjhaṅgaihi  sampayuttā|

475. satta baujjhaṅgā – satisambaujjhaṅgau…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau|

476. tattha katamau satisambaujjhaṅgau? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai sati anussati sammāsati satisambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘satisambaujjhaṅgau’’| avasaisā dhammā satisambaujjhaṅgasampayuttā…pai॰… avasaisā dhammā  dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgasampayuttā…pai॰… avasaisā dhammā vīriyasambaujjhaṅgasampayuttā…pai॰… avasaisā dhammā pītisambaujjhaṅgasampayuttā…pai॰… avasaisā dhammā passaddhisambaujjhaṅgasampayuttā…pai॰… avasaisā dhammā samādhisambaujjhaṅgasampayuttā|

tattha katamau upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai upaikkhā upaikkhanā ajjhupaikkhanā majjhattatā cittassa upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau’’| avasaisā dhammā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgasampayuttā|

477. satta  baujjhaṅgā – satisambaujjhaṅgau…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau|

478. tattha katamai satta baujjhaṅgā? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi  …pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, yau tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti – imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva laukuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa katattā bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ, tasmiṃ samayai satta baujjhaṅgā haunti – satisambaujjhaṅgau…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau|

479. tattha katamau satisambaujjhaṅgau? yā sati anussati…pai॰… sammāsati satisambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘satisambaujjhaṅgau’’…pai॰…|

tattha katamau upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau? yā upaikkhā upaikkhanā ajjhupaikkhanā majjhattatā cittassa upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau’’| imai vuc‍canti ‘‘satta baujjhaṅgā’’| avasaisā dhammā sattahi baujjhaṅgaihi sampayuttā|

480. satta baujjhaṅgā – satisambaujjhaṅgau…pai॰… upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau|

481. tattha  katamau satisambaujjhaṅgau? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ  upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva laukuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa katattā bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai sati anussati sammāsati satisambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘satisambaujjhaṅgau’’| avasaisā dhammā satisambaujjhaṅgasampayuttā…pai॰… avasaisā dhammā dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgasampayuttā  …pai॰… avasaisā dhammā vīriyasambaujjhaṅgasampayuttā…pai॰… avasaisā dhammā pītisambaujjhaṅgasampayuttā…pai॰… avasaisā dhammā passaddhisambaujjhaṅgasampayuttā…pai॰… avasaisā dhammā samādhisambaujjhaṅgasampayuttā|

tattha katamau upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ  apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva laukuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa katattā bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai upaikkhā upaikkhanā ajjhupaikkhanā majjhattatā cittassa upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau’’| avasaisā dhammā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgasampayuttā|

abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ|

3. pañhāpucchakaṃ

482. satta baujjhaṅgā – satisambaujjhaṅgau, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau, vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau, pītisambaujjhaṅgau, passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau, samādhisambaujjhaṅgau, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau|

483. sattan‍naṃ  baujjhaṅgānaṃ kati kusalā, kati akusalā, kati abyākatā…pai॰… kati saraṇā, kati araṇā?

1. tikaṃ

484. siyā kusalā, siyā abyākatā| pītisambaujjhaṅgau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau; cha baujjhaṅgā siyā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā| siyā vipākā, siyā vipākadhammadhammā| anupādin‍naanupādāniyā| asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikā| siyā savitak‍kasavicārā, siyā avitak‍kavicāramattā, siyā avitak‍kaavicārā| pītisambaujjhaṅgau na pītisahagatau, sukhasahagatau, na upaikkhāsahagatau; cha baujjhaṅgā siyā pītisahagatā, siyā sukhasahagatā, siyā upaikkhāsahagatā| naiva dassanaina na bhāvanāya pahātabbā| naiva dassanaina  na bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā| siyā apacayagāminau, siyā naivācayagāmināpacayagāminau| siyā saikkhā, siyā asaikkhā| appamāṇā| appamāṇārammaṇā| paṇītā| siyā sammattaniyatā, siyā aniyatā| na maggārammaṇā, siyā maggahaitukā, siyā maggādhipatinau; siyā na vattabbā maggahaitukātipi, maggādhipatinautipi| siyā uppan‍nā, siyā  anuppan‍nā, siyā uppādinau| siyā atītā, siyā anāgatā, siyā pac‍cuppan‍nā| na vattabbā atītārammaṇātipi, anāgatārammaṇātipi, pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇātipi| siyā ajjhattā, siyā bahiddhā, siyā ajjhattabahiddhā| bahiddhārammaṇā| anidassanaappaṭighā|

2. dukaṃ

485. dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau haitu, cha baujjhaṅgā haitusampayuttā| dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau haitu caiva sahaitukau ca, cha baujjhaṅgā na vattabbā haitū caiva sahaitukā cāti, sahaitukā caiva na ca haitū| dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau haitu caiva haitusampayuttau ca, cha baujjhaṅgā na vattabbā haitū caiva haitusampayuttā cāti, haitusampayuttā caiva na ca haitū| cha baujjhaṅgā na haitū sahaitukā, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau na vattabbau na haitusahaitukautipi, na haituahaitukautipi| sappac‍cayā| saṅkhatā| anidassanā| appaṭighā| arūpā| laukuttarā| kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyā, kainaci  na viñ‍ñaiyyā| nau āsavā| anāsavā āsavavippayuttā| na vattabbā āsavā caiva sāsavā cātipi, sāsavā caiva nau ca āsavātipi| āsavā caiva āsavasampayuttā cātipi, āsavasampayuttā caiva nau ca āsavātipi| āsavavippayuttā| anāsavā| nau saṃyaujanā…pai॰… nau ganthā…pai॰… nau aughā…pai॰… nau yaugā…pai॰… nau nīvaraṇā…pai॰… nau parāmāsā…pai॰… sārammaṇā| nau cittā| caitasikā| cittasampayuttā| cittasaṃsaṭṭhā| cittasamuṭṭhānā| cittasahabhunau| cittānuparivattinau| cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānā| cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhunau| cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattinau| bāhirā| nau upādā| anupādin‍nā| nau upādānā…pai॰… nau kilaisā…pai॰… na dassanaina pahātabbā| na bhāvanāya pahātabbā| na dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā| na bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā| siyā savitak‍kā, siyā avitak‍kā| siyā savicārā, siyā avicārā|

pītisambaujjhaṅgau  appītikau, cha baujjhaṅgā siyā sappītikā, siyā appītikā| pītisambaujjhaṅgau na pītisahagatau, cha baujjhaṅgā siyā pītisahagatā, siyā na pītisahagatā| pītisambaujjhaṅgau sukhasahagatau, cha baujjhaṅgā siyā  sukhasahagatā, siyā na sukhasahagatā| pītisambaujjhaṅgau na upaikkhāsahagatau, cha baujjhaṅgā siyā upaikkhāsahagatā, siyā na upaikkhāsahagatā| na kāmāvacarā| na rūpāvacarā| na arūpāvacarā| apariyāpan‍nā| siyā niyyānikā, siyā aniyyānikā| siyā niyatā, siyā aniyatā| anuttarā| araṇāti|

pañhāpucchakaṃ|

baujjhaṅgavibhaṅgau niṭṭhitau|

11. maggaṅgavibhaṅgau

1. suttantabhājanīyaṃ

486. ariyau  aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvācā, sammākammantau, sammāājīvau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi|

487. tattha katamā sammādiṭṭhi? dukkhai ñāṇaṃ, dukkhasamudayai ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhai ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ñāṇaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sammādiṭṭhi’’|

tattha katamau sammāsaṅkappau? naikkhammasaṅkappau, abyāpādasaṅkappau, avihiṃsāsaṅkappau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sammāsaṅkappau’’|

tattha katamā sammāvācā? musāvādā vairamaṇī, pisuṇāya vācāya vairamaṇī, pharusāya vācāya vairamaṇī, samphappalāpā vairamaṇī – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sammāvācā’’|

tattha katamau sammākammantau? pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī, adin‍nādānā vairamaṇī, kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇī – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sammākammantau’’|

tattha katamau sammāājīvau? idha ariyasāvakau micchāājīvaṃ pahāya sammāājīvaina jīvikaṃ kappaiti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sammāājīvau’’|

tattha  katamau sammāvāyāmau? idha bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ  ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sammāvāyāmau’’|

tattha  katamā sammāsati? idha bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ, vaidanāsu  vaidanānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ, cittai cittānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ, dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sammāsati’’|

tattha katamau sammāsamādhi? idha bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati satau ca sampajānau sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti, yaṃ taṃ ariyā  ācikkhanti ‘‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sammāsamādhi’’|

488. ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvācā, sammākammantau, sammāājīvau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi|

489. tattha katamā sammādiṭṭhi? idha bhikkhu sammādiṭṭhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ  niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ, sammāsaṅkappaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… sammāvācaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… sammākammantaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… sammāājīvaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… sammāvāyāmaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… sammāsatiṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… sammāsamādhiṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ|

suttantabhājanīyaṃ|

2. abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ

490. aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvācā, sammākammantau, sammāājīvau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi|

491. tattha  katamau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ  jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau hauti – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

492. tattha  katamā sammādiṭṭhi? yā pañ‍ñā pajānanā…pai॰… amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sammādiṭṭhi’’|

tattha katamau sammāsaṅkappau? yau tak‍kau vitak‍kau saṅkappau appanā byappanā caitasau abhiniraupanā sammāsaṅkappau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sammāsaṅkappau’’|

tattha katamā sammāvācā? yā catūhi vacīduc‍caritaihi ārati virati paṭivirati vairamaṇī akiriyā akaraṇaṃ anajjhāpatti vailāanatik‍kamau saitughātau sammāvācā maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sammāvācā’’|

tattha katamau sammākammantau? yā tīhi kāyaduc‍caritaihi ārati virati paṭivirati vairamaṇī  akiriyā akaraṇaṃ anajjhāpatti vailāanatik‍kamau saitughātau sammākammantau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sammākammantau’’|

tattha katamau sammāājīvau? yā micchāājīvā ārati virati paṭivirati vairamaṇī akiriyā akaraṇaṃ anajjhāpatti vailāanatik‍kamau saitughātau sammāājīvau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sammāājīvau’’|

tattha katamau sammāvāyāmau? yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau…pai॰… sammāvāyāmau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ  vuc‍cati ‘‘sammāvāyāmau’’|

tattha katamā sammāsati? yā sati anussati…pai॰… sammāsati satisambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sammāsati’’|

tattha  katamau sammāsamādhi? yā cittassa ṭhiti…pai॰… sammāsamādhi samādhisambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sammāsamādhi’’| ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau’’| avasaisā dhammā aṭṭhaṅgikaina maggaina sampayuttā|

493. pañ‍caṅgikau maggau – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi|

494. tattha  katamau pañ‍caṅgikau maggau? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍caṅgikau maggau hauti – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi|

495. tattha  katamā sammādiṭṭhi? yā pañ‍ñā pajānanā…pai॰… amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sammādiṭṭhi’’|

tattha katamau sammāsaṅkappau? yau tak‍kau vitak‍kau…pai॰… sammāsaṅkappau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sammāsaṅkappau’’|

tattha katamau  sammāvāyāmau? yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau…pai॰… sammāvāyāmau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sammāvāyāmau’’|

tattha katamā sammāsati? yā sati anussati…pai॰… sammāsati satisambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sammāsati’’|

tattha katamau sammāsamādhi? yā cittassa ṭhiti…pai॰… sammāsamādhi samādhisambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sammāsamādhi’’| ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘pañ‍caṅgikau maggau’’| avasaisā dhammā pañ‍caṅgikaina maggaina sampayuttā|

496. pañ‍caṅgikau maggau – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi|

497. tattha  katamā sammādiṭṭhi? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñā pajānanā…pai॰… amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sammādiṭṭhi’’| avasaisā dhammā sammādiṭṭhiyā sampayuttā…pai॰… avasaisā dhammā sammāsaṅkappaina sampayuttā…pai॰… avasaisā dhammā sammāvāyāmaina sampayuttā…pai॰… avasaisā dhammā sammāsatiyā sampayuttā|

tattha  katamau sammāsamādhi? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya  paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai  cittassa ṭhiti…pai॰… sammāsamādhi samādhisambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sammāsamādhi’’| avasaisā dhammā sammāsamādhinā sampayuttā|

498. aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

499. tattha katamau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva laukuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa katattā bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ, tasmiṃ samayai aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau hauti – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau’’| avasaisā dhammā aṭṭhaṅgikaina maggaina sampayuttā|

500. pañ‍caṅgikau maggau – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi|

501. tattha katamau pañ‍caṅgikau maggau? idha  bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ  jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati  dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva laukuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa katattā bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ, tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍caṅgikau maggau hauti – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘pañ‍caṅgikau maggau’’| avasaisā dhammā pañ‍caṅgikaina maggaina sampayuttā|

502. pañ‍caṅgikau maggau – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi|

503. tattha  katamā sammādiṭṭhi? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva laukuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa katattā bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍ñā pajānanā…pai॰… amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sammādiṭṭhi’’| avasaisā dhammā sammādiṭṭhiyā sampayuttā…pai॰… avasaisā dhammā sammāsaṅkappaina  sampayuttā…pai॰… avasaisā dhammā sammāvāyāmaina sampayuttā…pai॰… avasaisā dhammā sammāsatiyā sampayuttā|

tattha katamau sammāsamādhi? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau  hauti| imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva laukuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa katattā bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhārau avikkhaipau avisāhaṭamānasatā  samathau samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi samādhisambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sammāsamādhi’’| avasaisā dhammā sammāsamādhinā sampayuttā|

abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ|

3. pañhāpucchakaṃ

504. ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvācā, sammākammantau, sammāājīvau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi|

505. aṭṭhan‍naṃ  maggaṅgānaṃ kati kusalā, kati akusalā, kati abyākatā…pai॰… kati saraṇā, kati araṇā?

1. tikaṃ

506. siyā kusalā, siyā abyākatā| sammāsaṅkappau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau; satta maggaṅgā siyā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā| siyā vipākā, siyā vipākadhammadhammā| anupādin‍naanupādāniyā| asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikā| sammāsaṅkappau avitak‍kavicāramattau; satta maggaṅgā siyā savitak‍kasavicārā, siyā avitak‍kavicāramattā, siyā avitak‍kaavicārā| sammāsaṅkappau pītisahagatau, sukhasahagatau, na upaikkhāsahagatau; satta maggaṅgā siyā pītisahagatā, siyā sukhasahagatā, siyā upaikkhāsahagatā| naiva dassanaina na bhāvanāya pahātabbā| naiva dassanaina na bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā| siyā apacayagāminau, siyā naivācayagāmināpacayagāminau| siyā saikkhā, siyā asaikkhā| appamāṇā| appamāṇārammaṇā| paṇītā| siyā sammattaniyatā, siyā aniyatā| na maggārammaṇā, siyā maggahaitukā, siyā maggādhipatinau; siyā na vattabbā maggahaitukātipi, maggādhipatinautipi| siyā uppan‍nā, siyā anuppan‍nā, siyā uppādinau| siyā atītā, siyā anāgatā, siyā  pac‍cuppan‍nā| na vattabbā atītārammaṇātipi, anāgatārammaṇātipi, pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇātipi| siyā ajjhattā, siyā bahiddhā, siyā ajjhattabahiddhā| bahiddhārammaṇā| anidassanaappaṭighā|

2. dukaṃ

507. sammādiṭṭhi  haitu, satta maggaṅgā na haitū| sahaitukā| haitusampayuttā| sammādiṭṭhi haitu caiva sahaitukā ca, satta maggaṅgā na vattabbā haitū caiva sahaitukā cāti, sahaitukā caiva na ca haitū| sammādiṭṭhi haitu caiva haitusampayuttā ca, satta maggaṅgā  na vattabbā haitū caiva haitusampayuttā cāti, haitusampayuttā caiva na ca haitū| satta maggaṅgā na haitū sahaitukā, sammādiṭṭhi na vattabbā na haitu sahaitukātipi, na haitu ahaitukātipi|

sappac‍cayā| saṅkhatā| anidassanā| appaṭighā| arūpā| laukuttarā| kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyā, kainaci na viñ‍ñaiyyā|

nau āsavā| anāsavā| āsavavippayuttā| na vattabbā āsavā caiva sāsavā cātipi, sāsavā caiva nau ca āsavātipi| na vattabbā āsavā caiva āsavasampayuttā cātipi, āsavasampayuttā caiva nau ca āsavātipi| āsavavippayuttā| anāsavā|

nau saṃyaujanā…pai॰… nau ganthā…pai॰… nau aughā…pai॰… nau yaugā…pai॰… nau nīvaraṇā…pai॰… nau parāmāsā…pai॰… sārammaṇā| nau cittā| caitasikā| cittasampayuttā| cittasaṃsaṭṭhā| cittasamuṭṭhānā| cittasahabhunau| cittānuparivattinau| cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānā| cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhunau| cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattinau| bāhirā| nau upādā| anupādin‍nā|

nau upādānā…pai॰… nau kilaisā…pai॰… na dassanaina pahātabbā| na bhāvanāya pahātabbā| na dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā| na bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā| sammāsaṅkappau avitak‍kau, satta maggaṅgā siyā savitak‍kā, siyā avitak‍kā| sammāsaṅkappau savicārau, satta maggaṅgā siyā savicārā, siyā avicārā| sammāsaṅkappau sappītikau, satta maggaṅgā siyā  sappītikā, siyā appītikā| sammāsaṅkappau pītisahagatau, satta maggaṅgā siyā pītisahagatā, siyā na pītisahagatā| sammāsaṅkappau sukhasahagatau, satta maggaṅgā siyā sukhasahagatā, siyā na sukhasahagatā| sammāsaṅkappau na upaikkhāsahagatau, satta maggaṅgā siyā upaikkhāsahagatā, siyā na upaikkhāsahagatā| na  kāmāvacarā| na rūpāvacarā| na arūpāvacarā| apariyāpan‍nā| siyā niyyānikā, siyā aniyyānikā| siyā niyatā, siyā aniyatā| anuttarā| araṇāti|

pañhāpucchakaṃ|

maggaṅgavibhaṅgau niṭṭhitau|

12. jhānavibhaṅgau

1. suttantabhājanīyaṃ

508. idha  bhikkhu pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati, ācāragaucarasampan‍nau, aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu indriyaisu guttadvārau bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū pubbarattāpararattaṃ jāgariyānuyaugamanuyuttau sātac‍caṃ naipak‍kaṃ baudhipakkhikānaṃ dhammānaṃ bhāvanānuyaugamanuyuttau| sau abhik‍kantai paṭik‍kantai sampajānakārī hauti, ālaukitai vilaukitai sampajānakārī hauti, samiñ‍jitai sammiñ‍jitai (sī॰ syā॰) pasāritai sampajānakārī hauti, saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇai sampajānakārī hauti, asitai pītai khāyitai sāyitai sampajānakārī hauti, uc‍cārapassāvakammai sampajānakārī  hauti, gatai ṭhitai nisin‍nai suttai jāgaritai bhāsitai tuṇhībhāvai sampajānakārī hauti| sau vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajati arañ‍ñaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ pabbataṃ kandaraṃ giriguhaṃ susānaṃ vanapatthaṃ abbhaukāsaṃ palālapuñ‍jaṃ appasaddaṃ appanigghausaṃ vijanavātaṃ manussarāhassaiyyakaṃ manussarāhasaiyyakaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) paṭisal‍lānasāruppaṃ| sau arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau abhijjhaṃ laukai pahāya vigatābhijjhaina caitasā viharati, abhijjhāya cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| byāpādapadausaṃ pahāya abyāpan‍nacittau viharati sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī, byāpādapadausā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| thinamiddhaṃ pahāya vigatathinamiddhau viharati ālaukasañ‍ñī satau sampajānau, thinamiddhā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ pahāya anuddhatau viharati ajjhattaṃ vūpasantacittau, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| vicikicchaṃ pahāya tiṇṇavicikicchau viharati akathaṃkathī kusalaisu dhammaisu, vicikicchāya cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| sau imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai vivic‍caiva kāmaihi  vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; vitak‍kavicārānaṃ  vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati satau ca sampajānau sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti  – ‘‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘‘anantau ākāsau’’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘‘natthi kiñ‍cī’’ti ākiñ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

mātikā

509. ‘‘idhā’’ti imissā diṭṭhiyā, imissā khantiyā, imissā ruciyā, imasmiṃ ādāyai, imasmiṃ dhammai, imasmiṃ vinayai, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai, imasmiṃ pāvacanai, imasmiṃ brahmacariyai, imasmiṃ satthusāsanai| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘idhā’’ti|

510. ‘‘bhikkhū’’ti samañ‍ñāya bhikkhu, paṭiñ‍ñāya bhikkhu, bhikkhatīti bhikkhu, bhikkhakauti bhikkhu, bhikkhācariyaṃ ajjhupagatauti bhikkhu, bhin‍napaṭadharauti bhikkhu, bhindati pāpakai akusalai dhammaiti bhikkhu, bhin‍nattā pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ bhikkhu, audhisau  kilaisānaṃ pahānā bhikkhu, anaudhisau kilaisānaṃ pahānā bhikkhu, saikkhau bhikkhu, asaikkhau bhikkhu, naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau  bhikkhu, aggau bhikkhu, bhadrau bhikkhu, maṇḍau bhikkhu, sārau bhikkhu, samaggaina saṅghaina ñatticatutthaina kammaina akuppaina ṭhānārahaina upasampan‍nau bhikkhu|

511. ‘‘pātimaukkha’’nti sīlaṃ patiṭṭhā ādi caraṇaṃ saṃyamau saṃvarau maukkhaṃ pāmaukkhaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā| saṃvarauti| kāyikau  avītik‍kamau, vācasikau avītik‍kamau, kāyikavācasikau avītik‍kamau| saṃvutauti| iminā pātimaukkhasaṃvaraina upaitau hauti samupaitau upāgatau samupāgatau upapan‍nau sampan‍nau saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau’’ti|

512. ‘‘viharatī’’ti iriyati vattati pālaiti yapaiti yāpaiti carati viharati| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘viharatī’’ti|

513. ‘‘ācāragaucarasampan‍nau’’ti atthi ācārau, atthi anācārau|

tattha katamau anācārau? kāyikau vītik‍kamau, vācasikau vītik‍kamau, kāyikavācasikau vītik‍kamau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘anācārau’’| sabbampi dussīlyaṃ anācārau| idhaikac‍cau vaiḷudānaina vā pattadānaina vā pupphadānaina vā phaladānaina vā sinānadānaina vā dantakaṭṭhadānaina vā cāṭukamyatāya vā muggasūpyatāya muggasuppatāya (sī॰) vā pāribhaṭayatāya vā jaṅghapaisanikaina vā añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina vā buddhapaṭikuṭṭhaina micchāājīvaina jīvikaṃ kappaiti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘anācārau’’|

tattha katamau ācārau? kāyikau avītik‍kamau, vācasikau avītik‍kamau, kāyikavācasikau avītik‍kamau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘ācārau’’| sabbaupi sīlasaṃvarau ācārau| idhaikac‍cau na vaiḷudānaina na pattadānaina na pupphadānaina na phaladānaina na sinānadānaina na dantakaṭṭhadānaina na cāṭukamyatāya na muggasūpyatāya na pāribhaṭayatāya na jaṅghapaisanikaina na añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina buddhapaṭikuṭṭhaina micchāājīvaina jīvikaṃ kappaiti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘ācārau’’|

514. ‘‘gaucarau’’ti atthi gaucarau, atthi agaucarau|

tattha katamau agaucarau? idhaikac‍cau  vaisiyāgaucarau vā hauti vidhavāgaucarau vā thul‍lakumārigaucarau vā paṇḍakagaucarau vā bhikkhunigaucarau vā pānāgāragaucarau vā, saṃsaṭṭhau viharati rājūhi rājamahāmattaihi titthiyaihi titthiyasāvakaihi ananulaumikaina saṃsaggaina; yāni vā  pana tāni kulāni assaddhāni appasan‍nāni anaupānabhūtāni ak‍kausakaparibhāsakāni anatthakāmāni ahitakāmāni aphāsukakāmāni ayaugakkhaimakāmāni bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ upāsakānaṃ upāsikānaṃ, tathārūpāni kulāni saivati bhajati payirupāsati – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘agaucarau’’|

tattha katamau gaucarau? idhaikac‍cau na vaisiyāgaucarau hauti na vidhavāgaucarau na thul‍lakumārigaucarau na paṇḍakagaucarau na bhikkhunigaucarau na pānāgāragaucarau, asaṃsaṭṭhau viharati rājūhi rājamahāmattaihi titthiyaihi titthiyasāvakaihi ananulaumikaina saṃsaggaina; yāni vā pana tāni kulāni saddhāni pasan‍nāni aupānabhūtāni kāsāvapaj‍jautāni isivātapaṭivātāni atthakāmāni hitakāmāni phāsukakāmāni yaugakkhaimakāmāni bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ upāsakānaṃ upāsikānaṃ, tathārūpāni kulāni saivati bhajati payirupāsati – ayaṃ vuc‍cati  ‘‘gaucarau’’| iti iminā ca ācāraina iminā ca gaucaraina upaitau hauti…pai॰… saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘ācāragaucarasampan‍nau’’ti|

515. ‘‘aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī’’ti tattha katamai aṇumattā vaj‍jā? yāni tāni vaj‍jāni appamattakāni auramattakāni lahusāni lahusammatāni saṃyamakaraṇīyāni saṃvarakaraṇīyāni cittuppādakaraṇīyāni manasikārapaṭibaddhāni – imai vuc‍canti ‘‘aṇumattā vaj‍jā’’| iti imaisu aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu vaj‍jadassāvī ca hauti bhayadassāvī ca ādīnavadassāvī ca nissaraṇadassāvī ca| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī’’ti|

516. ‘‘samādāya  sikkhati sikkhāpadaisū’’ti tattha katamā sikkhā? catassau sikkhā – bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhusikkhā, bhikkhunīnaṃ bhikkhunisikkhā, upāsakānaṃ upāsakasikkhā, upāsikānaṃ upāsikasikkhā| imā vuc‍canti ‘‘sikkhāyau’’| iti imāsu sikkhāsu sabbaina sabbaṃ sabbathā sabbaṃ asaisaṃ nissaisaṃ samādāya vattati| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisū’’ti|

517. ‘‘indriyaisu guttadvārau’’ti atthi indriyaisu guttadvāratā, atthi aguttadvāratā|

tattha  katamā indriyaisu aguttadvāratā? idhaikac‍cau cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā nimittaggāhī hauti anubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya na paṭipaj‍jati, na rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ, cakkhundriyai na saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā…pai॰… jivhāya  rasaṃ sāyitvā…pai॰… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā…pai॰… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya nimittaggāhī hauti anubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya na paṭipaj‍jati, na rakkhati manindriyaṃ, manindriyai na saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| yā imaisaṃ chan‍naṃ indriyānaṃ agutti agaupanā anārakkhau asaṃvarau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘indriyaisu aguttadvāratā’’|

tattha katamā indriyaisu guttadvāratā? idhaikac‍cau cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ, cakkhundriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā…pai॰… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā…pai॰… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā…pai॰… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| ñatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati manindriyaṃ, manindriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| yā imaisaṃ chan‍naṃ indriyānaṃ gutti gaupanā ārakkhau saṃvarau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘indriyaisu  guttadvāratā’’| imāya indriyaisu guttadvāratāya upaitau hauti samupaitau…pai॰… saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘indriyaisu guttadvārau’’ti|

518. ‘‘bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū’’ti atthi bhaujanai mattañ‍ñutā, atthi bhaujanai amattañ‍ñutā|

tattha katamā bhaujanai amattañ‍ñutā? idhaikac‍cau appaṭisaṅkhā ayaunisau āhāraṃ āhāraiti davāya madāya maṇḍanāya vibhūsanāya| yā tattha asantuṭṭhitā amattañ‍ñutā appaṭisaṅkhā bhaujanai – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘bhaujanai amattañ‍ñutā’’ti|

tattha  katamā bhaujanai mattañ‍ñutā? idhaikac‍cau paṭisaṅkhā yaunisau āhāraṃ āhāraiti – ‘‘naiva davāya na madāya na maṇḍanāya na vibhūsanāya, yāvadaiva imassa kāyassa ṭhitiyā yāpanāya vihiṃsūparatiyā brahmacariyānuggahāya, iti purāṇañ‍ca vaidanaṃ paṭihaṅkhāmi navañ‍ca vaidanaṃ na uppādaissāmi yātrā ca mai bhavissati anavaj‍jatā ca phāsu vihārau cā’’ti| yā tattha santuṭṭhitā mattañ‍ñutā paṭisaṅkhā bhaujanai – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘bhaujanai mattañ‍ñutā’’| imāya bhaujanai mattañ‍ñutāya upaitau hauti…pai॰… saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘bhaujanai mattañ‍ñū’’ti|

519. kathañ‍ca bhikkhu pubbarattāpararattaṃ jāgariyānuyaugamanuyuttau hauti? idha bhikkhu divasaṃ caṅkamaina nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi cittaṃ parisaudhaiti, rattiyā paṭhamayāmaṃ caṅkamaina nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi cittaṃ parisaudhaiti, rattiyā majjhimayāmaṃ dakkhiṇaina passaina sīhasaiyyaṃ kappaiti pādai pādaṃ pādaina pādaṃ (syā॰) ac‍cādhāya satau sampajānau uṭṭhānasañ‍ñaṃ manasikaritvā, rattiyā pacchimayāmaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya caṅkamaina nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| aivaṃ bhikkhu  pubbarattāpararattaṃ jāgariyānuyaugamanuyuttau|

520. ‘‘sātac‍ca’’nti| yau caitasikau vīriyārambhau…pai॰… sammāvāyāmau|

521. ‘‘naipak‍ka’’nti| yā pañ‍ñā pajānanā…pai॰… amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi|

522. ‘‘baudhipakkhikānaṃ dhammānaṃ bhāvanānuyaugamanuyuttau’’ti| tattha katamai baudhipakkhikā dhammā? satta baujjhaṅgā – satisambaujjhaṅgau, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau, vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau, pītisambaujjhaṅgau, passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau, samādhisambaujjhaṅgau, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau| imai vuc‍canti ‘‘baudhipakkhikā dhammā’’| iti tai baudhipakkhikai dhammai āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘baudhipakkhikānaṃ dhammānaṃ bhāvanānuyaugamanuyuttau’’ti|

523. kathañ‍ca  bhikkhu abhik‍kantai paṭik‍kantai sampajānakārī hauti, ālaukitai vilaukitai sampajānakārī hauti, samiñ‍jitai pasāritai sampajānakārī hauti, saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇai sampajānakārī hauti, asitai pītai khāyitai sāyitai sampajānakārī hauti, uc‍cārapassāvakammai sampajānakārī hauti; gatai ṭhitai nisin‍nai suttai jāgaritai bhāsitai tuṇhībhāvai sampajānakārī hauti? idha bhikkhu satau sampajānau abhik‍kamati, satau sampajānau paṭik‍kamati, satau sampajānau ālaukaiti, satau sampajānau vilaukaiti, satau sampajānau samiñ‍jaiti, satau sampajānau pasāraiti, satau sampajānakārī hauti, saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇai  satau sampajānakārī hauti, asitai pītai khāyitai sāyitai satau sampajānakārī hauti, uc‍cārapassāvakammai satau sampajānakārī hauti, gatai ṭhitai nisin‍nai suttai jāgaritai bhāsitai tuṇhībhāvai satau sampajānakārī hautīti|

524. tattha katamā sati? yā sati anussati paṭissati sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā apilāpanatā asammusanatā sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sati’’|

525. ‘‘sampajānau’’ti tattha katamaṃ sampajañ‍ñaṃ? yā pañ‍ñā pajānanā vicayau pavicayau dhammavicayau sal‍lakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā pac‍cupalakkhaṇā paṇḍic‍caṃ kausal‍laṃ naipuñ‍ñaṃ vaibhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrīmaidhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajañ‍ñaṃ pataudau pañ‍ñā pañ‍ñindriyaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ pañ‍ñāsatthaṃ pañ‍ñāpāsādau pañ‍ñāālaukau pañ‍ñāaubhāsau pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau pañ‍ñāratanaṃ amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sampajañ‍ñaṃ’’| iti imāya ca satiyā iminā ca sampajañ‍ñaina upaitau hauti…pai॰… saman‍nāgatau| aivaṃ bhikkhu satau sampajānau abhik‍kamati, satau sampajānau paṭik‍kamati, satau sampajānau ālaukaiti, satau sampajānau vilaukaiti, satau sampajānau samiñ‍jaiti, satau sampajānau pasāraiti, satau sampajānakārī hauti, saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇai satau sampajānakārī  hauti, asitai pītai khāyitai sāyitai satau sampajānakārī hauti, uc‍cārapassāvakammai satau sampajānakārī  hauti, gatai ṭhitai nisin‍nai suttai jāgaritai bhāsitai tuṇhībhāvai sampajānakārī hauti|

526. ‘‘vivitta’’nti  santikai caipi saināsanaṃ hauti, tañ‍ca anākiṇṇaṃ gahaṭṭhaihi pabbajitaihi| taina taṃ vivittaṃ| dūrai caipi saināsanaṃ hauti, tañ‍ca anākiṇṇaṃ gahaṭṭhaihi pabbajitaihi| taina taṃ vivittaṃ|

527. ‘‘saināsana’’nti mañ‍caupi saināsanaṃ, pīṭhampi saināsanaṃ, bhisipi saināsanaṃ, bibbauhanampi  bimbauhanampi (sī॰ syā॰) saināsanaṃ, vihāraupi saināsanaṃ, aḍḍhayaugaupi saināsanaṃ, pāsādaupi saināsanaṃ, aṭṭaupi saināsanaṃ, māḷaupi saināsanaṃ, laiṇampi saināsanaṃ, guhāpi saināsanaṃ, rukkhamūlampi saināsanaṃ, vaiḷugumbaupi saināsanaṃ| yattha vā pana bhikkhū paṭik‍kamanti sabbamaitaṃ saināsanaṃ|

528. ‘‘vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajatī’’ti imaṃ vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajati sambhajati saivati nisaivati saṃsaivati| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajatī’’ti|

529. ‘‘arañ‍ña’’nti nikkhamitvā bahi indakhīlā sabbamaitaṃ arañ‍ñaṃ|

530. ‘‘rukkhamūla’’nti rukkhamūlaṃyaiva rukkhamūlaṃ| pabbatauyaiva pabbatau| kandarāyaiva kandarā| giriguhāyaiva giriguhā| susānaṃyaiva susānaṃ| abbhaukāsauyaiva abbhaukāsau| palālapuñ‍jauyaiva palālapuñ‍jau|

531. ‘‘vanapattha’’nti dūrānamaitaṃ saināsanānaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘‘vanapattha’’nti vanasaṇḍānamaitaṃ saināsanānaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘‘vanapattha’’nti bhīsanakānamaitaṃ bhiṃsanakānamaitaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) saināsanānaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘‘vanapattha’’nti salaumahaṃsānamaitaṃ saināsanānaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘‘vanapattha’’nti pariyantānamaitaṃ saināsanānaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘‘vanapattha’’nti na manussūpacārānamaitaṃ saināsanānaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘‘vanapattha’’nti durabhisambhavānamaitaṃ  saināsanānaṃ adhivacanaṃ|

532. ‘‘appasadda’’nti  santikai caipi saināsanaṃ hauti, tañ‍ca anākiṇṇaṃ gahaṭṭhaihi pabbajitaihi| taina taṃ appasaddaṃ| dūrai caipi saināsanaṃ hauti, tañ‍ca anākiṇṇaṃ gahaṭṭhaihi pabbajitaihi| taina taṃ appasaddaṃ|

533. ‘‘appanigghausa’’nti yadaiva taṃ appasaddaṃ tadaiva taṃ appanigghausaṃ| yadaiva taṃ appanigghausaṃ tadaiva  taṃ vijanavātaṃ| yadaiva taṃ vijanavātaṃ tadaiva taṃ manussarāhassaiyyakaṃ| yadaiva taṃ manussarāhassaiyyakaṃ tadaiva taṃ paṭisal‍lānasāruppaṃ|

534. ‘‘arañ‍ñagatau  vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā’’ti arañ‍ñagatau vā hauti rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā|

535. ‘‘nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā’’ti nisin‍nau hauti pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā|

536. ‘‘ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāyā’’ti ujukau hauti kāyau ṭhitau paṇihitau|

537. ‘‘parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā’’ti tattha katamā sati? yā sati anussati paṭissati…pai॰… sammāsati – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sati’’| ayaṃ sati upaṭṭhitā hauti supaṭṭhitā nāsikaggai vā mukhanimittai vā| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā’’ti|

538. ‘‘abhijjhaṃ laukai pahāyā’’ti tattha katamā abhijjhā? yau rāgau  sārāgau…pai॰… cittassa sārāgau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘abhijjhā’’|

tattha katamau laukau? pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā laukau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘laukau’’| ayaṃ abhijjhā imamhi laukai santā hauti samitā vūpasantā atthaṅgatā abbhatthaṅgatā appitā byappitā sausitā visausitā byantīkatā| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘abhijjhaṃ laukai pahāyā’’ti|

539. ‘‘vigatābhijjhaina caitasā’’ti tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘cittaṃ’’| idaṃ cittaṃ vigatābhijjhaṃ hauti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘vigatābhijjhaina caitasā’’ti|

540. ‘‘viharatī’’ti  iriyati vattati pālaiti yapaiti yāpaiti carati viharati| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘viharatī’’ti|

541. ‘‘abhijjhāya cittaṃ parisaudhaitī’’ti tattha katamā abhijjhā? yau rāgau sārāgau…pai॰… cittassa sārāgau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘abhijjhā’’|

tattha  katamaṃ cittaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘cittaṃ’’| idaṃ cittaṃ imāya abhijjhāya saudhaiti visaudhaiti parisaudhaiti maucaiti vimaucaiti parimaucaiti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘abhijjhāya cittaṃ parisaudhaitī’’ti|

542. ‘‘byāpādapadausaṃ  pahāyā’’ti atthi byāpādau, atthi padausau|

tattha katamau byāpādau? yau cittassa āghātau paṭighātau paṭighaṃ paṭiviraudhau kaupau pakaupau sampakaupau dausau padausau sampadausau cittassa byāpatti manaupadausau kaudhau kujjhanā kujjhitattaṃ dausau  dussanā dussitattaṃ dūsanā dūsitattaṃ (syā॰) byāpatti byāpaj‍janā byāpaj‍jitattaṃ viraudhau paṭiviraudhau caṇḍik‍kaṃ asuraupau anattamanatā cittassa – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘byāpādau’’|

tattha katamau padausau? yau byāpādau sau padausau, yau padausau sau byāpādau| iti ayañ‍ca byāpādau ayañ‍ca padausau santā haunti samitā vūpasantā atthaṅgatā abbhatthaṅgatā appitā byappitā sausitā visausitā byantīkatā| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘byāpādapadausaṃ pahāyā’’ti|

543. ‘‘abyāpan‍nacittau’’ti tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘cittaṃ’’| idaṃ cittaṃ abyāpan‍naṃ hauti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘abyāpan‍nacittau’’ti|

544. ‘‘viharatī’’ti…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘viharatī’’ti|

545. ‘‘byāpādapadausā  cittaṃ parisaudhaitī’’ti| atthi byāpādau atthi padausau|

tattha katamau byāpādau? yau cittassa āghātau…pai॰… caṇḍik‍kaṃ asuraupau anattamanatā cittassa – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘byāpādau’’|

tattha katamau padausau? yau byāpādau sau padausau, yau padausau sau byāpādau|

tattha  katamaṃ cittaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ  vuc‍cati ‘‘cittaṃ’’| idaṃ cittaṃ imamhā byāpādapadausā saudhaiti visaudhaiti parisaudhaiti maucaiti vimaucaiti parimaucaiti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘byāpādapadausā cittaṃ parisaudhaitī’’ti|

546. ‘‘thinamiddhaṃ pahāyā’’ti atthi thinaṃ thīnaṃ (sī॰ syā॰), atthi middhaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ thinaṃ? yā cittassa akalyatā akammañ‍ñatā aulīyanā sal‍līyanā līnaṃ līyanā līyitattaṃ thinaṃ thiyanā thiyitattaṃ thīyanā thīyitattaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) cittassa – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘thinaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ middhaṃ? yā  kāyassa akalyatā akammañ‍ñatā aunāhau pariyaunāhau antausamauraudhau middhaṃ suppaṃ pacalāyikā suppaṃ suppanā suppitattaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘middhaṃ’’| iti idañ‍ca thinaṃ idañ‍ca middhaṃ santā haunti samitā vūpasantā atthaṅgatā abbhatthaṅgatā appitā byappitā sausitā visausitā byantīkatā| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘thinamiddhaṃ pahāyā’’ti|

547. ‘‘vigatathinamiddhau’’ti| tassa thinamiddhassa cattattā vantattā muttattā pahīnattā paṭinissaṭṭhattā pahīnapaṭinissaṭṭhattā| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘vigatathinamiddhau’’ti|

548. ‘‘viharatī’’ti…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘viharatī’’ti|

549. ‘‘ālaukasañ‍ñī’’ti| tattha katamā sañ‍ñā? yā sañ‍ñā sañ‍jānanā sañ‍jānitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sañ‍ñā’’| ayaṃ sañ‍ñā ālaukā hauti  vivaṭā parisuddhā pariyaudātā| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘ālaukasañ‍ñī’’ti|

550. ‘‘satau sampajānau’’ti| tattha katamā sati? yā sati anussati  …pai॰… sammāsati – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sati’’|

tattha katamaṃ sampajañ‍ñaṃ? yā pañ‍ñā pajānanā…pai॰… amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi  – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sampajañ‍ñaṃ’’| iti imāya ca satiyā iminā ca sampajañ‍ñaina upaitau hauti…pai॰… saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘satau sampajānau’’ti|

551. ‘‘thinamiddhā cittaṃ parisaudhaitī’’ti| atthi thinaṃ, atthi middhaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ thinaṃ…pai॰… idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘thinaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ middhaṃ…pai॰… idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘middhaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ…pai॰… idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘cittaṃ’’| idaṃ cittaṃ imamhā thinamiddhā saudhaiti visaudhaiti parisaudhaiti maucaiti vimaucaiti parimaucaiti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘thinamiddhā cittaṃ parisaudhaitī’’ti|

552. ‘‘uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ pahāyā’’ti atthi uddhac‍caṃ, atthi kuk‍kuc‍caṃ|

tattha  katamaṃ uddhac‍caṃ? yaṃ cittassa uddhac‍caṃ avūpasamau caitasau vikkhaipau bhantattaṃ cittassa – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘uddhac‍caṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ? akappiyai kappiyasañ‍ñitā, kappiyai akappiyasañ‍ñitā, avaj‍jai vaj‍jasañ‍ñitā, vaj‍jai avaj‍jasañ‍ñitā, yaṃ aivarūpaṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ kuk‍kuc‍cāyanā kuk‍kuc‍cāyitattaṃ caitasau vippaṭisārau manauvilaikhā – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘kuk‍kuc‍caṃ’’| iti idañ‍ca uddhac‍caṃ idañ‍ca kuk‍kuc‍caṃ  santā haunti samitā vūpasantā atthaṅgatā abbhatthaṅgatā appitā byappitā sausitā visausitā byantīkatā| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ pahāyā’’ti|

553. ‘‘anuddhatau’’ti tassa uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassa cattattā vantattā muttattā pahīnattā paṭinissaṭṭhattā pahīnapaṭinissaṭṭhattā| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘anuddhatau’’ti|

554. ‘‘viharatī’’ti  …pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘viharatī’’ti|

555. ‘‘vūpasantacittau’’ti| tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘cittaṃ’’| idaṃ cittaṃ ajjhattaṃ santaṃ hauti samitaṃ vūpasantaṃ| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘ajjhattaṃ vūpasantacittau’’ti|

556. ‘‘uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cā cittaṃ parisaudhaitī’’ti atthi uddhac‍caṃ, atthi kuk‍kuc‍caṃ|

tattha katamaṃ uddhac‍caṃ? yaṃ cittassa uddhac‍caṃ avūpasamau caitasau vikkhaipau bhantattaṃ cittassa – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘uddhac‍caṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ…pai॰… idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘kuk‍kuc‍caṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ…pai॰… idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘cittaṃ’’| idaṃ cittaṃ imamhā uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cā saudhaiti visaudhaiti parisaudhaiti maucaiti vimaucaiti parimaucaiti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cā cittaṃ parisaudhaitī’’ti|

557. ‘‘vicikicchaṃ pahāyā’’ti, tattha katamā vicikicchā? yā kaṅkhā kaṅkhāyanā  kaṅkhāyitattaṃ vimati vicikicchā dvaiḷhakaṃ dvidhāpathau saṃsayau anaikaṃsaggāhau āsappanā parisappanā apariyaugāhaṇā chambhitattaṃ cittassa manauvilaikhau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vicikicchā’’| ayaṃ vicikicchā  santā hauti samitā vūpasantā atthaṅgatā abbhatthaṅgatā appitā byappitā sausitā visausitā byantīkatā| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘vicikicchaṃ pahāyā’’ti|

558. ‘‘tiṇṇavicikicchau’’ti, imaṃ vicikicchaṃ tiṇṇau hauti uttiṇṇau nittiṇṇau pāraṅgatau pāramanuppattau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘tiṇṇavicikicchau’’ti|

559. ‘‘akathaṃkathī kusalaisu dhammaisū’’ti imāya vicikicchāya kusalaisu dhammaisu na kaṅkhati  na vicikicchati akathaṃkathī hauti nik‍kathaṃkathī vikathaṃkathau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘akathaṃkathī kusalaisu dhammaisū’’ti|

560. ‘‘vicikicchāya cittaṃ parisaudhaitī’’ti, tattha katamā vicikicchā? yā kaṅkhā kaṅkhāyanā kaṅkhāyitattaṃ chambhitattaṃ cittassa manauvilaikhau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vicikicchā’’|

tattha  katamaṃ cittaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘cittaṃ’’| idaṃ cittaṃ imāya vicikicchāya saudhaiti visaudhaiti parisaudhaiti maucaiti vimaucaiti parimaucaiti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘vicikicchāya cittaṃ parisaudhaitī’’ti|

561. ‘‘imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāyā’’ti imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā santā haunti samitā vūpasantā atthaṅgatā abbhatthaṅgatā appitā byappitā sausitā visausitā byantīkatā| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāyā’’ti|

562. ‘‘caitasau upak‍kilaisai’’ti imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā cittassa upak‍kilaisā|

563. ‘‘pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai’’ti imaihi pañ‍cahi nīvaraṇaihi anuppan‍nā caiva pañ‍ñā na uppaj‍jati uppan‍nā ca pañ‍ñā nirujjhati| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai’’ti|

564. ‘‘vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihī’’ti tattha katamai kāmā? chandau kāmau, rāgau kāmau, chandarāgau kāmau, saṅkappau kāmau, rāgau kāmau, saṅkapparāgau kāmau – imai vuc‍canti ‘‘kāmā’’|

tattha katamai akusalā dhammā? kāmacchandau, byāpādau, thinaṃ, middhaṃ, uddhac‍caṃ, kuk‍kuc‍caṃ, vicikicchā – imai vuc‍canti ‘‘akusalā dhammā’’| iti  imaihi ca kāmaihi imaihi ca akusalaihi dhammaihi vivittau hauti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihī’’ti|

565. ‘‘savitak‍kaṃ  savicāra’’nti atthi vitak‍kau, atthi vicārau|

tattha katamau vitak‍kau? yau tak‍kau vitak‍kau saṅkappau appanā byappanā caitasau abhiniraupanā sammāsaṅkappau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vitak‍kau’’|

tattha katamau vicārau? yau cārau vicārau anuvicārau upavicārau cittassa  anusandhanatā anupaikkhanatā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati vicārau| iti  iminā ca vitak‍kaina iminā ca vicāraina upaitau hauti…pai॰… saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘savitak‍kaṃ savicāra’’nti|

566. ‘‘vivaikaja’’nti vitak‍kau, vicārau, pīti, sukhaṃ, cittassaikaggatā – tai imasmiṃ vivaikai jātā haunti sañ‍jātā nibbattā abhinibbattā pātubhūtā| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘vivaikaja’’nti|

567. ‘‘pītisukha’’nti atthi pīti, atthi sukhaṃ|

tattha katamā pīti? yā pīti pāmauj‍jaṃ āmaudanā pamaudanā hāsau pahāsau vitti audagyaṃ attamanatā cittassa – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘pīti’’|

tattha katamaṃ sukhaṃ? yaṃ caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sukhaṃ’’| idaṃ sukhaṃ imāya pītiyā sahagataṃ hauti sahajātaṃ saṃsaṭṭhaṃ sampayuttaṃ| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘pītisukha’’nti|

568. ‘‘paṭhama’’nti gaṇanānupubbatā gaṇanānupubbatau (syā॰) aivamuparipi paṭhamaṃ| idaṃ paṭhamaṃ samāpaj‍jatīti paṭhamaṃ|

569. ‘‘jhāna’’nti vitak‍kau, vicārau, pīti, sukhaṃ, cittassaikaggatā|

570. ‘‘upasampaj‍jā’’ti yau paṭhamassa jhānassa lābhau paṭilābhau patti sampatti phusanā sacchikiriyā upasampadā|

571. ‘‘viharatī’’ti  …pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘viharatī’’ti|

572. ‘‘vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā’’ti, atthi vitak‍kau, atthi vicārau|

tattha  katamau vitak‍kau? yau tak‍kau vitak‍kau…pai॰… sammāsaṅkappau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vitak‍kau’’|

tattha katamau vicārau? yau  cārau vicārau anuvicārau upavicārau cittassa anusandhanatā anupaikkhanatā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vicārau’’| iti ayañ‍ca vitak‍kau ayañ‍ca vicārau santā haunti samitā vūpasantā atthaṅgatā  abbhatthaṅgatā appitā byappitā sausitā visausitā byantīkatā| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā’’ti|

573. ‘‘ajjhatta’’nti yaṃ ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ|

574. ‘‘sampasādana’’nti yā saddhā saddahanā aukappanā abhippasādau|

575. ‘‘caitasau aikaudibhāva’’nti yā cittassa ṭhiti…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

576. ‘‘avitak‍kaṃ avicāra’’nti atthi vitak‍kau, atthi vicārau|

tattha katamau vitak‍kau? yau tak‍kau vitak‍kau…pai॰… sammāsaṅkappau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vitak‍kau’’|

tattha katamau vicārau? yau cārau anucārau vicārau anuvicārau upavicārau cittassa anusandhanatā anupaikkhanatā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘vicārau’’| iti ayañ‍ca vitak‍kau ayañ‍ca vicārau santā hauti samitā vūpasantā atthaṅgatā abbhatthaṅgatā appitā byappitā sausitā visausitā byantīkatā| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘avitak‍kaṃ avicāra’’nti|

577. ‘‘samādhija’’nti  sampasādau pītisukhaṃ – tai imasmiṃ samādhimhi jātā haunti sañ‍jātā nibbattā abhinibbattā pātubhūtā| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘samādhija’’nti|

578. ‘‘pītisukha’’nti  atthi pīti, atthi sukhaṃ|

tattha katamā pīti…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘pīti’’|

tattha katamaṃ sukhaṃ…pai॰… idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sukhaṃ’’| idaṃ sukhaṃ imāya pītiyā sahagataṃ hauti sahajātaṃ saṃsaṭṭhaṃ sampayuttaṃ| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘pītisukha’’nti|

579. ‘‘dutiya’’nti gaṇanānupubbatā dutiyaṃ| idaṃ dutiyaṃ samāpaj‍jatīti dutiyaṃ|

580. ‘‘jhāna’’nti sampasādau, pītisukhaṃ, cittassaikaggatā|

581. ‘‘upasampaj‍jā’’ti  yau dutiyassa jhānassa lābhau paṭilābhau patti sampatti phusanā sacchikiriyā upasampadā|

582. ‘‘viharatī’’ti  …pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘viharatī’’ti|

583. ‘‘pītiyā ca virāgā’’ti tattha katamā pīti? yā pīti pāmauj‍jaṃ āmaudanā pamaudanā hāsau pahāsau vitti audagyaṃ attamanatā cittassa – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘pīti’’| ayaṃ pīti santā hauti samitā vūpasantā atthaṅgatā abbhatthaṅgatā appitā byappitā sausitā visausitā byantīkatā| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘pītiyā ca virāgā’’ti|

584. ‘‘upaikkhakau’’ti tattha katamā upaikkhā? yā upaikkhā upaikkhanā ajjhupaikkhanā majjhattatā cittassa – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘upaikkhā’’| imāya upaikkhāya upaitau hauti…pai॰… saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘upaikkhakau’’ti|

585. ‘‘viharatī’’ti  …pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘viharatī’’ti|

586. ‘‘satau ca sampajānau’’ti tattha katamā sati? yā sati anussati…pai॰… sammāsati – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sati’’|

tattha katamaṃ sampajañ‍ñaṃ? yā pañ‍ñā pajānanā…pai॰… amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sampajañ‍ñaṃ’’| iti imāya ca satiyā iminā ca sampajañ‍ñaina upaitau hauti…pai॰… saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘satau ca sampajānau’’ti|

587. ‘‘sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaitī’’ti tattha katamaṃ sukhaṃ? yaṃ caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sukhaṃ’’|

tattha katamau kāyau? sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘kāyau’’| idaṃ sukhaṃ iminā kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaitī’’ti|

588. ‘‘yaṃ  taṃ ariyā ācikkhantī’’ti tattha katamai ariyā? ariyā vuc‍canti buddhā ca buddhasāvakā ca| tai imaṃ ācikkhanti daisainti pañ‍ñapainti pañ‍ñāpainti (sī॰ syā॰) paṭṭhapainti vivaranti vibhajanti uttāniṃ karaunti pakāsainti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhantī’’ti|

589. ‘‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’’ti tattha katamā upaikkhā? yā upaikkhā upaikkhanā ajjhupaikkhanā majjhattatā cittassa – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘upaikkhā’’|

tattha  katamā sati? yā sati anussati…pai॰… sammāsati  – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sati’’|

tattha katamaṃ sukhaṃ? yaṃ caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ  caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sukhaṃ’’| iti imāya ca upaikkhāya imāya ca satiyā iminā ca sukhaina saman‍nāgatau iriyati vattati pālaiti yapaiti yāpaiti carati viharati| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’’ti|

590. ‘‘tatiya’’nti gaṇanānupubbatā tatiyaṃ| idaṃ tatiyaṃ samāpaj‍jatīti tatiyaṃ|

591. ‘‘jhāna’’nti upaikkhā, sati, sampajañ‍ñaṃ, sukhaṃ, cittassaikaggatā|

592. ‘‘upasampaj‍jā’’ti yau tatiyassa jhānassa lābhau paṭilābhau patti sampatti phusanā sacchikiriyā upasampadā|

593. ‘‘viharatī’’ti…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘viharatī’’ti|

594. ‘‘sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā’’ti, atthi sukhaṃ, atthi dukkhaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ sukhaṃ? yaṃ kāyikaṃ sātaṃ kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ kāyasamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ kāyasamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sukhaṃ’’|

tattha  katamaṃ dukkhaṃ? yaṃ kāyikaṃ asātaṃ kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ kāyasamphassajaṃ asātaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidayitaṃ kāyasamphassajā asātā dukkhā vaidanā – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘dukkhaṃ’’| iti idañ‍ca sukhaṃ idañ‍ca dukkhaṃ santā haunti samitā vūpasantā atthaṅgatā abbhatthaṅgatā  appitā byappitā sausitā visausitā byantīkatā| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā’’ti|

595. ‘‘pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā’’ti atthi saumanassaṃ, atthi daumanassaṃ|

tattha  katamaṃ saumanassaṃ? yaṃ caitasikaṃ sātaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā sātā sukhā vaidanā – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘saumanassaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ daumanassaṃ? yaṃ caitasikaṃ asātaṃ caitasikaṃ dukkhaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ asātaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā asātā dukkhā vaidanā – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘daumanassaṃ’’| iti idañ‍ca saumanassaṃ idañ‍ca daumanassaṃ pubbaiva santā  haunti samitā vūpasantā atthaṅgatā abbhatthaṅgatā appitā byappitā sausitā visausitā byantīkatā| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā’’ti|

596. ‘‘adukkhamasukha’’nti yaṃ caitasikaṃ naiva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ caitausamphassajaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidayitaṃ caitausamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘adukkhamasukha’’nti|

597. ‘‘upaikkhāsatipārisuddhi’’nti, tattha katamā upaikkhā? yā upaikkhā upaikkhanā ajjhupaikkhanā majjhattatā cittassa – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘upaikkhā’’|

tattha katamā sati? yā sati anussati…pai॰… sammāsati – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sati’’| ayaṃ sati imāya upaikkhāya vivaṭā hauti parisuddhā pariyaudātā| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘upaikkhāsatipārisuddhi’’nti|

598. ‘‘catuttha’’nti  gaṇanānupubbatā catutthaṃ, idaṃ catutthaṃ samāpaj‍jatīti catutthaṃ|

599. ‘‘jhāna’’nti upaikkhā, sati, cittassaikaggatā|

600. ‘‘upasampaj‍jā’’ti  yau catutthassa jhānassa lābhau paṭilābhau patti sampatti phusanā sacchikiriyā upasampadā|

601. ‘‘viharatī’’ti…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘viharatī’’ti|

602. ‘‘sabbasau  rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā’’ti tattha katamā rūpasañ‍ñā? rūpāvacarasamāpattiṃ samāpan‍nassa vā upapan‍nassa vā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārissa vā sañ‍ñā sañ‍jānanā sañ‍jānitattaṃ – imā vuc‍canti ‘‘rūpasañ‍ñāyau’’| imā rūpasañ‍ñāyau atik‍kantau hauti vītik‍kantau samatik‍kantau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā’’ti|

603. ‘‘paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā’’ti tattha katamā paṭighasañ‍ñā? rūpasañ‍ñā saddasañ‍ñā…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbasañ‍ñā – imā vuc‍canti paṭighasañ‍ñāyau| imā paṭighasañ‍ñāyau santā haunti samitā vūpasantā atthaṅgatā abbhatthaṅgatā appitā byappitā sausitā visausitā byantīkatā| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā’’ti|

604. ‘‘nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā’’ti tattha katamā nānattasañ‍ñā? asamāpan‍nassa manaudhātu samaṅgissa vā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu  samaṅgissa vā sañ‍ñā sañ‍jānanā sañ‍jānitattaṃ – imā vuc‍canti ‘‘nānattasañ‍ñāyau’’| imā nānattasañ‍ñāyau na manasi karauti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā’’ti|

605. ‘‘anantau ākāsau’’ti, tattha katamau ākāsau? yau ākāsau ākāsagataṃ aghaṃ aghagataṃ vivarau vivaragataṃ asamphuṭṭhaṃ catūhi mahābhūtaihi – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘ākāsau’’| tasmiṃ ākāsai cittaṃ ṭhapaiti saṇṭhapaiti anantaṃ pharati| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘anantau ākāsau’’ti|
