606. ‘‘ākāsānañ‍cāyatana’’nti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samāpan‍nassa vā upapan‍nassa vā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārissa vā cittacaitasikā dhammā|

607. ‘‘upasampaj‍jā’’ti yau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanassa lābhau paṭilābhau patti sampatti phusanā sacchikiriyā upasampadā|

608. ‘‘viharatī’’ti…pai॰…  taina vuc‍cati ‘‘viharatī’’ti|

609. ‘‘sabbasau  ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kammā’’ti imaṃ ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ atik‍kantau hauti vītik‍kantau samatik‍kantau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kammā’’ti|

610. ‘‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’’nti taṃyaiva ākāsaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaina phuṭṭhaṃ manasi karauti anantaṃ pharati| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’’nti|

611. ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatana’’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samāpan‍nassa vā upapan‍nassa vā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārissa vā cittacaitasikā dhammā|

612. ‘‘upasampaj‍jā’’ti  yau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanassa lābhau paṭilābhau patti sampatti phusanā sacchikiriyā upasampadā|

613. ‘‘viharatī’’ti…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘viharatī’’ti|

614. ‘‘sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kammā’’ti imaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ atik‍kantau hauti vītik‍kantau samatik‍kantau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kammā’’ti|

615. ‘‘natthi kiñ‍cī’’ti taṃyaiva viñ‍ñāṇaṃ bhāvaiti vibhāvaiti antarabhāvaiti, ‘‘natthi kiñ‍cī’’ti passati| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘natthi kiñ‍cī’’ti|

616. ‘‘ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatana’’nti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpan‍nassa vā upapan‍nassa vā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārissa vā cittacaitasikā dhammā|

617. ‘‘upasampaj‍jā’’ti  yau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanassa lābhau paṭilābhau patti sampatti phusanā sacchikiriyā upasampadā|

618. ‘‘viharatī’’ti  …pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘viharatī’’ti|

619. ‘‘sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kammā’’ti imaṃ ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ atik‍kantau hauti vītik‍kantau samatik‍kantau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kammā’’ti|

‘‘naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñī’’ti  taṃyaiva ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ santatau manasi karauti saṅkhārāvasaisasamāpattiṃ bhāvaiti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñī’’ti ayaṃ pāṭhau mātikāyaṃ natthi, niddaisai pana sabbapautthakaisu dissati|

620. ‘‘naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatana’’nti  naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpan‍nassa vā upapan‍nassa vā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārissa vā cittacaitasikā dhammā|

621. ‘‘upasampaj‍jā’’ti yau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanassa lābhau paṭilābhau patti sampatti phusanā sacchikiriyā upasampadā|

622. ‘‘viharatī’’ti iriyati vattati pālaiti yapaiti yāpaiti carati viharati| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘viharatī’’ti|

suttantabhājanīyaṃ|

2. abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ

1. rūpāvacarakusalaṃ

623. cattāri  jhānāni – paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ, dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ|

624. tattha katamaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍caṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti – vitak‍kau, vicārau, pīti, sukhaṃ, cittassaikaggatā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā jhānasampayuttā|

tattha katamaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai tivaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti – pīti, sukhaṃ, cittassaikaggatā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā jhānasampayuttā|

tattha  katamaṃ tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai duvaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti – sukhaṃ, cittassaikaggatā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā jhānasampayuttā|

tattha katamaṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai duvaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti – upaikkhā, cittassaikaggatā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘catutthaṃ jhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā jhānasampayuttā|

catuk‍kaṃ|

625. idha  bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍caṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti – vitak‍kau, vicārau, pīti, sukhaṃ, cittassaikaggatā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā jhānasampayuttā|

idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi avitak‍kaṃ vicāramattaṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai caturaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti – vicārau, pīti, sukhaṃ, cittassaikaggatā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā jhānasampayuttā|

idha  bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai tivaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti – pīti, sukhaṃ, cittassaikaggatā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā jhānasampayuttā|

idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai duvaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti – sukhaṃ, cittassaikaggatā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘catutthaṃ jhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā jhānasampayuttā|

idha  bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… pañ‍camaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai duvaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti – upaikkhā, cittassaikaggatā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘pañ‍camaṃ jhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā jhānasampayuttā|

pañ‍cakaṃ|

2. arūpāvacarakusalaṃ

626. idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai arūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti  sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ  samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, tasmiṃ samayai duvaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti – upaikkhā, cittassaikaggatā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘catutthaṃ jhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā jhānasampayuttā|

3. laukuttarakusalaṃ

627. cattāri  jhānāni – paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ, dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ|

628. tattha katamaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍caṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti – vitak‍kau, vicārau, pīti, sukhaṃ, cittassaikaggatā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā jhānasampayuttā|

tattha katamaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai tivaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti – pīti, sukhaṃ, cittassaikaggatā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā jhānasampayuttā|

tattha  katamaṃ tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai duvaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti – sukhaṃ, cittassaikaggatā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā jhānasampayuttā|

tattha  katamaṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā sukhassa ca pahānā  …pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai duvaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti – upaikkhā, cittassaikaggatā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘catutthaṃ jhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā jhānasampayuttā|

catuk‍kaṃ|

629. idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya  bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍caṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti – vitak‍kau, vicārau, pīti, sukhaṃ, cittassaikaggatā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā jhānasampayuttā|

idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi avitak‍kaṃ vicāramattaṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai caturaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti vicārau pīti sukhaṃ cittassaikaggatā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā jhānasampayuttā|

idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vitak‍kavicārānaṃ  vūpasamā…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai tivaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti – pīti, sukhaṃ, cittassaikaggatā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā jhānasampayuttā|

idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰…  catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai duvaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti – sukhaṃ, cittassaikaggatā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘catutthaṃ jhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā jhānasampayuttā|

idha  bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… pañ‍camaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai duvaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti – upaikkhā, cittassaikaggatā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘pañ‍camaṃ jhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā jhānasampayuttā|

pañ‍cakaṃ|

4. rūpāvacaravipākā

630. cattāri jhānāni – paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ, dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ|

631. tattha katamaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja  viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva rūpāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai  pañ‍caṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti – vitak‍kau, vicārau, pīti, sukhaṃ, cittassaikaggatā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā jhānasampayuttā|

tattha katamaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva rūpāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍camaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai duvaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti – upaikkhā, cittassaikaggatā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘pañ‍camaṃ jhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā jhānasampayuttā…pai॰…|

5. arūpāvacaravipākā

632. idha  bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai arūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva arūpāvacarassa kusalassa  kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, tasmiṃ samayai duvaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti – upaikkhā, cittassaikaggatā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘catutthaṃ jhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā jhānasampayuttā|

6. laukuttaravipākā

633. cattāri jhānāni – paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ, dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ|

634. tattha katamaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau  hauti| imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva laukuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa katattā bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ, tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍caṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti – vitak‍kau, vicārau, pīti, sukhaṃ, cittassaikaggatā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā jhānasampayuttā|

tattha katamaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati  dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva laukuttarassa kusalassa jhānassa  katattā bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ  …pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍camaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ, tasmiṃ samayai duvaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti – upaikkhā, cittassaikaggatā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘pañ‍camaṃ jhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā jhānasampayuttā|

7. rūpārūpāvacarakiriyā

635. cattāri jhānāni – paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ, dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ|

636. tattha katamaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpāvacaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti kiriyaṃ naiva kusalaṃ nākusalaṃ na ca kammavipākaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāraṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai pañ‍caṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti – vitak‍kau, vicārau, pīti, sukhaṃ, cittassaikaggatā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā jhānasampayuttā|

tattha katamaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpāvacaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti kiriyaṃ naiva kusalaṃ nākusalaṃ na ca kammavipākaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāraṃ vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍camaṃ  jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai duvaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti – upaikkhā, cittassaikaggatā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘pañ‍camaṃ  jhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā jhānasampayuttā|

637. idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai arūpāvacaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti kiriyaṃ naiva kusalaṃ nākusalaṃ na ca kammavipākaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāraṃ sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, tasmiṃ samayai duvaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hauti – upaikkhā, cittassaikaggatā| idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘catutthaṃ jhānaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā jhānasampayuttāti|

abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ|

3. pañhāpucchakaṃ

638. cattāri  jhānāni – idha bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati…pai॰… vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati…pai॰… pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati satau ca sampajānau sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati…pai॰… sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

639. catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ kati kusalā, kati akusalā, kati  abyākatā…pai॰… kati saraṇā, kati araṇā?

1. tikaṃ

640. siyā kusalā, siyā abyākatā| tīṇi jhānāni – aitthuppan‍naṃ sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ ṭhapaitvā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ – aitthuppan‍naṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ ṭhapaitvā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ| siyā vipākā, siyā vipākadhammadhammā, siyā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā| siyā upādin‍nupādāniyā, siyā anupādin‍nupādāniyā, siyā anupādin‍naanupādāniyā| siyā asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā, siyā asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilaisikā| paṭhamaṃ  jhānaṃ – aitthuppan‍nai vitak‍kavicārai ṭhapaitvā savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ, tīṇi jhānāni avitak‍kaavicārā| dvai jhānāni – aitthuppan‍naṃ pītiṃ ṭhapaitvā pītisahagatā, tīṇi jhānāni – aitthuppan‍naṃ sukhaṃ ṭhapaitvā sukhasahagatā, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ – aitthuppan‍naṃ upaikkhaṃ ṭhapaitvā upaikkhāsahagataṃ| naiva dassanaina na bhāvanāya pahātabbā| naiva dassanaina na bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā| siyā ācayagāminau, siyā apacayagāminau, siyā naivācayagāmināpacayagāminau| siyā saikkhā, siyā asaikkhā, siyā naivasaikkhanāsaikkhā| siyā mahaggatā, siyā appamāṇā| tīṇi jhānāni na vattabbā parittārammaṇātipi, mahaggatārammaṇātipi, siyā appamāṇārammaṇā, siyā na vattabbā appamāṇārammaṇāti; catutthaṃ  jhānaṃ siyā parittārammaṇaṃ, siyā mahaggatārammaṇaṃ, siyā appamāṇārammaṇaṃ; siyā na vattabbaṃ parittārammaṇantipi, mahaggatārammaṇantipi, appamāṇārammaṇantipi| siyā majjhimā, siyā paṇītā| siyā sammattaniyatā, siyā aniyatā| tīṇi jhānāni na maggārammaṇā, siyā maggahaitukā, siyā maggādhipatinau, siyā na vattabbā maggahaitukātipi, maggādhipatinautipi; catutthaṃ jhānaṃ siyā maggārammaṇaṃ, siyā maggahaitukaṃ, siyā maggādhipati, siyā na vattabbaṃ maggārammaṇantipi, maggahaitukantipi maggādhipatītipi| siyā uppan‍nā, siyā anuppan‍nā, siyā uppādinau| siyā atītā, siyā anāgatā, siyā pac‍cuppan‍nā| tīṇi jhānāni na vattabbā atītārammaṇātipi, anāgatārammaṇātipi, pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇātipi; catutthaṃ jhānaṃ siyā atītārammaṇaṃ, siyā anāgatārammaṇaṃ, siyā pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇaṃ, siyā na vattabbaṃ atītārammaṇantipi, anāgatārammaṇantipi, pac‍cuppan‍nāramaṇantipi| siyā ajjhattā, siyā bahiddhā, siyā ajjhattabahiddhā| tīṇi jhānāni bahiddhārammaṇā, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ siyā ajjhattārammaṇaṃ, siyā bahiddhārammaṇaṃ, siyā ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇaṃ, siyā na vattabbaṃ ajjhattārammaṇantipi, bahiddhārammaṇantipi, ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇantipi| anidassanaappaṭighā|

2. dukaṃ

641. na haitū, sahaitukā, haitusampayuttā, na vattabbā ‘‘haitū caiva sahaitukā cā’’ti, sahaitukā caiva na ca haitū, na vattabbā ‘‘haitū caiva haitusampayuttā cā’’ti, haitusampayuttā caiva na ca haitū, na haitū sahaitukā|

sappac‍cayā, saṅkhatā, anidassanā, appaṭighā, arūpā, siyā  laukiyā, siyā laukuttarā, kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyā, kainaci na viñ‍ñaiyyā|

nau āsavā, siyā sāsavā, siyā anāsavā, āsavavippayuttā, na vattabbā ‘‘āsavā caiva sāsavā cā’’ti, siyā sāsavā caiva nau ca āsavā, siyā na vattabbā ‘‘sāsavā caiva nau ca āsavā’’ti| na vattabbā ‘‘āsavā caiva āsavasampayuttā cāti’’pi, āsavasampayuttā caiva  nau ca āsavātipi| siyā āsavavippayuttā sāsavā, siyā āsavavippayuttā anāsavā|

nau  saṃyaujanā…pai॰… nau ganthā…pai॰… nau aughā…pai॰… nau yaugā…pai॰… nau nīvaraṇā…pai॰… nau parāmāsā…pai॰… sārammaṇā, nau cittā, caitasikā, cittasampayuttā, cittasaṃsaṭṭhā, cittasamuṭṭhānā, cittasahabhunau, cittānuparivattinau, cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānā, cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhunau, cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattinau, bāhirā, nau upādā, siyā upādin‍nā, siyā anupādin‍nā|

nau upādānā…pai॰… nau kilaisā…pai॰… na dassanaina pahātabbā, na bhāvanāya pahātabbā, na dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā, na bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā| paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ – aitthuppan‍naṃ vitak‍kaṃ ṭhapaitvā savitak‍kaṃ, tīṇi jhānāni avitak‍kā| paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ – aitthuppan‍naṃ vicāraṃ ṭhapaitvā savicāraṃ, tīṇi jhānāni avicārā| dvai jhānāni – aitthuppan‍naṃ pītiṃ ṭhapaitvā  sappītikā, dvai jhānāni appītikā| dvai jhānāni – aitthuppan‍naṃ pītiṃ ṭhapaitvā pītisahagatā, dvai jhānāni na pītisahagatā| tīṇi jhānāni – aitthuppan‍naṃ sukhaṃ ṭhapaitvā sukhasahagatā, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ na sukhasahagataṃ| catutthaṃ jhānaṃ – aitthuppan‍naṃ upaikkhaṃ ṭhapaitvā upaikkhāsahagataṃ, tīṇi jhānāni upaikkhāsahagatā, na kāmāvacarā, siyā rūpāvacarā, siyā na rūpāvacarā, tīṇi jhānāni na arūpāvacarā, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ siyā arūpāvacaraṃ, siyā na arūpāvacaraṃ, siyā pariyāpan‍nā, siyā apariyāpan‍nā, siyā niyyānikā, siyā aniyyānikā, siyā niyatā, siyā aniyatā, siyā sauttarā, siyā anuttarā, araṇāti|

pañhāpucchakaṃ|

jhānavibhaṅgau niṭṭhitau|

13. appamañ‍ñāvibhaṅgau

1. suttantabhājanīyaṃ

642. catassau  appamañ‍ñāyau – idha bhikkhu maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ catutthiṃ (sī॰)| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ maittāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina abyāpajjhaina (sī॰ syā॰) pharitvā viharati| karuṇāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ karuṇāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina  mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati| muditāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ muditāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati| upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati|

1. maittā

643. kathañ‍ca bhikkhu maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati? saiyyathāpi nāma aikaṃ puggalaṃ piyaṃ manāpaṃ disvā maittāyaiyya, aivamaiva sabbai sattai maittāya pharati|

tattha  katamā maittā? yā sattaisu maitti maittāyanā maittāyitattaṃ maittācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘maittā’’|

tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ manau manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu  – idaṃ  vuc‍cati ‘‘cittaṃ’’| idaṃ cittaṃ imāya maittāya sahagataṃ hauti sahajātaṃ saṃsaṭṭhaṃ sampayuttaṃ| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘maittāsahagataina caitasā’’ti|

644. ‘‘aikaṃ disa’’nti puratthimaṃ vā disaṃ pacchimaṃ vā disaṃ uttaraṃ vā disaṃ dakkhiṇaṃ vā disaṃ uddhaṃ vā adhau vā tiriyaṃ vā vidisaṃ vā|

645. ‘‘pharitvā’’ti  pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā|

646. ‘‘viharatī’’ti iriyati vattati pālaiti yapaiti yāpaiti carati viharati| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘viharatī’’ti|

647. ‘‘tathā dutiya’’nti yathaiva aikaṃ disaṃ tathā dutiyaṃ disaṃ tathā tatiyaṃ disaṃ tathā catutthaṃ disaṃ tathā uddhaṃ tathā adhau tathā tiriyaṃ tathā vidisaṃ|

648. ‘‘sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ lauka’’nti sabbaina sabbaṃ sabbathā sabbaṃ asaisaṃ nissaisaṃ| pariyādāyavacanamaitaṃ – ‘‘sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ lauka’’nti|

649. ‘‘maittāsahagataina caitasā’’ti tattha katamā maittā? yā sattaisu maitti maittāyanā maittāyitattaṃ maittācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘maittā’’|

tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘cittaṃ’’| idaṃ cittaṃ imāya maittāya sahagataṃ hauti sahajātaṃ saṃsaṭṭhaṃ sampayuttaṃ| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘maittāsahagataina caitasā’’ti|

650. ‘‘vipulainā’’ti  yaṃ vipulaṃ taṃ mahaggataṃ, yaṃ mahaggataṃ taṃ appamāṇaṃ, yaṃ appamāṇaṃ sau avairau, yau avairau sau abyāpaj‍jau abyāpajjhau (sī॰ syā॰)|

651. ‘‘pharitvā’’ti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā|

652. ‘‘viharatī’’ti…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘viharatī’’ti|

2. karuṇā

653. kathañ‍ca  bhikkhu karuṇāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati? saiyyathāpi nāma aikaṃ puggalaṃ duggataṃ durūpaitaṃ disvā karuṇāyaiyya, aivamaiva sabbai sattai karuṇāya pharati|

tattha katamā karuṇā? yā sattaisu karuṇā karuṇāyanā karuṇāyitattaṃ karuṇācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘karuṇā’’|

tattha katamaṃ  cittaṃ? yaṃ  cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘cittaṃ’’| idaṃ cittaṃ imāya karuṇāya sahagataṃ hauti sahajātaṃ saṃsaṭṭhaṃ sampayuttaṃ| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘karuṇāsahagataina caitasā’’ti|

654. ‘‘aikaṃ disa’’nti puratthimaṃ vā disaṃ pacchimaṃ vā disaṃ uttaraṃ vā disaṃ dakkhiṇaṃ vā disaṃ uddhaṃ vā adhau vā tiriyaṃ vā vidisaṃ vā|

655. ‘‘pharitvā’’ti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā|

656. ‘‘viharatī’’ti iriyati vattati pālaiti yapaiti yāpaiti carati viharati| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘viharatī’’ti|

657. ‘‘tathā  dutiya’’nti yathaiva aikaṃ disaṃ tathā dutiyaṃ disaṃ tathā tatiyaṃ disaṃ tathā catutthaṃ disaṃ tathā uddhaṃ tathā adhau tathā tiriyaṃ tathā vidisaṃ|

658. ‘‘sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ lauka’’nti sabbaina sabbaṃ sabbathā sabbaṃ asaisaṃ nissaisaṃ| pariyādāyavacanamaitaṃ – ‘‘sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ lauka’’nti|

659. ‘‘karuṇāsahagataina caitasā’’ti tattha katamā karuṇā? yā sattaisu karuṇā karuṇāyanā karuṇāyitattaṃ karuṇācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘karuṇā’’|

tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘cittaṃ’’| idaṃ cittaṃ imāya karuṇāya sahagataṃ hauti sahajātaṃ  saṃsaṭṭhaṃ sampayuttaṃ| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘karuṇāsahagataina caitasā’’ti|

660. ‘‘vipulainā’’ti yaṃ vipulaṃ taṃ mahaggataṃ, yaṃ mahaggataṃ taṃ appamāṇaṃ, yaṃ appamāṇaṃ sau avairau, yau avairau sau abyāpaj‍jau|

661. ‘‘pharitvā’’ti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā|

662. ‘‘viharatī’’ti…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati  ‘‘viharatī’’ti|

3. muditā

663. kathañ‍ca bhikkhu muditāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati? saiyyathāpi nāma aikaṃ puggalaṃ piyaṃ manāpaṃ disvā muditau assa, aivamaiva sabbai sattai muditāya pharati|

tattha katamā muditā? yā sattaisu muditā muditāyanā muditāyitattaṃ muditācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘muditā’’|

tattha  katamaṃ cittaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘cittaṃ’’| idaṃ cittaṃ imāya muditāya sahagataṃ hauti sahajātaṃ saṃsaṭṭhaṃ sampayuttaṃ| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘muditāsahagataina caitasā’’ti|

664. ‘‘aikaṃ disa’’nti puratthimaṃ vā disaṃ pacchimaṃ vā disaṃ uttaraṃ vā disaṃ dakkhiṇaṃ vā disaṃ uddhaṃ vā adhau vā tiriyaṃ vā vidisaṃ vā|

665. ‘‘pharitvā’’ti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā|

666. ‘‘viharatī’’ti…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘viharatī’’ti|

667. ‘‘tathā dutiya’’nti yathaiva aikaṃ disaṃ tathā dutiyaṃ disaṃ tathā tatiyaṃ disaṃ tathā catutthaṃ disaṃ tathā uddhaṃ tathā adhau tathā tiriyaṃ tathā vidisaṃ|

668. ‘‘sabbadhi  sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ lauka’’nti sabbaina sabbaṃ sabbathā sabbaṃ asaisaṃ nissaisaṃ| pariyādāyavacanamaitaṃ – ‘‘sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ lauka’’nti|

669. ‘‘muditāsahagataina caitasā’’ti tattha katamā muditā? yā sattaisu muditā muditāyanā muditāyitattaṃ muditācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘muditā’’|

tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ  vuc‍cati ‘‘cittaṃ’’| idaṃ cittaṃ imāya muditāya sahagataṃ hauti sahajātaṃ saṃsaṭṭhaṃ sampayuttaṃ| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘muditāsahagataina caitasā’’ti|

670. ‘‘vipulainā’’ti yaṃ vipulaṃ taṃ mahaggataṃ, yaṃ mahaggataṃ taṃ appamāṇaṃ, yaṃ appamāṇaṃ sau avairau, yau avairau sau abyāpaj‍jau|

671. ‘‘pharitvā’’ti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā|

672. ‘‘viharatī’’ti  …pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘viharatī’’ti|

4. upaikkhā

673. kathañ‍ca bhikkhu upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati? saiyyathāpi nāma aikaṃ puggalaṃ naiva manāpaṃ na amanāpaṃ disvā upaikkhakau assa, aivamaiva sabbai sattai upaikkhāya pharati|

tattha katamā upaikkhā? yā  sattaisu upaikkhā upaikkhāyanā upaikkhāyitattaṃ upaikkhācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘upaikkhā’’|

tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘cittaṃ’’| idaṃ cittaṃ imāya upaikkhāya sahagataṃ hauti sahajātaṃ saṃsaṭṭhaṃ sampayuttaṃ| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā’’ti|

674. ‘‘aikaṃ disa’’nti puratthimaṃ vā disaṃ pacchimaṃ vā disaṃ uttaraṃ vā disaṃ dakkhiṇaṃ vā disaṃ uddhaṃ vā adhau vā tiriyaṃ vā vidisaṃ vā|

675. ‘‘pharitvā’’ti  pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā|

676. ‘‘viharatī’’ti…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘viharatī’’ti|

677. ‘‘tathā dutiya’’nti yathaiva aikaṃ disaṃ tathā dutiyaṃ disaṃ tathā tatiyaṃ disaṃ tathā catutthaṃ disaṃ tathā uddhaṃ tathā adhau tathā tiriyaṃ tathā vidisaṃ|

678. ‘‘sabbadhi sabbattatāya  sabbāvantaṃ lauka’’nti sabbaina sabbaṃ sabbathā sabbaṃ asaisaṃ nissaisaṃ| pariyādāyavacanamaitaṃ – ‘‘sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ lauka’’nti|

679. ‘‘upaikkhāsahagataina  caitasā’’ti, tattha katamā upaikkhā? yā sattaisu upaikkhā upaikkhāyanā upaikkhāyitattaṃ upaikkhācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘upaikkhā’’|

tattha katamaṃ cittaṃ? yaṃ cittaṃ manau mānasaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jāmanauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘cittaṃ’’| idaṃ cittaṃ imāya upaikkhāya sahagataṃ hauti sahajātaṃ saṃsaṭṭhaṃ sampayuttaṃ| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā’’ti|

680. ‘‘vipulainā’’ti yaṃ vipulaṃ taṃ mahaggataṃ, yaṃ mahaggataṃ taṃ appamāṇaṃ, yaṃ appamāṇaṃ sau avairau, yau avairau sau abyāpaj‍jau|

681. ‘‘pharitvā’’ti pharitvā adhimuc‍citvā|

682. ‘‘viharatī’’ti…pai॰… taina vuc‍cati ‘‘viharatī’’ti|

suttantabhājanīyaṃ|

2. abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ

683. catassau appamañ‍ñāyau – maittā, karuṇā, muditā, upaikkhā|

684. tattha katamā maittā? idha  bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati maittāsahagataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai maitti maittāyanā maittāyitattaṃ maittācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘maittā’’| avasaisā dhammā maittāya sampayuttā|

tattha katamā maittā? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vitak‍kavicārānaṃ  vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ  jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati maittāsahagataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai maitti maittāyanā maittāyitattaṃ maittācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘maittā’’| avasaisā dhammā maittāya sampayuttā|

tattha katamā maittā? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati maittāsahagataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai maitti maittāyanā maittāyitattaṃ maittācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘maittā’’| avasaisā dhammā maittāya sampayuttā|

685. idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati maittāsahagataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai maitti maittāyanā maittāyitattaṃ maittācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘maittā’’| avasaisā dhammā maittāya sampayuttā|

idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi avitak‍kaṃ vicāramattaṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati maittāsahagataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai maitti maittāyanā maittāyitattaṃ maittācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘maittā’’| avasaisā dhammā maittāya sampayuttā|

idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati maittāsahagataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai maitti maittāyanā maittāyitattaṃ maittācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘maittā’’| avasaisā dhammā maittāya sampayuttā|

idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti pītiyā  ca virāgā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati maittāsahagataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai  maitti maittāyanā maittāyitattaṃ maittācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘maittā’’| avasaisā dhammā maittāya sampayuttā|

686. tattha  katamā karuṇā? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati karuṇāsahagataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai karuṇā karuṇāyanā karuṇāyitattaṃ karuṇācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘karuṇā’’| avasaisā dhammā karuṇāya sampayuttā|

tattha katamā karuṇā? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati karuṇāsahagataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai karuṇā karuṇāyanā karuṇāyitattaṃ karuṇācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘karuṇā’’| avasaisā dhammā karuṇāya sampayuttā|

tattha katamā karuṇā? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati karuṇāsahagataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai karuṇā karuṇāyanā karuṇāyitattaṃ karuṇācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘karuṇā’’| avasaisā dhammā karuṇāya sampayuttā|

687. idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati karuṇāsahagataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai karuṇā karuṇāyanā karuṇāyitattaṃ karuṇācaitauvimutti  – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘karuṇā’’| avasaisā dhammā karuṇāya sampayuttā|

idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi avitak‍kaṃ vicāramattaṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati karuṇāsahagataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai karuṇā karuṇāyanā karuṇāyitattaṃ karuṇācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘karuṇā’’| avasaisā dhammā karuṇāya sampayuttā|

idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati karuṇāsahagataṃ, yā tasmiṃ  samayai karuṇā  karuṇāyanā karuṇāyitattaṃ karuṇācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘karuṇā’’| avasaisā dhammā karuṇāya sampayuttā|

idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati karuṇāsahagataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai karuṇā  karuṇāyanā karuṇāyitattaṃ karuṇācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘karuṇā’’| avasaisā dhammā karuṇāya sampayuttā|

688. tattha katamā muditā? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati muditāsahagataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai muditā muditāyanā muditāyitattaṃ muditācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘muditā’’| avasaisā dhammā muditāya sampayuttā|

tattha katamā muditā? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ  jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati muditāsahagataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai muditā muditāyatanā muditāyitattaṃ muditācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘muditā’’| avasaisā dhammā muditāya sampayuttā|

tattha katamā muditā? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati muditāsahagataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai muditā muditāyanā muditāyitattaṃ muditācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘muditā’’| avasaisā dhammā muditāya sampayuttā|

689. idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati muditāsahagataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai muditā muditāyanā muditāyitattaṃ muditācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘muditā’’| avasaisā dhammā muditāya sampayuttā|

idha  bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi avitak‍kaṃ vicāramattaṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ  dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati muditāsahagataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai muditā muditāyanā muditāyitattaṃ muditācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘muditā’’| avasaisā dhammā muditāya sampayuttā|

idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati muditāsahagataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai muditā muditāyanā muditāyitattaṃ muditācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘muditā’’| avasaisā dhammā muditāya sampayuttā|

idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti pītiyā  ca virāgā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati muditāsahagataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai muditā muditāyanā muditāyitattaṃ muditācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘muditā’’| avasaisā dhammā muditāya sampayuttā|

690. tattha katamā upaikkhā? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati upaikkhāsahagataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai upaikkhā upaikkhāyanā upaikkhāyitattaṃ upaikkhācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘upaikkhā’’| avasaisā dhammā upaikkhāya sampayuttā|

691. catassau appamañ‍ñāyau – maittā, karuṇā, muditā, upaikkhā|

692. tattha katamā maittā? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati maittāsahagataṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva rūpāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati maittāsahagataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai maitti maittāyanā maittāyitattaṃ maittācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘maittā’’| avasaisā dhammā maittāya sampayuttā|

tattha  katamā maittā? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ  upasampaj‍ja viharati maittāsahagataṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva rūpāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā  upacitattā vipākaṃ vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati maittāsahagataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai maitti maittāyanā maittāyitattaṃ maittācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘maittā’’| avasaisā dhammā maittāya sampayuttā|

693. tattha katamā karuṇā? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati karuṇāsahagataṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva rūpāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati karuṇāsahagataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai karuṇā karuṇāyanā karuṇāyitattaṃ karuṇācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘karuṇā’’| avasaisā dhammā karuṇāya sampayuttā|

tattha katamā karuṇā? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati karuṇāsahagataṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva rūpāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ  jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati karuṇāsahagataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai karuṇā karuṇāyanā karuṇāyitattaṃ karuṇācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘karuṇā’’| avasaisā dhammā karuṇāya sampayuttā|

694. tattha  katamā muditā? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati muditāsahagataṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva rūpāvacarassa kusalassa  kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati muditāsahagataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai muditā muditāyanā muditāyitattaṃ muditācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘muditā’’| avasaisā dhammā muditāya sampayuttā|

tattha  katamā muditā? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati muditāsahagataṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva rūpāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati muditāsahagataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai muditā muditāyanā muditāyitattaṃ muditācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘muditā’’| avasaisā dhammā muditāya sampayuttā|

695. tattha  katamā upaikkhā? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati upaikkhāsahagataṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva rūpāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati upaikkhāsahagataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai upaikkhā upaikkhāyanā upaikkhāyitattaṃ upaikkhācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘upaikkhā’’| avasaisā dhammā upaikkhāya sampayuttā|

696. catassau appamañ‍ñāyau – maittā, karuṇā, muditā, upaikkhā|

697. tattha  katamā maittā? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpāvacaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti kiriyaṃ naiva kusalaṃ nākusalaṃ na ca kammavipākaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāraṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati maittāsahagataṃ, yā  tasmiṃ samayai maitti maittāyanā maittāyitattaṃ maittācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘maittā’’| avasaisā dhammā maittāya sampayuttā|

tattha katamā maittā? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpāvacaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti kiriyaṃ naiva kusalaṃ nākusalaṃ na ca kammavipākaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāraṃ vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati maittāsahagataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai maitti maittāyanā maittāyitattaṃ maittācaitauvimutti  – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘maittā’’| avasaisā dhammā maittāya sampayuttā|

698. tattha  katamā karuṇā…pai॰… tattha katamā muditā…pai॰… tattha katamā upaikkhā? idha bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai rūpāvacaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti kiriyaṃ naiva kusalaṃ nākusalaṃ na ca kammavipākaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāraṃ sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati upaikkhāsahagataṃ, yā tasmiṃ samayai upaikkhā upaikkhāyanā upaikkhāyitattaṃ upaikkhācaitauvimutti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘upaikkhā’’| avasaisā dhammā upaikkhāya sampayuttā|

abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ|

3. pañhāpucchakaṃ

699. catassau appamañ‍ñāyau – idha bhikkhu maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati tathā dutiyaṃ tathā tatiyaṃ tathā catutthaṃ, iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ maittāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati; karuṇāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati tathā dutiyaṃ tathā tatiyaṃ tathā catutthaṃ, iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ karuṇāsahagataina caitasā  vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati; muditāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati tathā dutiyaṃ tathā tatiyaṃ tathā catutthaṃ, iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ muditāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina  appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati; upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati tathā dutiyaṃ tathā tatiyaṃ tathā catutthaṃ, iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati|

700. catun‍naṃ appamañ‍ñānaṃ kati kusalā, kati akusalā, kati abyākatā…pai॰… kati saraṇā, kati araṇā?

1. tikaṃ

701. siyā  kusalā, siyā abyākatā| tissau appamañ‍ñāyau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, upaikkhā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā| siyā vipākā, siyā vipākadhammadhammā, siyā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā| siyā upādin‍nupādāniyā, siyā anupādin‍nupādāniyā| asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā| tissau appamañ‍ñāyau siyā savitak‍kasavicārā, siyā avitak‍kavicāramattā, siyā avitak‍kaavicārā; upaikkhā avitak‍kaavicārā| tissau appamañ‍ñāyau siyā pītisahagatā, siyā sukhasahagatā, na upaikkhāsahagatā, siyā na vattabbā pītisahagatāti; upaikkhā upaikkhāsahagatā| naiva dassanaina na bhāvanāya pahātabbā| naiva dassanaina na bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā| siyā ācayagāminau, siyā naivācayagāmināpacayagāminau, naivasaikkhanāsaikkhā, mahaggatā, na vattabbā parittārammaṇātipi, mahaggatārammaṇātipi, appamāṇārammaṇātipi| majjhimā, aniyatā, na vattabbā maggārammaṇātipi, maggahaitukātipi, maggādhipatinautipi| siyā uppan‍nā, siyā anuppan‍nā, siyā uppādinau| siyā atītā, siyā anāgatā, siyā pac‍cuppan‍nā| na vattabbā atītārammaṇātipi, anāgatārammaṇātipi, pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇātipi| siyā ajjhattā, siyā bahiddhā, siyā ajjhattabahiddhā, bahiddhārammaṇā, anidassanaappaṭighā|

2. dukaṃ

702. maittā haitu, tissau appamañ‍ñāyau na haitū, sahaitukā, haitusampayuttā| maittā haitu  caiva sahaitukā ca; tissau appamañ‍ñāyau na vattabbā haitū caiva sahaitukā cāti, sahaitukā caiva na ca haitū| maittā haitu caiva haitusampayuttā ca; tissau appamañ‍ñāyau na vattabbā haitū caiva haitusampayuttā cāti, haitusampayuttā caiva na ca haitū| tissau appamañ‍ñāyau na haitū sahaitukā; maittā na vattabbā na haitu sahaitukātipi, na haitu ahaitukātipi|

sappac‍cayā, saṅkhatā, anidassanā, appaṭighā, arūpā, laukiyā, kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyā, kainaci na viñ‍ñaiyyā, nau āsavā, sāsavā, āsavavippayuttā, na vattabbā āsavā caiva sāsavā cāti, sāsavā caiva nau ca āsavā, na vattabbā āsavā  caiva āsavasampayuttā cātipi, āsavasampayuttā caiva nau ca āsavātipi| āsavavippayuttā sāsavā|

nau saṃyaujanā…pai॰… nau ganthā…pai॰… nau aughā…pai॰… nau yaugā…pai॰… nau nīvaraṇā…pai॰… nau parāmāsā  …pai॰… sārammaṇā, nau cittā, caitasikā, cittasampayuttā, cittasaṃsaṭṭhā, cittasamuṭṭhānā, cittasahabhunau, cittānuparivattinau, cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānā, cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhunau, cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattinau, bāhirā, nau upādā, siyā upādin‍nā, siyā anupādin‍nā|

nau upādānā…pai॰… nau kilaisā…pai॰… na dassanaina pahātabbā, na bhāvanāya pahātabbā, na dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā, na bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā| tissau appamañ‍ñāyau siyā savitak‍kā, siyā avitak‍kā; upaikkhā avitak‍kā| tissau appamañ‍ñāyau siyā savicārā, siyā avicārā; upaikkhā avicārā| tissau appamañ‍ñāyau siyā sappītikā, siyā appītikā; upaikkhā appītikā| tissau appamañ‍ñāyau siyā pītisahagatā, siyā na pītisahagatā; upaikkhā na pītisahagatā| tissau appamañ‍ñāyau sukhasahagatā, upaikkhā na sukhasahagatā| upaikkhā upaikkhāsahagatā, tissau appamañ‍ñāyau na upaikkhāsahagatā, na kāmāvacarā, rūpāvacarā, na arūpāvacarā, pariyāpan‍nā, aniyyānikā, aniyatā, sauttarā, araṇāti|

pañhāpucchakaṃ|

appamañ‍ñāvibhaṅgau niṭṭhitau|

14. sikkhāpadavibhaṅgau

1. abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ

703 . pañ‍ca  sikkhāpadāni – pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ, adin‍nādānā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ, kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ, musāvādā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ|

704. tattha katamaṃ pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ pāṇātipātā viramantassa, yā tasmiṃ samayai pāṇātipātā ārati virati paṭivirati vairamaṇī akiriyā akaraṇaṃ anajjhāpatti vailāanatik‍kamau saitughātau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā vairamaṇiyā sampayuttā|

tattha katamaṃ pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ pāṇātipātā viramantassa, yā tasmiṃ samayai caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā caitanāya sampayuttā|

tattha katamaṃ pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ pāṇātipātā viramantassa, yau tasmiṃ samayai  phassau…pai॰… paggāhau avikkhaipau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ…pai॰…  saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina pāṇātipātā viramantassa, yā tasmiṃ samayai pāṇātipātā ārati virati paṭivirati vairamaṇī akiriyā akaraṇaṃ anajjhāpatti vailāanatik‍kamau saitughātau  – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā vairamaṇiyā sampayuttā|

705. tattha katamaṃ pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina pāṇātipātā viramantassa, yā tasmiṃ samayai caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā caitanāya sampayuttā|

tattha katamaṃ pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina pāṇātipātā viramantassa phassau…pai॰… paggāhau avikkhaipau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ’’|

706. tattha katamaṃ adin‍nādānā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ…pai॰… kāmaisumicchācārā  vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ…pai॰… musāvādā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ…pai॰… surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā viramantassa, yā tasmiṃ samayai surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā ārati virati paṭivirati vairamaṇī akiriyā akaraṇaṃ anajjhāpatti vailāanatik‍kamau saitughātau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā vairamaṇiyā sampayuttā|

tattha  katamaṃ surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ, yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā viramantassa, yā tasmiṃ samayai caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā caitanāya sampayuttā|

tattha katamaṃ surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti  saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā viramantassa, yau tasmiṃ samayai phassau…pai॰… paggāhau avikkhaipau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ’’|

tattha  katamaṃ surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ…pai॰… saumanassasahagataṃ  ñāṇavippayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā viramantassa, yā tasmiṃ samayai surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā ārati virati paṭivirati vairamaṇī akiriyā akaraṇaṃ anajjhāpatti vailāanatik‍kamau saitughātau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā vairamaṇiyā sampayuttā|

707. tattha katamaṃ surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā viramantassa, yā tasmiṃ samayai caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā caitanāya sampayuttā|

tattha katamaṃ surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā viramantassa, yau tasmiṃ samayai phassau…pai॰…  paggāhau avikkhaipau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ’’|

708. pañ‍ca sikkhāpadāni – pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ, adin‍nādānā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ, kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ, musāvādā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ|

709. tattha katamaṃ pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ hīnaṃ… majjhimaṃ… paṇītaṃ… chandādhipataiyyaṃ… vīriyādhipataiyyaṃ… cittādhipataiyyaṃ… vīmaṃsādhipataiyyaṃ… chandādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ… majjhimaṃ… paṇītaṃ… vīriyādhipataiyyaṃ  hīnaṃ… majjhimaṃ… paṇītaṃ… cittādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ… majjhimaṃ… paṇītaṃ… vīmaṃsādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ… majjhimaṃ… paṇītaṃ pāṇātipātā viramantassa, yā tasmiṃ samayai pāṇātipātā ārati virati paṭivirati vairamaṇī akiriyā akaraṇaṃ anajjhāpatti vailāanatik‍kamau saitughātau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā vairamaṇiyā sampayuttā|

tattha katamaṃ pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ hīnaṃ… majjhimaṃ… paṇītaṃ… chandādhipataiyyaṃ… vīriyādhipataiyyaṃ… cittādhipataiyyaṃ… vīmaṃsādhipataiyyaṃ… chandādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ… majjhimaṃ… paṇītaṃ… vīriyādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ… majjhimaṃ… paṇītaṃ… cittādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ… majjhimaṃ… paṇītaṃ… vīmaṃsādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ… majjhimaṃ… paṇītaṃ pāṇātipātā viramantassa, yā tasmiṃ samayai caitanā sañ‍caitanā sañ‍caitayitattaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā caitanāya sampayuttā|

tattha katamaṃ pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ? yasmiṃ  samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ hīnaṃ… majjhimaṃ… paṇītaṃ… chandādhipataiyyaṃ… vīriyādhipataiyyaṃ… cittādhipataiyyaṃ… vīmaṃsādhipataiyyaṃ… chandādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ… majjhimaṃ… paṇītaṃ… vīriyādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ… majjhimaṃ… paṇītaṃ… cittādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ… majjhimaṃ… paṇītaṃ… vīmaṃsādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ… majjhimaṃ  … paṇītaṃ pāṇātipātā viramantassa, yau tasmiṃ samayai phassau…pai॰… paggāhau avikkhaipau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ’’|

tattha katamaṃ pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ…pai॰… saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina hīnaṃ… majjhimaṃ… paṇītaṃ… chandādhipataiyyaṃ… vīriyādhipataiyyaṃ… cittādhipataiyyaṃ… chandādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ… majjhimaṃ… paṇītaṃ… vīriyādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ… majjhimaṃ… paṇītaṃ… cittādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ… majjhimaṃ… paṇītaṃ pāṇātipātā viramantassa, yā tasmiṃ  samayai pāṇātipātā ārati virati paṭivirati vairamaṇī akiriyā akaraṇaṃ anajjhāpatti vailāanatik‍kamau saitughātau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā vairamaṇiyā sampayuttā…pai॰… avasaisā dhammā caitanāya sampayuttā…pai॰… phassau…pai॰… paggāhau avikkhaipau  – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ’’|

710. tattha katamaṃ adin‍nādānā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ…pai॰… kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ…pai॰… musāvādā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ…pai॰… surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ hīnaṃ… majjhimaṃ… paṇītaṃ… chandādhipataiyyaṃ… vīriyādhipataiyyaṃ … cittādhipataiyyaṃ… vīmaṃsādhipataiyyaṃ… chandādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ… majjhimaṃ… paṇītaṃ… vīriyādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ… majjhimaṃ… paṇītaṃ… cittādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ… majjhimaṃ… paṇītaṃ… vīmaṃsādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ… majjhimaṃ… paṇītaṃ surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā viramantassa, yā tasmiṃ samayai surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā ārati virati paṭivirati vairamaṇī akiriyā akaraṇaṃ anajjhāpatti vailāanatik‍kamau saitughātau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā vairamaṇiyā sampayuttā  …pai॰… avasaisā dhammā caitanāya sampayuttā…pai॰… phassau…pai॰… paggāhau avikkhaipau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ’’|

711. tattha katamaṃ surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacarai kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ…pai॰… saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ  ñāṇasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina hīnaṃ… majjhimaṃ… paṇītaṃ… chandādhipataiyyaṃ… vīriyādhipataiyyaṃ… cittādhipataiyyaṃ… chandādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ… majjhimaṃ… paṇītaṃ… vīriyādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ… majjhimaṃ… paṇītaṃ… cittādhipataiyyaṃ hīnaṃ… majjhimaṃ paṇītaṃ surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā viramantassa, yā tasmiṃ samayai surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā ārati virati paṭivirati vairamaṇī akiriyā akaraṇaṃ anajjhāpatti vailāanatik‍kamau  saitughātau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ’’| avasaisā dhammā vairamaṇiyā sampayuttā…pai॰… avasaisā dhammā caitanāya sampayuttā…pai॰… phassau…pai॰… paggāhau avikkhaipau – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ’’|

712. katamai dhammā sikkhā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pai॰… dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā sikkhā|

katamai  dhammā sikkhā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ…pai॰… saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ  ñāṇasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pai॰… dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā sikkhā|

713. katamai  dhammā sikkhā? yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… arūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti…pai॰… laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā sikkhā|

abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ|

2. pañhāpucchakaṃ

714. pañ‍ca sikkhāpadāni – pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ, adin‍nādānā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ, kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ, musāvādā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇī sikkhāpadaṃ|

715. pañ‍can‍naṃ  sikkhāpadānaṃ kati kusalā, kati akusalā, kati abyākatā…pai॰… kati saraṇā, kati araṇā?

1. tikaṃ

716. kusalāyaiva| siyā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, vipākadhammadhammā, anupādin‍nupādāniyā, asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilaisikā, savitak‍kasavicārā, siyā pītisahagatā, siyā sukhasahagatā, siyā upaikkhāsahagatā|

naiva dassanaina na  bhāvanāya pahātabbā, naiva dassanaina na bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā, ācayagāminau, naivasaikkhanāsaikkhā, parittā, parittārammaṇā, majjhimā, aniyatā, na vattabbā maggārammaṇātipi, maggahaitukātipi, maggādhipatinautipi| siyā uppan‍nā, siyā anuppan‍nā, na vattabbā uppādinauti, siyā atītā, siyā anāgatā, siyā pac‍cuppan‍nā, pac‍cuppan‍nārammaṇā, siyā ajjhattā, siyā bahiddhā, siyā ajjhattabahiddhā, bahiddhārammaṇā, anidassanaappaṭighā|

2. dukaṃ

717. na haitū sahaitukā, haitusampayuttā| na vattabbā ‘‘haitū caiva sahaitukā cā’’ti, sahaitukā caiva na ca haitū, na vattabbā ‘‘haitū caiva haitusampayuttā cā’’ti, haitusampayuttā caiva na ca haitū, na haitu sahaitūkā, sappac‍cayā, saṅkhatā, anidassanā, appaṭighā, arūpā, laukiyā, kainaci viñ‍ñaiyyā, kainaci na viñ‍ñaiyyā|

nau āsavā, sāsavā, āsavavippayuttā, na vattabbā ‘‘āsavā caiva sāsavā cā’’ti, sāsavā caiva nau ca āsavā, na vattabbā ‘‘āsavā caiva āsavasampayuttā cā’’tipi, ‘‘āsavasampayuttā caiva nau ca āsavā’’tipi| āsavavippayuttā sāsavā, nau saṃyaujanā…pai॰… nau ganthā…pai॰… nau aughā…pai॰… nau yaugā…pai॰… nau nīvaraṇā…pai॰… nau parāmāsā…pai॰… sārammaṇā, nau cittā, caitasikā, cittasampayuttā, cittasaṃsaṭṭhā, cittasamuṭṭhānā, cittasahabhunau, cittānuparivattinau, cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānā, cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhunau, cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattinau, bāhirā, nau upādā, anupādin‍nā, nau upādānā…pai॰… nau kilaisā|

na dassanaina pahātabbā, na bhāvanāyapahātabbā, na dassanaina pahātabbahaitukā, na bhāvanāya pahātabbahaitukā, savitak‍kā, savicārā, siyā sappītikā, siyā appītikā, siyā pītisahagatā, siyā na pītisahagatā, siyā sukhasahagatā, siyā na sukhasahagatā, siyā upaikkhāsahagatā, siyā na upaikkhāsahagatā, kāmāvacarā, na rūpāvacarā, na arūpāvacarā, pariyāpan‍nā, aniyyānikā, aniyatā, sauttarā, araṇāti|

pañhāpucchakaṃ|

sikkhāpadavibhaṅgau niṭṭhitau|

15. paṭisambhidāvibhaṅgau

1. suttantabhājanīyaṃ

1. saṅgahavārau

718. catassau  paṭisambhidā – atthapaṭisambhidā, dhammapaṭisambhidā, niruttipaṭisambhidā, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā paṭibhāṇapaṭisambhidā (syā॰) aivamuparipi| atthai ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā, dhammai ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā, tatra dhammaniruttābhilāpai ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā, ñāṇaisu ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā| ayaṃ saṅgahavārau|

2. sac‍cavārau

719. catassau paṭisambhidā – atthapaṭisambhidā, dhammapaṭisambhidā, niruttipaṭisambhidā, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā| dukkhai ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā, dukkhasamudayai ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā, dukkhaniraudhai ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā, tatra dhammaniruttābhilāpai ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā, ñāṇaisu ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā| ayaṃ sac‍cavārau|

3. haituvārau

720. catassau paṭisambhidā – atthapaṭisambhidā, dhammapaṭisambhidā niruttipaṭisambhidā, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā| haitumhi ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā, haituphalai ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā, tatra dhammaniruttābhilāpai  ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā, ñāṇaisu ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā| ayaṃ haituvārau|

4. dhammavārau

721. catassau paṭisambhidā – atthapaṭisambhidā, dhammapaṭisambhidā, niruttipaṭisambhidā, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā| yai dhammā jātā bhūtā sañ‍jātā nibbattā abhinibbattā pātubhūtā, imaisu dhammaisu ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā; yamhā dhammā tai dhammā jātā bhūtā sañ‍jātā nibbattā abhinibbattā pātubhūtā, taisu  dhammaisu ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā; tatra dhammaniruttābhilāpai ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā; ñāṇaisu ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā| ayaṃ dhammavārau|

5. paṭic‍casamuppādavārau

722. catassau  paṭisambhidā – atthapaṭisambhidā, dhammapaṭisambhidā, niruttipaṭisambhidā, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā| jarāmaraṇai ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā, jarāmaraṇasamudayai ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā, jarāmaraṇaniraudhai ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā, jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā, tatra dhammaniruttābhilāpai ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā, ñāṇaisu ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

723. catassau  paṭisambhidā – atthapaṭisambhidā, dhammapaṭisambhidā, niruttipaṭisambhidā, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā| jātiyā ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… bhavai ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… upādānai ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… taṇhāya ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… vaidanāya ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… phassai ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… saḷāyatanai ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… nāmarūpai ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇai ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… saṅkhāraisu ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā, saṅkhārasamudayai ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā, saṅkhāraniraudhai ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā, saṅkhāraniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā, tatra dhammaniruttābhilāpai ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā, ñāṇaisu ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā| ayaṃ paṭic‍casamuppādavārau|

6. pariyattivārau

724. catassau  paṭisambhidā – atthapaṭisambhidā, dhammapaṭisambhidā, niruttipaṭisambhidā, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

tattha katamā dhammapaṭisambhidā? idha bhikkhu dhammaṃ jānāti – suttaṃ gaiyyaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ gāthaṃ udānaṃ itivuttakaṃ jātakaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ vaidal‍laṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘dhammapaṭisambhidā’’| sau tassa tassaiva bhāsitassa atthaṃ jānāti – ‘‘ayaṃ imassa bhāsitassa atthau, ayaṃ imassa bhāsitassa atthau’’ti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘atthapaṭisambhidā’’| tatra dhammaniruttābhilāpai ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā| ñāṇaisu ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā| ayaṃ pariyattivārau|

suttantabhājanīyaṃ|

2. abhidhammabhājanīyaṃ

1. kusalavārau

725. catassau  paṭisambhidā – atthapaṭisambhidā, dhammapaṭisambhidā, niruttipaṭisambhidā, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ  samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pai॰… dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti, imai dhammā kusalā| imaisu dhammaisu ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā| taisaṃ vipākai ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā, yāya niruttiyā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ pañ‍ñatti hauti, tatra dhammaniruttābhilāpai ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā| yaina ñāṇaina tāni  ñāṇāni jānāti – ‘‘imāni ñāṇāni idamatthajautakānī’’ti, ñāṇaisu ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

726. catassau paṭisambhidā – atthapaṭisambhidā, dhammapaṭisambhidā, niruttipaṭisambhidā, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ…pai॰… saumanassasahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pai॰… dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā kusalā| imaisu dhammaisu ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā| taisaṃ vipākai ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā| yāya niruttiyā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ pañ‍ñatti hauti tatra dhammaniruttābhilāpai ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā| yaina  ñāṇaina tāni ñāṇāni jānāti – ‘‘imāni ñāṇāni idamatthajautakānī’’ti, ñāṇaisu ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

727. catassau paṭisambhidā – atthapaṭisambhidā, dhammapaṭisambhidā, niruttipaṭisambhidā, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

katamai  dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā kusalā| imaisu dhammaisu ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā| taisaṃ vipākai ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā| yāya niruttiyā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ pañ‍ñatti hauti tatra dhammaniruttābhilāpai ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā| yaina ñāṇaina tāni ñāṇāni jānāti – ‘‘imāni ñāṇāni idamatthajautakānī’’ti, ñāṇaisu ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

728. catassau  paṭisambhidā – atthapaṭisambhidā, dhammapaṭisambhidā, niruttipaṭisambhidā, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai arūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā kusalā| imaisu dhammaisu ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā| taisaṃ vipākai ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā| yāya niruttiyā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ pañ‍ñatti hauti tatra dhammaniruttābhilāpai ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā| yaina ñāṇaina tāni ñāṇāni  jānāti – ‘‘imāni ñāṇāni idamatthajautakānī’’ti, ñāṇaisu ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

729. catassau paṭisambhidā – atthapaṭisambhidā, dhammapaṭisambhidā, niruttipaṭisambhidā, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

katamai dhammā kusalā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā kusalā| imaisu dhammaisu ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā| taisaṃ vipākai ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā| yāya niruttiyā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ pañ‍ñatti hauti tatra dhammaniruttābhilāpai ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā| yaina  ñāṇaina tāni ñāṇāni jānāti – ‘‘imāni ñāṇāni idamatthajautakānī’’ti, ñāṇaisu ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

2. akusalavārau

730. catassau  paṭisambhidā – atthapaṭisambhidā, dhammapaṭisambhidā, niruttipaṭisambhidā, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

katamai dhammā akusalā? yasmiṃ samayai akusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ diṭṭhigatasampayuttaṃ  rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pai॰… dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā akusalā| imaisu dhammaisu ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā| taisaṃ vipākai ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā| yāya niruttiyā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ pañ‍ñatti hauti tatra dhammaniruttābhilāpai ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā| yaina ñāṇaina tāni ñāṇāni jānāti – ‘‘imāni ñāṇāni idamatthajautakānī’’ti, ñāṇaisu ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

731. catassau  paṭisambhidā – atthapaṭisambhidā, dhammapaṭisambhidā, niruttipaṭisambhidā, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

katamai dhammā akusalā? yasmiṃ samayai akusalaṃ cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti saumanassasahagataṃ diṭṭhigatasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… saumanassasahagataṃ diṭṭhigatavippayuttaṃ…pai॰… saumanassasahagataṃ diṭṭhigatavippayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ diṭṭhigatasampayuttaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ diṭṭhigatasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ diṭṭhigatavippayuttaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ diṭṭhigatavippayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… daumanassasahagataṃ paṭighasampayuttaṃ…pai॰… daumanassasahagataṃ paṭighasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ vicikicchāsampayuttaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataṃ uddhac‍casampayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pai॰… dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā akusalā| imaisu dhammaisu ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā| taisaṃ vipākai ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā| yāya niruttiyā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ pañ‍ñatti hauti tatra dhammaniruttābhilāpai ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā| yaina ñāṇaina tāni ñāṇāni jānāti – ‘‘imāni ñāṇāni idamatthajautakānī’’ti, ñāṇaisu ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

3. vipākavārau

732. tissau  paṭisambhidā – atthapaṭisambhidā, niruttipaṭisambhidā, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

katamai  dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti, vaidanā hauti, sañ‍ñā hauti, caitanā hauti, cittaṃ hauti, upaikkhā hauti, cittassaikaggatā hauti, manindriyaṃ hauti, upaikkhindriyaṃ hauti, jīvitindriyaṃ hauti, yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā| imai dhammā abyākatā| imaisu dhammaisu ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā| yāya niruttiyā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ pañ‍ñatti hauti tatra dhammaniruttābhilāpai ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā| yaina ñāṇaina tāni ñāṇāni jānāti – ‘‘imāni ñāṇāni idamatthajautakānī’’ti, ñāṇaisu ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

733. tissau paṭisambhidā – atthapaṭisambhidā, niruttipaṭisambhidā, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ  saddārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ gandhārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ rasārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti sukhasahagataṃ phauṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti, vaidanā hauti, sañ‍ñā hauti, caitanā hauti, cittaṃ hauti, sukhaṃ hauti, cittassaikaggatā hauti, manindriyaṃ hauti, sukhindriyaṃ hauti, jīvitindriyaṃ hauti, yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā| imai dhammā abyākatā| imaisu dhammaisu ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā| yāya niruttiyā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ pañ‍ñatti hauti tatra dhammaniruttābhilāpai ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā| yaina ñāṇaina tāni ñāṇāni jānāti – ‘‘imāni ñāṇāni idamatthajautakānī’’ti, ñāṇaisu ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

734. tissau  paṭisambhidā – atthapaṭisambhidā, niruttipaṭisambhidā, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā| katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākā manaudhātu uppan‍nā hauti upaikkhāsahagatā rūpārammaṇā vā…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbārammaṇā vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti, vaidanā hauti, sañ‍ñā hauti, caitanā hauti, cittaṃ hauti, vitak‍kau hauti, vicārau hauti, upaikkhā hauti, cittassaikaggatā hauti, manindriyaṃ hauti, upaikkhindriyaṃ hauti, jīvitindriyaṃ hauti, yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā| imai dhammā abyākatā| imaisu dhammaisu ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā| yāya niruttiyā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ pañ‍ñatti hauti tatra dhammaniruttābhilāpai ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā| yaina ñāṇaina tāni ñāṇāni jānāti – ‘‘imāni ñāṇāni idamatthajautakānī’’ti, ñāṇaisu ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

735. tissau paṭisambhidā – atthapaṭisambhidā, niruttipaṭisambhidā, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ  samayai kāmāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu uppan‍nā hauti saumanassasahagatā rūpārammaṇā vā…pai॰… dhammārammaṇā vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti, vaidanā hauti, sañ‍ñā hauti, caitanā hauti, cittaṃ hauti, vitak‍kau hauti, vicārau hauti, pīti hauti, sukhaṃ hauti, cittassaikaggatā hauti, manindriyaṃ hauti, saumanassindriyaṃ hauti, jīvitindriyaṃ hauti, yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā| imai dhammā abyākatā| imaisu dhammaisu ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā| yāya niruttiyā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ pañ‍ñatti hauti tatra dhammaniruttābhilāpai ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā| yaina ñāṇaina tāni ñāṇāni jānāti – ‘‘imāni ñāṇāni idamatthajautakānī’’ti, ñāṇaisu ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

736. tissau paṭisambhidā – atthapaṭisambhidā, niruttipaṭisambhidā, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu uppan‍nā  hauti upaikkhāsahagatā rūpārammaṇā vā…pai॰… dhammārammaṇā vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti, vaidanā hauti, sañ‍ñā hauti, caitanā hauti, cittaṃ hauti, vitak‍kau hauti, vicārau hauti, upaikkhā hauti, cittassaikaggatā hauti, manindriyaṃ hauti, upaikkhindriyaṃ hauti, jīvitindriyaṃ hauti, yai vā pana  tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi  paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā| imai dhammā abyākatā| imaisu dhammaisu ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā| yāya niruttiyā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ pañ‍ñatti hauti tatra dhammaniruttābhilāpai ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā| yaina ñāṇaina tāni ñāṇāni jānāti – ‘‘imāni ñāṇāni idamatthajautakānī’’ti, ñāṇaisu ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

737. tissau paṭisambhidā – atthapaṭisambhidā, niruttipaṭisambhidā, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai kāmāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu uppan‍nā hauti saumanassasahagatā ñāṇasampayuttā…pai॰… saumanassasahagatā ñāṇasampayuttā sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… saumanassasahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā…pai॰… saumanassasahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagatā ñāṇasampayuttā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagatā ñāṇasampayuttā sasaṅkhāraina…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagatā ñāṇavippayuttā sasaṅkhāraina rūpārammaṇā vā…pai॰… dhammārammaṇā vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā abyākatā| imaisu dhammaisu ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā| yāya niruttiyā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ pañ‍ñatti hauti tatra dhammaniruttābhilāpai ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā| yaina ñāṇaina tāni ñāṇāni jānāti – ‘‘imāni ñāṇāni idamatthajautakānī’’ti, ñāṇaisu ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

738. tissau  paṭisambhidā – atthapaṭisambhidā, niruttipaṭisambhidā, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva rūpāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā abyākatā| imaisu dhammaisu ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā| yāya niruttiyā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ pañ‍ñatti hauti  tatra dhammaniruttābhilāpai ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā| yaina ñāṇaina  tāni ñāṇāni jānāti – ‘‘imāni ñāṇāni idamatthajautakānī’’ti, ñāṇaisu ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

739. tissau paṭisambhidā – atthapaṭisambhidā, niruttipaṭisambhidā, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai arūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāvaiti sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva arūpāvacarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñāsahagataṃ sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, tasmiṃ  samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā abyākatā| imaisu dhammaisu ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā| yāya niruttiyā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ pañ‍ñatti hauti tatra dhammaniruttābhilāpai ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā| yaina ñāṇaina tāni ñāṇāni jānāti – ‘‘imāni ñāṇāni idamatthajautakānī’’ti, ñāṇaisu ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

740. tissau paṭisambhidā – atthapaṭisambhidā, niruttipaṭisambhidā, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai laukuttaraṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaiti niyyānikaṃ apacayagāmiṃ diṭṭhigatānaṃ pahānāya paṭhamāya bhūmiyā pattiyā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā kusalā| tassaiva laukuttarassa kusalassa kammassa katattā bhāvitattā vipākaṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati  dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti…pai॰… avikkhaipau hauti| imai dhammā abyākatā| imaisu dhammaisu ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā| yāya niruttiyā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ pañ‍ñatti hauti tatra dhammaniruttābhilāpai ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā| yaina ñāṇaina tāni  ñāṇāni jānāti – ‘‘imāni ñāṇāni idamatthajautakānī’’ti, ñāṇaisu ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

741. tissau paṭisambhidā – atthapaṭisambhidā, niruttipaṭisambhidā, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

katamai  dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai akusalassa  kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ saddārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ gandhārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti upaikkhāsahagataṃ rasārammaṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti dukkhasahagataṃ phauṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti, vaidanā hauti, sañ‍ñā hauti, caitanā hauti, cittaṃ hauti, dukkhaṃ hauti, cittassaikaggatā hauti, manindriyaṃ hauti, dukkhindriyaṃ hauti, jīvitindriyaṃ hauti, yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā| imai dhammā abyākatā| imaisu dhammaisu ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā| yāya niruttiyā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ pañ‍ñatti hauti tatra dhammaniruttābhilāpai ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā| yaina ñāṇaina tāni ñāṇāni jānāti – ‘‘imāni ñāṇāni idamatthajautakānī’’ti, ñāṇaisu ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

742. tissau paṭisambhidā – atthapaṭisambhidā, niruttipaṭisambhidā, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai akusalassa kammassa katattā upacitattā vipākā manaudhātu uppan‍nā hauti upaikkhāsahagatā rūpārammaṇā vā…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbārammaṇā vā…pai॰… manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu uppan‍nā hauti upaikkhāsahagatā rūpārammaṇā vā…pai॰… dhammārammaṇā vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti, vaidanā hauti, sañ‍ñā hauti, caitanā hauti, cittaṃ hauti, vitak‍kau hauti, vicārau  hauti, upaikkhā hauti, cittassaikaggatā hauti, manindriyaṃ hauti, upaikkhindriyaṃ hauti, jīvitindriyaṃ hauti, yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā| imai dhammā abyākatā| imaisu  dhammaisu ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā| yāya niruttiyā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ pañ‍ñatti hauti tatra dhammaniruttābhilāpai ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā| yaina ñāṇaina tāni ñāṇāni jānāti – ‘‘imāni ñāṇāni idamatthajautakānī’’ti, ñāṇaisu ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

4. kiriyavārau

743. tissau paṭisambhidā – atthapaṭisambhidā, niruttipaṭisambhidā, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

katamai dhammā abyākatā? yasmiṃ samayai manaudhātu uppan‍nā hauti kiriyā naiva kusalā nākusalā na ca kammavipākā upaikkhāsahagatā rūpārammaṇā  vā…pai॰… phauṭṭhabbārammaṇā vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samayai phassau hauti, vaidanā hauti, sañ‍ñā hauti, caitanā hauti, cittaṃ hauti, vitak‍kau hauti, vicārau hauti, upaikkhā hauti, cittassaikaggatā hauti, manindriyaṃ hauti, upaikkhindriyaṃ hauti, jīvitindriyaṃ hauti, yai vā pana tasmiṃ samayai añ‍ñaipi atthi paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā arūpinau dhammā| imai dhammā abyākatā| imaisu dhammaisu ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā| yāya niruttiyā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ pañ‍ñatti hauti tatra dhammaniruttābhilāpai ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā| yaina ñāṇaina tāni ñāṇāni jānāti – ‘‘imāni ñāṇāni idamatthajautakānī’’ti, ñāṇaisu ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|

744. tissau paṭisambhidā – atthapaṭisambhidā, niruttipaṭisambhidā, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā|
