(15) kathañ‍ca iminā apāhaṃ siyanti hauti? kañ‍ci dhammaṃ avakāriṃ karitvā rūpaṃ…pai॰… vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ iminā apāhaṃ siyanti chandaṃ paṭilabhati, iminā apāhaṃ siyanti mānaṃ paṭilabhati, iminā apāhaṃ siyanti diṭṭhiṃ paṭilabhati| tasmiṃ sati imāni papañ‍citāni haunti – iminā apāhaṃ itthaṃ siyanti vā, iminā apāhaṃ aivaṃ siyanti vā, iminā apāhaṃ añ‍ñathā siyanti vā|

(16) kathañ‍ca iminā apāhaṃ itthaṃ siyanti hauti? iminā apāhaṃ khattiyau siyanti vā, iminā  apāhaṃ brāhmaṇau siyanti vā, iminā apāhaṃ vaissau siyanti vā, iminā apāhaṃ suddau siyanti vā, iminā apāhaṃ gahaṭṭhau siyanti vā, iminā apāhaṃ pabbajitau siyanti vā, iminā apāhaṃ daivau siyanti vā, iminā apāhaṃ manussau siyanti vā, iminā apāhaṃ rūpī siyanti vā, iminā apāhaṃ arūpī siyanti vā, iminā apāhaṃ sañ‍ñī  siyanti vā, iminā apāhaṃ asañ‍ñī siyanti vā, iminā apāhaṃ naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñī siyanti vā – aivaṃ iminā apāhaṃ itthaṃ siyanti hauti|

(17) kathañ‍ca iminā apāhaṃ aivaṃ siyanti hauti? parapuggalaṃ upanidhāya yathā sau khattiyau iminā apāhaṃ tathā khattiyau siyanti vā, yathā sau brāhmaṇau iminā apāhaṃ tathā brāhmaṇau siyanti vā…pai॰… yathā sau naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñī iminā apāhaṃ tathā naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñī siyanti vā – aivaṃ iminā apāhaṃ aivaṃ siyanti hauti|

(18) kathañ‍ca  iminā apāhaṃ añ‍ñathā siyanti hauti? parapuggalaṃ  upanidhāya yathā sau khattiyau iminā apāhaṃ na tathā khattiyau siyanti vā, yathā sau brāhmaṇau iminā apāhaṃ na tathā brāhmaṇau siyanti vā…pai॰… yathā sau naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñī iminā apāhaṃ na tathā naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñī siyanti vā – aivaṃ iminā apāhaṃ añ‍ñathā siyanti hauti|

imāni aṭṭhārasa taṇhāvicaritāni bāhirassa upādāya|

iti imāni aṭṭhārasa taṇhāvicaritāni ajjhattikassa upādāya, imāni  aṭṭhārasa taṇhāvicaritāni bāhirassa upādāya, tadaikajjhaṃ abhisañ‍ñūhitvā abhisaṅkhipitvā chattiṃsa taṇhāvicaritāni haunti| iti aivarūpāni atītāni chattiṃsa taṇhāvicaritāni, anāgatāni chattiṃsa taṇhāvicaritāni, pac‍cuppan‍nāni chattiṃsa taṇhāvicaritāni, tadaikajjhaṃ abhisañ‍ñūhitvā abhisaṅkhipitvā aṭṭhataṇhāvicaritasataṃ hauti|

977. tattha katamāni dvāsaṭṭhi diṭṭhigatāni brahmajālai vaiyyākaraṇai vuttāni bhagavatā? cattārau sassatavādā, cattārau aikac‍casassatikā, cattārau antānantikā, cattārau amarāvikkhaipikā, dvai adhic‍casamuppan‍nikā, sauḷasa sañ‍ñīvādā, aṭṭha asañ‍ñīvādā, aṭṭha naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñīvādā, satta ucchaidavādā, pañ‍ca diṭṭhadhammanibbānavādā – imāni dvāsaṭṭhi diṭṭhigatāni brahmajālai vaiyyākaraṇai vuttāni bhagavatāti|

khuddakavatthuvibhaṅgau niṭṭhitau|

18. dhammahadayavibhaṅgau

1. sabbasaṅgāhikavārau

978. kati  khandhā, kati āyatanāni, kati dhātuyau, kati sac‍cāni, kati indriyāni, kati haitū, kati āhārā, kati phassā, kati vaidanā, kati sañ‍ñā, kati caitanā, kati cittāni?

pañ‍cakkhandhā, dvādasāyatanāni, aṭṭhārasa dhātuyau, cattāri  sac‍cāni, bāvīsatindriyāni, nava haitū, cattārau āhārā, satta phassā, satta vaidanā, satta sañ‍ñā, satta caitanā, satta cittāni|

979. tattha katamai pañ‍cakkhandhā? rūpakkhandhau, vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – imai vuc‍canti ‘‘pañ‍cakkhandhā’’|

980. tattha katamāni dvādasāyatanāni? cakkhāyatanaṃ, rūpāyatanaṃ, sautāyatanaṃ, saddāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, gandhāyatanaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ, rasāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ – imāni vuc‍canti ‘‘dvādasāyatanāni’’|

981. tattha katamā aṭṭhārasa dhātuyau? cakkhudhātu, rūpadhātu, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, sautadhātu, saddadhātu, sautaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, ghānadhātu, gandhadhātu, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, jivhādhātu, rasadhātu, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, kāyadhātu, phauṭṭhabbadhātu, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, manaudhātu, dhammadhātu, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – imā vuc‍canti ‘‘aṭṭhārasa dhātuyau’’|

982. tattha katamāni cattāri sac‍cāni? dukkhasac‍caṃ, samudayasac‍caṃ, niraudhasac‍caṃ, maggasac‍caṃ – imāni vuc‍canti ‘‘cattāri sac‍cāni’’|

983. tattha katamāni bāvīsatindriyāni? cakkhundriyaṃ, sautindriyaṃ, ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, kāyindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ, itthindriyaṃ, purisindriyaṃ, jīvitindriyaṃ, sukhindriyaṃ, dukkhindriyaṃ, saumanassindriyaṃ, daumanassindriyaṃ, upaikkhindriyaṃ, saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ, anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ, añ‍ñindriyaṃ, añ‍ñātāvindriyaṃ – imāni vuc‍canti ‘‘bāvīsatindriyāni’’|

984. tattha  katamai nava haitū? tayau kusalahaitū, tayau akusalahaitū, tayau abyākatahaitū|

tattha katamai tayau kusalahaitū? alaubhau kusalahaitu, adausau kusalahaitu, amauhau kusalahaitu – imai tayau kusalahaitū|

tattha katamai tayau akusalahaitū? laubhau akusalahaitu, dausau akusalahaitu, mauhau akusalahaitu – imai tayau akusalahaitū|

tattha katamai tayau abyākatahaitū? kusalānaṃ vā dhammānaṃ vipākatau kiriyābyākataisu vā dhammaisu alaubhau, adausau, amauhau – imai tayau abyākatahaitū| imai vuc‍canti ‘‘nava haitū’’|

985. tattha katamai cattārau āhārā? kabaḷīkārāhārau kabaḷiṃkārau āhārau (sī॰ syā॰), phassāhārau, manausañ‍caitanāhārau, viñ‍ñāṇāhārau – imai vuc‍canti ‘‘cattārau āhārā’’|

986. tattha  katamai satta phassā? cakkhusamphassau, sautasamphassau, ghānasamphassau, jivhāsamphassau, kāyasamphassau, manaudhātusamphassau, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassau – imai vuc‍canti ‘‘satta phassā’’|

987. tattha katamā satta vaidanā? cakkhusamphassajā vaidanā, sautasamphassajā vaidanā, ghānasamphassajā vaidanā, jivhāsamphassajā vaidanā, kāyasamphassajā vaidanā, manaudhātusamphassajā vaidanā, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassajā  vaidanā – imā vuc‍canti ‘‘satta vaidanā’’|

988. tattha katamā satta sañ‍ñā? cakkhusamphassajā sañ‍ñā, sautasamphassajā sañ‍ñā, ghānasamphassajā sañ‍ñā, jivhāsamphassajā sañ‍ñā, kāyasamphassajā sañ‍ñā, manaudhātusamphassajā sañ‍ñā, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassajā sañ‍ñā – imā vuc‍canti ‘‘satta sañ‍ñā’’|

989. tattha  katamā satta caitanā? cakkhusamphassajā caitanā, sautasamphassajā caitanā, ghānasamphassajā caitanā, jivhāsamphassajā caitanā, kāyasamphassajā caitanā, manaudhātusamphassajā caitanā, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassajā caitanā – imā vuc‍canti ‘‘satta caitanā’’|

990. tattha katamāni satta cittāni? cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, manaudhātu, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – imāni vuc‍canti ‘‘satta cittāni’’|

2. uppattānuppattivārau

1. kāmadhātu

991. kāmadhātuyā kati khandhā, kati āyatanāni, kati dhātuyau, kati sac‍cāni, kati  indriyāni, kati haitū, kati āhārā, kati phassā, kati vaidanā, kati sañ‍ñā, kati caitanā, kati cittāni?

kāmadhātuyā pañ‍cakkhandhā, dvādasāyatanāni, aṭṭhārasa dhātuyau, tīṇi sac‍cāni, bāvīsatindriyāni, nava haitū, cattārau āhārā, satta phassā, satta vaidanā, satta sañ‍ñā, satta caitanā, satta cittāni|

992. tattha katamai kāmadhātuyā pañ‍cakkhandhā? rūpakkhandhau, vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – imai vuc‍canti ‘‘kāmadhātuyā pañ‍cakkhandhā’’|

tattha katamāni kāmadhātuyā dvādasāyatanāni? cakkhāyatanaṃ, rūpāyatanaṃ, sautāyatanaṃ, saddāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, gandhāyatanaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ, rasāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ – imāni vuc‍canti ‘‘kāmadhātuyā dvādasāyatanāni’’|

tattha katamā kāmadhātuyā aṭṭhārasa dhātuyau? cakkhudhātu, rūpadhātu, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, sautadhātu, saddadhātu, sautaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, ghānadhātu, gandhadhātu, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, jivhādhātu, rasadhātu, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, kāyadhātu, phauṭṭhabbadhātu, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, manaudhātu, dhammadhātu, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – imā vuc‍canti ‘‘kāmadhātuyā aṭṭhārasa dhātuyau’’|

tattha katamāni kāmadhātuyā tīṇi sac‍cāni? dukkhasac‍caṃ, samudayasac‍caṃ, maggasac‍caṃ – imāni vuc‍canti ‘‘kāmadhātuyā tīṇi sac‍cāni’’|

tattha katamāni kāmadhātuyā bāvīsatindriyāni? cakkhundriyaṃ, sautindriyaṃ…pai॰… añ‍ñātāvindriyaṃ – imāni vuc‍canti ‘‘kāmadhātuyā bāvīsatindriyāni’’|

tattha katamai kāmadhātuyā nava haitū? tayau kusalahaitū, tayau akusalahaitū, tayau abyākatahaitū…pai॰… imai vuc‍canti ‘‘kāmadhātuyā nava haitū’’|

tattha  katamai kāmadhātuyā cattārau āhārā? kabaḷīkārāhārau, phassāhārau, manausañ‍caitanāhārau, viñ‍ñāṇāhārau – imai vuc‍canti ‘‘kāmadhātuyā cattārau āhārā’’|

tattha katamai kāmadhātuyā satta phassā? cakkhusamphassau sautasamphassau, ghānasamphassau, jivhāsamphassau, kāyasamphassau, manaudhātusamphassau, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassau – imai vuc‍canti ‘‘kāmadhātuyā satta phassā’’|

tattha katamā kāmadhātuyā satta vaidanā? cakkhusamphassajā vaidanā, sautasamphassajā vaidanā, ghānasamphassajā vaidanā, jivhāsamphassajā vaidanā, kāyasamphassajā vaidanā, manaudhātusamphassajā vaidanā, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassajā vaidanā – imā vuc‍catti ‘‘kāmadhātuyā satta vaidanā’’|

tattha katamā kāmadhātuyā satta sañ‍ñā? cakkhusamphassajā sañ‍ñā, sautasamphassajā sañ‍ñā, ghānasamphassajā sañ‍ñā, jivhāsamphassajā sañ‍ñā, kāyasamphassajā sañ‍ñā, manaudhātusamphassajā sañ‍ñā, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassajā sañ‍ñā – imā vuc‍canti ‘‘kāmadhātuyā satta sañ‍ñā’’|

tattha  katamā kāmadhātuyā satta caitanā? cakkhusamphassajā caitanā, sautasamphassajā caitanā, ghānasamphassajā caitanā, jivhāsamphassajā caitanā, kāyasamphassajā caitanā, manaudhātusamphassajā caitanā, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassajā caitanā – imā vuc‍canti ‘‘kāmadhātuyā satta caitanā’’|

tattha katamāni kāmadhātuyā satta cittāni? cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, manaudhātu, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – imāni vuc‍canti ‘‘kāmadhātuyā satta cittāni’’|

2. rūpadhātu

993. rūpadhātuyā  kati khandhā, kati āyatanā, kati dhātuyau, kati sac‍cāni, kati indriyāni…pai॰… kati cittāni?

rūpadhātuyā pañ‍cakkhandhā, cha āyatanāni, nava dhātuyau, tīṇi sac‍cāni, cuddasindriyāni, aṭṭha haitū, tayau āhārā, cattārau phassā, catassau vaidanā, catassau sañ‍ñā, catassau caitanā, cattāri cittāni|

994. tattha katamai rūpadhātuyā pañ‍cakkhandhā? rūpakkhandhau, vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – imai vuc‍canti ‘‘rūpadhātuyā pañ‍cakkhandhā’’|

tattha katamāni rūpadhātuyā cha āyatanāni? cakkhāyatanaṃ, rūpāyatanaṃ, sautāyatanaṃ, saddāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ – imāni vuc‍canti ‘‘rūpadhātuyā cha āyatanāni’’|

tattha katamā rūpadhātuyā nava dhātuyau? cakkhudhātu, rūpadhātu, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, sautadhātu, saddadhātu, sautaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, manaudhātu, dhammadhātu, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – imā vuc‍canti ‘‘rūpadhātuyā nava dhātuyau’’|

tattha katamāni rūpadhātuyā tīṇi sac‍cāni? dukkhasac‍caṃ, samudayasac‍caṃ, maggasac‍caṃ – imāni vuc‍canti ‘‘rūpadhātuyā tīṇi sac‍cāni’’|

tattha  katamāni rūpadhātuyā cuddasindriyāni? cakkhundriyaṃ, sautindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ, jīvitindriyaṃ, saumanassindriyaṃ, upaikkhindriyaṃ, saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ, anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ, añ‍ñindriyaṃ, añ‍ñātāvindriyaṃ – imāni vuc‍canti ‘‘rūpadhātuyā cuddasindriyāni’’|

tattha  katamai rūpadhātuyā aṭṭha haitū? tayau kusalahaitū, dvai akusalahaitū, tayau abyākatahaitū|

tattha katamai tayau kusalahaitū? alaubhau kusalahaitu, adausau kusalahaitu, amauhau kusalahaitu – imai tayau kusalahaitū|

tattha katamai dvai akusalahaitū? laubhau akusalahaitu, mauhau akusalahaitu – imai dvai akusalahaitū|

tattha katamai  tayau abyākatahaitū? kusalānaṃ vā dhammānaṃ vipākatau kiriyābyākataisu vā dhammaisu alaubhau, adausau, amauhau – imai tayau abyākatahaitū| imai vuc‍canti rūpadhātuyā aṭṭha haitū|

tattha katamai rūpadhātuyā tayau āhārā? phassāhārau, manausañ‍caitanāhārau, viñ‍ñāṇāhārau – imai vuc‍canti ‘‘rūpadhātuyā tayau āhārā’’|

tattha katamai rūpadhātuyā cattārau phassā? cakkhusamphassau, sautasamphassau, manaudhātusamphassau, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassau – imai vuc‍canti ‘‘rūpadhātuyā cattārau phassā’’|

tattha katamā rūpadhātuyā catassau vaidanā…pai॰… catassau sañ‍ñā…pai॰… catassau caitanā…pai॰… cattāri cittāni? cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, manaudhātu, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – imāni vuc‍canti ‘‘rūpadhātuyā cattāri cittāni’’|

3. arūpadhātu

995. arūpadhātuyā  kati khandhā…pai॰… kati cittāni?

arūpadhātuyā cattārau khandhā, dvai āyatanāni, dvai dhātuyau, tīṇi sac‍cāni, aikādasindriyāni, aṭṭha haitū, tayau āhārā, aikau phassau, aikā vaidanā, aikā sañ‍ñā, aikā caitanā, aikaṃ cittaṃ|

996. tattha  katamai arūpadhātuyā cattārau khandhā? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – imai vuc‍canti ‘‘arūpadhātuyā cattārau khandhā’’|

tattha katamāni arūpadhātuyā dvai āyatanāni? manāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ – imāni vuc‍canti ‘‘arūpadhātuyā dvai āyatanāni’’|

tattha katamā arūpadhātuyā dvai dhātuyau? manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, dhammadhātu – imā vuc‍canti ‘‘arūpadhātuyā dvai dhātuyau’’|

tattha katamāni arūpadhātuyā tīṇi sac‍cāni? dukkhasac‍caṃ, samudayasac‍caṃ, maggasac‍caṃ – imāni vuc‍canti ‘‘arūpadhātuyā tīṇi sac‍cāni’’|

tattha katamāni arūpadhātuyā aikādasindriyāni? manindriyaṃ, jīvitindriyaṃ, saumanassindriyaṃ, upaikkhindriyaṃ, saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ, añ‍ñindriyaṃ, añ‍ñātāvindriyaṃ – imāni vuc‍canti ‘‘arūpadhātuyā aikādasindriyāni’’|

tattha katamai arūpadhātuyā aṭṭha haitū? tayau kusalahaitū, dvai akusalahaitū, tayau abyākatahaitū…pai॰… imai vuc‍canti ‘‘arūpadhātuyā aṭṭha haitū’’|

tattha katamai arūpadhātuyā tayau āhārā? phassāhārau, manausañ‍caitanāhārau, viñ‍ñāṇāhārau – imai vuc‍canti ‘‘arūpadhātuyā tayau āhārā’’|

tattha katamau arūpadhātuyā aikau phassau? manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘arūpadhātuyā aikau phassau’’|

tattha  katamā arūpadhātuyā aikā vaidanā…pai॰… aikā sañ‍ñā…pai॰… aikā caitanā…pai॰… aikaṃ cittaṃ? manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘arūpadhātuyā aikaṃ cittaṃ’’|

4. apariyāpan‍naṃ

997. apariyāpan‍nai kati khandhā…pai॰… kati cittāni?

apariyāpan‍nai cattārau khandhā, dvai āyatanāni, dvai dhātuyau, dvai sac‍cāni, dvādasindriyāni, cha haitū, tayau āhārā, aikau  phassau, aikā vaidanā, aikā sañ‍ñā, aikā caitanā, aikaṃ cittaṃ|

998. tattha katamai apariyāpan‍nai cattārau khandhā? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – imai vuc‍canti ‘‘apariyāpan‍nai cattārau khandhā’’|

tattha katamāni apariyāpan‍nai dvai āyatanāni? manāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ – imāni vuc‍canti ‘‘apariyāpan‍nai dvai āyatanāni’’|

tattha katamā apariyāpan‍nai dvai dhātuyau? manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, dhammadhātu – imā vuc‍canti ‘‘apariyāpan‍nai dvai dhātuyau’’|

tattha katamāni apariyāpan‍nai dvai sac‍cāni? maggasac‍caṃ, niraudhasac‍caṃ – imāni vuc‍canti ‘‘apariyāpan‍nai dvai sac‍cāni’’|

tattha katamāni apariyāpan‍nai dvādasindriyāni? manindriyaṃ, jīvitindriyaṃ, saumanassindriyaṃ, upaikkhindriyaṃ, saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ, anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ, añ‍ñindriyaṃ, añ‍ñātāvindriyaṃ – imāni vuc‍canti ‘‘apariyāpan‍nai dvādasindriyāni’’|

tattha katamai apariyāpan‍nai cha haitū? tayau kusalahaitū, tayau abyākatahaitū|

tattha  katamai tayau kusalahaitū? alaubhau kusalahaitu, adausau kusalahaitu, amauhau kusalahaitu – imai tayau kusalahaitū|

tattha  katamai tayau abyākatahaitū? kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ vipākatau alaubhau, adausau, amauhau – imai tayau abyākatahaitū| imai vuc‍canti ‘‘apariyāpan‍nai cha haitū’’|

tattha katamai apariyāpan‍nai tayau āhārā? phassāhārau, manausañ‍caitanāhārau, viñ‍ñāṇāhārau – imai vuc‍canti ‘‘apariyāpan‍nai tayau āhārā’’|

tattha katamau apariyāpan‍nai aikau phassau? manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘apariyāpan‍nai aikau phassau’’|

tattha  katamā apariyāpan‍nai aikā vaidanā…pai॰… aikā sañ‍ñā…pai॰… aikā caitanā…pai॰… aikaṃ cittaṃ? manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu  – idaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘apariyāpan‍nai aikaṃ cittaṃ’’|

3. pariyāpan‍nāpariyāpan‍navārau

1. kāmadhātu

999. pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ kati kāmadhātupariyāpan‍nā, kati na kāmadhātupariyāpan‍nā…pai॰… sattan‍naṃ cittānaṃ kati kāmadhātupariyāpan‍nā, kati na kāmadhātupariyāpan‍nā?

1000. rūpakkhandhau kāmadhātupariyāpan‍nau; cattārau khandhā siyā kāmadhātupariyāpan‍nā, siyā na kāmadhātupariyāpan‍nā|

dasāyatanā  kāmadhātupariyāpan‍nā; dvai āyatanā siyā kāmadhātupariyāpan‍nā, siyā na kāmadhātupariyāpan‍nā|

sauḷasa dhātuyau kāmadhātupariyāpan‍nā; dvai dhātuyau siyā kāmadhātupariyāpan‍nā, siyā na kāmadhātupariyāpan‍nā|

samudayasac‍caṃ kāmadhātupariyāpan‍naṃ; dvai sac‍cā na kāmadhātupariyāpan‍nā; dukkhasac‍caṃ siyā kāmadhātupariyāpan‍naṃ, siyā na kāmadhātupariyāpan‍naṃ|

dasindriyā kāmadhātupariyāpan‍nā; tīṇindriyā na kāmadhātupariyāpan‍nā; navindriyā siyā kāmadhātupariyāpan‍nā, siyā na kāmadhātupariyāpan‍nā|

tayau akusalahaitū kāmadhātupariyāpan‍nā; cha haitū siyā kāmadhātupariyāpan‍nā, siyā na kāmadhātupariyāpan‍nā|

kabaḷīkārau  āhārau kāmadhātupariyāpan‍nau; tayau āhārā siyā kāmadhātupariyāpan‍nā, siyā na kāmadhātupariyāpan‍nā|

cha phassā kāmadhātupariyāpan‍nā; manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassau  siyā kāmadhātu pariyāpan‍nau, siyā na kāmadhātupariyāpan‍nau|

cha vaidanā…pai॰… cha sañ‍ñā… cha caitanā… cha cittā kāmadhātupariyāpan‍nā; manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu siyā kāmadhātupariyāpan‍nā, siyā na kāmadhātupariyāpan‍nā|

2. rūpadhātu

1001. pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ kati rūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā, kati na rūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā  …pai॰… sattan‍naṃ cittānaṃ kati rūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā, kati na rūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā?

1002. rūpakkhandhau na rūpadhātupariyāpan‍nau; cattārau khandhā  siyā rūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā, siyā na rūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā|

dasāyatanā na rūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā; dvai āyatanā siyā rūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā, siyā na rūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā|

sauḷasa dhātuyau na rūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā; dvai dhātuyau siyā rūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā, siyā na rūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā|

tīṇi sac‍cāni na rūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā; dukkhasac‍caṃ siyā rūpadhātupariyāpan‍naṃ, siyā na rūpadhātupariyāpan‍naṃ|

tairasindriyā na rūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā; navindriyā siyā rūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā, siyā na rūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā|

tayau akusalahaitū na rūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā; cha haitū siyā rūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā, siyā na rūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā|

kabaḷīkārau āhārau na rūpadhātupariyāpan‍nau; tayau āhārā siyā rūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā, siyā na rūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā|

cha phassā na rūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā; manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassau siyā rūpadhātupariyāpan‍nau, siyā na rūpadhātupariyāpan‍nau|

cha  vaidanā…pai॰… cha sañ‍ñā… cha caitanā… cha cittā na rūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā; manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu siyā rūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā, siyā na rūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā|

3. arūpadhātu

1003. pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ kati arūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā, kati na arūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā…pai॰… sattan‍naṃ cittānaṃ kati arūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā, kati na arūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā?

1004. rūpakkhandhau na arūpadhātupariyāpan‍nau; cattārau khandhā siyā arūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā, siyā na arūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā|

dasāyatanā na arūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā; dvai āyatanā siyā arūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā, siyā na arūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā|

sauḷasa dhātuyau na arūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā; dvai dhātuyau siyā arūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā, siyā na arūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā|

tīṇi sac‍cāni na arūpadhātupariyāpan‍nāni|

dukkhasac‍caṃ siyā arūpadhātupariyāpan‍naṃ, siyā na arūpadhātupariyāpan‍naṃ|

cuddasindriyā na arūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā; aṭṭhindriyā siyā  arūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā, siyā na arūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā|

tayau akusalahaitū na arūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā; cha haitū siyā arūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā, siyā na arūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā|

kabaḷīkārau  āhārau na arūpadhātupariyāpan‍nau; tayau āhārā siyā arūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā, siyā na arūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā|

cha phassā na arūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā; manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassau siyā arūpadhātupariyāpan‍nau, siyā na arūpadhātupariyāpan‍nau|

cha vaidanā…pai॰… cha sañ‍ñā … cha caitanā… cha cittā na arūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā; manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu siyā arūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā, siyā na arūpadhātupariyāpan‍nā|

4. pariyāpan‍nāpariyāpan‍naṃ

1005. pañ‍can‍naṃ  khandhānaṃ kati pariyāpan‍nā, kati apariyāpan‍nā…pai॰… sattan‍naṃ cittānaṃ kati pariyāpan‍nā, kati apariyāpan‍nā?

1006. rūpakkhandhau pariyāpan‍nau; cattārau khandhā siyā pariyāpan‍nā, siyā apariyāpan‍nā|

dasāyatanā pariyāpan‍nā; dvai āyatanā siyā pariyāpan‍nā, siyā apariyāpan‍nā|

sauḷasa dhātuyau pariyāpan‍nā; dvai dhātuyau siyā pariyāpan‍nā, siyā apariyāpan‍nā|

dvai sac‍cā pariyāpan‍nā; dvai sac‍cā apariyāpan‍nā|

dasindriyā pariyāpan‍nā, tīṇindriyā apariyāpan‍nā; navindriyā siyā pariyāpan‍nā, siyā apariyāpan‍nā|

tayau akusalahaitū pariyāpan‍nā; cha haitū siyā pariyāpan‍nā, siyā apariyāpan‍nā|

kabaḷīkārau  āhārau pariyāpan‍nau; tayau āhārā siyā pariyāpan‍nā, siyā apariyāpan‍nā|

cha phassā pariyāpan‍nā; manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassau siyā pariyāpan‍nau, siyā apariyāpan‍nau|

cha vaidanā…pai॰… cha sañ‍ñā… cha caitanā… cha cittā pariyāpan‍nā; manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu siyā pariyāpan‍nā, siyā apariyāpan‍nā|

4. dhammadassanavārau

1. kāmadhātu

1007. kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai kati khandhā pātubhavanti…pai॰… kati cittāni pātubhavanti?

kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai sabbaisaṃ  pañ‍cakkhandhā pātubhavanti; kassaci aikādasāyatanāni pātubhavanti; kassaci  dasāyatanāni pātubhavanti; kassaci  aparāni dasāyatanāni pātubhavanti; kassaci navāyatanāni pātubhavanti; kassaci sattāyatanāni pātubhavanti; kassaci aikādasa dhātuyau pātubhavanti; kassaci dasa dhātuyau pātubhavanti; kassaci aparā dasa dhātuyau pātubhavanti; kassaci nava dhātuyau pātubhavanti; kassaci satta dhātuyau pātubhavanti; sabbaisaṃ aikaṃ sac‍caṃ pātubhavati; kassaci cuddasindriyāni pātubhavanti; kassaci tairasindriyāni pātubhavanti; kassaci aparāni tairasindriyāni pātubhavanti; kassaci dvādasindriyāni pātubhavanti; kassaci dasindriyāni pātubhavanti; kassaci navindriyāni pātubhavanti; kassaci aparāni navindriyāni pātubhavanti; kassaci aṭṭhindriyāni pātubhavanti; kassaci aparāni aṭṭhindriyāni pātubhavanti; kassaci sattindriyāni pātubhavanti; kassaci pañ‍cindriyāni pātubhavanti; kassaci cattārindriyāni pātubhavanti; kassaci tayau haitū pātubhavanti; kassaci dvai haitū pātubhavanti; kassaci kaici (syā॰) ahaitukā pātubhavanti; sabbaisaṃ cattārau āhārā pātubhavanti; sabbaisaṃ aikau phassau pātubhavati; sabbaisaṃ aikā vaidanā… aikā sañ‍ñā… aikā caitanā… aikaṃ cittaṃ pātubhavati|

1008. kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai sabbaisaṃ katamai pañ‍cakkhandhā pātubhavanti? rūpakkhandhau…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – kāmadhātuyā  upapattikkhaṇai sabbaisaṃ imai pañ‍cakkhandhā pātubhavanti|

1009. kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai kassa aikādasāyatanāni pātubhavanti? kāmāvacarānaṃ daivānaṃ, paṭhamakappikānaṃ manussānaṃ, aupapātikānaṃ paitānaṃ, aupapātikānaṃ asurānaṃ, aupapātikānaṃ tiracchānagatānaṃ, nairayikānaṃ paripuṇṇāyatanānaṃ upapattikkhaṇai aikādasāyatanāni pātubhavanti – cakkhāyatanaṃ, rūpāyatanaṃ, sautāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, gandhāyatanaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ, rasāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ| kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai aitaisaṃ imāni aikādasāyatanāni pātubhavanti|

kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai kassa dasāyatanāni pātubhavanti? aupapātikānaṃ paitānaṃ, aupapātikānaṃ asurānaṃ, aupapātikānaṃ tiracchānagatānaṃ nairayikānaṃ, jac‍candhānaṃ upapattikkhaṇai dasāyatanāni pātubhavanti  – rūpāyatanaṃ, sautāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, gandhāyatanaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ, rasāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ| kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai aitaisaṃ imāni dasāyatanāni pātubhavanti|

kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai kassa aparāni dasāyatanāni pātubhavanti? aupapātikānaṃ paitānaṃ, aupapātikānaṃ asurānaṃ, aupapātikānaṃ tiracchānagatānaṃ nairayikānaṃ, jac‍cabadhirānaṃ upapattikkhaṇai dasāyatanāni pātubhavanti – cakkhāyatanaṃ, rūpāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, gandhāyatanaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ, rasāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ| kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai aitaisaṃ imāni dasāyatanāni  pātubhavanti|

kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai kassa navāyatanāni pātubhavanti? aupapātikānaṃ paitānaṃ, aupapātikānaṃ asurānaṃ, aupapātikānaṃ tiracchānagatānaṃ nairayikānaṃ, jac‍candhabadhirānaṃ upapattikkhaṇai  navāyatanāni pātubhavanti – rūpāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, gandhāyatanaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ, rasāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ| kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai aitaisaṃ imāni navāyatanāni pātubhavanti|

kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai kassa sattāyatanāni pātubhavanti? gabbhasaiyyakānaṃ sattānaṃ upapattikkhaṇai sattāyatanāni pātubhavanti – rūpāyatanaṃ, gandhāyatanaṃ, rasāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ| kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai aitaisaṃ imāni sattāyatanāni pātubhavanti|

1010. kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai kassa aikādasa dhātuyau pātubhavanti? kāmāvacarānaṃ daivānaṃ, paṭhamakappikānaṃ manussānaṃ, aupapātikānaṃ paitānaṃ, aupapātikānaṃ asurānaṃ, aupapātikānaṃ tiracchānagatānaṃ nairayikānaṃ paripuṇṇāyatanānaṃ upapattikkhaṇai aikādasa dhātuyau pātubhavanti – cakkhudhātu, rūpadhātu, sautadhātu, ghānadhātu, gandhadhātu, jivhādhātu, rasadhātu, kāyadhātu, phauṭṭhabbadhātu, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, dhammadhātu| kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai aitaisaṃ imā aikādasa dhātuyau pātubhavanti|

kāmadhātuyā  upapattikkhaṇai kassa dasa dhātuyau pātubhavanti? aupapātikānaṃ paitānaṃ, aupapātikānaṃ asurānaṃ, aupapātikānaṃ  tiracchānagatānaṃ nairayikānaṃ, jac‍candhānaṃ upapattikkhaṇai dasa dhātuyau pātubhavanti – rūpadhātu, sautadhātu, ghānadhātu, gandhadhātu, jivhādhātu, rasadhātu, kāyadhātu, phauṭṭhabbadhātu, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, dhammadhātu| kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai aitaisaṃ imā dasa dhātuyau pātubhavanti|

kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai kassa aparā dasa dhātuyau pātubhavanti? aupapātikānaṃ paitānaṃ, aupapātikānaṃ asurānaṃ, aupapātikānaṃ tiracchānagatānaṃ nairayikānaṃ, jac‍cabadhirānaṃ upapattikkhaṇai dasa dhātuyau pātubhavanti – cakkhudhātu, rūpadhātu, ghānadhātu, gandhadhātu, jivhādhātu, rasadhātu, kāyadhātu, phauṭṭhabbadhātu, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, dhammadhātu| kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai aitaisaṃ imā dasa dhātuyau pātubhavanti|

kāmadhātuyā  upapattikkhaṇai kassa nava dhātuyau pātubhavanti? aupapātikānaṃ paitānaṃ, aupapātikānaṃ asurānaṃ, aupapātikānaṃ tiracchānagatānaṃ nairayikānaṃ, jac‍candhabadhirānaṃ upapattikkhaṇai nava dhātuyau pātubhavanti – rūpadhātu, ghānadhātu, gandhadhātu, jivhādhātu, rasadhātu, kāyadhātu, phauṭṭhabbadhātu, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, dhammadhātu| kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai aitaisaṃ imā nava dhātuyau pātubhavanti|

kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai kassa satta dhātuyau pātubhavanti? gabbhasaiyyakānaṃ sattānaṃ upapattikkhaṇai satta dhātuyau pātubhavanti – rūpadhātu, gandhadhātu, rasadhātu, kāyadhātu, phauṭṭhabbadhātu, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, dhammadhātu| kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai aitaisaṃ imā satta dhātuyau pātubhavanti|

1011. kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai  sabbaisaṃ katamaṃ aikaṃ sac‍caṃ pātubhavati? dukkhasac‍caṃ – kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai sabbaisaṃ idaṃ aikaṃ sac‍caṃ pātubhavati|

1012. kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai kassa cuddasindriyāni pātubhavanti? kāmāvacarānaṃ daivānaṃ, sahaitukānaṃ ñāṇasampayuttānaṃ upapattikkhaṇai cuddasindriyāni pātubhavanti – cakkhundriyaṃ, sautindriyaṃ, ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, kāyindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ, itthindriyaṃ vā purisindriyaṃ vā, jīvitindriyaṃ, saumanassindriyaṃ vā upaikkhindriyaṃ vā, saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai aitaisaṃ imāni cuddasindriyāni pātubhavanti|

kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai kassa tairasindriyāni pātubhavanti? kāmāvacarānaṃ daivānaṃ sahaitukānaṃ ñāṇavippayuttānaṃ upapattikkhaṇai tairasindriyāni pātubhavanti – cakkhundriyaṃ, sautindriyaṃ, ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, kāyindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ, itthindriyaṃ vā purisindriyaṃ vā, jīvitindriyaṃ, saumanassindriyaṃ vā upaikkhindriyaṃ vā, saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ| kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai aitaisaṃ imāni tairasindriyāni pātubhavanti|

kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai kassa aparāni tairasindriyāni pātubhavanti? paṭhamakappikānaṃ  manussānaṃ sahaitukānaṃ ñāṇasampayuttānaṃ upapattikkhaṇai tairasindriyāni pātubhavanti – cakkhundriyaṃ, sautindriyaṃ, ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, kāyindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ, jīvitindriyaṃ, saumanassindriyaṃ vā upaikkhindriyaṃ vā, saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai aitaisaṃ imāni tairasindriyāni pātubhavanti|

kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai kassa dvādasindriyāni pātubhavanti? paṭhamakappikānaṃ manussānaṃ sahaitukānaṃ ñāṇavippayuttānaṃ upapattikkhaṇai dvādasindriyāni pātubhavanti – cakkhundriyaṃ, sautindriyaṃ, ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, kāyindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ, jīvitindriyaṃ, saumanassindriyaṃ vā upaikkhindriyaṃ vā, saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ| kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai aitaisaṃ imāni dvādasindriyāni pātubhavanti|

kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai kassa dasindriyāni pātubhavanti? gabbhasaiyyakānaṃ sattānaṃ sahaitukānaṃ ñāṇasampayuttānaṃ upapattikkhaṇai dasindriyāni pātubhavanti – kāyindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ, itthindriyaṃ vā purisindriyaṃ vā, jīvitindriyaṃ, saumanassindriyaṃ vā upaikkhindriyaṃ vā, saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai aitaisaṃ imāni dasindriyāni pātubhavanti|

kāmadhātuyā  upapattikkhaṇai kassa navindriyāni pātubhavanti? gabbhasaiyyakānaṃ sattānaṃ sahaitukānaṃ ñāṇavippayuttānaṃ upapattikkhaṇai navindriyāni  pātubhavanti – kāyindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ, itthindriyaṃ vā purisindriyaṃ vā, jīvitindriyaṃ, saumanassindriyaṃ vā upaikkhindriyaṃ vā, saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ| kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai aitaisaṃ imāni navindriyāni pātubhavanti|

kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai  kassa aparāni navindriyāni pātubhavanti? aupapātikānaṃ paitānaṃ, aupapātikānaṃ asurānaṃ, aupapātikānaṃ tiracchānagatānaṃ nairayikānaṃ paripuṇṇāyatanānaṃ upapattikkhaṇai navindriyāni pātubhavanti – cakkhundriyaṃ, sautindriyaṃ, ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, kāyindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ, itthindriyaṃ vā purisindriyaṃ vā, jīvitindriyaṃ, upaikkhindriyaṃ| kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai aitaisaṃ imāni navindriyāni pātubhavanti|

kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai kassa aṭṭhindriyāni pātubhavanti? aupapātikānaṃ paitānaṃ, aupapātikānaṃ asurānaṃ, aupapātikānaṃ tiracchānagatānaṃ nairayikānaṃ, jac‍candhānaṃ upapattikkhaṇai aṭṭhindriyāni pātubhavanti – sautindriyaṃ, ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, kāyindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ, itthindriyaṃ vā purisindriyaṃ vā, jīvitindriyaṃ, upaikkhindriyaṃ| kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai aitaisaṃ imāni aṭṭhindriyāni pātubhavanti|

kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai kassa aparāni aṭṭhindriyāni pātubhavanti? aupapātikānaṃ paitānaṃ, aupapātikānaṃ asurānaṃ, aupapātikānaṃ tiracchānagatānaṃ nairayikānaṃ, jac‍cabadhirānaṃ upapattikkhaṇai aṭṭhindriyāni pātubhavanti – cakkhundriyaṃ, ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, kāyindriyaṃ manindriyaṃ, itthindriyaṃ vā purisindriyaṃ vā, jīvitindriyaṃ, upaikkhindriyaṃ| kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai aitaisaṃ imāni aṭṭhindriyāni pātubhavanti|

kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai kassa sattindriyāni pātubhavanti? aupapātikānaṃ paitānaṃ, aupapātikānaṃ asurānaṃ, aupapātikānaṃ tiracchānagatānaṃ nairayikānaṃ, jac‍candhabadhirānaṃ  upapattikkhaṇai sattindriyāni pātubhavanti – ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, kāyindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ, itthindriyaṃ vā purisindriyaṃ vā, jīvitindriyaṃ, upaikkhindriyaṃ| kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai aitaisaṃ imāni sattindriyāni pātubhavanti|

kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai kassa pañ‍cindriyāni pātubhavanti? gabbhasaiyyakānaṃ sattānaṃ ahaitukānaṃ, ṭhapaitvā napuṃsakānaṃ, upapattikkhaṇai pañ‍cindriyāni  pātubhavanti – kāyindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ, itthindriyaṃ vā purisindriyaṃ vā, jīvitindriyaṃ, upaikkhindriyaṃ| kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai aitaisaṃ imāni pañ‍cindriyāni pātubhavanti|

kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai kassa cattārindriyāni pātubhavanti? gabbhasaiyyakānaṃ sattānaṃ ahaitukānaṃ, napuṃsakānaṃ upapattikkhaṇai cattārindriyāni pātubhavanti – kāyindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ, jīvitindriyaṃ, upaikkhindriyaṃ| kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai aitaisaṃ imāni cattārindriyāni pātubhavanti|

1013. kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai kassa tayau haitū pātubhavanti? kāmāvacarānaṃ daivānaṃ, paṭhamakappikānaṃ manussānaṃ, gabbhasaiyyakānaṃ sattānaṃ sahaitukānaṃ ñāṇasampayuttānaṃ upapattikkhaṇai tayau haitū pātubhavanti – alaubhau vipākahaitu, adausau vipākahaitu, amauhau vipākahaitu| kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai aitaisaṃ imai tayau haitū pātubhavanti|

kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai kassa dvai haitū pātubhavanti? kāmāvacarānaṃ daivānaṃ, paṭhamakappikānaṃ manussānaṃ, gabbhasaiyyakānaṃ sattānaṃ sahaitukānaṃ ñāṇavippayuttānaṃ upapattikkhaṇai  dvai haitū pātubhavanti – alaubhau vipākahaitu, adausau vipākahaitu| kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai aitaisaṃ imai dvai haitū pātubhavanti| avasaisānaṃ sattānaṃ avasaisā sattā (?) ahaitukā pātubhavanti|

1014. kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai sabbaisaṃ katamai cattārau āhārā pātubhavanti| kabaḷīkārau āhārau, phassāhārau, manausañ‍caitanāhārau, viñ‍ñāṇāhārau – kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai sabbaisaṃ imai cattārau āhārā pātubhavanti|

kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai sabbaisaṃ katamau aikau phassau pātubhavati? manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassau – kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai sabbaisaṃ ayaṃ aikau phassau pātubhavati|

kāmadhātuyā  upapattikkhaṇai sabbaisaṃ katamā aikā vaidanā … aikā sañ‍ñā… aikā caitanā… aikaṃ cittaṃ pātubhavati? manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – kāmadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai sabbaisaṃ idaṃ aikaṃ cittaṃ pātubhavati|

2. rūpadhātu

1015. rūpadhātuyā  upapattikkhaṇai kati khandhā pātubhavanti…pai॰… kati cittāni pātubhavanti?

rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai, ṭhapaitvā asañ‍ñasattānaṃ daivānaṃ, pañ‍cakkhandhā pātubhavanti, pañ‍cāyatanāni pātubhavanti, pañ‍ca dhātuyau pātubhavanti, aikaṃ sac‍caṃ pātubhavati, dasindriyāni pātubhavanti, tayau haitū pātubhavanti, tayau āhārā pātubhavanti, aikau phassau pātubhavati, aikā vaidanā… aikā sañ‍ñā… aikā caitanā… aikaṃ cittaṃ pātubhavati|

1016. rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai katamai pañ‍cakkhandhā pātubhavanti? rūpakkhandhau, vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai imai pañ‍cakkhandhā pātubhavanti|

rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai katamāni pañ‍cāyatanāni pātubhavanti? cakkhāyatanaṃ, rūpāyatanaṃ, sautāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ – rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai imāni pañ‍cāyatanāni pātubhavanti|

rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai katamā pañ‍ca dhātuyau pātubhavanti? cakkhudhātu, rūpadhātu, sautadhātu, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, dhammadhātu – rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai imā pañ‍ca dhātuyau pātubhavanti|

rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai katamaṃ aikaṃ sac‍caṃ pātubhavati? dukkhasac‍caṃ – rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai idaṃ aikaṃ sac‍caṃ pātubhavati|

rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai katamāni dasindriyāni pātubhavanti? cakkhundriyaṃ, sautindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ, jīvitindriyaṃ, saumanassindriyaṃ vā upaikkhindriyaṃ vā, saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ – rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai imāni dasindriyāni pātubhavanti|

rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai katamai tayau haitū pātubhavanti? alaubhau vipākahaitu, adausau vipākahaitu, amauhau vipākahaitu  – rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai imai tayau haitū pātubhavanti|

rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai katamai tayau āhārā pātubhavanti? phassāhārau, manausañ‍caitanāhārau, viñ‍ñāṇāhārau – rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai imai tayau āhārā pātubhavanti|

rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai katamau aikau phassau pātubhavati? manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassau  – rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai ayaṃ aikau phassau pātubhavati|

rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai katamā aikā vaidanā… aikā sañ‍ñā… aikā caitanā… aikaṃ cittaṃ pātubhavati? manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – rūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai idaṃ aikaṃ cittaṃ pātubhavati|

3. asañ‍ñasattā

1017. asañ‍ñasattānaṃ daivānaṃ upapattikkhaṇai kati khandhā pātubhavanti…pai॰… kati cittāni pātubhavanti?

asañ‍ñasattānaṃ daivānaṃ upapattikkhaṇai aikau khandhau pātubhavati – rūpakkhandhau; dvai āyatanāni pātubhavanti – rūpāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ; dvai dhātuyau pātubhavanti – rūpadhātu, dhammadhātu; aikaṃ sac‍caṃ pātubhavati – dukkhasac‍caṃ; aikindriyaṃ pātubhavati – rūpajīvitindriyaṃ| asañ‍ñasattā daivā ahaitukā anāhārā aphassakā avaidanakā asañ‍ñakā acaitanakā acittakā pātubhavanti|

4. arūpadhātu

1018. arūpadhātuyā  upapattikkhaṇai kati khandhā pātubhavanti…pai॰… kati cittāni pātubhavanti?

arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai cattārau khandhā pātubhavanti, dvai āyatanāni pātubhavanti, dvai dhātuyau pātubhavanti, aikaṃ sac‍caṃ pātubhavati, aṭṭhindriyāni pātubhavanti, tayau haitū pātubhavanti, tayau āhārā pātubhavanti, aikau phassau pātubhavati, aikā vaidanā… aikā sañ‍ñā… aikā  caitanā… aikaṃ cittaṃ pātubhavati|

1019. arūpadhātuyā  upapattikkhaṇai katamai cattārau khandhā pātubhavanti? vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai imai cattārau khandhā pātubhavanti|

arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai katamāni dvai āyatanāni pātubhavanti? manāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ – arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai imāni dvai āyatanāni pātubhavanti|

arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai katamā dvai dhātuyau pātubhavanti? manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, dhammadhātu – arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai imā dvai dhātuyau pātubhavanti|

arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai katamaṃ aikaṃ sac‍caṃ pātubhavati? dukkhasac‍caṃ – arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai idaṃ aikaṃ sac‍caṃ pātubhavati|

arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai katamāni aṭṭhindriyāni pātubhavanti? manindriyaṃ, jīvitindriyaṃ, upaikkhindriyaṃ, saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñindriyaṃ – arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai imāni aṭṭhindriyāni pātubhavanti|

arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai katamai tayau haitū pātubhavanti? alaubhau vipākahaitu, adausau  vipākahaitu, amauhau vipākahaitu – arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai imai tayau haitū pātubhavanti|

arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai katamai tayau āhārā pātubhavanti? phassāhārau, manausañ‍caitanāhārau, viñ‍ñāṇāhārau – arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai imai tayau āhārā pātubhavanti|

arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai katamau aikau phassau pātubhavati? manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassau  – arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai ayaṃ aikau phassau pātubhavati|

arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai katamā aikā vaidanā…pai॰… aikā sañ‍ñā… aikā caitanā… aikaṃ cittaṃ pātubhavati? manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu – arūpadhātuyā upapattikkhaṇai idaṃ aikaṃ cittaṃ pātubhavati|

5. bhūmantaradassanavārau

1020. kāmāvacarā  dhammā, na kāmāvacarā dhammā, rūpāvacarā dhammā, na rūpāvacarā dhammā, arūpāvacarā dhammā, na arūpāvacarā dhammā, pariyāpan‍nā dhammā, apariyāpan‍nā dhammā|

katamai dhammā kāmāvacarā? haiṭṭhatau avīcinirayaṃ pariyantaṃ karitvā, uparitau paranimmitavasavattī daivai antaukaritvā, yaṃ aitasmiṃ antarai aitthāvacarā aittha pariyāpan‍nā khandhadhātuāyatanā; rūpaṃ, vaidanā, sañ‍ñā, saṅkhārā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ – imai dhammā kāmāvacarā|

katamai dhammā na kāmāvacarā? rūpāvacarā, arūpāvacarā, apariyāpan‍nā – imai dhammā na kāmāvacarā|

katamai dhammā rūpāvacarā? haiṭṭhatau brahmalaukaṃ pariyantaṃ karitvā, uparitau akaniṭṭhai daivai antaukaritvā, yaṃ aitasmiṃ antarai aitthāvacarā aittha pariyāpan‍nā samāpan‍nassa vā upapan‍nassa vā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārissa vā cittacaitasikā dhammā – imai dhammā rūpāvacarā|

katamai dhammā na rūpāvacarā? kāmāvacarā, arūpāvacarā, apariyāpan‍nā – imai dhammā na rūpāvacarā|

katamai dhammā arūpāvacarā? haiṭṭhatau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanūpagai  daivai pariyantaṃ karitvā, uparitau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanūpagai daivai antaukaritvā, yaṃ aitasmiṃ antarai aitthāvacarā aittha pariyāpan‍nā samāpan‍nassa vā upapan‍nassa vā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārissa vā cittacaitasikā dhammā – imai dhammā arūpāvacarā|

katamai dhammā na arūpāvacarā? kāmāvacarā, rūpāvacarā, apariyāpan‍nā – imai dhammā na arūpāvacarā|

katamai dhammā pariyāpan‍nā? sāsavā kusalākusalabyākatā dhammā kāmāvacarā, rūpāvacarā, arūpāvacarā, rūpakkhandhau, vaidanākkhandhau, sañ‍ñākkhandhau, saṅkhārakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau – imai dhammā pariyāpan‍nā|

katamai dhammā apariyāpan‍nā? maggā ca, maggaphalāni ca, asaṅkhatā ca dhātu – imai dhammā apariyāpan‍nā|

6. uppādakakammaāyuppamāṇavārau

1. uppādakakammaṃ

1021. daivāti| tayau daivā – sammutidaivā sammatidaivā (syā॰), upapattidaivā, visuddhidaivā|

sammutidaivā nāma – rājānau, daiviyau, kumārā|

upapattidaivā  nāma – cātumahārājikai cātummahārājikai (sī॰ syā॰) daivai upādāya tadupari daivā|

visuddhidaivā nāma – arahantau vuc‍canti|

dānaṃ datvā, sīlaṃ samādiyitvā, upausathakammaṃ katvā kattha upapaj‍janti? dānaṃ datvā, sīlaṃ samādiyitvā, upausathakammaṃ katvā appaikac‍cai khattiyamahāsālānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍janti, appaikac‍cai brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ  sahabyataṃ upapaj‍janti, appaikac‍cai gahapatimahāsālānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍janti, appaikac‍cai cātumahārājikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍janti, appaikac‍cai tāvatiṃsānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍janti, appaikac‍cai yāmānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍janti, appaikac‍cai tusitānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍janti, appaikac‍cai nimmānaratīnaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍janti, appaikac‍cai paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍janti|

2. āyuppamāṇaṃ

1022. manussānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? vassasataṃ, appaṃ vā bhiyyau appaṃ vā bhiyyau vā (syā॰ ka॰) dī॰ ni॰ 2.7|

1023. cātumahārājikānaṃ daivānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? yāni mānusakāni pañ‍ñāsa vassāni, cātummahārājikānaṃ daivānaṃ aisau aikau rattindivau rattidivau (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 3.71| tāya rattiyā tiṃsa rattiyau māsau| taina māsaina dvādasamāsiyau saṃvaccharau| taina saṃvaccharaina dibbāni pañ‍ca vassasatāni cātummahārājikānaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ| manussānaṃ gaṇanāya kittakaṃ hauti? navuti vassasatasahassāni|

tāvatiṃsānaṃ  daivānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? yaṃ mānusakaṃ vassasataṃ, tāvatiṃsānaṃ daivānaṃ aisau aikau rattindivau| tāya rattiyā tiṃsa rattiyau māsau| taina māsaina dvādasamāsiyau saṃvaccharau| taina saṃvaccharaina dibbaṃ vassasahassaṃ tāvatiṃsānaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ| manussānaṃ gaṇanāya kittakaṃ hauti? tissau ca vassakauṭiyau saṭṭhi ca vassasatasahassāni|

yāmānaṃ  daivānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? yāni mānusakāni dvai vassasatāni, yāmānaṃ daivānaṃ aisau aikau  rattindivau| tāya rattiyā tiṃsarattiyau māsau| taina māsaina dvādasamāsiyau saṃvaccharau| taina saṃvaccharaina dibbāni dvai vassasahassāni yāmānaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ| manussānaṃ gaṇanāya kittakaṃ hauti? cuddasañ‍ca vassakauṭiyau cattārīsañ‍ca vassasatasahassāni|

tusitānaṃ daivānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? yāni mānusakāni cattāri vassasatāni, tusitānaṃ daivānaṃ aisau aikau rattindivau| tāya rattiyā tiṃsarattiyau māsau| taina māsaina dvādasamāsiyau saṃvaccharau| taina saṃvaccharaina dibbāni cattāri vassasahassāni tusitānaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ| manussānaṃ gaṇanāya kittakaṃ hauti? sattapañ‍ñāsa vassakauṭiyau saṭṭhi ca vassasatasahassāni|

nimmānaratīnaṃ daivānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? yāni mānusakāni aṭṭha vassasatāni, nimmānaratīnaṃ daivānaṃ aisau aikau rattindivau| tāya rattiyā tiṃsarattiyau māsau| taina māsaina dvādasamāsiyau saṃvaccharau| taina saṃvaccharaina dibbāni aṭṭha vassasahassāni nimmānaratīnaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ| manussānaṃ gaṇanāya kittakaṃ hauti? dvai vassakauṭisatāni tiṃsañ‍ca vassakauṭiyau cattārīsañ‍ca vassasatasahassāni|

paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ daivānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? yāni mānusakāni sauḷasa vassasatāni, paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ daivānaṃ aisau aikau rattindivau| tāya rattiyā tiṃsarattiyau māsau| taina māsaina dvādasamāsiyau saṃvaccharau| taina saṃvaccharaina dibbāni sauḷasa vassasahassāni paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ daivānaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ| manussānaṃ gaṇanāya kittakaṃ  hauti? nava ca vassakauṭisatāni aikavīsañ‍ca vassakauṭiyau saṭṭhi ca vassasatasahassānīti|

cha aitai chapi tai (syā॰) kāmāvacarā, sabbakāmasamiddhinau|

sabbaisaṃ aikasaṅkhātau, āyu bhavati kittakau||

dvādasa  kauṭisataṃ taisaṃ, aṭṭhavīsañ‍ca kauṭiyau|

pañ‍ñāsa satasahassāni, vassaggaina pakāsitāti||

1024. paṭhamaṃ  jhānaṃ parittaṃ bhāvaitvā kattha upapaj‍janti? paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ parittaṃ bhāvaitvā brahmapārisaj‍jānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍janti| taisaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? kappassa tatiyau bhāgau|

paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ majjhimaṃ bhāvaitvā kattha upapaj‍janti? paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ majjhimaṃ bhāvaitvā brahmapurauhitānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍janti| taisaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? upaḍḍhakappau|

paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ paṇītaṃ bhāvaitvā kattha upapaj‍janti? paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ paṇītaṃ bhāvaitvā mahābrahmānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍janti| taisaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? kappau aikau kappau (syā॰)|

1025. dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ parittaṃ bhāvaitvā kattha upapaj‍janti? dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ parittaṃ bhāvaitvā parittābhānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍janti| taisaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? dvai kappā|

dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ majjhimaṃ bhāvaitvā kattha upapaj‍janti? dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ majjhimaṃ bhāvaitvā appamāṇābhānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍janti| taisaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? cattārau kappā|

dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ paṇītaṃ  bhāvaitvā kattha upapaj‍janti? dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ paṇītaṃ bhāvaitvā ābhassarānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍janti| taisaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? aṭṭha kappā|

1026. tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ parittaṃ bhāvaitvā kattha upapaj‍janti? tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ parittaṃ bhāvaitvā parittasubhānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍janti| taisaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? sauḷasa kappā|

tatiyaṃ  jhānaṃ majjhimaṃ bhāvaitvā kattha upapaj‍janti? tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ majjhimaṃ bhāvaitvā appamāṇasubhānaṃ  daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍janti| taisaṃ  kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? bāttiṃsa kappā|

tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ paṇītaṃ bhāvaitvā kattha upapaj‍janti? tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ paṇītaṃ bhāvaitvā subhakiṇhānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍janti| taisaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? catusaṭṭhi kappā|

1027. catutthaṃ jhānaṃ bhāvaitvā ārammaṇanānattatā manasikāranānattatā chandanānattatā paṇidhinānattatā adhimaukkhanānattatā abhinīhāranānattatā pañ‍ñānānattatā appaikac‍cai asañ‍ñasattānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍janti, appaikac‍cai vaihapphalānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍janti, appaikac‍cai avihānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍janti, appaikac‍cai atappānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍janti, appaikac‍cai sudassānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍janti, appaikac‍cai sudassīnaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍janti, appaikac‍cai akaniṭṭhānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍janti, appaikac‍cai ākāsānañ‍cāyatanūpagānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍janti, appaikac‍cai viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanūpagānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ  upapaj‍janti, appaikac‍cai ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanūpagānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍janti, appaikac‍cai naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanūpagānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍janti|

asañ‍ñasattānañ‍ca vaihapphalānañ‍ca daivānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? pañ‍cakappasatāni|

avihānaṃ daivānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? kappasahassaṃ|

atappānaṃ daivānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? dvai kappasahassāni|

sudassānaṃ daivānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? cattāri kappasahassāni|

sudassīnaṃ daivānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? aṭṭha kappasahassāni|

akaniṭṭhānaṃ daivānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? sauḷasa kappasahassāni|

1028. ākāsānañ‍cāyatanūpagānaṃ daivānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? vīsati kappasahassāni|

viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanūpagānaṃ  daivānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? cattārīsa kappasahassāni|

ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanūpagānaṃ  daivānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? saṭṭhi kappasahassāni|

naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanūpagānaṃ daivānaṃ kittakaṃ āyuppamāṇaṃ? caturāsīti kappasahassānīti|

1029. ukkhittā puñ‍ñataijaina, kāmarūpagatiṃ gatā|

bhavaggatampi bhavaggaṃ vāpi (syā॰) sampattā, punāgacchanti puna gacchanti (syā॰) duggatiṃ||

tāva dīghāyukā sattā, cavanti āyusaṅkhayā|

natthi kauci bhavau nic‍cau, iti vuttaṃ mahaisinā||

tasmā hi dhīrā nipakā, nipuṇā atthacintakā|

jarāmaraṇamaukkhāya, bhāvainti maggamuttamaṃ||

bhāvayitvā  suciṃ maggaṃ, nibbānaugadhagāminaṃ|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, parinibbanti anāsavāti||

7. abhiñ‍ñaiyyādivārau

1030. pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ kati abhiñ‍ñaiyyā, kati pariñ‍ñaiyyā, kati pahātabbā, kati bhāvaitabbā, kati sacchikātabbā, kati na pahātabbā, na bhāvaitabbā, na sacchikātabbā…pai॰… sattan‍naṃ cittānaṃ kati abhiñ‍ñaiyyā, kati pariñ‍ñaiyyā, kati pahātabbā, kati bhāvaitabbā, kati sacchikātabbā, kati na pahātabbā na bhāvaitabbā na sacchikātabbā?

1031. rūpakkhandhau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau pariñ‍ñaiyyau na pahātabbau na bhāvaitabbau na sacchikātabbau| cattārau khandhā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā pariñ‍ñaiyyā, siyā pahātabbā, siyā bhāvaitabbā, siyā sacchikātabbā, siyā na pahātabbā na bhāvaitabbā na sacchikātabbā|

dasāyatanā  abhiñ‍ñaiyyā pariñ‍ñaiyyā na pahātabbā na bhāvaitabbā na sacchikātabbā| dvai āyatanā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā pariñ‍ñaiyyā, siyā pahātabbā, siyā bhāvaitabbā, siyā sacchikātabbā, siyā na pahātabbā na bhāvaitabbā na sacchikātabbā|

sauḷasa dhātuyau abhiñ‍ñaiyyā pariñ‍ñaiyyā na pahātabbā na bhāvaitabbā na sacchikātabbā| dvai dhātuyau abhiñ‍ñaiyyā pariñ‍ñaiyyā, siyā pahātabbā, siyā bhāvaitabbā, siyā sacchikātabbā, siyā na pahātabbā na bhāvaitabbā na sacchikātabbā|

samudayasac‍caṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ pahātabbaṃ na  bhāvaitabbaṃ na sacchikātabbaṃ| maggasac‍caṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ na pahātabbaṃ bhāvaitabbaṃ na sacchikātabbaṃ| niraudhasac‍caṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ na pahātabbaṃ na bhāvaitabbaṃ sacchikātabbaṃ| dukkhasac‍caṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ, siyā pahātabbaṃ, na bhāvaitabbaṃ, na sacchikātabbaṃ, siyā na pahātabbaṃ|

navindriyā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā pariñ‍ñaiyyā na pahātabbā na bhāvaitabbā na sacchikātabbā| daumanassindriyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ pahātabbaṃ na bhāvaitabbaṃ na sacchikātabbaṃ| anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ na pahātabbaṃ bhāvaitabbaṃ na sacchikātabbaṃ| añ‍ñindriyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ na pahātabbaṃ, siyā bhāvaitabbaṃ, siyā sacchikātabbaṃ| añ‍ñātāvindriyaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ na pahātabbaṃ na bhāvaitabbaṃ sacchikātabbaṃ| tīṇindriyā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā pariñ‍ñaiyyā na pahātabbā, siyā bhāvaitabbā, siyā sacchikātabbā, siyā na bhāvaitabbā, sacchikātabbā| cha indriyā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā pariñ‍ñaiyyā, siyā pahātabbā, siyā bhāvaitabbā, siyā sacchikātabbā, siyā na pahātabbā na bhāvaitabbā na sacchikātabbā|

tayau akusalahaitū abhiñ‍ñaiyyā pariñ‍ñaiyyā pahātabbā na bhāvaitabbā na sacchikātabbā| tayau kusalahaitū abhiñ‍ñaiyyā pariñ‍ñaiyyā na pahātabbā, siyā bhāvaitabbā, na sacchikātabbā, siyā na bhāvaitabbā| tayau abyākatahaitū abhiñ‍ñaiyyā pariñ‍ñaiyyā na pahātabbā na bhāvaitabbā, siyā sacchikātabbā, siyā na sacchikātabbā|

kabaḷīkārau  āhārau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau pariñ‍ñaiyyau na pahātabbau na bhāvaitabbau na sacchikātabbau| tayau āhārā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā pariñ‍ñaiyyā, siyā pahātabbā, siyā bhāvaitabbā, siyā sacchikātabbā, siyā na pahātabbā na bhāvaitabbā na sacchikātabbā|

cha phassā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā pariñ‍ñaiyyā na pahātabbā na bhāvaitabbā na sacchikātabbā| manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassau abhiñ‍ñaiyyau pariñ‍ñaiyyau, siyā pahātabbau, siyā bhāvaitabbau, siyā sacchikātabbau, siyā na pahātabbau na bhāvaitabbau na sacchikātabbau|

cha vaidanā…pai॰… cha sañ‍ñā… cha caitanā… cha cittā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā pariñ‍ñaiyyā na pahātabbā na bhāvaitabbā na sacchikātabbā| manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu abhiñ‍ñaiyyā pariñ‍ñaiyyā, siyā pahātabbā, siyā bhāvaitabbā, siyā sacchikātabbā, siyā na pahātabbā na bhāvaitabbā na sacchikātabbā|

8. sārammaṇānārammaṇavārau

1032. pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ kati sārammaṇā, kati anārammaṇā…pai॰… sattan‍naṃ cittānaṃ kati sārammaṇā, kati anārammaṇā?

1033. rūpakkhandhau anārammaṇau| cattārau khandhā sārammaṇā|

dasāyatanā anārammaṇā| manāyatanaṃ sārammaṇaṃ| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā sārammaṇaṃ, siyā anārammaṇaṃ|

dasa dhātuyau anārammaṇā| satta dhātuyau sārammaṇā| dhammadhātu siyā sārammaṇā, siyā anārammaṇā|

dvai  sac‍cā sārammaṇā| niraudhasac‍caṃ anārammaṇaṃ| dukkhasac‍caṃ siyā sārammaṇaṃ, siyā anārammaṇaṃ|

sattindriyā  anārammaṇā| cuddasindriyā sārammaṇā| jīvitindriyaṃ siyā sārammaṇaṃ, siyā anārammaṇaṃ| nava haitū sārammaṇā| kabaḷīkārau āhārau anārammaṇau| tayau āhārā sārammaṇā| satta phassā… satta vaidanā… satta sañ‍ñā… satta caitanā… satta cittā sārammaṇā|

1034. pañ‍can‍naṃ  khandhānaṃ kati sārammaṇārammaṇā, kati anārammaṇārammaṇā…pai॰… sattan‍naṃ cittānaṃ kati sārammaṇārammaṇā, kati anārammaṇārammaṇā?

1035. rūpakkhandhau anārammaṇau| cattārau khandhā siyā sārammaṇārammaṇā, siyā anārammaṇārammaṇā|

dasāyatanā anārammaṇā| manāyatanaṃ siyā sārammaṇārammaṇaṃ, siyā anārammaṇārammaṇaṃ| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā sārammaṇārammaṇaṃ, siyā anārammaṇārammaṇaṃ, siyā anārammaṇaṃ|

dasa dhātuyau anārammaṇā| cha dhātuyau anārammaṇārammaṇā| manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu siyā sārammaṇārammaṇā, siyā anārammaṇārammaṇā| dhammadhātu siyā sārammaṇārammaṇā, siyā anārammaṇārammaṇā, siyā anārammaṇā|

niraudhasac‍caṃ anārammaṇaṃ| maggasac‍caṃ anārammaṇārammaṇaṃ| samudayasac‍caṃ siyā sārammaṇārammaṇaṃ, siyā anārammaṇārammaṇaṃ| dukkhasac‍caṃ  siyā sārammaṇārammaṇaṃ, siyā anārammaṇārammaṇaṃ, siyā anārammaṇaṃ|

sattindriyā anārammaṇā| pañ‍cindriyā  anārammaṇārammaṇā| navindriyā siyā sārammaṇārammaṇā, siyā anārammaṇārammaṇā| jīvitindriyaṃ siyā sārammaṇārammaṇaṃ, siyā anārammaṇārammaṇaṃ, siyā anārammaṇaṃ|

nava haitū siyā sārammaṇārammaṇā, siyā anārammaṇārammaṇā| kabaḷīkārau āhārau anārammaṇau| tayau āhārā siyā sārammaṇārammaṇā, siyā anārammaṇārammaṇā| cha phassā anārammaṇārammaṇā| manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassau siyā sārammaṇārammaṇau siyā anārammaṇārammaṇau| cha vaidanā… cha sañ‍ñā… cha caitanā… cha cittā anārammaṇārammaṇā| manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu siyā sārammaṇārammaṇā, siyā anārammaṇārammaṇā|

9. diṭṭhasutādidassanavārau

1036. pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ kati diṭṭhā, kati sutā, kati mutā, kati viñ‍ñātā, kati na diṭṭhā na sutā na mutā na viñ‍ñātā…pai॰… sattan‍naṃ cittānaṃ kati  diṭṭhā, kati sutā, kati mutā, kati viñ‍ñātā, kati na diṭṭhā na sutā na mutā na viñ‍ñātā?

1037. rūpakkhandhau siyā diṭṭhau, siyā sutau, siyā mutau, siyā viñ‍ñātau, siyā na diṭṭhau na sutau na mutau, viñ‍ñātau| cattārau khandhā na diṭṭhā na sutā na mutā, viñ‍ñātā|

rūpāyatanaṃ diṭṭhaṃ, na sutaṃ na mutaṃ, viñ‍ñātaṃ| saddāyatanaṃ na diṭṭhaṃ, sutaṃ, na mutaṃ, viñ‍ñātaṃ| gandhāyatanaṃ… rasāyatanaṃ… phauṭṭhabbāyatanaṃ na diṭṭhaṃ na sutaṃ, mutaṃ, viñ‍ñātaṃ| sattāyatanā na diṭṭhā na sutā na  mutā, viñ‍ñātā|

rūpadhātu diṭṭhā, na sutā na mutā, viñ‍ñātā| saddadhātu na diṭṭhā, sutā, na mutā, viñ‍ñātā| gandhadhātu… rasadhātu… phauṭṭhabbadhātu na diṭṭhā na sutā, mutā, viñ‍ñātā| tairasa dhātuyau na diṭṭhā na sutā na mutā, viñ‍ñātā|

tīṇi sac‍cāni na diṭṭhā na sutā na mutā, viñ‍ñātā| dukkhasac‍caṃ siyā diṭṭhaṃ, siyā sutaṃ, siyā mutaṃ, siyā viñ‍ñātaṃ, siyā na diṭṭhaṃ na sutaṃ na mutaṃ, viñ‍ñātaṃ|

bāvīsatindriyā na diṭṭhā na sutā na mutā, viñ‍ñātā| nava haitū na diṭṭhā na sutā na mutā, viñ‍ñātā| cattārau āhārā na diṭṭhā na sutā na mutā, viñ‍ñātā| satta  phassā  na diṭṭhā na sutā na mutā, viñ‍ñātā| satta vaidanā… satta sañ‍ñā… satta caitanā… satta cittā na diṭṭhā na sutā na mutā, viñ‍ñātā|

10. tikādidassanavārau

1. kusalattikaṃ

1038. pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ kati kusalā, kati akusalā, kati abyākatā…pai॰… sattan‍naṃ cittānaṃ kati kusalā, kati akusalā, kati abyākatā?

rūpakkhandhau abyākatau| cattārau khandhā siyā kusalā, siyā akusalā, siyā abyākatā| dasāyatanā abyākatā| dvāyatanā siyā kusalā, siyā akusalā, siyā abyākatā| sauḷasa dhātuyau  abyākatā| dvai dhātuyau siyā kusalā, siyā akusalā, siyā abyākatā| samudayasac‍caṃ akusalaṃ| maggasac‍caṃ kusalaṃ| niraudhasac‍caṃ abyākataṃ| dukkhasac‍caṃ siyā kusalaṃ, siyā akusalaṃ, siyā abyākataṃ|

dasindriyā  abyākatā| daumanassindriyaṃ akusalaṃ| anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ kusalaṃ| cattārindriyā siyā kusalā, siyā abyākatā| cha indriyā siyā kusalā, siyā akusalā, siyā abyākatā|

tayau kusalahaitū kusalā| tayau akusalahaitū akusalā| tayau abyākatahaitū abyākatā| kabaḷīkārau āhārau abyākatau| tayau āhārā siyā kusalā, siyā akusalā, siyā abyākatā| cha phassā abyākatā| manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassau siyā kusalau, siyā akusalau, siyā abyākatau| cha vaidanā… cha sañ‍ñā… cha caitanā… cha cittā abyākatā| manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu siyā kusalā, siyā akusalā, siyā abyākatā|

2. vaidanātikaṃ

1039. pañ‍can‍naṃ  khandhānaṃ kati sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, kati dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, kati adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā…pai॰… sattan‍naṃ cittānaṃ kati sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, kati dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, kati adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā?

dvai khandhā na vattabbā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttātipi, dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttātipi, adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttātipi| tayau khandhā siyā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā|

dasāyatanā  na vattabbā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttātipi, dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttātipi, adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttātipi| manāyatanaṃ siyā  sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ, siyā dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ, siyā dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ, siyā na vattabbaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya  sampayuttantipi, dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttantipi, adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttantipi|

dasa dhātuyau na vattabbā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttātipi, dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttātipi, adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttātipi| pañ‍ca dhātuyau adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu siyā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā| manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu siyā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā| dhammadhātu siyā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā na vattabbā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttātipi, dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttātipi, adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttātipi|

dvai  sac‍cā siyā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā| niraudhasac‍caṃ na vattabbaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttantipi, dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttantipi, adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttantipi| dukkhasac‍caṃ siyā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ, siyā dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya  sampayuttaṃ, siyā na vattabbaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttantipi, dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttantipi, adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttantipi|

dvādasindriyā na vattabbā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttātipi, dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttātipi, adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttātipi| cha indriyā siyā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā| tīṇindriyā siyā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā| jīvitindriyaṃ siyā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ, siyā dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttaṃ, siyā na vattabbaṃ sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttantipi, dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttantipi, adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttantipi|

dausau  akusalahaitu dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau| satta haitū siyā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā| mauhau akusalahaitu siyā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau, siyā dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau, siyā dukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau|

kabaḷīkārau āhārau na vattabbau sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttauti pi, dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttautipi, adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttautipi| tayau āhārā siyā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā|

pañ‍ca phassā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā| kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassau siyā  sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau, siyā dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau| manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassau siyā  sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau, siyā dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttau|

satta vaidanā na vattabbā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttātipi, dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttātipi, adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttātipi| pañ‍ca sañ‍ñā… pañ‍ca caitanā… pañ‍ca cittā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu siyā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā| manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu siyā sukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā dukkhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā, siyā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya sampayuttā|

3. vipākattikaṃ

1040. pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ kati vipākā, kati vipākadhammadhammā, kati naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā…pai॰… sattan‍naṃ cittānaṃ kati vipākā, kati vipākadhammadhammā, kati naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā?

rūpakkhandhau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau| cattārau khandhā siyā vipākā, siyā vipākadhammadhammā, siyā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā|

dasāyatanā  naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā| dvāyatanā siyā vipākā, siyā vipākadhammadhammā, siyā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā|

dasa dhātuyau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā| pañ‍ca dhātuyau vipākā| manaudhātu siyā vipākā, siyā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā| dvai dhātuyau siyā vipākā siyā vipākadhammadhammā, siyā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā|

dvai  sac‍cā vipākadhammadhammā| niraudhasac‍caṃ naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ| dukkhasac‍caṃ siyā vipākaṃ, siyā vipākadhammadhammaṃ, siyā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ|

sattindriyā  naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā| tīṇindriyā vipākā| dvindriyā vipākadhammadhammā| añ‍ñindriyaṃ siyā vipākaṃ, siyā vipākadhammadhammaṃ| navindriyā siyā vipākā, siyā vipākadhammadhammā, siyā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā|

cha haitū vipākadhammadhammā| tayau abyākatahaitū siyā vipākā, siyā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā|

kabaḷīkārau āhārau naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau| tayau āhārā siyā vipākā, siyā vipākadhammadhammā, siyā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā| pañ‍ca phassā vipākā| manaudhātusamphassau siyā vipākau, siyā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau| manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassau siyā vipākau, siyā vipākadhammadhammau, siyā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammau| pañ‍ca vaidanā… pañ‍ca sañ‍ñā… pañ‍ca caitanā… pañ‍ca cittā vipākā| manaudhātu siyā vipākā, siyā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā| manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu siyā vipākā, siyā vipākadhammadhammā, siyā naivavipākanavipākadhammadhammā|

4. upādin‍nattikaṃ

1041. pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ kati upādin‍nupādāniyā, kati anupādin‍nupādāniyā, kati anupādin‍naanupādāniyā…pai॰… sattan‍naṃ cittānaṃ kati upādin‍nupādāniyā, kati anupādin‍nupādāniyā, kati anupādin‍naanupādāniyā?

rūpakkhandhau  siyā upādin‍nupādāniyau, siyā anupādin‍nupādāniyau 4| cattārau khandhā siyā upādin‍nupādāniyā, siyā anupādin‍nupādāniyā, siyā anupādin‍naanupādāniyā|

pañ‍cāyatanā  upādin‍nupādāniyā| saddāyatanaṃ anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ| cattārau āyatanā siyā upādin‍nupādāniyā, siyā anupādin‍nupādāniyā| dvāyatanā siyā upādin‍nupādāniyā, siyā anupādin‍nupādāniyā, siyā anupādin‍naanupādāniyā|

dasa  dhātuyau upādin‍nupādāniyā| saddadhātu anupādin‍nupādāniyā| pañ‍ca dhātuyau siyā upādin‍nupādāniyā, siyā anupādin‍nupādāniyā| dvai dhātuyau siyā upādin‍nupādāniyā, siyā anupādin‍nupādāniyā, siyā anupādin‍naanupādāniyā|

samudayasac‍caṃ anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ| dvai sac‍cā anupādin‍naanupādāniyā| dukkhasac‍caṃ siyā upādin‍nupādāniyaṃ, siyā anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ|

navindriyā upādin‍nupādāniyā| daumanassindriyaṃ anupādin‍nupādāniyaṃ| tīṇindriyā anupādin‍naanupādāniyā| navindriyā siyā upādin‍nupādāniyā, siyā anupādin‍nupādāniyā, siyā anupādin‍naanupādāniyā| tayau akusalahaitū anupādin‍nupādāniyā| tayau kusalahaitū siyā anupādin‍nupādāniyā, siyā anupādin‍naanupādāniyā| tayau abyākatahaitū siyā upādin‍nupādāniyā, siyā anupādin‍nupādāniyā, siyā anupādin‍naanupādāniyā|

kabaḷīkārau āhārau siyā upādin‍nupādāniyau, siyā anupādin‍nupādāniyau| tayau āhārā siyā upādin‍nupādāniyā, siyā anupādin‍nupādāniyā, siyā anupādin‍naanupādāniyā|

pañ‍ca phassā upādin‍nupādāniyā| manaudhātusamphassau siyā upādin‍nupādāniyau, siyā anupādin‍nupādāniyau| manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassau siyā upādin‍nupādāniyau, siyā anupādin‍nupādāniyau, siyā anupādin‍naanupādāniyau| pañ‍ca vaidanā… pañ‍ca sañ‍ñā… pañ‍ca caitanā… pañ‍ca cittā upādin‍nupādāniyā| manaudhātu siyā upādin‍nupādāniyā, siyā anupādin‍nupādāniyā| manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu siyā upādin‍nupādāniyā, siyā anupādin‍nupādāniyā, siyā anupādin‍naanupādāniyā|

5. vitak‍kattikaṃ

1042. pañ‍can‍naṃ  khandhānaṃ kati savitak‍kasavicārā, kati avitak‍kavicāramattā, kati  avitak‍kaavicārā…pai॰… sattan‍naṃ cittānaṃ kati savitak‍kasavicārā, kati avitak‍kavicāramattā, kati avitak‍kaavicārā?

rūpakkhandhau avitak‍kaavicārau| tayau khandhā siyā savitak‍kasavicārā, siyā avitak‍kavicāramattā, siyā avitak‍kaavicārā| saṅkhārakkhandhau siyā savitak‍kasavicārau, siyā avitak‍kavicāramattau, siyā avitak‍kaavicārau siyā na vattabbau savitak‍kasavicārautipi, avitak‍kavicāramattautipi, avitak‍kaavicārautipi|

dasāyatanā avitak‍kaavicārā| manāyatanaṃ siyā savitak‍kasavicāraṃ, siyā avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ, siyā avitak‍kaavicāraṃ| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā savitak‍kasavicāraṃ, siyā avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ, siyā avitak‍kaavicāraṃ, siyā na vattabbaṃ savitak‍kasavicārantipi, avitak‍kavicāramattantipi, avitak‍kaavicārantipi|

pan‍narasa dhātuyau avitak‍kaavicārā| manaudhātu  savitak‍kasavicārā| manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu siyā savitak‍kasavicārā, siyā avitak‍kavicāramattā, siyā avitak‍kaavicārā| dhammadhātu siyā savitak‍kasavicārā, siyā avitak‍kavicāramattā, siyā avitak‍kaavicārā, siyā na vattabbā savitak‍kasavicārātipi, avitak‍kavicāramattātipi, avitak‍kaavicārātipi|

samudayasac‍caṃ savitak‍kasavicāraṃ| niraudhasac‍caṃ avitak‍kaavicāraṃ| maggasac‍caṃ siyā savitak‍kasavicāraṃ, siyā avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ, siyā avitak‍kaavicāraṃ| dukkhasac‍caṃ siyā savitak‍kasavicāraṃ, siyā avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ, siyā avitak‍kaavicāraṃ, siyā na vattabbaṃ savitak‍kasavicārantipi, avitak‍kavicāramattantipi, avitak‍kaavicārantipi|

navindriyā avitak‍kaavicārā| daumanassindriyaṃ savitak‍kasavicāraṃ| upaikkhindriyaṃ siyā savitak‍kasavicāraṃ, siyā avitak‍kaavicāraṃ| aikādasindriyā siyā savitak‍kasavicārā, siyā avitak‍kavicāramattā, siyā avitak‍kaavicārā|

tayau  akusalahaitū savitak‍kasavicārā| cha haitū siyā savitak‍kasavicārā, siyā avitak‍kavicāramattā, siyā avitak‍kaavicārā| kabaḷīkārau āhārau avitak‍kaavicārau| tayau āhārā siyā savitak‍kasavicārā, siyā avitak‍kavicāramattā, siyā avitak‍kaavicārā| pañ‍ca phassā avitak‍kaavicārā| manaudhātusamphassau savitak‍kasavicārau| manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassau siyā savitak‍kasavicārau, siyā avitak‍kavicāramattau, siyā avitak‍kaavicārau| pañ‍ca vaidanā… pañ‍ca sañ‍ñā… pañ‍ca caitanā… pañ‍ca cittā avitak‍kaavicārā manaudhātu savitak‍kasavicārā, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu siyā savitak‍kasavicārā, siyā avitak‍kavicāramattā, siyā avitak‍kaavicārā|

(1) rūpadukaṃ

1043. pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ kati rūpā, kati arūpā…pai॰… sattan‍naṃ cittānaṃ kati rūpā, kati arūpā?

rūpakkhandhau rūpaṃ| cattārau khandhā arūpā| dasāyatanā  rūpā| manāyatanaṃ arūpaṃ| dhammāyatanaṃ siyā rūpaṃ, siyā arūpaṃ| dasa dhātuyau rūpā| satta dhātuyau arūpā| dhammadhātu siyā rūpā, siyā arūpā| tīṇi sac‍cāni arūpā| dukkhasac‍caṃ siyā rūpaṃ, siyā arūpaṃ| sattindriyā rūpā| cuddasindriyā arūpā| jīvitindriyaṃ siyā rūpaṃ, siyā arūpaṃ| nava haitū arūpā| kabaḷīkārau āhārau rūpaṃ| tayau āhārā arūpā| satta phassā arūpā| satta vaidanā… satta sañ‍ñā… satta caitanā satta cittā arūpā|

(2) laukiyadukaṃ

1044. pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ kati laukiyā, kati laukuttarā? dvādasan‍naṃ āyatanānaṃ kati laukiyā, kati laukuttarā? aṭṭhārasan‍naṃ dhātūnaṃ kati laukiyā, kati laukuttarā? catun‍naṃ sac‍cānaṃ kati laukiyā, kati laukuttarā…pai॰… sattan‍naṃ cittānaṃ kati laukiyā, kati laukuttarā?

rūpakkhandhau laukiyau| cattārau khandhā siyā laukiyā, siyā laukuttarā| dasāyatanā laukiyā| dvai āyatanā siyā laukiyā, siyā laukuttarā| sauḷasa dhātuyau laukiyā| dvai dhātuyau siyā laukiyā, siyā laukuttarā| dvai sac‍cā laukiyā| dvai sac‍cā laukuttarā|

dasindriyā laukiyā| tīṇindriyā laukuttarā| navindriyā siyā laukiyā, siyā laukuttarā| tayau akusalahaitū laukiyā| cha haitū siyā laukiyā, siyā laukuttarā| kabaḷīkārau āhārau laukiyau| tayau āhārā siyā laukiyā, siyā laukuttarā| cha phassā laukiyā| manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassau siyā laukiyau, siyā laukuttarau| cha vaidanā laukiyā| manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassajā vaidanā siyā laukiyā, siyā laukuttarā| cha sañ‍ñā laukiyā| manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassajā sañ‍ñā siyā laukiyā, siyā laukuttarā| cha caitanā laukiyā| manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātusamphassajā caitanā siyā laukiyā, siyā laukuttarā| cha cittā laukiyā| manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu siyā laukiyā, siyā laukuttarāti|

abhiñ‍ñā dvai sārammaṇā, diṭṭhā kusalavaidanā|

vipākā ca upādin‍nā, vitak‍kaṃ rūpalaukiyāti||

dhammahadayavibhaṅgau niṭṭhitau|

vibhaṅgapakaraṇaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|
